==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/anim_sync.cpp (sha256 945aa3b40b87edb2d2e7a7834a8d31fc3b41e5dcc5311e68091e5dd3ad523f44) ====================
// Experimental i_animch synchronization. A checked, instance-local vtable copy holds back
// local setters for a remote-owned soldier only after a recent owner snapshot has arrived.
// The snapshot contains selected scalars and a group name, never pointers. Game ABI and thread
// assumptions remain unverified without the game; unknown layouts are refused.
#include "common.h"
#include "anim_wire.h"
#include "env_value.h"
#include "hooks.h"
#include <atomic>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>

namespace {
typedef uintptr_t (*U32Fn)(Object *, u32, u32);
typedef uintptr_t (*GroupFn)(Object *, u32, const char *);
typedef void (*FloatFn)(Object *, u32, float);
typedef void (*VoidU32Fn)(Object *, u32, u32);
typedef void (*VoidFn)(Object *, u32);
typedef void (*BoolFn)(Object *, u32, bool);
typedef void (*VectorFn)(Object *, u32, bool, float, float, float);
static const unsigned indices[] = {4,8,10,11,12,13,14,16,20,21,26};
static void *originals[41], *table_copy[42]; // copy[-1] preserves MSVC's complete-object locator
static Object *anim;
static ObjMan *manager;
static void **original_table;
static DWORD thread_id, last_scan, wait_at;
static u32 wait_seq;
static std::atomic<bool> installed;
static std::atomic<unsigned long long> other_thread;
static bool attempted, enabled = true;
static unsigned long long sent, applied, held, refused, full;
struct Index { u64 key; bool remote, ambiguous; };
struct Local { unsigned char last[anim_wire::snapshot_size]; DWORD at; bool has; };
static std::unordered_map<u32, Index> by_index;
static std::unordered_map<u64, Local> local;
static std::unordered_map<u64, DWORD> received_at;

static bool writable(void *p, size_t n)
{
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION m;
    if (!p || !VirtualQuery(p, &m, sizeof m) || m.State != MEM_COMMIT || (m.Protect & PAGE_GUARD)) return false;
    DWORD prot = m.Protect & 0xff;
    if (prot != PAGE_READWRITE && prot != PAGE_WRITECOPY && prot != PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE && prot != PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY) return false;
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t)p, b = (uintptr_t)m.BaseAddress;
    return a >= b && a - b <= m.RegionSize && n <= m.RegionSize - (a - b);
}
static bool human(Object *o, u64 *key, u32 *index)
{
    if (!readable(o, 0x1efc) || o->objman != manager || !readable(o->iface, 8) ||
        memcmp(o->iface, "i_human", 8) || !memchr(o->full, 0, sizeof o->full) ||
        !session_unit_key(o, key) || (*key >> 32) != 2 || session_unit(*key) != o) return false;
    memcpy(index, (char *)o + 0x1d60, 4);
    return *index < anim_wire::max_index;
}
static unsigned char *instance(u32 index)
{
    if (!anim || index >= anim_wire::max_index || !readable(anim, 0x148)) return nullptr;
    unsigned char *base = nullptr; memcpy(&base, (char *)anim + 0x140, sizeof base);
    uintptr_t at = (uintptr_t)base + (size_t)index * anim_wire::instance_size;
    if (!base || at < (uintptr_t)base || !readable((void *)at, anim_wire::instance_size)) return nullptr;
    return (unsigned char *)at;
}
// A group pointer points 0x3e bytes after its inline name in the checked engine layout.
static bool group_name(const unsigned char *inst, char out[28])
{
    memset(out, 0, 28);
    void *group = nullptr; memcpy(&group, inst + 0x70, sizeof group);
    if (!group) return true;
    uintptr_t p = (uintptr_t)group;
    if (p < 0x3e || !readable(group, 16) || !readable((const void *)(p - 0x3e), 28)) return false;
    const char *name = (const char *)(p - 0x3e);
    size_t n = 0;
    while (n < 28 && name[n]) {
        if ((unsigned char)name[n] < 0x20 || (unsigned char)name[n] > 0x7e) return false;
        ++n;
    }
    if (!n || n == 28) return false;
    memcpy(out, name, n);
    return true;
}
static bool block(u32 index)
{
    if (GetCurrentThreadId() != thread_id) {
        other_thread.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return false;
    }
    if (!installed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || !enabled || !session_connected() ||
        !(session_peer_features() & PEER_ANIM_SYNC) || !xlate_ready()) return false;
    auto it = by_index.find(index);
    if (it == by_index.end() || !it->second.remote || it->second.ambiguous) return false;
    auto ready = received_at.find(it->second.key);
    if (ready == received_at.end() || (DWORD)(GetTickCount() - ready->second) >= 3000) return false;
    ++held;
    return true;
}
static uintptr_t slot4(Object *o, u32 i, u32 v) { return block(i) ? 0 : ((U32Fn)originals[4])(o,i,v); }
static void slot8(Object *o, u32 i, float v) { if (!block(i)) ((FloatFn)originals[8])(o,i,v); }
static uintptr_t slot10(Object *o, u32 i, u32 v) { return block(i) ? 0 : ((U32Fn)originals[10])(o,i,v); }
static uintptr_t slot11(Object *o, u32 i, const char *v) { return block(i) ? 0 : ((GroupFn)originals[11])(o,i,v); }
static void slot12(Object *o, u32 i, u32 v) { if (!block(i)) ((VoidU32Fn)originals[12])(o,i,v); }
static void slot13(Object *o, u32 i) { if (!block(i)) ((VoidFn)originals[13])(o,i); }
static void slot14(Object *o, u32 i, bool v, float x, float y, float z) { if (!block(i)) ((VectorFn)originals[14])(o,i,v,x,y,z); }
static void slot16(Object *o, u32 i, float v) { if (!block(i)) ((FloatFn)originals[16])(o,i,v); }
static void slot20(Object *o, u32 i, float v) { if (!block(i)) ((FloatFn)originals[20])(o,i,v); }
static void slot21(Object *o, u32 i, u32 v) { if (!block(i)) ((VoidU32Fn)originals[21])(o,i,v); }
static void slot26(Object *o, u32 i, bool v) { if (!block(i)) ((BoolFn)originals[26])(o,i,v); }
static void *const hooks[] = {(void *)slot4,(void *)slot8,(void *)slot10,(void *)slot11,(void *)slot12,(void *)slot13,(void *)slot14,(void *)slot16,(void *)slot20,(void *)slot21,(void *)slot26};

// Code signatures are checked before the matching table is used. These cover every hook slot.
struct Sig { unsigned rva, size; unsigned char bytes[32]; };
static const Sig sigs[] = {
 {0xa4040,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x44,0x89,0x84,0x02,0xa8,0,0}},
 {0xa1350,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0xf3,0x0f,0x11,0x94,0x02,0xd4,0}},
 {0xa13b0,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x44,0x89,0x84,0x02,0x00,0x01,0}},
 {0xa4ec0,24,{0x48,0x89,0x5c,0x24,0x08,0x57,0x48,0x83,0xec,0x60,0x8b,0xc2,0x4d,0x8b,0xc8,0x48,0x8d,0x3c,0x80,0x48,0xc1,0xe7,0x06,0x48}},
 {0xa4ff0,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x48,0x03,0x91,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0x8b,0x42,0x70,0x48,0x85,0xc0}},
 {0xa13d0,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x48,0x03,0x91,0x40,0x01,0,0,0xc7,0x82,0x04,0x01,0,0,0}},
 {0xa5030,24,{0xf3,0x0f,0x10,0x44,0x24,0x28,0xf3,0x0f,0x10,0x4c,0x24,0x30,0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x48,0x03}},
 {0xa1420,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0xf3,0x0f,0x11,0x94,0x02,0x28,0x01}},
 {0xa1440,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0xf3,0x0f,0x11,0x94,0x02,0x2c,0x01}},
 {0xa1460,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x44,0x89,0x84,0x02,0x14,0x01}},
 {0xa14c0,24,{0x8b,0xc2,0x48,0x8d,0x14,0x80,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x40,0x01,0,0,0x48,0xc1,0xe2,0x06,0x44,0x88,0x84,0x02,0x39,0x01}}
};
static_assert(sizeof indices / sizeof *indices == sizeof sigs / sizeof *sigs, "animation hook count");

static bool invoke_group(GroupFn fn, Object *a, u32 index, const char *name)
{
    __try { fn(a, index, name); return true; }
    __except (GetExceptionCode() == EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION ? EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER : EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH) { return false; }
}
static bool wait_or_refuse(u32 seq)
{
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    if (seq != wait_seq) { wait_seq = seq; wait_at = now; return false; }
    if ((DWORD)(now - wait_at) < 5000) return false;
    ++refused; wait_seq = 0; mp_log("anim: snapshot %u refused after waiting for its soldier", seq); return true;
}
} // namespace

void anim_install(ObjMan *om)
{
    if (attempted || !GetModuleHandleA("adv_game.codelib") || !readable(om, sizeof *om) ||
        !readable(om->vtbl, 10 * sizeof(void *)) || !om->vtbl[9]) return;
    Object *object = engine_find(om, "i_animch", "animch");
    if (!object) return; // the interface may appear later during tactical loading
    attempted = true; manager = om; thread_id = GetCurrentThreadId(); enabled = true;
    int value = 0;
    if (env_integer("MP_SYNC_ANIM", &value) && !value) { enabled = false; mp_log("anim: local animation only (MP_SYNC_ANIM=0)"); return; }
    if (!hook_image_identity("adv_game.codelib", 0x66f2d3a9, 0x178000)) {
        enabled = false; mp_log("anim: unrecognised game image/layout; hook refused"); return;
    }
    if (!readable(object, 0x148) || object->objman != om) { enabled = false; mp_log("anim: invalid i_animch object; hook refused"); return; }
    HookVtableMember members[sizeof indices / sizeof *indices];
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof indices / sizeof *indices; ++i) {
        HookSig sig = {sigs[i].rva, sigs[i].bytes, sigs[i].size};
        char *code = hook_resolve_checked("adv_game.codelib", &sig, 1, "animation setter");
        if (!code) { enabled = false; mp_log("anim: a setter is unrecognised; all animation hooks refused"); return; }
        members[i] = {indices[i], code};
    }
    void **table = hook_vtable_checked("adv_game.codelib", 0x12f128, members, (unsigned)(sizeof members / sizeof *members), "animation");
    if (!table || object->vtbl != table || !readable(table - 1, sizeof table_copy)) {
        enabled = false; mp_log("anim: interface table does not match the checked table; hook refused"); return;
    }
    memcpy(table_copy, table - 1, sizeof table_copy);
    memcpy(originals, table, sizeof originals);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof indices / sizeof *indices; ++i) table_copy[1 + indices[i]] = hooks[i];
    anim = object; original_table = table;
    if (!hook_swap_vtable_slot((void **)&object->vtbl, table, table_copy + 1)) {
        anim = nullptr; original_table = nullptr; enabled = false;
        mp_log("anim: interface table changed or could not be replaced; hook refused"); return;
    }
    installed.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    mp_log("anim: experimental instance-local hooks installed; enabled only when both peers and number tables are ready");
}

bool anim_available(void) { return enabled && installed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && xlate_ready(); }

void anim_frame(ObjMan *om, DWORD now)
{
    if (!anim_available() || !session_connected() || !(session_peer_features() & PEER_ANIM_SYNC) || om != manager ||
        GetCurrentThreadId() != thread_id || (last_scan && (DWORD)(now - last_scan) < 200)) return;
    last_scan = now ? now : 1;
    if (!readable(om, 0x100) || !readable(anim, 0x148) || anim->vtbl != table_copy + 1) return;
    u32 count = 0; Object **objects = all_objects(om, &count);
    if (count > 1000000 || (count && !readable(objects, (size_t)count * sizeof *objects))) return;
    by_index.clear();
    std::unordered_set<u64> alive;
    struct Candidate { Object *object; u64 key; u32 index; };
    std::vector<Candidate> owned;
    for (u32 i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        u64 key = 0; u32 index = 0;
        if (!human(objects[i], &key, &index)) continue;
        if (alive.size() >= 20000) { ++full; break; }
        alive.insert(key);
        bool ours = session_owns(objects[i]);
        auto inserted = by_index.emplace(index, Index{key, !ours, false});
        if (!inserted.second) { inserted.first->second.ambiguous = true; continue; }
        if (ours) owned.push_back({objects[i], key, index});
    }
    // Discover duplicate indices before emitting either soldier's state.
    for (const Candidate &c : owned) {
        if (by_index[c.index].ambiguous) continue;
        unsigned char *inst = instance(c.index);
        char group[28];
        if (!inst || !group_name(inst, group)) continue;
        ShotRec rec;
        anim_wire::encode(rec, c.key, inst, group[0] ? group : nullptr, (const unsigned char *)c.object);
        if (!anim_wire::valid(rec)) continue;
        Local &l = local[c.key];
        bool changed = !l.has || memcmp(l.last, rec.launch, sizeof l.last) != 0;
        if (l.has && (DWORD)(now - l.at) < (changed ? 200u : 1000u)) continue;
        if (!shots_queue_latest(rec, anim_wire::queue_limit)) { ++full; continue; }
        memcpy(l.last, rec.launch, sizeof l.last); l.has = true; l.at = now; ++sent;
    }
    for (auto it = local.begin(); it != local.end();) {
        if (!alive.count(it->first)) it = local.erase(it); else ++it;
    }
    for (auto it = received_at.begin(); it != received_at.end();) {
        if (!alive.count(it->first)) it = received_at.erase(it); else ++it;
    }
}

bool anim_received(const ShotRec &s, ObjMan *om)
{
    if (!anim_wire::valid(s)) { ++refused; mp_log("anim: malformed snapshot %u refused", s.seq); return true; }
    if (!installed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || !enabled || om != manager || GetCurrentThreadId() != thread_id) {
        ++refused; mp_log("anim: snapshot %u refused: hook/thread unavailable", s.seq); return true;
    }
    Object *o = session_unit(s.shooter);
    if (!o) return wait_or_refuse(s.seq);
    u64 key = 0; u32 index = 0;
    if (!human(o, &key, &index) || key != s.shooter || session_owns(o)) { ++refused; mp_log("anim: snapshot %u refused: soldier/ownership mismatch", s.seq); return true; }
    auto slot = by_index.find(index);
    if (slot == by_index.end()) return wait_or_refuse(s.seq);
    if (slot->second.ambiguous || slot->second.key != key || !slot->second.remote ||
        !readable(anim, 0x148) || anim->vtbl != table_copy + 1) {
        ++refused; mp_log("anim: snapshot %u refused: ambiguous index or changed interface", s.seq); return true;
    }
    unsigned char *inst = instance(index);
    if (!inst || !writable(inst, anim_wire::instance_size) || !writable((char *)o + 0x1210, 12) ||
        !writable((char *)o + 0x1e70, 4) || !writable((char *)o + 0x1eac, 4) || !writable((char *)o + 0x1ef8, 4)) {
        ++refused; mp_log("anim: snapshot %u refused: animation memory not writable", s.seq); return true;
    }
    char current[28];
    if (!group_name(inst, current)) { ++refused; return true; }
    const unsigned char *p = s.launch;
    if (p[50] && strcmp(current, (const char *)p + 52)) {
        if (!invoke_group((GroupFn)originals[11], anim, index, (const char *)p + 52)) {
            enabled = false; ++refused; mp_log("anim: engine group setter faulted; synchronization disabled (effects may be partial)"); return true;
        }
        inst = instance(index);
        if (!inst || !writable(inst, anim_wire::instance_size) || !group_name(inst, current) || strcmp(current, (const char *)p + 52)) {
            ++refused; mp_log("anim: snapshot %u refused: local group not found", s.seq); return true;
        }
    }
    // Check the owner's sequence number against the local group's size before applying it.
    u32 active = 0, frame = 0, count = 0;
    memcpy(&active, p + 8, 4); memcpy(&frame, p + 12, 4);
    void *group = nullptr; memcpy(&group, inst + 0x70, sizeof group);
    if (!active || !p[50] || !group || !readable(group, 16)) { active = 0; frame = 0xffffffffu; }
    else { memcpy(&count, (char *)group + 12, 4); if (frame >= count) { active = 0; frame = 0xffffffffu; } }
    memcpy(inst + 0xa8, p + 4, 4); memcpy(inst + 0x100, p + 16, 4); memcpy(inst + 0x114, p + 20, 4);
    memcpy(inst + 0xd4, p + 24, 4); memcpy(inst + 0x11c, p + 28, 12); memcpy(inst + 0x128, p + 40, 8);
    inst[0x136] = p[48]; inst[0x139] = p[49];
    memcpy(inst + 0x104, &active, 4); memcpy(inst + 0x10c, &frame, 4);
    u32 state = 0; memcpy(&state, p + 80, 4);
    if (state < 8) memcpy((char *)o + 0x1e70, &state, 4);
    memcpy((char *)o + 0x1210, p + 84, 12); memcpy((char *)o + 0x1eac, p + 96, 4); memcpy((char *)o + 0x1ef8, p + 100, 4);
    received_at[key] = GetTickCount(); wait_seq = 0; ++applied;
    return true;
}

void anim_report(void)
{
    unsigned long long other = other_thread.exchange(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (sent || applied || held || refused || full || other)
        mp_log("anim: 5 s: %llu snapshots queued, %llu applied, %llu local setters held, %llu refused, %llu deferred by queue limit, %llu calls on other threads passed through", sent, applied, held, refused, full, other);
    sent = applied = held = refused = full = 0;
}

void anim_reset_peer(void)
{
    wait_seq = 0; wait_at = last_scan = 0;
    by_index.clear(); local.clear(); received_at.clear();
}

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
// Install fake functions and an in-memory fixture only; never compiled into the game module.
void anim_test_engine(ObjMan *om, Object *object)
{
    manager = om; anim = object; thread_id = GetCurrentThreadId(); attempted = enabled = true;
    original_table = object->vtbl;
    memcpy(originals, original_table, sizeof originals);
    memcpy(table_copy, original_table - 1, sizeof table_copy);
    object->vtbl = table_copy + 1;
    last_scan = wait_seq = 0; by_index.clear(); local.clear(); received_at.clear();
    installed.store(true);
}
uintptr_t anim_test_slot4(Object *o, u32 index, u32 value) { return slot4(o, index, value); }
void anim_test_stale(u64 key) { received_at[key] = GetTickCount() - 3001; }
#endif

void anim_stop(void)
{
    installed.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    if (anim && original_table && readable(anim, sizeof *anim) &&
        !hook_swap_vtable_slot((void **)&anim->vtbl, table_copy + 1, original_table))
        mp_log("anim: interface table changed by another module; not overwritten");
    anim = nullptr; manager = nullptr; original_table = nullptr;
    attempted = false; enabled = true; last_scan = wait_seq = 0;
    by_index.clear(); local.clear(); received_at.clear();
}

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/anim_wire.h (sha256 f850948fbcd283166b6b2e5f6ad310131d9cfc8018a9d8c0bf038baca1269b17) ====================
// Selected scalar animation values. The 0x140-byte engine instance contains pointers, so never
// put it on the wire wholesale. This layout follows the v20 snapshot fields, not its wire format.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>

namespace anim_wire {
static const unsigned kind = 8;
static const size_t snapshot_size = 104;
static const size_t instance_size = 0x140;
static const size_t queue_limit = 2048;
static const unsigned max_index = 100000;

inline bool finite_field(const unsigned char *p)
{
    float v; memcpy(&v, p, sizeof v);
    return v >= -1000000.f && v <= 1000000.f; // also rejects NaN and infinity
}
inline bool valid(const ShotRec &s)
{
    if (s.kind != kind || s.flags || s.has_pos || s.pad || (s.shooter >> 32) != 2 ||
        s.ammo || s.weapon || s.desc3 || s.arg6 || s.ammo_code || s.obj2 || s.obj5) return false;
    const unsigned char *p = s.launch;
    uint32_t id = 0; memcpy(&id, p, sizeof id);
    if (id != (uint32_t)s.shooter || p[50] > 1 || p[51]) return false;
    uint32_t active = 0; memcpy(&active, p + 8, sizeof active);
    if (active > 1) return false;
    const size_t floats[] = {24,28,32,36,40,44,84,88,92,96,100};
    for (size_t off : floats) if (!finite_field(p + off)) return false;
    if (p[50]) {
        size_t n = 0;
        while (n < 28 && p[52 + n]) {
            if (p[52 + n] < 0x20 || p[52 + n] > 0x7e) return false;
            ++n;
        }
        if (!n || n >= 28) return false;
        for (size_t i = n + 1; i < 28; ++i) if (p[52 + i]) return false;
    } else for (size_t i = 52; i < 80; ++i) if (p[i]) return false;
    const unsigned char *checks = (const unsigned char *)s.ammo_check;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof s.ammo_check; ++i) if (checks[i]) return false;
    const unsigned char *position = (const unsigned char *)s.pos;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof s.pos; ++i) if (position[i]) return false;
    for (size_t i = snapshot_size; i < sizeof s.launch; ++i) if (p[i]) return false;
    return true;
}

// Only scalar fields are copied. Group names and soldier extras are supplied separately after
// the caller has independently validated the relevant engine memory.
inline void encode(ShotRec &s, uint64_t key, const unsigned char *instance,
                   const char *group, const unsigned char *human)
{
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof s);
    s.kind = kind; s.shooter = key;
    unsigned char *p = s.launch;
    uint32_t id = (uint32_t)key; memcpy(p, &id, 4);
    memcpy(p + 4, instance + 0xa8, 4);
    memcpy(p + 8, instance + 0x104, 4);
    memcpy(p + 12, instance + 0x10c, 4);
    memcpy(p + 16, instance + 0x100, 4);
    memcpy(p + 20, instance + 0x114, 4);
    memcpy(p + 24, instance + 0xd4, 4);
    memcpy(p + 28, instance + 0x11c, 12);
    memcpy(p + 40, instance + 0x128, 8);
    p[48] = instance[0x136]; p[49] = instance[0x139];
    if (group) {
        size_t n = 0; while (n < 28 && group[n]) ++n;
        if (n && n < 28) { p[50] = 1; memcpy(p + 52, group, n); }
    }
    memcpy(p + 80, human + 0x1e70, 4);
    memcpy(p + 84, human + 0x1210, 12);
    memcpy(p + 96, human + 0x1eac, 4);
    memcpy(p + 100, human + 0x1ef8, 4);
}
} // namespace anim_wire
==================== END development/net-v21/src/anim_wire.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/atomic_file.h (sha256 83ce882831d943348f84a9629ae0af28ee06aa7d3b86f3bcebe898d0d5ca2210) ====================
// Same-directory Windows replacement for M4 battle descriptions and recovery markers.
// On failure the destination is never opened for writing or truncated. A unique temporary file is
// fully written and flushed before a single replacement rename; failed temporaries are removed.
#pragma once
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <string>

namespace atomic_file {
// Create a durable file without ever replacing a prior backup/marker. A failed partial file is removed.
inline bool create_new(const char *path, const void *data, size_t size)
{
    if (!path || !*path || (!data && size)) { SetLastError(ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER); return false; }
    HANDLE file = CreateFileA(path, GENERIC_WRITE, 0, nullptr, CREATE_NEW, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;
    DWORD error = ERROR_SUCCESS;
    const unsigned char *bytes = (const unsigned char *)data;
    size_t at = 0;
    while (at < size) {
        DWORD wanted = size - at > 0x7fffffffu ? 0x7fffffffu : (DWORD)(size - at), written = 0;
        if (!WriteFile(file, bytes + at, wanted, &written, nullptr)) {
            error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT; break;
        }
        if (!written || written > wanted) { error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT; break; }
        at += written;
    }
    if (!error && !FlushFileBuffers(file)) { error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT; }
    if (!CloseHandle(file) && !error) { error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_INVALID_HANDLE; }
    if (error) { DeleteFileA(path); SetLastError(error); return false; }
    return true;
}

inline bool replace(const char *path, const void *data, size_t size)
{
    if (!path || !*path || (!data && size)) { SetLastError(ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER); return false; }
    static volatile LONG serial = 0;
    HANDLE file = INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE;
    std::string temporary;
    for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 32; ++attempt) {
        const unsigned long ticket = (unsigned long)InterlockedIncrement(&serial);
        temporary = std::string(path) + ".mp_tmp_" + std::to_string((unsigned long)GetCurrentProcessId()) +
                    "_" + std::to_string(ticket);
        file = CreateFileA(temporary.c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE, 0, nullptr, CREATE_NEW, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
        if (file != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) break;
        DWORD e = GetLastError();
        if (e != ERROR_FILE_EXISTS && e != ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS) return false;
    }
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) { SetLastError(ERROR_FILE_EXISTS); return false; }
    DWORD error = ERROR_SUCCESS;
    const unsigned char *bytes = (const unsigned char *)data;
    size_t at = 0;
    while (at < size) {
        size_t left = size - at;
        DWORD wanted = left > 0x7fffffffu ? 0x7fffffffu : (DWORD)left;
        DWORD written = 0;
        if (!WriteFile(file, bytes + at, wanted, &written, nullptr)) {
            error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT; break;
        }
        if (!written || written > wanted) { error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT; break; }
        at += written;
    }
    if (!error && !FlushFileBuffers(file)) { error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT; }
    if (!CloseHandle(file) && !error) { error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_INVALID_HANDLE; }
    if (!error && !MoveFileExA(temporary.c_str(), path, MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING | MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH)) {
        error = GetLastError(); if (!error) error = ERROR_WRITE_FAULT;
    }
    if (error) {
        DeleteFileA(temporary.c_str());
        SetLastError(error);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/atomic_file.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/basic_network.cpp (sha256 1f757f9c3d3c73e8ad5789814dde8883f436fbfe5ded20eaeabc435c754ed5ef) ====================
// basic_network.codelib -- multiplayer module for GRAVITECH3 v6.00 (GTMF build 3598).
// Fills the "i_network" slot the engine reserves. Stage 2: proper command table, per-frame tick,
// and a survey of every engine object (commands + handler addresses) written to out\mp_objects_<program>.txt.
#include "common.h"
#include "netlog.h"
#include "input_imports.h"
#include <intrin.h>
#include <malloc.h>
#include <share.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- logging
static FILE *g_log;
static CRITICAL_SECTION g_cs;
char g_out_dir[MAX_PATH];
char g_program[32] = "opgame";

static void log_open(HMODULE self)
{
    GetModuleFileNameA(self, g_out_dir, MAX_PATH);
    // <root>\bin\rel\x64\basic_network.codelib -> <root>\out
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        char *s = strrchr(g_out_dir, '\\');
        if (!s) break;
        *s = 0;
    }
    strcat_s(g_out_dir, "\\out");
    // one log per engine program: the operational and tactical modes are separate processes
    const char *prog = strstr(GetCommandLineA(), "root\\programs\\");
    if (prog) {
        prog += 14;
        size_t n = strcspn(prog, ".,\" ");
        if (n && n < sizeof g_program) { memcpy(g_program, prog, n); g_program[n] = 0; }
    }
    char path[MAX_PATH];
    sprintf_s(path, "%s\\basic_network_%s_%lu.log", g_out_dir, g_program, GetCurrentProcessId());
    InitializeCriticalSection(&g_cs);
    g_log = _fsopen(path, "w", _SH_DENYNO);
}

void mp_log(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    // Every line also goes to the run log (netlog.h): one file, in order, with the build identity at
    // the top, is what a bug report needs.  The old per-pid file stays, because it is what the
    // published module writes and support instructions still point at it.
    char text[1024];
    va_list a;
    va_start(a, fmt);
    vsprintf_s(text, sizeof text, fmt, a);
    va_end(a);
    if (g_log) {
        SYSTEMTIME t;
        GetLocalTime(&t);
        EnterCriticalSection(&g_cs);
        fprintf(g_log, "%02u:%02u:%02u.%03u [t%05lu] ", t.wHour, t.wMinute, t.wSecond, t.wMilliseconds, GetCurrentThreadId());
        fprintf(g_log, "%s", text);
        fputc('\n', g_log);
        fflush(g_log);
        LeaveCriticalSection(&g_cs);
    }
    netlog_mirror(text);
}

bool readable(const void *p, size_t n)
{
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi;
    if (!p || !VirtualQuery(p, &mbi, sizeof mbi)) return false;
    if (mbi.State != MEM_COMMIT || (mbi.Protect & (PAGE_NOACCESS | PAGE_GUARD))) return false;
    return (const char *)p + n <= (const char *)mbi.BaseAddress + mbi.RegionSize;
}

static const char *safe_str(const void *p, char *buf, size_t n)
{
    if (!readable(p, 1)) return "<bad ptr>";
    size_t i = 0;
    const unsigned char *s = (const unsigned char *)p;
    for (; i + 1 < n && readable(s + i, 1) && s[i]; i++) {
        if (s[i] < 0x20 || s[i] > 0x7e) return "<not text>";
        buf[i] = (char)s[i];
    }
    buf[i] = 0;
    return buf;
}

static void log_hex(const char *what, const void *p, size_t n)
{
    char line[3 * 64 + 1];
    if (!readable(p, n)) { mp_log("%s: %p <unreadable>", what, p); return; }
    for (size_t o = 0; o < n; o += 32) {
        size_t k = n - o < 32 ? n - o : 32, w = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < k; i++)
            w += sprintf_s(line + w, sizeof line - w, "%02x%s", ((const unsigned char *)p)[o + i], (i & 7) == 7 ? " " : "");
        mp_log("%s +0x%02zx: %s", what, o, line);
    }
}

// "adv_game.codelib+0x1234" for an address inside a loaded module
static const char *where(const void *addr, char *buf, size_t n)
{
    HMODULE m = 0;
    if (!GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT, (LPCSTR)addr, &m) || !m) {
        sprintf_s(buf, n, "%p", addr);
        return buf;
    }
    char path[MAX_PATH];
    GetModuleFileNameA(m, path, MAX_PATH);
    const char *base = strrchr(path, '\\');
    sprintf_s(buf, n, "%s+0x%llx", base ? base + 1 : path, (u64)((const char *)addr - (const char *)m));
    return buf;
}

// Switches, set from the environment for now (a network session will set g_lockstep itself):
//   MP_LOCKSTEP=1  fixed frame time and locked game speed, as needed for step-identical simulation
//   MP_DEBUG=1     object survey, per-second unit fingerprints, random-number probe
static bool g_lockstep, g_debug;
static const u64 k_start_frame = 4500;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- object survey
static const struct { const char *iface, *name; } k_objects[] = {
#include "objects.inc"
};

static void survey(ObjMan *om)
{
    char path[MAX_PATH], b1[96], b2[96], b3[64];
    sprintf_s(path, "%s\\mp_objects_%s.txt", g_out_dir, g_program);
    FILE *f = _fsopen(path, "w", _SH_DENYNO);
    if (!f) return;
    int found = 0;
    for (const auto &k : k_objects) {
        Object *o = 0;
        try { o = engine_find(om, k.iface, k.name); } catch (...) { o = 0; }
        if (!o || !readable(o, sizeof(Object))) continue;
        found++;
        fprintf(f, "%s:%s  obj=%p  vtbl=%s  refs=%u flags=%u\n", k.iface, k.name, o, where(o->vtbl, b1, sizeof b1), o->refs, o->flags);
        if (readable(o->vtbl, 24))
            fprintf(f, "  dispatch=%s\n", where(o->vtbl[2], b1, sizeof b1));
        if (o->dep_n < 256 && readable(o->deps, o->dep_n * sizeof(Dep))) {
            fprintf(f, "  deps:");
            for (u32 i = 0; i < o->dep_n; i++)
                fprintf(f, " %s:%s", safe_str(o->deps[i].iface, b2, sizeof b2), safe_str(o->deps[i].name, b3, sizeof b3));
            fprintf(f, "\n");
        }
        CmdTable &t = o->cmds;
        if (t.count < 4096 && readable(t.vals, t.count * 8) && readable(t.keys, t.count * 8))
            for (u32 i = 0; i < t.count; i++)
                fprintf(f, "  cmd %-28s %s\n", safe_str(t.keys[i], b2, sizeof b2), where((void *)t.vals[i], b1, sizeof b1));
    }
    fclose(f);
    mp_log("object survey: %d objects written to %s", found, path);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- random-number probe
// Every module draws from the CRT rand(). Routing those calls through here shows how much
// randomness each simulation step consumes, which is the cheapest fingerprint of "did two
// instances compute the same step".
static int (*g_real_rand)(void);
static void (*g_real_srand)(unsigned);
static volatile LONG64 g_rand_calls, g_rand_sum;
static DWORD g_rand_threads[32];
static volatile LONG g_rand_nthreads;

static void note_thread(void)
{
    DWORD id = GetCurrentThreadId();
    LONG n = g_rand_nthreads;
    for (LONG i = 0; i < n; i++)
        if (g_rand_threads[i] == id) return;
    EnterCriticalSection(&g_cs);
    if (g_rand_nthreads < 32) g_rand_threads[g_rand_nthreads++] = id;
    LeaveCriticalSection(&g_cs);
}

static int hook_rand(void)
{
    int v = g_real_rand();
    InterlockedIncrement64(&g_rand_calls);
    InterlockedAdd64(&g_rand_sum, v);
    note_thread();
    return v;
}

static void hook_srand(unsigned seed)
{
    char b[96];
    mp_log("srand(%u) from %s", seed, where(_ReturnAddress(), b, sizeof b));
    g_real_srand(seed);
}

static int patch_imports(HMODULE mod, void *from, void *to)
{
    return input_imports::replace(mod, from, to);
}

static void hook_random(void)
{
    static const char *mods[] = {
        "adv_game.codelib", "adv_interf.codelib", "adv_opgame.codelib", "adv_physic.codelib", "adv_qbattle.codelib",
        "adv_render.codelib", "base_main.codelib", "base_shell.codelib", "basic_game.codelib", "basic_input.codelib",
        "basic_physic.codelib", "basic_render.codelib", "basic_sound.codelib",
    };
    HMODULE crt = GetModuleHandleA("ucrtbase.dll");
    if (!crt || g_real_rand) return;
    g_real_rand = (int (*)(void))GetProcAddress(crt, "rand");
    g_real_srand = (void (*)(unsigned))GetProcAddress(crt, "srand");
    for (const char *m : mods)
        if (HMODULE h = GetModuleHandleA(m)) {
            int a = patch_imports(h, (void *)g_real_rand, (void *)hook_rand);
            int b = patch_imports(h, (void *)g_real_srand, (void *)hook_srand);
            if (a < 0 || b < 0) mp_log("random probe: could not safely patch %s", m);
            else if (a || b) mp_log("random probe: %s rand x%d srand x%d", m, a, b);
        }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- keep running without focus
// The interface pauses the battle when the window is deactivated. In a network game the other side
// keeps playing, so deactivation is hidden from the engine.
static WNDPROC g_prev_wndproc;
static HWND g_hooked_window;

static LRESULT CALLBACK focus_wndproc(HWND w, UINT m, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp)
{
    if ((m == WM_ACTIVATEAPP && !wp) || (m == WM_ACTIVATE && LOWORD(wp) == WA_INACTIVE) || m == WM_KILLFOCUS)
        return 0;
    if (ui_blocks_input() && ((m >= WM_MOUSEFIRST && m <= WM_MOUSELAST) || (m >= WM_KEYFIRST && m <= WM_KEYLAST)))
        return 0;
    return CallWindowProcA(g_prev_wndproc, w, m, wp, lp);
}

static BOOL CALLBACK find_main_window(HWND w, LPARAM out)
{
    DWORD pid = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(w, &pid);
    if (pid != GetCurrentProcessId() || !IsWindowVisible(w) || GetWindow(w, GW_OWNER)) return TRUE;
    *(HWND *)out = w;
    return FALSE;
}

HWND main_window(void)
{
    HWND w = 0;
    EnumWindows(find_main_window, (LPARAM)&w);
    return w;
}

static void keep_running_unfocused(ObjMan *om)
{
    HWND w = 0;
    if (g_prev_wndproc && g_hooked_window && IsWindow(g_hooked_window)) return;
    g_prev_wndproc = nullptr; g_hooked_window = nullptr;
    // The engine's own "work without focus" switch: i_loader +0x2ac is the setting, i_window vtbl[8] applies it.
    // Without it an inactive window only gets the reduced "napump" frame.
    Object *loader = engine_find(om, "i_loader", "loader"), *window = engine_find(om, "i_window", "window");
    if (loader) ((char *)loader)[0x2ac] = 1;
    if (window) ((void (*)(Object *, char))window->vtbl[8])(window, 1);
    EnumWindows(find_main_window, (LPARAM)&w);
    if (!w) return;
    g_prev_wndproc = (WNDPROC)SetWindowLongPtrA(w, GWLP_WNDPROC, (LONG_PTR)focus_wndproc);
    if (!g_prev_wndproc) { mp_log("window %p: input/window hook failed (%lu)", w, GetLastError()); return; }
    g_hooked_window = w;
    mp_log("window %p: deactivation hidden from the engine", w);
    if (GetForegroundWindow() != w) {
        // already in the background (e.g. a second instance started on top): tell the engine it is active
        CallWindowProcA(g_prev_wndproc, w, WM_ACTIVATEAPP, TRUE, 0);
        CallWindowProcA(g_prev_wndproc, w, WM_ACTIVATE, WA_ACTIVE, 0);
        CallWindowProcA(g_prev_wndproc, w, WM_SETFOCUS, 0, 0);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- test helpers (environment-driven)
// MP_WINDOW=x,y,w,h places the game window, so two instances can sit side by side on one screen.
static void place_window_from_env(void)
{
    char v[64];
    int x, y, w, h;
    HWND win = 0;
    if (!GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_WINDOW", v, sizeof v) || sscanf_s(v, "%d,%d,%d,%d", &x, &y, &w, &h) != 4) return;
    EnumWindows(find_main_window, (LPARAM)&win);
    if (win && SetWindowPos(win, HWND_TOP, x, y, w, h, SWP_NOACTIVATE | SWP_NOZORDER))
        mp_log("window placed at %d,%d %dx%d", x, y, w, h);
}

// MP_PROFILE=1 samples the main thread twice a second and logs where it is, to find what stalls a frame.
static DWORD WINAPI profile_thread(LPVOID main_thread)
{
    char b[96];
    for (int i = 0; i < 240; i++) {
        Sleep(500);
        CONTEXT c;
        c.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_CONTROL | CONTEXT_INTEGER;
        if (SuspendThread((HANDLE)main_thread) == (DWORD)-1) break;
        BOOL ok = GetThreadContext((HANDLE)main_thread, &c);
        u64 stack[48] = {0};
        if (ok) memcpy(stack, (void *)c.Rsp, readable((void *)c.Rsp, sizeof stack) ? sizeof stack : 0);
        ResumeThread((HANDLE)main_thread);
        if (!ok) continue;
        char line[1024];
        size_t w = sprintf_s(line, "profile: rip=%s stack:", where((void *)c.Rip, b, sizeof b));
        int shown = 0;
        for (u64 s : stack) {
            HMODULE m = 0;
            if (shown < 7 && s > 0x10000 && GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT, (LPCSTR)s, &m) && m) {
                w += sprintf_s(line + w, sizeof line - w, " %s", where((void *)s, b, sizeof b));
                shown++;
            }
        }
        mp_log("%s", line);
    }
    return 0;
}

static void start_profiler_from_env(void)
{
    char v[8];
    if (!GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_PROFILE", v, sizeof v)) return;
    HANDLE main_thread = OpenThread(THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME | THREAD_GET_CONTEXT | THREAD_QUERY_INFORMATION, FALSE, GetCurrentThreadId());
    if (main_thread) CloseHandle(CreateThread(0, 0, profile_thread, main_thread, 0, 0));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- input isolation for the multiplayer menu
// While the menu is open the game must not react to the mouse or keyboard. The engine reads them through
// raw input and key-state polling, so those calls are routed through here and blanked while the menu is up.
static UINT (WINAPI *g_real_GetRawInputData)(HRAWINPUT, UINT, LPVOID, PUINT, UINT);
static SHORT (WINAPI *g_real_GetAsyncKeyState)(int);
static SHORT (WINAPI *g_real_GetKeyState)(int);
static BOOL (WINAPI *g_real_GetKeyboardState)(PBYTE);

static UINT WINAPI hook_GetRawInputData(HRAWINPUT h, UINT cmd, LPVOID data, PUINT size, UINT header)
{
    UINT r = g_real_GetRawInputData(h, cmd, data, size, header);
    if (cmd == RID_INPUT && data && r != (UINT)-1 && r >= sizeof(RAWINPUTHEADER) && ui_blocks_input()) {
        RAWINPUT *in = (RAWINPUT *)data;
        if (in->header.dwType == RIM_TYPEMOUSE && r >= offsetof(RAWINPUT, data) + sizeof(RAWMOUSE)) {
            in->data.mouse.usButtonFlags = 0;
            in->data.mouse.usButtonData = 0;
        } else if (in->header.dwType == RIM_TYPEKEYBOARD && r >= offsetof(RAWINPUT, data) + sizeof(RAWKEYBOARD)) {
            in->data.keyboard.VKey = 0xff;
            in->data.keyboard.MakeCode = 0;
            in->data.keyboard.Flags = RI_KEY_BREAK;
            in->data.keyboard.Message = WM_KEYUP;
        }
    }
    return r;
}

static SHORT WINAPI hook_GetAsyncKeyState(int vk) { return ui_blocks_input() ? 0 : g_real_GetAsyncKeyState(vk); }
static SHORT WINAPI hook_GetKeyState(int vk) { return ui_blocks_input() ? 0 : g_real_GetKeyState(vk); }

static BOOL WINAPI hook_GetKeyboardState(PBYTE state)
{
    BOOL r = g_real_GetKeyboardState(state);
    if (r && state && ui_blocks_input()) memset(state, 0, 256);
    return r;
}

static void isolate_input(void)
{
    static const char *mods[] = {
        "adv_game.codelib", "adv_interf.codelib", "adv_opgame.codelib", "adv_qbattle.codelib", "base_main.codelib",
        "base_shell.codelib", "basic_game.codelib", "basic_input.codelib", "base_encycl.codelib",
    };
    static input_imports::Tracker<sizeof(mods) / sizeof(mods[0])> tracker;
    if (!g_real_GetRawInputData || !g_real_GetAsyncKeyState || !g_real_GetKeyState || !g_real_GetKeyboardState) {
        HMODULE user = GetModuleHandleA("user32.dll");
        if (!user) return;
        g_real_GetRawInputData = (UINT (WINAPI *)(HRAWINPUT, UINT, LPVOID, PUINT, UINT))GetProcAddress(user, "GetRawInputData");
        g_real_GetAsyncKeyState = (SHORT (WINAPI *)(int))GetProcAddress(user, "GetAsyncKeyState");
        g_real_GetKeyState = (SHORT (WINAPI *)(int))GetProcAddress(user, "GetKeyState");
        g_real_GetKeyboardState = (BOOL (WINAPI *)(PBYTE))GetProcAddress(user, "GetKeyboardState");
        if (!g_real_GetRawInputData || !g_real_GetAsyncKeyState || !g_real_GetKeyState || !g_real_GetKeyboardState) return;
    }
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(mods) / sizeof(mods[0]); ++i) {
        HMODULE h = nullptr;
        if (!GetModuleHandleExA(0, mods[i], &h)) { tracker.needs(i, nullptr); continue; }
        if (tracker.needs(i, h)) {
            int a = patch_imports(h, (void *)g_real_GetRawInputData, (void *)hook_GetRawInputData);
            int b = patch_imports(h, (void *)g_real_GetAsyncKeyState, (void *)hook_GetAsyncKeyState);
            int c = patch_imports(h, (void *)g_real_GetKeyState, (void *)hook_GetKeyState);
            int d = patch_imports(h, (void *)g_real_GetKeyboardState, (void *)hook_GetKeyboardState);
            if (a < 0 || b < 0 || c < 0 || d < 0)
                mp_log("input isolation: could not safely patch %s; will retry", mods[i]);
            else {
                tracker.complete(i, h);
                if (a + b + c + d) mp_log("input isolation: %s patched %d entry points", mods[i], a + b + c + d);
            }
        }
        FreeLibrary(h); // release only our GetModuleHandleEx reference
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- frame pacing between game instances
// A game that is not the foreground window keeps simulating but does not need to render flat out; it is held
// to MP_BG_FPS frames per second (default 30, 0 = no cap).
// When several instances run on one PC (a host and a joining game side by side) the one in front would
// still take the whole graphics card at 100 frames per second and starve the other down to a few frames per
// second, which stalls its simulation. The instances therefore publish their frame rate in a small shared
// memory block, and the one in front lowers its own rate until the others reach theirs.
// MP_FG_FPS pins the rate of the game in front instead (0 = never limit it).
struct PaceSlot { DWORD pid, when; float fps; DWORD front; };
static const int k_pace_slots = 8;

static void frame_pacing(void)
{
    static int bg_cap = -1, fg_fixed = -1;
    static double front_cap = 60;
    static LARGE_INTEGER freq, last;
    static PaceSlot *slots, *mine;
    static DWORD window_start, frames, others;
    static DWORD diag_start, diag_ticks, diag_front, diag_background, diag_unknown, diag_sleeps;
    static unsigned long long diag_sleep_ticks;
    static float slowest_other = 1000;
    if (bg_cap < 0) {
        char v[16];
        bg_cap = GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_BG_FPS", v, sizeof v) ? atoi(v) : 30;
        if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_FG_FPS", v, sizeof v)) fg_fixed = atoi(v);
        QueryPerformanceFrequency(&freq);
        QueryPerformanceCounter(&last);
        HANDLE map = CreateFileMappingA(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE, 0, PAGE_READWRITE, 0, sizeof(PaceSlot) * k_pace_slots, "Local\\gtmf_mp_frame_pacing");
        if (map) slots = (PaceSlot *)MapViewOfFile(map, FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS, 0, 0, 0);
        window_start = GetTickCount();
        diag_start = window_start;
    }
    HWND w = main_window();
    bool front = w && GetForegroundWindow() == w;
    DWORD tick = GetTickCount();
    bool diagnose = !strcmp(g_program, "tacgame");
    if (diagnose) {
        ++diag_ticks;
        if (!w) ++diag_unknown;
        else if (front) ++diag_front;
        else ++diag_background;
    }
    frames++;
    if (tick - window_start >= 500) {
        float fps = frames * 1000.0f / (tick - window_start);
        frames = 0;
        window_start = tick;
        others = 0;
        slowest_other = 1000;
        if (slots) {
            DWORD pid = GetCurrentProcessId();
            if (!mine || mine->pid != pid) {
                mine = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < k_pace_slots && !mine; i++)
                    if (slots[i].pid == pid || !slots[i].pid || tick - slots[i].when > 5000) mine = &slots[i];
            }
            if (mine) { mine->pid = pid; mine->when = tick; mine->fps = fps; mine->front = front; }
            for (int i = 0; i < k_pace_slots; i++) {
                if (&slots[i] == mine || !slots[i].pid || tick - slots[i].when > 2000) continue;
                others++;
                if (slots[i].fps < slowest_other) slowest_other = slots[i].fps;
            }
        }
        // in front, with other instances on this PC: give way until the slowest of them is near its own cap
        float enough = bg_cap > 0 ? bg_cap * 0.9f : 27;
        if (slowest_other < enough) front_cap = front_cap > 36 ? front_cap * 0.85 : 30;
        else front_cap = front_cap < 96 ? front_cap + 2 : 100;
    }
    double cap = 0;
    if (!front) cap = bg_cap;
    else if (fg_fixed >= 0) cap = fg_fixed;
    else if (others) cap = front_cap;
    LARGE_INTEGER now;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
    if (cap > 0) {
        double elapsed = (double)(now.QuadPart - last.QuadPart) / freq.QuadPart, want = 1.0 / cap;
        if (elapsed < want) {
            LARGE_INTEGER before_sleep = now;
            Sleep((DWORD)((want - elapsed) * 1000));
            QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
            if (diagnose && now.QuadPart >= before_sleep.QuadPart) {
                ++diag_sleeps;
                diag_sleep_ticks += (unsigned long long)(now.QuadPart - before_sleep.QuadPart);
            }
        }
    }
    last = now;
    if (diagnose && tick - diag_start >= 5000) {
        DWORD interval = tick - diag_start;
        double slept_ms = freq.QuadPart > 0 ? (double)diag_sleep_ticks * 1000.0 / (double)freq.QuadPart : 0.0;
        mp_log("pacing: %lu ms, %lu network ticks (%.1f/s, not FPS), focus front/background/unknown %lu/%lu/%lu, current cap %.1f, other instances %lu, Sleep calls %lu wall %.1f ms",
               (unsigned long)interval, (unsigned long)diag_ticks, diag_ticks * 1000.0 / interval,
               (unsigned long)diag_front, (unsigned long)diag_background, (unsigned long)diag_unknown,
               cap, (unsigned long)others, (unsigned long)diag_sleeps, slept_ms);
        diag_start = tick;
        diag_ticks = diag_front = diag_background = diag_unknown = diag_sleeps = 0;
        diag_sleep_ticks = 0;
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the network object
struct Network : Object {
    Handler     val_store[32];
    const char *key_store[32];
    HashSlot    slot_store[64];
    u32         cfg_port, cfg_clients;
    u64         cfg_delay_init, cfg_direct;
    u64         ticks, generates, ctrl_ticks, sim_ms;
    Object     *ctrltimer;
    DWORD       tick_t0;
    bool        surveyed;
    FILE       *trace;
};

static void add_cmd(Network *n, const char *name, Handler fn)
{
    CmdTable &t = n->cmds;
    u32 idx = t.count++;
    t.key_count = t.count;
    t.keys[idx] = name;
    t.vals[idx] = fn;
    u32 h = t.hash(&name, t.nslots);
    for (u32 i = 0; i < t.nslots; i++) {
        HashSlot &s = t.slots[(i + h) & (t.nslots - 1)];
        if (!(s.hash & 0x80000000u)) { s.hash = h | 0x80000000u; s.index = idx; break; }
    }
}

// "sync": the engine asks every object to resolve its dependencies, then marks it ready
static void on_sync(Object *o, void *)
{
    o->flags &= ~1u;
    for (u32 i = 0; i < o->dep_n; i++) {
        Dep &d = o->deps[i];
        *d.dest = ((Object *(*)(ObjMan *, Dep *))o->objman->vtbl[9])(o->objman, &d);
    }
    o->flags |= 1;
    mp_log("sync: objman=%p", o->objman);
    if (g_debug) hook_random();
}

// "ctrl_tick": simulation step from the p_ctrl pipeline, before every other object.
// Message: +0x10 float scaled frame time, +0x14 float step seconds, +0x18 u32 step ms, +0x1c flags (1 first, 2 last, 4 paused)
static void on_ctrl_tick(Object *o, void *msg)
{
    Network *n = (Network *)o;
    if (!n->ctrl_ticks) {
        mp_log("first ctrl_tick at frame %llu (msg=%p)", n->ticks, msg);
        char path[MAX_PATH];
        sprintf_s(path, "%s\\mp_trace_%s_%lu.txt", g_out_dir, g_program, GetCurrentProcessId());
        if (g_debug) n->trace = _fsopen(path, "w", _SH_DENYNO);
    }
    u32 ms = *(u32 *)((char *)msg + 0x18), fl = *(u32 *)((char *)msg + 0x1c);
    session_note_sim_ms(ms);
    session_sim_step(o->objman, ms);
    if (g_debug) state_debug_step(o->objman, n->sim_ms);
    n->ctrl_ticks++;
    u64 before = n->sim_ms;
    n->sim_ms += ms;
    // MP_DUMP=1: full unit positions at a few fixed moments, to see which units differ between instances
    if (ms && (n->sim_ms == 10 || n->sim_ms == 1000 || n->sim_ms == 5000 || n->sim_ms == 20000) &&
        GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_DUMP", 0, 0)) {
        char path[MAX_PATH];
        sprintf_s(path, "%s\\mp_units_%lu_%llu.txt", g_out_dir, GetCurrentProcessId(), n->sim_ms);
        if (FILE *f = _fsopen(path, "w", _SH_DENYNO)) {
            Object **all = *(Object ***)((char *)o->objman + 0xf0);
            u32 count = *(u32 *)((char *)o->objman + 0xfc);
            for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                Object *u = all[i];
                if (strcmp(u->iface, "i_techn") && strcmp(u->iface, "i_human")) continue;
                float *p = (float *)((char *)u + 0x1b0);
                fprintf(f, "%s %.6f %.6f %.6f\n", u->full, p[0], p[1], p[2]);
            }
            fclose(f);
        }
    }
    // once per simulated second: fingerprint of every unit position, to compare instances step for step
    if (n->trace && ms && before / 1000 != n->sim_ms / 1000) {
        ObjMan *om = o->objman;
        Object **all = *(Object ***)((char *)om + 0xf0);
        u32 count = *(u32 *)((char *)om + 0xfc), units = 0;
        u64 hash = 1469598103934665603ull;
        float first[3] = {0, 0, 0};
        for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            Object *u = all[i];
            if (strcmp(u->iface, "i_techn") && strcmp(u->iface, "i_human")) continue;
            const unsigned char *pos = (const unsigned char *)u + 0x1b0;   // world position x, height, z
            if (!units++) memcpy(first, pos, 12);
            for (int b = 0; b < 12; b++) hash = (hash ^ pos[b]) * 1099511628211ull;
        }
        fprintf(n->trace, "sim=%llu step=%llu frame=%llu ms=%u fl=%u dt=%g rng=%08x units=%u hash=%016llx first=%.3f,%.3f,%.3f\n",
                n->sim_ms, n->ctrl_ticks, n->ticks, ms, fl, *(float *)((char *)msg + 0x10), *(u32 *)((char *)om + 0xac8),
                units, hash, first[0], first[1], first[2]);
        fflush(n->trace);
    }
}

// "tick": once per rendered frame from the p_main pipeline
static void on_tick(Object *o, void *msg)
{
    Network *n = (Network *)o;
    if (!n->ticks++) {
        n->tick_t0 = GetTickCount();
        mp_log("first tick (msg=%p)", msg);
        keep_running_unfocused(o->objman);
        place_window_from_env();
        start_profiler_from_env();
        session_start(o->objman);
        ui_start(o->objman);
    }
    // Libraries and the main window may appear after the first tick. Retry lightly rather than
    // assuming a one-time scan caught every input source; this still needs a real-game check.
    static DWORD input_poll;
    DWORD input_now = GetTickCount();
    if (!input_poll || input_now - input_poll >= 250) {
        isolate_input();
        if (!g_prev_wndproc || !g_hooked_window || !IsWindow(g_hooked_window)) keep_running_unfocused(o->objman);
        input_poll = input_now ? input_now : 1;
    }
    // Sample the module's own menu/session work separately from engine rendering and Sleep.
    static DWORD perf_since;
    static unsigned perf_samples;
    static unsigned long long perf_session, perf_ui;
    static LARGE_INTEGER perf_freq;
    bool sample = !strcmp(g_program, "tacgame") && (n->ticks & 31u) == 0;
    LARGE_INTEGER t0 = {}, t1 = {}, t2 = {};
    if (sample) sample = QueryPerformanceCounter(&t0) != 0;
    session_frame(o->objman);
    if (sample) sample = QueryPerformanceCounter(&t1) != 0;
    ui_frame(o->objman);
    if (sample && QueryPerformanceCounter(&t2) && t1.QuadPart >= t0.QuadPart && t2.QuadPart >= t1.QuadPart) {
        ++perf_samples;
        perf_session += (unsigned long long)(t1.QuadPart - t0.QuadPart);
        perf_ui += (unsigned long long)(t2.QuadPart - t1.QuadPart);
    }
    frame_pacing();
    DWORD perf_now = GetTickCount();
    if (!perf_since) perf_since = perf_now ? perf_now : 1;
    if (!strcmp(g_program, "tacgame") && perf_now - perf_since >= 5000) {
        if (!perf_freq.QuadPart) QueryPerformanceFrequency(&perf_freq);
        if (perf_samples && perf_freq.QuadPart > 0) {
            double scale = 1000000.0 / ((double)perf_freq.QuadPart * perf_samples);
            mp_log("module load: 1/32 network ticks sampled %u, session avg %.2f us, UI avg %.2f us (wall time, excludes pacing and engine)",
                   perf_samples, perf_session * scale, perf_ui * scale);
        }
        perf_since = perf_now;
        perf_samples = 0; perf_session = perf_ui = 0;
    }
    // Lockstep only: lock the simulation clock (no automatic speed adjustment, speed x1). With the fixed
    // frame time this makes every frame advance the battle by exactly the same step on every machine.
    // Never done in ordinary single-player use, where the player's speed buttons must keep working.
    //   i_ctrltimer: +0x108 requested speed, +0x10c current speed, +0x15c pause, +0x15d automatic speed control
    if (g_lockstep && !n->ctrltimer) n->ctrltimer = engine_find(o->objman, "i_ctrltimer", "ctrltimer");
    if (g_lockstep && n->ctrltimer) {
        char *ct = (char *)n->ctrltimer;
        ct[0x15d] = 0;
        *(float *)(ct + 0x108) = 1.0f;
        *(float *)(ct + 0x10c) = 1.0f;
        // Experiment: start the simulation on the same frame number in every instance. The engine lifts its
        // opening pause after a varying number of frames; hold the pause until a fixed frame instead.
        if (n->ticks < k_start_frame) ct[0x15c] = 1;
        else if (n->ticks == k_start_frame) { ct[0x15c] = 0; mp_log("simulation released at frame %llu", n->ticks); }
    }
    if (g_debug && !n->surveyed && n->ticks == 120) {
        n->surveyed = true;
        survey(o->objman);
    }
    if (n->ticks % 3000 == 0)
        mp_log("tick %llu (%.1f per second)", n->ticks, n->ticks * 1000.0 / (GetTickCount() - n->tick_t0 + 1));
}

// "render": from the r_main pipeline, placed just below the game's cursor (see tools\make_progpack.py)
static void on_render(Object *o, void *)
{
    static bool logged;
    if (!logged) { logged = true; mp_log("render pipeline reached the network object"); }
    ui_render(o->objman);
}

// "generate": once per loading step from the g_main pipeline
static void on_generate(Object *o, void *msg)
{
    Network *n = (Network *)o;
    if (!n->generates++) {
        mp_log("first generate (msg=%p)", msg);
        // Fixed frame time: every rendered frame then advances the simulation by the same amount on every
        // machine (i_timer vtbl[3] = set_fixed_tick). 10.5 ms keeps the 10 ms quantisation stable.
        Object *timer = g_lockstep ? engine_find(o->objman, "i_timer", "timer") : 0;
        if (timer) {
            ((void (*)(Object *, float))timer->vtbl[3])(timer, 0.0105f);
            mp_log("fixed frame time set on %p", timer);
        }
    }
}

struct Seen { char name[48]; u64 count; };
static Seen g_seen[64];
static int g_nseen;

static void *net_dtor(Network *n, u64 flags)
{
    mp_log("destroy '%s' flags=%llu, ticks=%llu generates=%llu ctrl_ticks=%llu rand=%lld", n->full, flags, n->ticks,
         n->generates, n->ctrl_ticks, (long long)g_rand_calls);
    if (n->trace) fclose(n->trace);
    session_stop();
    for (int i = 0; i < g_nseen; i++) mp_log("  unhandled command '%s' received %llu times", g_seen[i].name, g_seen[i].count);
    if ((flags & 1) && !(flags & 4)) _aligned_free(n);
    return n;
}

static u64 net_query(Network *, u32 *out)
{
    if (out) *out = 0;
    return 0;
}

static void net_dispatch(Network *n, Msg *msg)
{
    char buf[48];
    const char *name = "<no msg>";
    if (readable(msg, 16)) name = safe_str(msg->name, buf, sizeof buf);
    if (Handler h = engine_lookup(n, name)) { h(n, msg); return; }
    if (!strcmp(name, "gamemode") && readable(msg, 0x18)) session_note_phase(*(u32 *)((char *)msg + 0x10));
    if (g_debug && (!strcmp(name, "gamemode") || !strcmp(name, "actmode"))) {
        // battle phase changes (deployment, battle, statistics) arrive as these broadcasts
        static int shown;
        if (shown++ < 60) {
            mp_log("%s at frame %llu sim %llu ms", name, n->ticks, n->sim_ms);
            log_hex("  msg", msg, 0x40);
        }
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < g_nseen; i++)
        if (!strcmp(g_seen[i].name, name)) { g_seen[i].count++; return; }
    if (g_nseen < 64) {
        strcpy_s(g_seen[g_nseen].name, name);
        g_seen[g_nseen++].count = 1;
    }
    mp_log("unhandled command '%s' (msg=%p)", name, msg);
    log_hex("  msg", msg, 0x40);
}

static u64 net_extra(void *o)
{
    mp_log("unexpected extra virtual call on %p", o);
    return 0;
}

static void *g_net_vtbl[8] = {
    (void *)net_dtor, (void *)net_query, (void *)net_dispatch,
    (void *)net_extra, (void *)net_extra, (void *)net_extra, (void *)net_extra, (void *)net_extra,
};

static Object *network_create(CreateCtx *ctx)
{
    Network *n = (Network *)_aligned_malloc(sizeof(Network), 0x10);
    if (!n) return 0;
    memset(n, 0, sizeof *n);
    n->vtbl = g_net_vtbl;
    sprintf_s(n->full, "i_network:%.31s", ctx->inst ? ctx->inst : "");
    n->iface = "i_network";
    n->inst = n->full + 10;
    n->author = "community";
    n->objman = ctx->objman;
    n->refs = 1;
    n->flags = 2;
    n->cmds.vals = n->val_store;
    n->cmds.keys = n->key_store;
    n->cmds.slots = n->slot_store;
    n->cmds.cap = n->cmds.key_cap = n->cmds.limit = 32;
    n->cmds.nslots = n->cmds.slot_cap = 64;
    n->cmds.hash = engine_hash;
    n->cmds.factor = 2;
    add_cmd(n, "sync", on_sync);
    add_cmd(n, "tick", on_tick);
    add_cmd(n, "generate", on_generate);
    add_cmd(n, "ctrl_tick", on_ctrl_tick);
    add_cmd(n, "render", on_render);

    void **reader = ctx->reader;
    u32 handle = 0;
    if (reader && ((char (*)(void *, const char *, u64, u32 *, u64))(*(void ***)reader)[2])(reader, n->full, 0, &handle, 0)) {
        Field f[] = {
            { "u:port", &n->cfg_port }, { "u:clients", &n->cfg_clients },
            { "b:delay_init", &n->cfg_delay_init }, { "b:direct_connect", &n->cfg_direct },
        };
        ((void (*)(void *, u32, Field *, u32))(*(void ***)reader)[6])(reader, handle, f, 4);
    }
    mp_log("created '%s' at %p: port=%u clients=%u delay_init=%u direct_connect=%u", n->full, n,
         n->cfg_port, n->cfg_clients, (u32)n->cfg_delay_init, (u32)n->cfg_direct);
    return n;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- factory
// GetFactory3() returns an object with three virtuals:
//   [0] create(factory, class, ctx)   [1] register(factory, class, creator)   [2] enumerate(factory, index, name*)
struct Factory { void **vtbl; };

static void *fac_create(Factory *, const char *name, CreateCtx *ctx)
{
    if (name && !strcmp(name, "i_network")) return network_create(ctx);
    return 0;
}

static u64 fac_register(Factory *, const char *, void *) { return 1; }

static u32 fac_enum(Factory *, u32 index, const char **out)
{
    if (!out) return 1;
    if (index) return 0;
    *out = "i_network";
    return 1;
}

static void *g_fac_vtbl[4] = { (void *)fac_create, (void *)fac_register, (void *)fac_enum, (void *)net_extra };
static Factory g_factory = { g_fac_vtbl };

extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) void *GetFactory3(void)
{
    return &g_factory;
}

BOOL WINAPI DllMain(HINSTANCE self, DWORD reason, LPVOID)
{
    if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH) {
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(self);
        log_open(self);
        netlog_open(self, g_program, g_out_dir);
        mp_log("basic_network.codelib loaded, program '%s', pid %lu", g_program, GetCurrentProcessId());
        mp_log("command line: %s", GetCommandLineA());
        g_lockstep = GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_LOCKSTEP", 0, 0) != 0;
        g_debug = GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_DEBUG", 0, 0) != 0;
        mp_log("lockstep %s, debug %s", g_lockstep ? "on" : "off", g_debug ? "on" : "off");
    } else if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_DETACH && g_log) {
        mp_log("unloaded");
        netlog_shutdown();
    }
    return TRUE;
}

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/battle_file.h (sha256 2b0fe865cd7d50f58b0b14c6dabf2d7361b8fc4879248f7a93ca4a4193b1f329) ====================
// Transaction order for M4 battle descriptions. The actual filesystem operations are injected so
// failures can be tested without the game. `write` must replace its destination atomically: a false
// return leaves the prior destination untouched. Both files are in the description's directory.
#pragma once
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace battle_file {
struct Io {
    void *context;
    bool (*copy)(void *, const char *from, const char *to);
    bool (*write)(void *, const char *to, const void *bytes, size_t size);
    bool (*remove)(void *, const char *path);
};
enum class Result { ok, invalid, backup, marker, image, image_and_marker_cleanup };

inline const char *reason(Result r)
{
    switch (r) {
    case Result::ok: return "ok";
    case Result::invalid: return "invalid file or data";
    case Result::backup: return "backup failed";
    case Result::marker: return "recovery marker write failed";
    case Result::image: return "image replacement failed";
    case Result::image_and_marker_cleanup: return "image replacement failed and marker cleanup failed";
    }
    return "unknown failure";
}

// The marker is installed BEFORE the new image. If a process stops between them, the old image's
// hash does not match the marker, so the next start treats it as stale; after image replacement the
// backup and matching marker let the next battle restore its original description. If replacement
// fails, remove the new marker; a failed cleanup is reported separately and the old image is intact.
inline Result install(const Io &io, const char *path, const std::vector<unsigned char> &data, uint32_t hash)
{
    if (!path || !*path || data.empty() || !io.copy || !io.write || !io.remove) return Result::invalid;
    const std::string backup = std::string(path) + ".before_mp";
    const std::string marker = std::string(path) + ".mp_combined";
    if (!io.copy(io.context, path, backup.c_str())) return Result::backup;
    if (!io.write(io.context, marker.c_str(), &hash, sizeof hash)) return Result::marker;
    if (!io.write(io.context, path, data.data(), data.size())) {
        if (!io.remove(io.context, marker.c_str())) return Result::image_and_marker_cleanup;
        return Result::image;
    }
    return Result::ok;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/battle_file.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/ceasefire.h (sha256 460e7e6c83f8616f1d7876cfe7910ed8e11a1fb5e2657252bfaf84ae5c093f88) ====================
// Tactical ceasefire agreement, independent of the engine and transport. Agreement is NOT an
// engine battle result: only an actual phase-3 announcement sets the end flag.
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>

namespace ceasefire {
enum Event {
    changed = 1, cancelled = 2, agreed = 4, finish_notice = 8,
    peer_end_wait = 16, natural_end = 32, stale_packet = 64
};

struct State {
    bool proposed = false, peer_proposed = false, peer_over = false;
    bool left = false, pending = false, have_peer_seq = false;
    uint16_t peer_seq = 0, tx_seq = 0;
    uint32_t peer_result = 0, deadline = 0, peer_deadline = 0;

    static uint32_t after(uint32_t now, uint32_t wait) {
        uint32_t result = now + wait;
        return result ? result : 1; // zero is the 'no deadline' sentinel
    }
    static bool due(uint32_t now, uint32_t when) {
        return when && (int32_t)(now - when) >= 0; // deadlines remain valid across GetTickCount wrap
    }

    bool toggle(bool connected, uint32_t phase) {
        if (!connected || phase != 2 || left || peer_over) return false;
        proposed = !proposed;
        if (!proposed) { deadline = 0; pending = false; }
        return true;
    }

    // Head.count is a 16-bit sequence only when the new flag is set. Legacy packets have no such
    // flag. Once sequenced packets are seen, ignore delayed legacy packets and old/duplicate values.
    unsigned observe(bool peer_wants, bool over, uint32_t result, bool sequenced, uint16_t sequence) {
        if (sequenced) {
            uint16_t delta = (uint16_t)(sequence - peer_seq);
            if (have_peer_seq && (!delta || delta >= 0x8000u)) return stale_packet;
            have_peer_seq = true;
            peer_seq = sequence;
        } else if (have_peer_seq) {
            return stale_packet;
        }
        unsigned events = peer_wants != peer_proposed ? changed : 0;
        peer_proposed = peer_wants;
        if (over && !peer_over) { peer_over = true; peer_result = result; }
        // A withdrawal cancels the notification while both players are still fighting.
        if (!peer_wants && !peer_over && !left && (deadline || pending)) {
            deadline = 0;
            pending = false;
            events |= cancelled;
        }
        return events;
    }

    // Called on the session thread. Only the engine announcing phase 3 sets the end flag.
    // pending is a notice to use the game's own finish controls, NEVER a request to abandon the battle.
    unsigned advance(uint32_t now, bool connected, uint32_t phase) {
        unsigned events = 0;
        if (phase == 3 && !left) {
            left = true; pending = false; deadline = 0;
            events |= natural_end;
        }
        if (!connected && !peer_over && !left) {
            proposed = peer_proposed = false;
            deadline = 0; pending = false; peer_deadline = 0; have_peer_seq = false;
            return events;
        }
        if (phase == 2 && connected && proposed && peer_proposed && !left && !pending && !deadline) {
            deadline = after(now, 5000);
            events |= agreed;
        }
        if (!left && deadline && due(now, deadline)) {
            deadline = 0; pending = true;
            events |= finish_notice;
        }
        if (peer_over && !left) {
            if (!peer_deadline) {
                peer_deadline = after(now, 3000);
                events |= peer_end_wait;
            } else if (!pending && due(now, peer_deadline)) {
                pending = true;
                events |= finish_notice;
            }
        }
        return events;
    }

    void new_peer() {
        bool already_left = left;
        uint16_t last_tx = tx_seq;
        *this = State{};
        left = already_left;
        tx_seq = last_tx; // do not make packets look older to a peer reconnecting at the same address
    }
};
} // namespace ceasefire
==================== END development/net-v21/src/ceasefire.h ====================

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/detour.h (sha256 7e6baf8ab9ad0a4a2423974fc02a7606dd0e814d4fdde636a84b802137dcbd67) ====================
// In-memory detour of an engine function: its first `len` bytes (whole instructions, none of them
// position dependent, at least 14) are replaced by a jump to `hook`; the returned trampoline runs those
// bytes and continues in the original, so the hook can still call it. Nothing on disk changes, and the
// caller checks the function's bytes against a known signature first, so an unknown build is left alone.
#pragma once
#include <windows.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <atomic>

// The engine's functions are reached through typed function pointers; a hook handed over by name is
// one of those types. This overload does the conversion once, so every call site reads the same way
// under both compilers the module has been built with (MSVC allows the implicit conversion, clang not).
template <class Hook>
inline void *install_detour(void *target, size_t len, Hook hook)
{
    return install_detour(target, len, (void *)hook);
}

inline void *install_detour(void *target, size_t len, void *hook)
{
    if (len < 14 || len > 32) return 0;
    unsigned char *fn = (unsigned char *)target;
    unsigned char *tramp = (unsigned char *)VirtualAlloc(0, 64, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE);
    if (!tramp) return 0;
    static const unsigned char jmp[6] = { 0xff, 0x25, 0, 0, 0, 0 };     // jmp [rip+0], address follows
    void *back = fn + len;
    memcpy(tramp, fn, len);
    memcpy(tramp + len, jmp, 6);
    memcpy(tramp + len + 6, &back, 8);
    DWORD old;
    if (!VirtualProtect(fn, len, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &old)) { VirtualFree(tramp, 0, MEM_RELEASE); return 0; }
    unsigned char patch[32];
    memset(patch, 0x90, sizeof patch);
    memcpy(patch, jmp, 6);
    memcpy(patch + 6, &hook, 8);
    memcpy(fn, patch, len);
    VirtualProtect(fn, len, old, &old);
    FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), fn, len);
    FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), tramp, 64);
    return tramp;
}

// Publish the trampoline before exposing the hook to other threads. This reduces the
// null-original race, but does not suspend engine threads while target bytes are patched.
template <class Fn, class Hook>
inline bool install_published_detour(void *target, size_t len, Hook hook, std::atomic<Fn> &original)
{
    if (!target || !hook || len < 14 || len > 32) return false;
    unsigned char *fn = (unsigned char *)target;
    unsigned char *tramp = (unsigned char *)VirtualAlloc(nullptr, 64, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE);
    if (!tramp) return false;
    static const unsigned char jmp[6] = { 0xff, 0x25, 0, 0, 0, 0 };
    void *back = fn + len, *destination = (void *)hook;
    memcpy(tramp, fn, len); memcpy(tramp + len, jmp, 6); memcpy(tramp + len + 6, &back, 8);
    FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), tramp, 64);
    DWORD old;
    if (!VirtualProtect(fn, len, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &old)) { VirtualFree(tramp, 0, MEM_RELEASE); return false; }
    unsigned char patch[32]; memset(patch, 0x90, sizeof patch);
    memcpy(patch, jmp, 6); memcpy(patch + 6, &destination, 8);
    original.store((Fn)tramp, std::memory_order_release);
    memcpy(fn, patch, len);
    DWORD ignored; VirtualProtect(fn, len, old, &ignored);
    FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), fn, len);
    return true;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/detour.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/engine.h (sha256 e759131029251822017a5b9636d88c643171b31a02ef5c0191dd6fe29537c4bc) ====================
// GRAVITECH3 v6.00 (GTMF build 3598) object ABI, recovered from basic_steam.codelib / base_shell.codelib.
#pragma once
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#include <windows.h>
#include <stddef.h>

typedef unsigned long long u64;
typedef unsigned int u32;

struct Object;
typedef void (*Handler)(Object *self, void *msg);
typedef u32 (*HashFn)(const char **key, u32 slots);

// Command table embedded in every object at +0x80: open-addressed hash, name -> handler.
// Pipelines look handlers up here directly, so it must be laid out exactly like the engine's.
struct HashSlot { u32 hash; u32 index; };   // hash has bit 31 set when the slot is used
struct CmdTable {
    Handler     *vals;      // +0x00
    u32          pad08, count;      // +0x08 / +0x0c
    u32          cap, pad14;        // +0x10
    const char **keys;      // +0x18
    u32          pad20, key_count;  // +0x20 / +0x24
    u32          key_cap, pad2c;    // +0x28
    HashSlot    *slots;     // +0x30
    u32          pad38, nslots;     // +0x38 / +0x3c
    u32          slot_cap, pad44;   // +0x40
    HashFn       hash;      // +0x48
    u32          factor, limit;     // +0x50 / +0x54
};

// Dependency entry: the stock "sync" handler asks the object manager for each of these.
struct Dep {
    const char *iface;      // "i_window"
    const char *name;       // "window"
    Object    **dest;
    u32         flags;      // bit 0: required, bit 1: reference counted
    u32         pad;
};

// Object manager, reachable from every object at +0x60.
//   vtbl[9]  (+0x48): Object *find(objman, Dep *)
//   vtbl[14] (+0x70): void log(objman, Object *self, u32 level, const char *fmt, va_list *)
struct ObjMan { void **vtbl; };

// Base header shared by every engine class (0xf0 bytes).
//   vtbl[0] deleting destructor(self, flags)   vtbl[1] query(self, u32 *out) -> 0   vtbl[2] dispatch(self, msg)
struct Object {
    void      **vtbl;        // +0x00
    char        full[64];    // +0x08  "i_network:network"
    const char *iface;       // +0x48
    char       *inst;        // +0x50  points into full[]
    const char *author;      // +0x58
    ObjMan     *objman;      // +0x60
    u32         z68;         // +0x68
    u32         refs;        // +0x6c
    u64         z70, z78;    // +0x70
    CmdTable    cmds;        // +0x80
    u64         zd8;         // +0xd8
    Dep        *deps;        // +0xe0
    u32         dep_n;       // +0xe8
    u32         flags;       // +0xec  bit 0: synced, bit 1: single instance
};
static_assert(offsetof(Object, iface) == 0x48, "layout");
static_assert(offsetof(Object, objman) == 0x60, "layout");
static_assert(offsetof(Object, cmds) == 0x80, "layout");
static_assert(offsetof(Object, cmds.slots) == 0xb0, "layout");
static_assert(offsetof(Object, cmds.hash) == 0xc8, "layout");
static_assert(offsetof(Object, deps) == 0xe0, "layout");
static_assert(offsetof(Object, flags) == 0xec, "layout");
static_assert(sizeof(Object) == 0xf0, "layout");

// Message passed to dispatch/handlers: a vtable, then the command name, then command-specific payload.
struct Msg { void **vtbl; const char *name; };

// Creation context passed to a class creator.
//   +0x10 instance name, +0x18 object manager, +0x20 config reader
// Config reader: vtbl[2] bool find(reader, "iface:name", 0, u32 *handle, 0)
//                vtbl[6] void read(reader, handle, Field[], count)   keys are "u:name", "b:name", "f:name", "$:name"
struct Field { const char *key; void *dest; };
struct CreateCtx { void *p0, *p8; const char *inst; ObjMan *objman; void **reader; };

inline u32 engine_hash(const char **key, u32 n)
{
    u32 h = 0, m = 0x7e9f;
    for (const char *s = *key; *s; s++) {
        h = (m * h + (int)*s) % n;
        m = (m * 0x6a2f) % (n - 1);
    }
    return h;
}

inline Object *engine_find(ObjMan *om, const char *iface, const char *name)
{
    Object *out = 0;
    Dep d = { iface, name, &out, 0, 0 };
    return ((Object *(*)(ObjMan *, Dep *))om->vtbl[9])(om, &d);
}

inline Handler engine_lookup(Object *o, const char *name)
{
    CmdTable &t = o->cmds;
    if (!t.nslots || !t.hash) return 0;
    u32 h = t.hash(&name, t.nslots);
    for (u32 i = 0; i < t.nslots; i++) {
        HashSlot &s = t.slots[(i + h) & (t.nslots - 1)];
        if (!(s.hash & 0x80000000u)) return 0;
        if ((s.hash & 0x7fffffffu) == h && !strcmp(t.keys[s.index], name)) return t.vals[s.index];
    }
    return 0;
}

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/env_value.h (sha256 6d58c9bcbb38d5dc1678b92757680b37b78ec8f17a3b14ddef895e7dd9328664) ====================
// Bounded access to Windows environment variables. GetEnvironmentVariableA returns the required
// size on an oversized value without initializing the destination; never parse that buffer.
#pragma once
#include <windows.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <limits.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

inline bool env_text(const char *name, char *out, size_t capacity)
{
    if (!name || !out || !capacity || capacity > 0xffffffffu) return false;
    out[0] = 0;
    DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableA(name, out, (DWORD)capacity);
    if (!n || n >= capacity) { out[0] = 0; return false; }
    return true;
}

inline bool env_integer(const char *name, int *out)
{
    char text[32];
    if (!out || !env_text(name, text, sizeof text)) return false;
    char *end = nullptr;
    errno = 0;
    long value = strtol(text, &end, 10);
    if (end == text || *end || errno == ERANGE || value < INT_MIN || value > INT_MAX) return false;
    *out = (int)value;
    return true;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/env_value.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/force.h (sha256 37701b63375c319868f65b12fd37494f005f6fb01eddf4989c73fcee3aacca0b) ====================
// Putting the other player's force into a battle (M4), as pure work on the battle description's bytes.
//
// A battle description is the CFG the engine starts a battle from (image.h describes the format). It holds,
// among everything else, two tables:
//
//   squads   one row per squad: id in column 1, a side code ('GER'/'USSR') and a command tag
//            ('USER' = the squads this game's player commands, 'ENEM' or a formation code for the rest)
//   units    one row per soldier or vehicle, named "i_techn:tec_<id>" / "i_human:hum_<id>"; a unit belongs
//            to the squad with id (unit id & ~0x1f)
//
// Each player picks his own force in his own Skirmish screen, so each game's description carries his own
// force on his side and whatever the setup gave the other side. A two-player battle needs both forces in
// both games, every unit keeping the id it has in its owner's description. That is what this file does: it
// takes the other player's description, lifts his own squads and units out of it, and writes them into this
// game's description on the side he commands.
//
// How the rows are written: the file's offsets are absolute, so nothing already in it may move. The new
// table (this game's rows plus the other player's) is therefore appended at the end of the file and the
// node's row count and data offset are pointed at it; the old table stays where it was, unreferenced. A
// row's text cell is (length, offset), so the other player's text is copied into the appended block and its
// offset rebased, while this game's own rows keep their offsets - their text is still where it was. Cells of
// a kind this file does not know (number lists that point somewhere) are refused rather than guessed at,
// and the result is parsed back and checked before anybody uses it.
#pragma once
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>
#include "image.h"

namespace force {

typedef unsigned char byte;

struct Table {
    bool ok = false;
    size_t header = 0, data = 0;
    unsigned cols = 0, rows = 0;
};

enum { k_kind_offset = 16, k_text = 2, k_numbers = 6 };

inline unsigned u32_at(const std::vector<byte> &b, size_t o)
{
