    unsigned v = 0;
    if (o + 4 <= b.size()) memcpy(&v, b.data() + o, 4);
    return v;
}

inline void put_u32(std::vector<byte> &b, size_t o, unsigned v)
{
    if (o + 4 <= b.size()) memcpy(b.data() + o, &v, 4);
}

inline Table table(const std::vector<byte> &b, const char *name)
{
    Table t;
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return t;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table_at = u32_at(b, 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table_at + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        if (strncmp((const char *)b.data() + o, name, 0x20)) continue;
        unsigned type = u32_at(b, o + 0x20), cols = u32_at(b, o + 0x24), rows = u32_at(b, o + 0x28), data = u32_at(b, o + 0x30);
        if (type != 2 || !cols) return t;
        if (data + (size_t)rows * cols * 0x20 > b.size()) return t;
        t.ok = true;
        t.header = o;
        t.cols = cols;
        t.rows = rows;
        t.data = data;
        return t;
    }
    return t;
}

inline size_t cell_at(const Table &t, unsigned row, unsigned col) { return t.data + ((size_t)row * t.cols + col) * 0x20; }

// A text cell: kind 2, first word the length, second the offset of the text itself (NUL terminated).
inline bool text_at(const std::vector<byte> &b, size_t cell, std::string *out)
{
    if (cell + 0x20 > b.size() || u32_at(b, cell + k_kind_offset) != k_text) return false;
    unsigned len = u32_at(b, cell), off = u32_at(b, cell + 4);
    if (off >= b.size() || len >= b.size() - off) return false;
    if (b[off + len] != 0) return false;
    out->assign((const char *)b.data() + off, len);
    return true;
}

// id of a unit row: the hex number after the last '_' of "i_techn:tec_<id>" / "i_human:hum_<id>"
inline unsigned unit_id(const std::vector<byte> &b, const Table &units, unsigned row)
{
    std::string name;
    if (!units.ok || row >= units.rows || !text_at(b, cell_at(units, row, 0), &name)) return 0;
    if (name.compare(0, 8, "i_techn:") && name.compare(0, 8, "i_human:")) return 0;
    size_t us = name.rfind('_');
    if (us == std::string::npos || us + 1 >= name.size()) return 0;
    return (unsigned)strtoul(name.c_str() + us + 1, 0, 16);
}

inline unsigned squad_id(const std::vector<byte> &b, const Table &squads, unsigned row)
{
    return squads.ok && row < squads.rows ? u32_at(b, cell_at(squads, row, 1)) : 0;
}

// The side and command-tag columns of `squads`, worked out the way image.h does.
struct Columns { int side = -1, tag = -1; };

inline Columns columns(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    Columns c;
    image::SquadColumns sc = image::squad_columns(b, image::find(b, "squads"));
    c.side = sc.side;
    c.tag = sc.tag;
    return c;
}

struct Counts { unsigned squads = 0, units = 0; bool ok = false; };

// What this description's own player commands: the squads tagged 'USER' and their units.
inline Counts user_force(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    Counts out;
    Table s = table(b, "squads"), u = table(b, "units");
    Columns c = columns(b);
    if (!s.ok || !u.ok || c.tag < 0) return out;
    std::unordered_set<unsigned> ids;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < s.rows; r++)
        if (u32_at(b, cell_at(s, r, (unsigned)c.tag)) == image::k_user) {
            unsigned id = squad_id(b, s, r);
            if (id) ids.insert(id);
            out.squads++;
        }
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < u.rows; r++) {
        unsigned id = unit_id(b, u, r);
        if (id && ids.count(id & ~0x1fu)) out.units++;
    }
    out.ok = true;
    return out;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- writing rows
// A row that is being written: its cells as they will go into the file, plus the text cells whose text has
// to be copied in as well (those of the other player's rows).
struct Pending { size_t at; std::string text; };

// Build a table body out of `rows` of `src` (table `t`): the cells are copied, `patch_side_col` and
// `patch_tag_col` (when given) are overwritten, and text is either kept as it is (`keep_text`: it already
// lives in the file being written) or collected so the caller can copy it in.
inline bool build_rows(const std::vector<byte> &src, const Table &t, const std::vector<unsigned> &rows, unsigned cols,
                       bool keep_text, int patch_side_col, unsigned side, int patch_tag_col, unsigned tag,
                       std::vector<byte> *body, std::vector<Pending> *pending, std::string *why)
{
    if (!t.ok || t.cols != cols) { *why = "a table's width is not the one this file has"; return false; }
    for (unsigned r : rows) {
        if (r >= t.rows) { *why = "a row is past the end of the table"; return false; }
        size_t row_start = body->size();
        for (unsigned col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
            size_t cell = cell_at(t, r, col);
            if (cell + 0x20 > src.size()) { *why = "a cell is past the end of the file"; return false; }
            unsigned kind = u32_at(src, cell + k_kind_offset);
            size_t at = body->size();
            body->insert(body->end(), src.begin() + cell, src.begin() + cell + 0x20);
            if (keep_text) continue;                     // this game's own row: its offsets stay valid as they are
            if (kind == k_text) {
                std::string text;
                if (!text_at(src, cell, &text)) { *why = "a text cell does not point at text"; return false; }
                put_u32(*body, at, (unsigned)text.size());
                Pending p;
                p.at = at;
                p.text = text;
                pending->push_back(p);
            } else if (kind == k_numbers && (u32_at(src, cell) || u32_at(src, cell + 4))) {
                *why = "a row carries a number list";
                return false;
            }
        }
        if (patch_side_col >= 0) put_u32(*body, row_start + (size_t)patch_side_col * 0x20, side);
        if (patch_tag_col >= 0) put_u32(*body, row_start + (size_t)patch_tag_col * 0x20, tag);
    }
    return true;
}

// Write a table: `body` at the end of `dst`, the pending text after it, then point the node at it.
inline bool write_table(std::vector<byte> &dst, const Table &node, const std::vector<byte> &body,
                        std::vector<Pending> pending)
{
    if (!node.ok) return false;
    size_t base = dst.size();
    dst.insert(dst.end(), body.begin(), body.end());
    for (Pending &p : pending) {
        size_t at = dst.size();
        dst.insert(dst.end(), p.text.begin(), p.text.end());
        dst.push_back(0);
        put_u32(dst, base + p.at + 4, (unsigned)at);
    }
    unsigned rows = (unsigned)(body.size() / ((size_t)node.cols * 0x20));
    put_u32(dst, node.header + 0x28, rows);
    put_u32(dst, node.header + 0x30, (unsigned)base);
    return true;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the force itself
struct Result {
    bool ok = false;
    char note[200] = "";
    unsigned kept_squads = 0, kept_units = 0, added_squads = 0, added_units = 0;
    unsigned mine_side = 0, peer_side = 0;
};

// Everything the insert does, without writing anything: which rows stay and which come in.
struct Plan {
    std::vector<unsigned> keep_squads, keep_units, add_squads, add_units;
    unsigned mine_side = 0, peer_side = 0, other_side = 0, enemy_tag = 0;
    int side_col = -1, tag_col = -1;
    unsigned cols_s = 0, cols_u = 0;
};

inline bool plan(const std::vector<byte> &mine, const std::vector<byte> &peer, Plan *p, std::string *why)
{
    unsigned mu = 0, me = 0, pu = 0, pe = 0;
    if (!image::sides(mine, &mu, &me)) { *why = "this battle's description has no sides"; return false; }
    if (!image::sides(peer, &pu, &pe)) { *why = "the other player's force has no sides"; return false; }
    Table ms = table(mine, "squads"), mu_t = table(mine, "units");
    Table ps = table(peer, "squads"), pu_t = table(peer, "units");
    if (!ms.ok || !mu_t.ok) { *why = "this battle's description has no squads or no units"; return false; }
    if (!ps.ok || !pu_t.ok) { *why = "the other player's force has no squads or no units"; return false; }
    if (ms.cols != ps.cols || mu_t.cols != pu_t.cols) { *why = "the two descriptions lay their tables out differently"; return false; }
    Columns c = columns(mine), pc = columns(peer);
    if (c.side < 0 || c.tag < 0) { *why = "this battle's squads table has no side or command column"; return false; }
    if (pc.side < 0 || pc.tag < 0) { *why = "the other player's squads table has no side or command column"; return false; }
    if (pc.side != c.side || pc.tag != c.tag) { *why = "the two descriptions keep their side and command columns in different places"; return false; }

    p->mine_side = mu;
    p->other_side = me;
    p->peer_side = pu;                                  // the side his own force is on, in his description
    p->side_col = c.side;
    p->tag_col = c.tag;
    p->cols_s = ms.cols;
    p->cols_u = mu_t.cols;

    // The tag the computer's side carries here (what the other player's squads become).
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < ms.rows && !p->enemy_tag; r++)
        if (u32_at(mine, cell_at(ms, r, (unsigned)c.side)) == me) p->enemy_tag = u32_at(mine, cell_at(ms, r, (unsigned)c.tag));
    if (!p->enemy_tag) p->enemy_tag = image::k_enem;

    std::unordered_set<unsigned> kept, mine_ids;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < ms.rows; r++) {
        unsigned side = u32_at(mine, cell_at(ms, r, (unsigned)c.side)), id = squad_id(mine, ms, r);
        if (side == me) continue;                        // the side the other player commands: replaced
        p->keep_squads.push_back(r);
        if (id) { kept.insert(id); mine_ids.insert(id); }
    }
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < mu_t.rows; r++) {
        unsigned id = unit_id(mine, mu_t, r);
        if (id && kept.count(id & ~0x1fu)) p->keep_units.push_back(r);
    }

    std::unordered_set<unsigned> added;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < ps.rows; r++) {
        if (u32_at(peer, cell_at(ps, r, (unsigned)pc.side)) != pu) continue;
        unsigned id = squad_id(peer, ps, r);
        if (id && mine_ids.count(id)) {
            char text[120];
            sprintf_s(text, "the two forces use the same squad number %u", id);
            *why = text;
            return false;
        }
        p->add_squads.push_back(r);
        if (id) added.insert(id);
    }
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < pu_t.rows; r++) {
        unsigned id = unit_id(peer, pu_t, r);
        if (id && added.count(id & ~0x1fu)) p->add_units.push_back(r);
    }
    if (p->add_squads.empty()) { *why = "the other player's force has no squads of the side he commands"; return false; }
    if (p->add_units.empty()) { *why = "the other player's force has no units"; return false; }
    return true;
}

// Parse the result back and check that it is the battle it was plus the other player's force: the node
// table is untouched, both tables have the expected row counts, every text cell resolves, every unit's
// squad is there.
inline bool check(const std::vector<byte> &was, const std::vector<byte> &now, const Plan &p, std::string *why)
{
    if (now.size() < was.size()) { *why = "the file got shorter"; return false; }
    // Everything that was in the file has to be where it was, except the two node headers this wrote to.
    Table ws = table(was, "squads"), wu = table(was, "units");
    for (size_t i = 0; i < was.size(); i++) {
        bool in_header = (i >= ws.header + 0x28 && i < ws.header + 0x34) || (i >= wu.header + 0x28 && i < wu.header + 0x34);
        if (!in_header && now[i] != was[i]) { *why = "bytes that were there have changed"; return false; }
    }
    Table s = table(now, "squads"), u = table(now, "units");
    if (!s.ok || !u.ok) { *why = "the rewritten file has no squads or no units"; return false; }
    if (s.rows != p.keep_squads.size() + p.add_squads.size()) { *why = "the squads table did not take the force"; return false; }
    if (u.rows != p.keep_units.size() + p.add_units.size()) { *why = "the units table did not take the force"; return false; }
    std::unordered_set<unsigned> squads;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < s.rows; r++) {
        unsigned id = squad_id(now, s, r);
        if (!id) { *why = "a squad row has no number"; return false; }
        squads.insert(id);
        for (unsigned col = 0; col < s.cols; col++)
            if (u32_at(now, cell_at(s, r, col) + k_kind_offset) == k_text) {
                std::string text;
                if (!text_at(now, cell_at(s, r, col), &text)) { *why = "a squad's text does not read back"; return false; }
            }
    }
    unsigned orphans = 0;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < u.rows; r++) {
        unsigned id = unit_id(now, u, r);
        if (!id) { *why = "a unit row has no number"; return false; }
        if (!squads.count(id & ~0x1fu)) orphans++;
    }
    if (orphans) { *why = "the units table has units of squads that are not in it"; return false; }
    unsigned user = 0, enemy = 0;
    if (!image::sides(now, &user, &enemy)) { *why = "the rewritten file has no sides"; return false; }
    return true;
}

// The whole operation: this description keeps its own side and takes the other player's squads and units
// for the side he commands. `mine` is modified in place only when everything checks out.
inline Result insert(std::vector<byte> &mine, const std::vector<byte> &peer)
{
    Result r;
    Plan p;
    std::string why;
    if (!plan(mine, peer, &p, &why)) { strcpy_s(r.note, sizeof r.note, why.c_str()); return r; }

    Table ms = table(mine, "squads"), mu = table(mine, "units");
    Table ps = table(peer, "squads"), pu = table(peer, "units");
    std::vector<byte> squads, units;
    std::vector<Pending> squads_text, units_text;

    // this game's rows: text stays where it is; the side and tag columns are written as they are
    if (!build_rows(mine, ms, p.keep_squads, ms.cols, true, -1, 0, -1, 0, &squads, &squads_text, &why) ||
        !build_rows(mine, mu, p.keep_units, mu.cols, true, -1, 0, -1, 0, &units, &units_text, &why)) {
        strcpy_s(r.note, sizeof r.note, why.c_str());
        return r;
    }
    // the other player's rows: text is copied in, his side and 'USER' tag become this battle's other side
    if (!build_rows(peer, ps, p.add_squads, ps.cols, false, p.side_col, p.other_side, p.tag_col, p.enemy_tag,
                    &squads, &squads_text, &why) ||
        !build_rows(peer, pu, p.add_units, pu.cols, false, -1, 0, -1, 0, &units, &units_text, &why)) {
        strcpy_s(r.note, sizeof r.note, why.c_str());
        return r;
    }

    std::vector<byte> out = mine;
    if (!write_table(out, ms, squads, squads_text) || !write_table(out, mu, units, units_text)) {
        strcpy_s(r.note, sizeof r.note, "the tables could not be written");
        return r;
    }
    if (!check(mine, out, p, &why)) { strcpy_s(r.note, sizeof r.note, why.c_str()); return r; }

    mine.swap(out);
    r.ok = true;
    r.kept_squads = (unsigned)p.keep_squads.size();
    r.kept_units = (unsigned)p.keep_units.size();
    r.added_squads = (unsigned)p.add_squads.size();
    r.added_units = (unsigned)p.add_units.size();
    r.mine_side = p.mine_side;
    r.peer_side = p.peer_side;
    r.note[0] = 0;
    return r;
}

// Is this file a battle description this code can work with? (header, node table, a units and a squads
// table). Used before anything is sent or applied, so a wrong file costs a log line, not a battle.
inline bool is_description(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    if (b.size() < 0x100 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return false;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table_at = u32_at(b, 8);
    if (table_at < 0x40 || table_at > b.size() || count > 100000) return false;
    if (table_at + (size_t)count * 0x80 > b.size()) return false;
    return table(b, "squads").ok && table(b, "units").ok;
}

} // namespace force
==================== END development/net-v21/src/force.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/hit_sync.cpp (sha256 979434d329a59e5270cd261e1b06c2ee5be2b5ee4d092f53aab9b00aa2fc2e13) ====================
// Experimental owner-authoritative hits. Only the checked human/vehicle vtable entries are
// patched; the game still performs the hit normally on its target's owner. The remote game
// replays a pointer-free record on its simulation thread. No game binary or save is modified.
// The calling convention and object layouts are recovered, not verified in the real game.
#include "common.h"
#include "env_value.h"
#include "hit_wire.h"
#include "hooks.h"
#include <atomic>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>

typedef uintptr_t (*HitFn)(Object *, void *, u32, Object *, u32, bool);
static std::atomic<HitFn> g_human, g_vehicle;
static std::atomic<bool> g_installed;
static std::atomic<unsigned long long> g_wrong_thread;
static void **g_human_slot, **g_vehicle_slot;
static ObjMan *g_om;
static DWORD g_thread, g_wait_at;
static u32 g_wait_seq;
static bool g_attempted, g_operational = true, g_log, g_injecting;
static unsigned long long g_sent, g_applied, g_suppressed, g_refused, g_overflow;

static bool writable(void *p, size_t n)
{
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION m;
    if (!p || !VirtualQuery(p, &m, sizeof m) || m.State != MEM_COMMIT || (m.Protect & PAGE_GUARD)) return false;
    DWORD prot = m.Protect & 0xff;
    if (prot != PAGE_READWRITE && prot != PAGE_WRITECOPY && prot != PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE && prot != PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY) return false;
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t)p, b = (uintptr_t)m.BaseAddress;
    return a >= b && a - b <= m.RegionSize && n <= m.RegionSize - (a - b);
}

static bool unit(Object *o, u64 *key)
{
    if (!readable(o, sizeof(Object)) || o->objman != g_om || !memchr(o->full, 0, sizeof o->full) || !readable(o->iface, 8)) return false;
    if (memcmp(o->iface, "i_human", 8) && memcmp(o->iface, "i_techn", 8)) return false;
    return session_unit_key(o, key) && hit_wire::unit_key(*key, false) && session_unit(*key) == o;
}

// A hit may contain a source object that is not an indexed combat unit. v20 also represents
// such pointers as null; keeping them as raw addresses across processes would be unsafe.
static u64 optional_unit(Object *o)
{
    u64 key = 0;
    return o && unit(o, &key) ? key : 0;
}

static bool description_id(void *desc, u32 *out)
{
    if (!desc) { *out = 0xffffffffu; return true; }
    if (!readable(desc, 0x60) || !readable(g_om, sizeof *g_om) || !readable(g_om->vtbl, 58 * sizeof(void *)) || !g_om->vtbl[56]) return false;
    *out = ((u32 (*)(ObjMan *, void *))g_om->vtbl[56])(g_om, desc);
    return *out != 0xffffffffu;
}

static bool component_rows(Object *target, const unsigned char **rows, u32 *count)
{
    const char *t = (const char *)target;
    if (!readable(t, 0x860)) return false;
    memcpy(rows, t + 0x850, sizeof *rows);
    memcpy(count, t + 0x85c, sizeof *count);
    return *count && *count <= hit_wire::max_parts && readable(*rows, (size_t)*count * 0x60);
}

static void disable_sync(const char *reason)
{
    if (g_operational) mp_log("hit: synchronization stopped for this session: %s; local game remains in control", reason);
    g_operational = false;
}

static uintptr_t capture(bool vehicle, Object *target, void *record, u32 a3, Object *source, u32 a5, bool a6)
{
    HitFn original = (vehicle ? g_vehicle : g_human).load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!original) return 0; // never deliberately publish a slot before its original
    if (GetCurrentThreadId() != g_thread) {
        g_wrong_thread.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
    }
    if (g_injecting || !g_operational || !g_installed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || !session_connected() ||
        !(session_peer_features() & PEER_HIT_SYNC) || !xlate_ready())
        return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
    u64 key = 0;
    if (!unit(target, &key) || (key >> 32) != (vehicle ? 1u : 2u) || !readable(record, hit_wire::engine_size))
        return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
    if (!session_owns(target)) {
        ++g_suppressed;
        return 0;
    }
    const size_t seed_offset = vehicle ? 0xb64 : 0x2768;
    if (!readable(target, seed_offset + sizeof(u32))) {
        disable_sync("target layout is unreadable");
        return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
    }
    void *d1 = 0, *d2 = 0; Object *object = 0;
    memcpy(&d1, (char *)record + 0x68, sizeof d1);
    memcpy(&d2, (char *)record + 0x70, sizeof d2);
    memcpy(&object, (char *)record + 0x78, sizeof object);
    u32 id1 = 0, id2 = 0, seed = 0;
    if (!description_id(d1, &id1) || !description_id(d2, &id2)) {
        disable_sync("hit descriptions could not be identified");
        return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
    }
    memcpy(&seed, (char *)target + seed_offset, sizeof seed);
    ShotRec s;
    hit_wire::encode(s, vehicle, key, optional_unit(source), optional_unit(object), id1, id2, a3, a5, a6, seed, record);
    u32 part = 0; memcpy(&part, (char *)record + 0x60, 4);
    if (part != 0xffffffffu) {
        const unsigned char *rows = nullptr; u32 count = 0; hit_wire::PartHint hint = {};
        if (!component_rows(target, &rows, &count) || !hit_wire::make_hint(rows, count, part, &hint)) {
            disable_sync("component list cannot be matched safely");
            return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
        }
        hit_wire::put_hint(s, hint);
    }
    if (!shots_try_queue(s, hit_wire::queue_limit)) {
        ++g_overflow;
        disable_sync("bounded event queue is full or its sequence was exhausted");
    } else {
        ++g_sent;
        if (g_log) mp_log("hit: queued target=%llx type=%u kind=%u seq=%u", key, vehicle ? 1u : 0u, a3, s.seq);
    }
    return original(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6);
}

static uintptr_t human_hook(Object *o, void *r, u32 a3, Object *a4, u32 a5, bool a6)
{ return capture(false, o, r, a3, a4, a5, a6); }
static uintptr_t vehicle_hook(Object *o, void *r, u32 a3, Object *a4, u32 a5, bool a6)
{ return capture(true, o, r, a3, a4, a5, a6); }

void hits_install(ObjMan *om)
{
    if (g_attempted || !GetModuleHandleA("adv_physic.codelib")) return;
    g_attempted = true;
    g_om = om; g_thread = GetCurrentThreadId(); g_operational = true;
    int value = 0;
    if (env_integer("MP_SYNC_HITS", &value) && !value) { g_operational = false; mp_log("hit: local simulation only (MP_SYNC_HITS=0)"); return; }
    g_log = env_integer("MP_LOG_HITS", &value) && value;
    if (!hook_image_identity("adv_physic.codelib", 0x66f2a91e, 0xf2000)) {
        disable_sync("unrecognised physics image/layout"); return;
    }
    static const unsigned char human_sig[] = { 0x48,0x8b,0xc4,0x48,0x89,0x58,0x20,0x55,0x56,0x41,0x57,0x48,0x83,0xec,0x60,0x80,0xbc,0x24,0xa8,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x4d,0x8b,0xf9,0x41,0x8b,0xe8,0x48,0x8b,0xf2 };
    static const unsigned char vehicle_sig[] = { 0x48,0x8b,0xc4,0x48,0x89,0x58,0x20,0x44,0x89,0x40,0x18,0x55,0x56,0x57,0x41,0x54,0x41,0x55,0x41,0x56,0x41,0x57,0x48,0x8d,0xa8,0xa8,0xfe,0xff,0xff,0x48,0x81,0xec };
    const HookSig hs[] = {{0x66c90, human_sig, sizeof human_sig}};
    const HookSig vs[] = {{0x43230, vehicle_sig, sizeof vehicle_sig}};
    char *h = hook_resolve_checked("adv_physic.codelib", hs, 1, "human hit");
    char *v = hook_resolve_checked("adv_physic.codelib", vs, 1, "vehicle hit");
    if (!h || !v) { disable_sync("unrecognised hit functions"); return; }
    HookVtableMember hm[] = {{0, h}}, vm[] = {{0, v}};
    void **ht = hook_vtable_checked("adv_physic.codelib", 0xc6760, hm, 1, "human hit slot");
    void **vt = hook_vtable_checked("adv_physic.codelib", 0xc79d8, vm, 1, "vehicle hit slot");
    if (!ht || !vt || ht == vt) { disable_sync("unrecognised or ambiguous hit slots"); return; }
    g_human.store((HitFn)h, std::memory_order_release);
    g_vehicle.store((HitFn)v, std::memory_order_release);
    if (!hook_swap_vtable_slot(ht, h, (void *)human_hook)) { disable_sync("human slot changed or could not be patched"); return; }
    if (!hook_swap_vtable_slot(vt, v, (void *)vehicle_hook)) {
        if (!hook_swap_vtable_slot(ht, (void *)human_hook, h)) {
            g_human_slot = ht; // retain the address for another conditional rollback on stop
            mp_log("hit: WARNING: human slot rollback was not possible");
        }
        disable_sync("vehicle slot changed or could not be patched"); return;
    }
    g_human_slot = ht; g_vehicle_slot = vt;
    g_installed.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    mp_log("hit: experimental checked hooks installed; enabled only when both peers and number tables are ready");
}

bool hits_available(void) { return g_operational && g_installed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && xlate_ready(); }

// Wait briefly for a unit/table to appear without acknowledging a later event. Refusal is
// explicit after the deadline; otherwise a deleted unit could stall every shot indefinitely.
static bool wait_or_refuse(u32 seq, const char *why)
{
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    if (g_wait_seq != seq) { g_wait_seq = seq; g_wait_at = now; return false; }
    if ((DWORD)(now - g_wait_at) < 5000) return false;
    ++g_refused;
    mp_log("hit: event %u refused after waiting: %s", seq, why);
    g_wait_seq = 0;
    return true;
}

// Isolate the structured exception guard from C++ objects. Catch only access violations, report
// the failure and disable new replication. This cannot undo partial engine side effects.
static bool invoke(HitFn fn, Object *target, void *record, u32 a3, Object *source, u32 a5, bool a6)
{
    __try { fn(target, record, a3, source, a5, a6); return true; }
    __except (GetExceptionCode() == EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION ? EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER : EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH) { return false; }
}

bool hits_received(const ShotRec &s, ObjMan *om)
{
    if (!hit_wire::valid(s)) { ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: malformed event %u refused", s.seq); return true; }
    if (!g_installed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || om != g_om) { ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: event %u refused: hook not installed", s.seq); return true; }
    if (!xlate_ready()) return wait_or_refuse(s.seq, "number tables are not ready");
    Object *target = session_unit(s.shooter);
    Object *source = s.obj2 ? session_unit(s.obj2) : 0;
    Object *object = s.obj5 ? session_unit(s.obj5) : 0;
    if (!target || (s.obj2 && !source) || (s.obj5 && !object)) return wait_or_refuse(s.seq, "unit not found");
    u64 key = 0;
    if (!unit(target, &key) || key != s.shooter || session_owns(target)) { ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: event %u refused: target/ownership mismatch", s.seq); return true; }
    u32 n1 = 0, n2 = 0;
    if (!xlate_desc_checked(s.ammo, &n1) || !xlate_desc_checked(s.weapon, &n2)) { ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: event %u refused: description number not mapped", s.seq); return true; }
    if (!readable(om, sizeof *om) || !readable(om->vtbl, 58 * sizeof(void *)) || !om->vtbl[57]) { ++g_refused; return true; }
    void *d1 = n1 == 0xffffffffu ? 0 : ((void *(*)(ObjMan *, u32))om->vtbl[57])(om, n1);
    void *d2 = n2 == 0xffffffffu ? 0 : ((void *(*)(ObjMan *, u32))om->vtbl[57])(om, n2);
    if ((n1 != 0xffffffffu && !readable(d1, 0x60)) || (n2 != 0xffffffffu && !readable(d2, 0x60))) { ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: event %u refused: local description unreadable", s.seq); return true; }
    char record[hit_wire::engine_size] = {};
    memcpy(record, s.launch, hit_wire::copied);
    u32 part = 0; memcpy(&part, record + 0x60, sizeof part);
    if (part != 0xffffffffu) {
        const unsigned char *rows = nullptr; u32 count = 0, local_part = 0; hit_wire::PartHint hint = {};
        memcpy(&hint, s.pos, sizeof hint);
        if (!component_rows(target, &rows, &count) || !hit_wire::translate(rows, count, part, hint, &local_part)) {
            ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: event %u refused: component layout/part %08x not matched", s.seq, part); return true;
        }
        memcpy(record + 0x60, &local_part, sizeof local_part);
    }
    memcpy(record + 0x68, &d1, sizeof d1);
    memcpy(record + 0x70, &d2, sizeof d2);
    memcpy(record + 0x78, &object, sizeof object);
    const size_t seed_offset = s.flags ? 0xb64 : 0x2768;
    if (!readable(target, seed_offset + sizeof(u32)) || !writable((char *)target + seed_offset, sizeof(u32))) { ++g_refused; mp_log("hit: event %u refused: target seed not writable", s.seq); return true; }
    HitFn original = (s.flags ? g_vehicle : g_human).load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!original) { ++g_refused; return true; }
    memcpy((char *)target + seed_offset, &s.desc3, sizeof s.desc3);
    g_injecting = true;
    bool ok = invoke(original, target, record, s.arg6, source, s.ammo_code, s.has_pos != 0);
    g_injecting = false;
    g_wait_seq = 0;
    if (!ok) { ++g_refused; disable_sync("the replayed engine call raised an access violation; effects may be partial"); }
    else { ++g_applied; if (g_log) mp_log("hit: replayed target=%llx kind=%u seq=%u", s.shooter, s.arg6, s.seq); }
    return true;
}

void hits_report(void)
{
    unsigned long long other = g_wrong_thread.exchange(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (g_sent || g_applied || g_suppressed || g_refused || g_overflow || other)
        mp_log("hit: 5 s: %llu queued, %llu replayed, %llu non-owner local calls held, %llu refused, %llu queue failures, %llu calls on other threads passed through", g_sent, g_applied, g_suppressed, g_refused, g_overflow, other);
    g_sent = g_applied = g_suppressed = g_refused = g_overflow = 0;
}

void hits_reset_peer(void) { g_wait_seq = 0; g_wait_at = 0; }

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
// No PE/vtable patching in the fixture. The production capture and replay paths are unchanged.
void hits_test_engine(ObjMan *om, HitFn human_fn, HitFn vehicle_fn)
{
    g_om = om; g_thread = GetCurrentThreadId(); g_operational = true; g_attempted = true;
    g_human.store(human_fn); g_vehicle.store(vehicle_fn); g_installed.store(true);
    g_wait_seq = 0;
}
uintptr_t hits_test_capture(bool vehicle, Object *target, void *record, u32 a3, Object *source, u32 a5, bool a6)
{ return capture(vehicle, target, record, a3, source, a5, a6); }
void hits_test_expire_wait() { g_wait_at = GetTickCount() - 5001; }
#endif

void hits_stop(void)
{
    g_installed.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    if (g_human_slot && !hook_swap_vtable_slot(g_human_slot, (void *)human_hook, (void *)g_human.load())) mp_log("hit: human slot was changed by another module; not overwritten");
    if (g_vehicle_slot && !hook_swap_vtable_slot(g_vehicle_slot, (void *)vehicle_hook, (void *)g_vehicle.load())) mp_log("hit: vehicle slot was changed by another module; not overwritten");
    g_human_slot = g_vehicle_slot = 0;
    g_attempted = false; g_wait_seq = 0; g_om = 0; g_operational = true;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/hit_sync.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/hit_wire.h (sha256 c884d4db769b3816eaac22d552872fd8e7558f4a7811fa2850a2009e0e377576) ====================
// Pointer-free envelope for an experimental target-owner hit on the existing ordered shot stream.
// The first 0x68 bytes have the same meaning as the captured engine record. The three pointer
// fields at 0x68/0x70/0x78 are never sent: they are reconstructed only after local validation.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>

namespace hit_wire {
static const unsigned kind = 7;
static const size_t copied = 0x68;
static const size_t engine_size = 0x80;
static const size_t queue_limit = 4096;
static const uint32_t max_parts = 4096, max_groups = 6;
struct PartHint { uint32_t count, ordinal; uint64_t shape; };
static_assert(sizeof(PartHint) == 16, "hit part metadata");

// v20 matched equal-length component lists by low ten bits, and mapped at most six distinct
// high-bit groups. Hash that same normalized group/low-bit sequence; raw process-specific
// group numbers never have to be guessed or sent as addresses. Callers validate the row span.
inline bool shape(const unsigned char *rows, uint32_t count, uint64_t *out)
{
    if (!rows || !out || !count || count > max_parts) return false;
    uint32_t groups[max_groups] = {}, used = 0;
    uint64_t h = 14695981039346656037ull;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        uint32_t id = 0; memcpy(&id, rows + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x48, 4);
        uint32_t high = id >> 10, group = 0;
        while (group < used && groups[group] != high) ++group;
        if (group == used) { if (used == max_groups) return false; groups[used++] = high; }
        uint32_t low = id & 1023u;
        const unsigned char b[3] = {(unsigned char)group, (unsigned char)low, (unsigned char)(low >> 8)};
        for (unsigned char x : b) h = (h ^ x) * 1099511628211ull;
    }
    *out = h ? h : 1;
    return true;
}
inline bool make_hint(const unsigned char *rows, uint32_t count, uint32_t part, PartHint *hint)
{
    if (!hint || part == 0xffffffffu || !shape(rows, count, &hint->shape)) return false;
    hint->count = count; hint->ordinal = count;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        uint32_t id = 0; memcpy(&id, rows + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x48, 4);
        if ((id >> 10) == (part >> 10)) { hint->ordinal = i; break; }
    }
    return hint->ordinal < count;
}
inline bool translate(const unsigned char *rows, uint32_t count, uint32_t part,
                      const PartHint &hint, uint32_t *translated)
{
    uint64_t local_shape = 0;
    if (!translated || !hint.shape || hint.count != count || hint.ordinal >= count ||
        !shape(rows, count, &local_shape) || local_shape != hint.shape) return false;
    uint32_t id = 0; memcpy(&id, rows + (size_t)hint.ordinal * 0x60 + 0x48, 4);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < hint.ordinal; ++i) {
        uint32_t before = 0; memcpy(&before, rows + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x48, 4);
        if ((before >> 10) == (id >> 10)) return false; // sender always chooses the first row in a group
    }
    *translated = (id & ~1023u) | (part & 1023u);
    return true;
}

inline bool unit_key(uint64_t key, bool nullable)
{
    if (!key) return nullable;
    uint64_t type = key >> 32;
    return type == 1 || type == 2;
}
inline bool valid(const ShotRec &s)
{
    if (s.kind != kind || s.flags > 1 || s.has_pos > 1 || s.pad > 1 || !unit_key(s.shooter, false) ||
        !unit_key(s.obj2, true) || !unit_key(s.obj5, true) ||
        (s.shooter >> 32) != (s.flags ? 1u : 2u)) return false;
    // Part metadata occupies the otherwise unused position bytes, never a pointer.
    uint32_t part = 0; memcpy(&part, s.launch + 0x60, 4);
    PartHint hint = {}; memcpy(&hint, s.pos, sizeof hint);
    if (part == 0xffffffffu) {
        if (s.pad || hint.count || hint.ordinal || hint.shape) return false;
    } else if (s.pad != 1 || !hint.count || hint.count > max_parts || hint.ordinal >= hint.count || !hint.shape) return false;
    const unsigned char *checks = (const unsigned char *)s.ammo_check;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof s.ammo_check; ++i) if (checks[i]) return false;
    for (size_t i = copied; i < sizeof s.launch; ++i) if (s.launch[i]) return false;
    return true;
}
inline void encode(ShotRec &s, bool vehicle, uint64_t target, uint64_t source, uint64_t object,
                   uint32_t desc1, uint32_t desc2, uint32_t argument, uint32_t argument5,
                   bool argument6, uint32_t seed, const void *record)
{
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof s);
    s.kind = kind; s.flags = vehicle ? 1 : 0; s.has_pos = argument6 ? 1 : 0;
    s.shooter = target; s.obj2 = source; s.obj5 = object;
    s.ammo = desc1; s.weapon = desc2; s.arg6 = argument; s.ammo_code = argument5; s.desc3 = seed;
    memcpy(s.launch, record, copied);
}
inline void put_hint(ShotRec &s, const PartHint &hint) {
    s.pad = 1; memcpy(s.pos, &hint, sizeof hint);
}
} // namespace hit_wire
==================== END development/net-v21/src/hit_wire.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/hooks.cpp (sha256 f64f29098ebf122148ba48f66ee909f0c7329b33ad7e1a8c73cd0b8ed47275e2) ====================
#include "hooks.h"
#include "common.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>

static bool safe_read(const void *p, size_t n)
{
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi;
    if (!p || !VirtualQuery(p, &mbi, sizeof mbi) || mbi.State != MEM_COMMIT || (mbi.Protect & PAGE_GUARD)) return false;
    DWORD protection = mbi.Protect & 0xff;
    if (protection != PAGE_READONLY && protection != PAGE_READWRITE && protection != PAGE_WRITECOPY &&
        protection != PAGE_EXECUTE_READ && protection != PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE && protection != PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY) return false;
    uintptr_t address = (uintptr_t)p, region = (uintptr_t)mbi.BaseAddress;
    return address >= region && address - region <= mbi.RegionSize && n <= mbi.RegionSize - (address - region);
}

// A codelib is a mapped PE image. All bounds and section flags are checked before looking at a
// fixed RVA or scanning it: an unknown/smaller build must not crash just because an RVA is stale.
struct Image {
    const char *base;
    unsigned size, count;
    const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER *sections;
};

static bool image_info(const char *base, Image *out)
{
    if (!base || !safe_read(base, sizeof(IMAGE_DOS_HEADER))) return false;
    const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *dos = (const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *)base;
    if (dos->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE || dos->e_lfanew < (long)sizeof(*dos) || dos->e_lfanew > 0x100000) return false;
    const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS *nt = (const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS *)(base + dos->e_lfanew);
    if (!safe_read(nt, sizeof(*nt)) || nt->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE ||
        nt->OptionalHeader.Magic != IMAGE_NT_OPTIONAL_HDR64_MAGIC ||
        nt->FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader < sizeof(IMAGE_OPTIONAL_HEADER) ||
        !nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage || !nt->FileHeader.NumberOfSections || nt->FileHeader.NumberOfSections > 96)
        return false;
    if ((unsigned)dos->e_lfanew > nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage ||
        sizeof(*nt) > nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage - (unsigned)dos->e_lfanew) return false;
    const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER *sections = IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(nt);
    size_t size = (size_t)nt->FileHeader.NumberOfSections * sizeof(*sections);
    uintptr_t offset = (uintptr_t)sections - (uintptr_t)base;
    if (offset > nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage || size > nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage - offset ||
        !safe_read(sections, size)) return false;
    out->base = base;
    out->size = nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage;
    out->count = nt->FileHeader.NumberOfSections;
    out->sections = sections;
    return true;
}

bool hook_image_identity(const char *module, unsigned timestamp, unsigned image_size)
{
    HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleA(module);
    Image image;
    if (!m || !image_info((const char *)m, &image) || image.size != image_size) return false;
    const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *dos = (const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *)m;
    const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS *nt = (const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS *)((const char *)m + dos->e_lfanew);
    return nt->FileHeader.TimeDateStamp == timestamp;
}

static bool executable_range(const Image &image, const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER &section, unsigned *start, unsigned *size)
{
    if (!(section.Characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE) || section.VirtualAddress >= image.size) return false;
    unsigned length = section.Misc.VirtualSize > section.SizeOfRawData ? section.Misc.VirtualSize : section.SizeOfRawData;
    if (length > image.size - section.VirtualAddress) length = image.size - section.VirtualAddress;
    if (!length) return false;
    *start = section.VirtualAddress;
    *size = length;
    return true;
}

static bool fixed_match(const Image &image, const HookSig &h)
{
    if (!h.rva || !h.sig || !h.size) return false;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < image.count; ++i) {
        unsigned start, size;
        if (!executable_range(image, image.sections[i], &start, &size) || h.rva < start) continue;
        unsigned delta = h.rva - start;
        if (delta > size || h.size > size - delta) continue;
        const char *p = image.base + h.rva;
        return safe_read(p, h.size) && !memcmp(p, h.sig, h.size);
    }
    return false;
}

// Search readable regions of executable sections. Never scan across inaccessible pages. A split
// signature at a protection boundary is conservatively missed rather than read outside a region.
static int scan_signature(const Image &image, const HookSig &h, const char **found)
{
    if (!h.sig || !h.size) return 0;
    int hits = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < image.count && hits < 2; ++i) {
        unsigned start, size;
        if (!executable_range(image, image.sections[i], &start, &size) || size < h.size) continue;
        const char *p = image.base + start, *end = p + size;
        while (p < end && hits < 2) {
            MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi;
            if (!VirtualQuery(p, &mbi, sizeof mbi)) break;
            uintptr_t next_address = (uintptr_t)mbi.BaseAddress + mbi.RegionSize;
            if (next_address <= (uintptr_t)p) break;
            const char *next = next_address < (uintptr_t)end ? (const char *)next_address : end;
            size_t available = (size_t)(next - p);
            if (available >= h.size && safe_read(p, available)) {
                const char *last = next - h.size;
                for (const char *q = p; q <= last; ++q) {
                    if (!memcmp(q, h.sig, h.size)) {
                        *found = q;
                        if (++hits == 2) break;
                    }
                }
            }
            p = next;
        }
    }
    return hits;
}

// Result code: 0 found, 1 bad image, 2 absent, 3 ambiguous. Fixed RVAs for all known builds are
// checked before *any* fallback scan. Different unique signatures resolving to different addresses
// are ambiguous too; do not guess which version of the function is intended.
static char *resolve_image(const char *base, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, int *reason)
{
    Image image;
    if (!list || !count || !image_info(base, &image)) { *reason = 1; return 0; }
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; ++i)
        if (fixed_match(image, list[i])) { *reason = 0; return (char *)base + list[i].rva; }
    const char *answer = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        const char *candidate = 0;
        int hits = scan_signature(image, list[i], &candidate);
        if (hits > 1 || (hits == 1 && answer && answer != candidate)) { *reason = 3; return 0; }
        if (hits == 1) answer = candidate;
    }
    *reason = answer ? 0 : 2;
    return (char *)answer;
}

static bool readonly_data_range(const Image &image, const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER &section, unsigned *start, unsigned *size)
{
    static const char name[8] = {'.','r','d','a','t','a',0,0};
    if (memcmp(section.Name, name, sizeof name) || !(section.Characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ) ||
        (section.Characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE) || section.VirtualAddress >= image.size) return false;
    unsigned length = section.Misc.VirtualSize > section.SizeOfRawData ? section.Misc.VirtualSize : section.SizeOfRawData;
    if (length > image.size - section.VirtualAddress) length = image.size - section.VirtualAddress;
    *start = section.VirtualAddress; *size = length;
    return length != 0;
}

static bool vtable_matches(const char *base, const HookVtableMember *members, unsigned count, size_t span)
{
    if (!safe_read(base, span)) return false;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        const void *value = 0;
        memcpy(&value, base + (size_t)members[i].index * sizeof(void *), sizeof value);
        if (value != members[i].target) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// reason: 0 found, 1 invalid image/specification, 2 absent, 3 ambiguous.
static void **resolve_vtable_image(const char *base, unsigned rva, const HookVtableMember *members,
                                   unsigned count, int *reason)
{
    Image image;
    if (!members || !count || count > 64 || !image_info(base, &image)) { *reason = 1; return 0; }
    unsigned last = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        if (!members[i].target || members[i].index > 256) { *reason = 1; return 0; }
        if (members[i].index > last) last = members[i].index;
    }
    size_t span = ((size_t)last + 1) * sizeof(void *);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < image.count; ++i) {
        unsigned start, size;
        if (!readonly_data_range(image, image.sections[i], &start, &size) || (rva & (sizeof(void *) - 1)) || rva < start) continue;
        unsigned delta = rva - start;
        if (delta <= size && span <= size - delta && vtable_matches(base + rva, members, count, span)) {
            *reason = 0; return (void **)(base + rva);
        }
    }
    void **found = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < image.count; ++i) {
        unsigned start, size;
        if (!readonly_data_range(image, image.sections[i], &start, &size) || size < span) continue;
        unsigned first = (start + (unsigned)sizeof(void *) - 1) & ~((unsigned)sizeof(void *) - 1);
        unsigned end = start + size - (unsigned)span;
        for (unsigned at = first; at <= end; at += (unsigned)sizeof(void *)) {
            const char *candidate = base + at;
            const void *value = 0;
            const char *first_member = candidate + (size_t)members[0].index * sizeof(void *);
            if (!safe_read(first_member, sizeof value)) continue;
            memcpy(&value, first_member, sizeof value);
            if (value != members[0].target || !vtable_matches(candidate, members, count, span)) continue;
            if (found) { *reason = 3; return 0; }
            found = (void **)candidate;
        }
    }
    *reason = found ? 0 : 2;
    return found;
}

void **hook_vtable_checked(const char *module, unsigned rva, const HookVtableMember *members,
                           unsigned count, const char *what)
{
    HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleA(module);
    int reason = 1;
    void **table = m ? resolve_vtable_image((const char *)m, rva, members, count, &reason) : 0;
    if (!table) {
        const char *why = reason == 1 ? "invalid or unreadable PE/specification" : reason == 3 ? "ambiguous tables" : "no matching table";
        mp_log("hooks: %s: %s in %s, the feature stays off", what, why, module);
    } else if ((char *)table != (char *)m + rva) {
        mp_log("hooks: %s table moved to +0x%lx; the unique data scan found it", what, (long)((char *)table - (char *)m));
    }
    return table;
}

bool hook_swap_vtable_slot(void **slot, void *expected, void *replacement)
{
    if (!slot || !expected || !replacement || ((uintptr_t)slot & (sizeof(void *) - 1)) || !safe_read(slot, sizeof *slot)) return false;
    DWORD old = 0;
    if (!VirtualProtect(slot, sizeof *slot, PAGE_READWRITE, &old)) return false;
    void *previous = InterlockedCompareExchangePointer((void *volatile *)slot, replacement, expected);
    DWORD ignored = 0;
    if (!VirtualProtect(slot, sizeof *slot, old, &ignored))
        mp_log("hooks: warning: could not restore vtable page protection (error %lu)", GetLastError());
    return previous == expected;
}

long hook_offset(const char *module, const void *address)
{
    HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleA(module);
    if (!m || !address) return -1;
    return (long)((const char *)address - (const char *)m);
}

char *hook_resolve_checked(const char *module, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, const char *what)
{
    HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleA(module);
    if (!m) {
        mp_log("hooks: %s: %s is not loaded, the feature stays off", what, module);
        return 0;
    }
    int reason = 0;
    char *p = resolve_image((const char *)m, list, count, &reason);
    if (!p) {
        const char *why = reason == 1 ? "invalid or unreadable PE image" : reason == 3 ? "ambiguous signatures" : "no matching signature";
        mp_log("hooks: %s: %s in %s, the feature stays off", what, why, module);
        return 0;
    }
    bool fixed = false;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; ++i)
        if ((uintptr_t)p == (uintptr_t)m + list[i].rva) fixed = true;
    if (!fixed)
        mp_log("hooks: %s moved to +0x%lx; the unique code scan found it", what, (long)(p - (char *)m));
    return p;
}

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
char *hook_test_resolve(const void *base, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, int *reason)
{
    return resolve_image((const char *)base, list, count, reason);
}
void **hook_test_vtable(const void *base, unsigned rva, const HookVtableMember *members, unsigned count, int *reason)
{
    return resolve_vtable_image((const char *)base, rva, members, count, reason);
}
#endif

// The operation-map hooks use their existing resolver, kept as it was so this M3/M4 change does not
// alter M5. Tactical hooks above use the stricter, bounded resolver.
static const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER *legacy_sections_of(const char *base, unsigned *count)
{
    const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *dos = (const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *)base;
    if (dos->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) return 0;
    const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS *nt = (const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS *)(base + dos->e_lfanew);
    if (nt->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) return 0;
    *count = nt->FileHeader.NumberOfSections;
    return IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(nt);
}

static char *legacy_find_one(const char *base, const HookSig &h)
{
    if (h.rva && !memcmp(base + h.rva, h.sig, h.size)) return (char *)base + h.rva;
    unsigned count = 0;
    const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER *sections = legacy_sections_of(base, &count);
    if (!sections) return 0;
    const char *found = 0;
    int hits = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count && hits <= 1; i++) {
        const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER &s = sections[i];
        if (!(s.Characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE) || s.SizeOfRawData < h.size) continue;
        const char *p = base + s.VirtualAddress;
        const char *end = p + s.SizeOfRawData - h.size;
        for (const char *q = p; q <= end; q++)
            if (!memcmp(q, h.sig, h.size)) {
                if (++hits > 1) break;
                found = q;
            }
    }
    return hits == 1 ? (char *)found : 0;
}

char *hook_resolve(const char *module, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, const char *what)
{
    HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleA(module);
    if (!m) {
        mp_log("hooks: %s: %s is not loaded, the feature stays off", what, module);
        return 0;
    }
    char *base = (char *)m;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        char *p = legacy_find_one(base, list[i]);
        if (!p) continue;
        long off = (long)(p - base);
        if (off != (long)list[i].rva)
            mp_log("hooks: %s is not where this build had it (now +0x%lx), the scan found it and it is used",
                   what, off);
        return p;
    }
    mp_log("hooks: %s: none of the %u known signatures is in %s, the feature stays off", what, count, module);
    return 0;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/hooks.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/hooks.h (sha256 195de929f240e7dc3b32f6db592dc3aa8183487f9c02db4426636b82361ee55b) ====================
// Finding an engine function in a game build we have not seen before.
//
// The module detours a handful of engine functions: the Start button and the box it opens, the battle
// clock, the operation map's "next turn" and the step that carries the turn out.  Each is known by the
// bytes at its start.  A game update moves them, and a moved function is not a reason to lose the feature:
// the bytes are still somewhere in the module's executable section.
//
// So: the offset a build is known to use is checked first (no scan, no cost, and it is what a bug report
// shows); if those bytes are not there, the section is searched for the signature and the function is only
// used when the signature occurs exactly once.  Two candidates is not a guess worth making - the hook is
// left out and the log says so. The checked tactical resolver verifies all fixed offsets first, and
// fallback succeeds only if all matching signatures point to the same unique address.
#pragma once

struct HookSig
{
    unsigned rva;           // the offset this build is known to use, 0 to only scan
    const void *sig;
    unsigned size;
};

// Existing operation-map resolver: kept unchanged for M5.
char *hook_resolve(const char *module, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, const char *what);
// Tactical resolver: validates mapped PE bounds and rejects ambiguous cross-version matches.
char *hook_resolve_checked(const char *module, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, const char *what);

// Resolve a read-only vtable by its expected code pointers. Check the known RVA first, then
// accept only one matching aligned table in .rdata. Never write a candidate here.
struct HookVtableMember { unsigned index; const void *target; };
void **hook_vtable_checked(const char *module, unsigned rva, const HookVtableMember *members,
                           unsigned count, const char *what);
// Replace only the pointer we checked, not an intervening hook; restore page protection.
bool hook_swap_vtable_slot(void **slot, void *expected, void *replacement);
// Layout-sensitive features additionally gate the known image timestamp and SizeOfImage.
bool hook_image_identity(const char *module, unsigned timestamp, unsigned image_size);

// The offset of an address inside a loaded module, for the log ("adv_interf+0x3b320"); -1 when the
// address is not in that module.
long hook_offset(const char *module, const void *address);

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
// Tests only: inspect an in-memory PE without finding or patching a real game module.
char *hook_test_resolve(const void *base, const HookSig *list, unsigned count, int *reason);
void **hook_test_vtable(const void *base, unsigned rva, const HookVtableMember *members, unsigned count, int *reason);
#endif
==================== END development/net-v21/src/hooks.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/image.h (sha256 795fc316f54778e75cb7630b62389b36b6a64934a0d8f7e8696d74d9ab90cc96) ====================
// The battle description (tacgame_image.cfgpack): the engine's binary config format, read and patched in place.
//
//   header:  'AZCF', node count, node table offset
//   node:    name[0x20], then u32 type (1 struct, 2 table), columns, rows, type string offset, data offset
//   struct:  rows of cell[0x20] + field name[0x20];   table: rows of `columns` cells
//   cell:    u32 value (or length), u32 string offset, u32, u32, u32 kind (2 string, 4 number, 6 number list)
//
// What matters for multiplayer:
//   i_gamespec:gamespec  user_side / enemy_side   four-character side codes ('USSR', 'GER')
//   squads               one row per squad: id in column 1, a side code and a command tag ('USER' = the player's)
//   units                one row per soldier or vehicle, named "i_techn:tec_<id>" / "i_human:hum_<id>";
//                        a unit belongs to the squad with id (unit id & ~0x1f)
// The owner tag decides who commands a squad. Exchanging the tags (and the two side fields) gives the
// joining player the host's opponent while every unit keeps its id, so both games still name every
// object the same way.
#pragma once
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>

namespace image {

typedef unsigned char byte;
static const unsigned k_user = 0x52455355, k_enem = 0x4d454e45;      // 'USER', 'ENEM'

struct Node { unsigned type, cols, rows, data; bool ok; };

inline unsigned u32_at(const byte *b, size_t o) { unsigned v; memcpy(&v, b + o, 4); return v; }
inline void put_u32(byte *b, size_t o, unsigned v) { memcpy(b + o, &v, 4); }

inline Node find(const std::vector<byte> &b, const char *name)
{
    Node n = { 0, 0, 0, 0, false };
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return n;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b.data(), 4), table = u32_at(b.data(), 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        if (strncmp((const char *)b.data() + o, name, 0x20)) continue;
        n.type = u32_at(b.data(), o + 0x20);
        n.cols = u32_at(b.data(), o + 0x24);
        n.rows = u32_at(b.data(), o + 0x28);
        n.data = u32_at(b.data(), o + 0x30);
        size_t row = n.type == 1 ? 0x40 : (size_t)n.cols * 0x20;
        n.ok = n.data + (size_t)n.rows * row <= b.size();
        return n;
    }
    return n;
}

inline size_t cell(const Node &n, unsigned row, unsigned col) { return n.data + ((size_t)row * n.cols + col) * 0x20; }

// offset of a struct field's value cell, or 0
inline size_t field(const std::vector<byte> &b, const Node &n, const char *name)
{
    if (!n.ok || n.type != 1) return 0;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < n.rows; r++) {
        size_t o = n.data + (size_t)r * 0x40;
        if (!strncmp((const char *)b.data() + o + 0x20, name, 0x20)) return o;
    }
    return 0;
}

inline bool sides(const std::vector<byte> &b, unsigned *user, unsigned *enemy)
{
    Node g = find(b, "i_gamespec:gamespec");
    size_t u = field(b, g, "user_side"), e = field(b, g, "enemy_side");
    if (!u || !e) return false;
    *user = u32_at(b.data(), u);
    *enemy = u32_at(b.data(), e);
    return true;
}

// Columns of the squads table that matter: the side code ('GER', 'USSR') and the command tag. The tag is
// 'USER' for the squads this game's player commands; every other value names a computer-led group ('ENEM'
// in a skirmish, formation codes such as 'U286' in an operation).
struct SquadColumns { int side, tag; };

inline SquadColumns squad_columns(const std::vector<byte> &b, const Node &s)
{
    SquadColumns c = { -1, -1 };
    unsigned user = 0, enemy = 0;
    if (!s.ok || s.type != 2 || !s.rows || !sides(b, &user, &enemy)) return c;
    for (unsigned col = 2; col < s.cols && (c.side < 0 || c.tag < 0); col++) {
        bool all_sides = true, any_user = false;
        for (unsigned r = 0; r < s.rows; r++) {
            size_t o = cell(s, r, col);
            unsigned v = u32_at(b.data(), o);
            if (u32_at(b.data(), o + 16) != 4) { all_sides = false; break; }
            if (v != user && v != enemy) all_sides = false;
            if (v == k_user) any_user = true;
        }
        if (all_sides && c.side < 0) c.side = (int)col;
        else if (any_user && c.tag < 0) c.tag = (int)col;
    }
    return c;
}

// squad id -> command tag
inline std::unordered_map<unsigned, unsigned> squad_owners(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::unordered_map<unsigned, unsigned> out;
    Node s = find(b, "squads");
    SquadColumns c = squad_columns(b, s);
    if (c.tag < 0 || s.cols < 2) return out;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < s.rows; r++)
        out[u32_at(b.data(), cell(s, r, 1))] = u32_at(b.data(), cell(s, r, c.tag));
    return out;
}

// The keys (kind << 32 | id; kind 1 vehicle, 2 soldier) of every unit whose squad carries `tag`.
inline std::unordered_set<unsigned long long> units_of(const std::vector<byte> &b, unsigned tag)
{
    std::unordered_set<unsigned long long> out;
    std::unordered_map<unsigned, unsigned> owners = squad_owners(b);
    Node u = find(b, "units");
    if (!u.ok || u.type != 2) return out;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < u.rows; r++) {
        size_t o = cell(u, r, 0);
        unsigned str = u32_at(b.data(), o + 4);
        if (u32_at(b.data(), o + 16) != 2 || !str || str >= b.size()) continue;
        const char *name = (const char *)b.data() + str;
        unsigned long long kind = !strncmp(name, "i_techn:", 8) ? 1 : !strncmp(name, "i_human:", 8) ? 2 : 0;
        const char *us = strrchr(name, '_');
        if (!kind || !us) continue;
        unsigned id = strtoul(us + 1, 0, 16);
        auto it = owners.find(id & ~0x1fu);
        if (it != owners.end() && it->second == tag) out.insert((kind << 32) | id);
    }
    return out;
}

// Turns the host's battle description into the one for a player commanding the host's opponent: the
// opponent's squads get the 'USER' tag, the host's own squads the generic computer tag, and the two side
// fields change places. Unit and squad ids stay as they are.
inline bool mirror(std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    Node g = find(b, "i_gamespec:gamespec"), s = find(b, "squads"), p = find(b, "pools");
    size_t u = field(b, g, "user_side"), e = field(b, g, "enemy_side");
    SquadColumns c = squad_columns(b, s);
    if (!u || !e || c.tag < 0 || c.side < 0) return false;
    unsigned us = u32_at(b.data(), u), es = u32_at(b.data(), e);
    unsigned turned = 0;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < s.rows; r++) {
        size_t tag = cell(s, r, c.tag);
        unsigned side = u32_at(b.data(), cell(s, r, c.side)), v = u32_at(b.data(), tag);
        if (v == k_user) put_u32(b.data(), tag, k_enem);
        else if (side == es) { put_u32(b.data(), tag, k_user); turned++; }
    }
    if (!turned) return false;
    put_u32(b.data(), u, es);
    put_u32(b.data(), e, us);
    if (p.ok && p.type == 2 && p.rows == 2 && p.cols >= 3)
        for (unsigned col = 0; col <= 2; col += 2) {      // skirmish: pool label and command-chain name
            byte tmp[0x20];
            memcpy(tmp, b.data() + cell(p, 0, col), 0x20);
            memcpy(b.data() + cell(p, 0, col), b.data() + cell(p, 1, col), 0x20);
            memcpy(b.data() + cell(p, 1, col), tmp, 0x20);
        }
    return true;
}

// unit key -> model name (column 2 of the units table, e.g. "opel_blitz_36")
inline std::unordered_map<unsigned long long, std::string> unit_models(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::unordered_map<unsigned long long, std::string> out;
    Node u = find(b, "units");
    if (!u.ok || u.type != 2 || u.cols < 3) return out;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < u.rows; r++) {
        size_t o = cell(u, r, 0), m = cell(u, r, 2);
        unsigned str = u32_at(b.data(), o + 4), model = u32_at(b.data(), m + 4);
        if (u32_at(b.data(), o + 16) != 2 || !str || str >= b.size()) continue;
        if (u32_at(b.data(), m + 16) != 2 || !model || model >= b.size()) continue;
        const char *name = (const char *)b.data() + str;
        unsigned long long kind = !strncmp(name, "i_techn:", 8) ? 1 : !strncmp(name, "i_human:", 8) ? 2 : 0;
        const char *us = strrchr(name, '_');
        if (!kind || !us) continue;
        out[(kind << 32) | strtoul(us + 1, 0, 16)] = (const char *)b.data() + model;
    }
    return out;
}

// "GER" -> "Germans", for the menu
inline const char *side_name(unsigned code)
{
    static char text[8];
    if (code == 0x00524547) return "Germans";
    if (code == 0x52535355) return "Soviets";
    memcpy(text, &code, 4);
    text[4] = 0;
    return text;
}

} // namespace image
==================== END development/net-v21/src/image.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/image_chunks.h (sha256 52cc56d9b154dd9de3a804239e764ea4f88dfba5b9a50a292eb65e842d89f151) ====================
// Validate an image/map fragment before it marks a piece received or touches the destination buffer.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stddef.h>

namespace image_chunks {
inline bool valid(const ImageData *piece, size_t packet_bytes, size_t total, unsigned flags,
                  unsigned epoch = 0, unsigned revision = 0) {
    const size_t header = sizeof(ImageData) - k_chunk;
    if (!piece || packet_bytes < header || piece->h.magic != k_magic || piece->h.type != PKT_IMAGE_DATA ||
        piece->h.flags != flags || !total || total > (64u << 20)) return false;
    if (flags == 1 && (!epoch || !revision || piece->map_epoch != epoch || piece->map_revision != revision)) return false;
    const size_t index = piece->index;
    if (index >= (total + k_chunk - 1) / k_chunk) return false;
    size_t offset = index * (size_t)k_chunk;
    size_t left = total - offset;
    size_t expected = left < k_chunk ? left : k_chunk;
    return piece->size == expected && packet_bytes >= header + expected;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/image_chunks.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/input_imports.h (sha256 bba37abb6b5ad6bdd5709bafef3e4330419ea39fc5db549273b9c09a80507150) ====================
// Patching a loaded module's imports, plus a small tracker for modules loaded after startup.
#pragma once
#include <windows.h>
#include <stddef.h>

namespace input_imports {
// Return the number of entries replaced, or -1 on malformed headers or a protection failure.
// This is intended only for trusted modules already loaded by the game process.
inline int replace(HMODULE module, void *from, void *to) {
    if (!module || !from || !to) return -1;
    BYTE *base = (BYTE *)module;
    const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *dos = (const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *)base;
    if (dos->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE || dos->e_lfanew < (LONG)sizeof(*dos) || dos->e_lfanew > 0x100000) return -1;
    const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64 *nt = (const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64 *)(base + dos->e_lfanew);
    if (nt->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE || nt->OptionalHeader.Magic != IMAGE_NT_OPTIONAL_HDR64_MAGIC) return -1;
    const size_t image_size = nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage;
    const IMAGE_DATA_DIRECTORY directory = nt->OptionalHeader.DataDirectory[IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT];
    if (!directory.VirtualAddress) return 0;
    if (directory.VirtualAddress >= image_size || directory.Size > image_size - directory.VirtualAddress ||
        directory.Size < sizeof(IMAGE_IMPORT_DESCRIPTOR)) return -1;
    const auto *descriptors = (const IMAGE_IMPORT_DESCRIPTOR *)(base + directory.VirtualAddress);
    size_t count = directory.Size / sizeof(IMAGE_IMPORT_DESCRIPTOR);
    int replaced = 0;
    bool terminated = false;
    for (size_t d = 0; d < count; ++d) {
        if (!descriptors[d].Name) { terminated = true; break; }
        size_t rva = descriptors[d].FirstThunk;
        if (!rva || rva > image_size || sizeof(void *) > image_size - rva) return -1;
        void **slot = (void **)(base + rva);
        size_t maximum = (image_size - rva) / sizeof(void *);
        bool thunk_terminated = false;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < maximum; ++i) {
            if (!slot[i]) { thunk_terminated = true; break; }
            if (slot[i] != from) continue;
            DWORD old = 0, ignored = 0;
            if (!VirtualProtect(&slot[i], sizeof(slot[i]), PAGE_READWRITE, &old)) return -1;
            slot[i] = to;
            if (!VirtualProtect(&slot[i], sizeof(slot[i]), old, &ignored)) return -1;
            ++replaced;
        }
        if (!thunk_terminated) return -1;
    }
    return terminated ? replaced : -1;
}

template <size_t N> struct Tracker {
    HMODULE installed[N] = {};
    bool needs(size_t i, HMODULE current) {
        if (i >= N) return false;
        if (!current) { installed[i] = nullptr; return false; }
        return installed[i] != current;
    }
    void complete(size_t i, HMODULE current) { if (i < N) installed[i] = current; }
};
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/input_imports.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/joiner.h (sha256 3b41d6bc311f454d052577dae547b82c041bf040760eecbb34aa9e3d26c551ad) ====================
// Joining side of the connection up to the moment the battle starts: find the host, wait until it is in a
// battle, download the battle description, start the game into that battle.
// Non-blocking; poll() is called every frame by the in-game menu and in a loop by mp_join.exe.
#pragma once
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#include <windows.h>
#include <winsock2.h>
#include <ws2tcpip.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include "steam.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <vector>
#include "protocol.h"
#include "image_chunks.h"
#include "operation_turn.h"
#include "image.h"
#include "opsave.h"
#include "opsave_checked.h"
#include "map_file_windows.h"

struct Joiner {
    enum State { IDLE, CONNECTING, WAITING_FOR_BATTLE, RECEIVING, DONE, FAILED };
    State state = IDLE;
    char error[160] = "";
    char address[128] = "";
    unsigned char host_mode = 0;
    unsigned prefer_side = 0;          // side code the player wants to command; set before start()
    unsigned host_side = 0, other_side = 0;
    bool versus = false;               // decided by the host from prefer_side
    char my_name[24] = "Player", host_name[24] = "";
    bool lobby_ready = false;          // this player pressed READY; the battle is only entered when set
    unsigned char swap_req = 0;        // number of SWAP SIDES presses
    bool swap = false, host_in_battle = false;
    DWORD last_welcome = 0;
    bool want_map = false;             // what is being received: the operational map instead of a battle
    char map_file[96] = "";
    unsigned map_turns = 0, map_epoch = 0, map_revision = 0;
    std::string map_backup; // retained when the profile already had a save
    SOCKET sock = INVALID_SOCKET;
    bool winsock_started = false, owns_steam_inbox = false;
    sockaddr_in host = {};
    std::vector<unsigned char> image, have;
    std::vector<DWORD> asked;
    unsigned image_hash = 0, got = 0;
    DWORD last_hello = 0, started = 0, last_ask = 0;

    bool fail(const char *why)
    {
        strcpy_s(error, why);
        close();
        state = FAILED;
        return false;
    }

    void close()
    {
        if (owns_steam_inbox) steam_lobby_release();
        owns_steam_inbox = false;
        if (sock != INVALID_SOCKET) closesocket(sock);
        sock = INVALID_SOCKET;
        if (winsock_started) WSACleanup();
        winsock_started = false;
    }

    void cancel()
    {
        close();
        state = IDLE;
    }

    bool start(const char *addr, bool reserve_steam_inbox = true)
    {
        cancel();
        error[0] = 0;
        strcpy_s(address, addr);
        char name[128];
        strcpy_s(name, addr);
        WSADATA wsa;
        if (WSAStartup(MAKEWORD(2, 2), &wsa) != 0) return fail("Windows networking could not start.");
        winsock_started = true;
        host = {};
        if (!name[0]) return fail("Enter the host's address first.");
        if (!_strnicmp(name, "steam:", 6)) {
            // A friend's game: the Steam ID is the whole address, and the bytes go through Steam's P2P
            // relay instead of the socket.  Checked before the host:port split, which would otherwise
            // read the ID as a port number.
            uint64_t id = 0;
            if (!steam_parse_id(name + 6, &id) || !steam_peer_make(id, &host))
                return fail("That Steam ID is not a valid number.");
            if (!steam_ready() && !steam_init())
                return fail("Steam is not available. Start the game through Steam, or use the IP address.");
        } else {
            unsigned short port = k_default_port;
            if (char *colon = strchr(name, ':')) { *colon = 0; port = (unsigned short)atoi(colon + 1); }
            host.sin_family = AF_INET;
            host.sin_port = htons(port);
            if (inet_pton(AF_INET, name, &host.sin_addr) != 1) {
                addrinfo hints = {}, *res = 0;
                hints.ai_family = AF_INET;
                if (getaddrinfo(name, 0, &hints, &res) || !res) return fail("That address could not be found.");
                host.sin_addr = ((sockaddr_in *)res->ai_addr)->sin_addr;
                freeaddrinfo(res);
            }
        }
        sock = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, IPPROTO_UDP);
        if (sock != INVALID_SOCKET) {
            u_long nonblocking = 1;
            if (ioctlsocket(sock, FIONBIO, &nonblocking)) {
                closesocket(sock);
                sock = INVALID_SOCKET;
            } else {
                DWORD off = 0, ret = 0;
                WSAIoctl(sock, _WSAIOW(IOC_VENDOR, 12) /* SIO_UDP_CONNRESET */, &off, sizeof off, 0, 0, &ret, 0, 0);
            }
        }
        if (host.sin_family != STEAM_FAMILY && sock == INVALID_SOCKET)
            return fail("Could not open a nonblocking UDP socket.");
        if (host.sin_family == STEAM_FAMILY && reserve_steam_inbox) {
            if (!steam_lobby_claim()) return fail("Another Steam lobby connection is already active.");
            owns_steam_inbox = true;
        }
        image.clear();
        have.clear();
        got = 0;
        last_hello = 0;
        started = GetTickCount();
        state = CONNECTING;
        return true;
    }

    float progress() const { return have.empty() ? 0 : (float)got / have.size(); }
    unsigned waited_seconds() const { return (GetTickCount() - started) / 1000; }

    // Same seam as the module: the address decides the transport.
    void send_host(const void *data, int size)
    {
        if (host.sin_family == STEAM_FAMILY) steam_send(steam_peer_id(&host), data, size);
        else sendto(sock, (const char *)data, size, 0, (sockaddr *)&host, sizeof host);
    }

    void poll()
    {
        if (state != CONNECTING && state != WAITING_FOR_BATTLE && state != RECEIVING) return;
        DWORD now = GetTickCount();
        if (now - last_hello >= 500) {
            Hello hello = { make_head(PKT_HELLO) };
            memcpy(hello.proto, k_protocol, sizeof k_protocol);
            hello.version = k_version;
            hello.prefer_side = prefer_side;
            hello.ready = 0;
            hello.phase = 0xff;         // not in a battle yet
            hello.lobby_ready = lobby_ready;
            hello.swap_req = swap_req;
            strncpy_s(hello.name, my_name, sizeof hello.name - 1);
            send_host(&hello, sizeof hello);
            last_hello = now;
        }
        // The pieces are asked for in small bursts, not one burst per frame: a menu that wants a whole
        // description used to put a thousand requests on the wire in half a second and get nothing back,
        // because the host was already in the battle.
        if (state == RECEIVING && now - last_ask >= 60) {
            last_ask = now;
            unsigned sent = 0;
            for (unsigned i = 0; i < have.size() && sent < 24; i++) {
                if (have[i] || now - asked[i] < 300) continue;
                ImageRequest r = { make_head(PKT_IMAGE_REQUEST), i };
                r.h.flags = want_map ? 1 : 0;
                if (want_map) { r.map_epoch = map_epoch; r.map_revision = map_revision; }
                send_host(&r, sizeof r);
                asked[i] = now;
                sent++;
            }
        }
        for (int guard = 0; guard < 256; guard++) {
            unsigned char buf[2048];
            sockaddr_in from = {};
            int n;
            if (host.sin_family == STEAM_FAMILY) {
                uint64_t id = 0;
                n = steam_recv(&id, buf, sizeof buf);
                if (n <= 0) break;
                if (!steam_peer_make(id, &from) || id != steam_peer_id(&host)) continue;
            } else {
                int flen = sizeof from;
                n = recvfrom(sock, (char *)buf, sizeof buf, 0, (sockaddr *)&from, &flen);
                if (n <= 0) break;
                if (from.sin_family != AF_INET || from.sin_addr.s_addr != host.sin_addr.s_addr ||
                    from.sin_port != host.sin_port) continue;
            }
            if (n < (int)sizeof(Head)) continue;
            const Head *h = (const Head *)buf;
            if (h->magic != k_magic) continue;
            if (h->type == PKT_WELCOME && n >= 24) {
                if (!valid_welcome(buf, (size_t)n)) { fail("The host runs a different version of the multiplayer files."); return; }
                const Welcome *w = (const Welcome *)buf;
                last_welcome = now;
                swap = w->swap != 0; host_in_battle = w->in_battle != 0;
                memcpy(host_name, w->name, sizeof host_name); host_name[sizeof host_name - 1] = 0;
                host_mode = w->host_mode; host_side = w->user_side; other_side = w->enemy_side;
                versus = w->versus != 0;
                bool map = !w->in_battle && w->map_size != 0;
                unsigned size = map ? w->map_size : w->image_size;
                unsigned offered_epoch = 0;
                operation_turn::Phase phase;
                bool identity_ok = operation_turn::decode(w->map_flags, &offered_epoch, &phase) && w->map_revision &&
                                   phase == operation_turn::Phase::host;
                if (state == RECEIVING && want_map && identity_ok) {
                    bool same = offered_epoch == map_epoch && w->map_revision == map_revision;
                    bool newer = (offered_epoch == map_epoch && operation_turn::newer_revision(w->map_revision, map_revision)) ||
                                 operation_turn::newer_epoch(offered_epoch, map_epoch);
                    if (!same && !newer) continue; // delayed old welcome
                    if (same && map && size == image.size() && w->map_hash == image_hash) continue;
                    if (same && map && (size != image.size() || w->map_hash != image_hash)) continue; // identity collision
                } else if (state == RECEIVING && !want_map && !w->in_battle) {
                    continue; // an old map welcome must not replace an in-flight battle
                } else if (state == RECEIVING && !want_map && w->in_battle &&
                           size == image.size() && w->image_hash == image_hash) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ((!map && !w->in_battle) || !size || !lobby_ready) {
                    image.clear(); have.clear(); asked.clear(); got = 0;
                    state = WAITING_FOR_BATTLE; continue;
                }
                if (size > (64u << 20)) { fail("The host sent an invalid battle or map."); return; }
                if (map) {
                    if (!identity_ok) { state = WAITING_FOR_BATTLE; continue; }
                    char offered_file[sizeof map_file];
                    memcpy(offered_file, w->map_file, sizeof offered_file);
                    if (offered_file[sizeof offered_file - 1] != 0 || !opsave::safe_file_name(offered_file)) {
                        fail("The host sent an invalid operation save."); return;
                    }
                    strcpy_s(map_file, offered_file);
                    map_turns = w->map_turns; map_epoch = offered_epoch; map_revision = w->map_revision;
                }
                // A newer offer supersedes an incomplete download; old fragments carry the old
                // identity and image_chunks::valid rejects them. Continue HELLOs while receiving.
                want_map = map; image.assign(size, 0); image_hash = map ? w->map_hash : w->image_hash;
                have.assign((size + k_chunk - 1) / k_chunk, 0); asked.assign(have.size(), 0);
                got = 0; last_ask = 0; state = RECEIVING;
            } else if (h->type == PKT_IMAGE_DATA && state == RECEIVING && n >= (int)(sizeof(ImageData) - k_chunk)) {
                const ImageData *d = (const ImageData *)buf;
                if (!image_chunks::valid(d, (size_t)n, image.size(), want_map ? 1 : 0, map_epoch, map_revision) ||
                    have[d->index]) continue;
                memcpy(image.data() + (size_t)d->index * k_chunk, d->data, d->size);
                have[d->index] = 1;
                got++;
            }
        }
        // A dead host must not leave an obsolete incomplete download alive forever.
        if (state == RECEIVING && now - last_welcome > 20000) {
            image.clear(); have.clear(); asked.clear(); got = 0;
            state = CONNECTING; started = now; host_name[0] = 0;
        }
        // a host that stops answering is looked for again
        if (state == WAITING_FOR_BATTLE && now - last_welcome > 6000) { state = CONNECTING; started = now; host_name[0] = 0; }
        if (state == RECEIVING && got == have.size()) {
            close();
            if (fnv(image.data(), image.size()) != image_hash) fail("The battle arrived damaged. Try again.");
            else state = DONE;
        }
    }
