    // Is there an operation of this name in the game's data? Operations are files
    // <pack>\shared\camps\<campaign>\<name>.campack2, where <pack> is a folder one or two levels below
    // data\ (data\oprum, data\addons\rage_bridge, data\common\ma_croat_leg, ...).
    static bool operation_in(const char *dir, const char *campaign, const char *name, int depth)
    {
        char find[MAX_PATH], path[MAX_PATH];
        sprintf_s(path, "%s\\shared\\camps\\%s\\%s.campack2", dir, campaign, name);
        if (GetFileAttributesA(path) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) return true;
        if (depth <= 0) return false;
        WIN32_FIND_DATAA fd;
        sprintf_s(find, "%s\\*", dir);
        HANDLE h = FindFirstFileA(find, &fd);
        if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;
        bool found = false;
        do {
            if (!(fd.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) || fd.cFileName[0] == '.') continue;
            if (sprintf_s(path, "%s\\%s", dir, fd.cFileName) > 0) found = operation_in(path, campaign, name, depth - 1);
        } while (!found && FindNextFileA(h, &fd));
        FindClose(h);
        return found;
    }

    static bool operation_exists(const char *root, const char *campaign, const char *name)
    {
        char data[MAX_PATH];
        sprintf_s(data, "%s\\data", root);
        return operation_in(data, campaign, name, 2);
    }

    // Load the exact host operation as a spectator copy, retaining a transactional backup.
    // Do not mirror sides or switch to a counterpart: the host is the only operational authority.
    bool launch_map(const char *root)
    {
        char path[MAX_PATH], id[64] = "cloud", text[512] = "", cmd[1024], local_file[96];
        std::string why;
        if (!root || !*root || !opsave::safe_file_name(map_file) || !operation_turn::valid_epoch(map_epoch) ||
            !map_revision || !opsave_checked::validate(image, &why))
            return fail("The operational map or its snapshot identity is invalid.");
        sprintf_s(path, "%s\\users\\profiles\\cur_profile.engcfg", root);
        FILE *f = 0;
        if (!fopen_s(&f, path, "rb") && f) {
            text[fread(text, 1, sizeof text - 1, f)] = 0;
            fclose(f);
            const char *p = strstr(text, "cur_id");
            if (p && (p = strchr(p, '='))) {
                for (p++; *p == ' ' || *p == '\t'; p++) {}
                size_t k = strcspn(p, ";\r\n");
                if (k && k < sizeof id) { memcpy(id, p, k); id[k] = 0; }
            }
        }
        if (!opsave_checked::safe_profile_id(id)) return fail("The profile id is not a safe folder name.");
        std::string name = opsave::operation_name(image);
        std::string op = opsave::operation_id(map_file, name);
        if (op.empty()) return fail("The host's operation save could not be recognised.");
        std::vector<unsigned char> prepared = image;
        strcpy_s(local_file, map_file);
        if (!opsave_checked::validate(prepared, &why)) return fail("The prepared operational map is invalid.");
        if (sprintf_s(path, "%s\\users\\profiles\\%s\\%s", root, id, local_file) < 0)
            return fail("The operational save path is too long.");
        if (sprintf_s(cmd, "\"%s\\starter_x64.exe\" root\\programs\\opgame.progpack, %s, %s, 0, %u", root, id,
                      op.c_str(), map_turns ? map_turns : opsave::turns(prepared)) < 0)
            return fail("The operational launch command is too long.");
        char epoch[16], revision[16];
        sprintf_s(epoch, "%u", map_epoch); sprintf_s(revision, "%u", map_revision);
        map_file_windows::Environment env;
        if (!env.set("MP_ROLE", "join") || !env.set("MP_PEER", address) || !env.set("MP_MAP", "1") ||
            !env.set("MP_HOST_MODE", "operation") || !env.set("MP_MAP_EPOCH", epoch) || !env.set("MP_MAP_REV", revision) ||
            !env.set("MP_AUTOJOIN", nullptr) || !env.set("MP_ENDTURN", nullptr) || !env.set("MP_TURNDONE", nullptr))
            return fail("Could not prepare the child game's environment; no save was replaced.");
        DWORD launch_error = 0;
        map_file::Result result = map_file_windows::replace_and_launch(root, cmd, path, prepared, &map_backup, &launch_error);
        if (!map_file::started(result)) {
            char message[160];
            sprintf_s(message, "Could not open the operation: %.100s (Windows %lu).", map_file::reason(result), launch_error);
            return fail(message);
        }
        if (!env.restore()) printf("Warning: could not fully restore the parent process environment.\n");
        if (result == map_file::Result::ok_marker_retained)
            printf("Operation started, but its recovery marker remains beside the save.\n");
        return true;
    }

    // Writes the received battle where the game expects it and starts the game straight into it, with the
    // multiplayer module told to follow the host. `root` is the game folder.
    bool launch(const char *root)
    {
        char path[MAX_PATH], id[64] = "cloud", text[512] = "", cmd[1024];
        sprintf_s(path, "%s\\users\\profiles\\cur_profile.engcfg", root);
        FILE *f = 0;
        if (!fopen_s(&f, path, "rb") && f) {
            text[fread(text, 1, sizeof text - 1, f)] = 0;
            fclose(f);
            const char *p = strstr(text, "cur_id");
            if (p && (p = strchr(p, '='))) {
                for (p++; *p == ' ' || *p == '\t'; p++) {}
                size_t k = strcspn(p, ";\r\n");
                if (k && k < sizeof id) { memcpy(id, p, k); id[k] = 0; }
            }
        }
        if (!opsave_checked::safe_profile_id(id)) return fail("The profile id is not a safe folder name.");
        std::string why;
        if (!opsave_checked::validate(image, &why)) return fail("The received battle description is invalid.");
        std::vector<unsigned char> prepared = image;
        // commanding the host's opponent: same battle, with the command of the two forces exchanged
        if (versus && !image::mirror(prepared)) return fail("The host's battle could not be prepared for the other side.");
        if (!opsave_checked::validate(prepared, &why)) return fail("The prepared battle description is invalid.");
        if (sprintf_s(path, "%s\\%s", root, k_image_path) < 0 ||
            sprintf_s(cmd, "\"%s\\starter_x64.exe\" root\\programs\\tacgame.progpack, %s, users\\profiles, %s, *:*, 0, 1",
                      root, k_image_path, id) < 0) return fail("The battle path or command is too long.");
        char code[8] = { 0 }; memcpy(code, &prefer_side, 4);
        map_file_windows::Environment env;
        if (!env.set("MP_ROLE", "join") || !env.set("MP_PEER", address) ||
            !env.set("MP_PREFER", prefer_side ? code : nullptr) || !env.set("MP_VERSUS", versus ? "1" : "0") ||
            !env.set("MP_HOST_MODE", host_mode == HOST_MODE_OPERATION ? "operation" : host_mode == HOST_MODE_MISSION ? "mission" : "skirmish") ||
            !env.set("MP_MAP", nullptr) || !env.set("MP_AUTOJOIN", host_mode == HOST_MODE_OPERATION ? address : nullptr))
            return fail("Could not prepare the child game's environment; no battle file was replaced.");
        DWORD launch_error = 0;
        map_file::Result result = map_file_windows::replace_and_launch(root, cmd, path, prepared, &map_backup, &launch_error);
        if (!map_file::started(result)) {
            char message[160];
            sprintf_s(message, "Could not start the battle: %.100s (Windows %lu).", map_file::reason(result), launch_error);
            return fail(message);
        }
        if (!env.restore()) printf("Warning: could not fully restore the parent process environment.\n");
        return true;
    }
};
==================== END development/net-v21/src/joiner.h ====================

// Transaction for replacing an operation save and starting the program which will use it.
// The caller supplies atomic writes, a create-new durable backup and a process launcher. Nothing
// truncates the destination in place. Failures retain the recovery marker and/or backup when needed.
#pragma once
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace map_file {
using byte = unsigned char;
struct Io {
    void *context;
    bool (*exists)(void *, const char *, bool *);
    bool (*read)(void *, const char *, std::vector<byte> *);
    bool (*create)(void *, const char *, const void *, size_t); // must not replace an existing backup
    bool (*write)(void *, const char *, const void *, size_t);  // atomic replacement
    bool (*remove)(void *, const char *);
    bool (*launch)(void *);
};
enum class Result { ok, ok_marker_retained, invalid, unresolved, inspect, read, backup, verify_backup,
                    marker, image, image_cleanup, launch_rolled_back, launch_rollback_failed, launch_cleanup };
inline const char *reason(Result r) {
    switch (r) {
    case Result::ok: return "ok";
    case Result::ok_marker_retained: return "started, but recovery marker could not be removed";
    case Result::invalid: return "invalid transaction input";
    case Result::unresolved: return "an earlier transaction needs recovery";
    case Result::inspect: return "cannot inspect the destination or recovery marker";
    case Result::read: return "cannot read the original save";
    case Result::backup: return "cannot create a new durable backup";
    case Result::verify_backup: return "backup could not be read back exactly";
    case Result::marker: return "cannot write the recovery marker";
    case Result::image: return "save replacement failed; original is intact";
    case Result::image_cleanup: return "save replacement failed and marker remains";
    case Result::launch_rolled_back: return "program did not start; original restored";
    case Result::launch_rollback_failed: return "program did not start; rollback failed, backup and marker retained";
    case Result::launch_cleanup: return "program did not start; original restored but marker remains";
    }
    return "unknown";
}
inline bool started(Result r) { return r == Result::ok || r == Result::ok_marker_retained; }

inline Result replace_and_launch(const Io &io, const char *path, const char *backup, const std::vector<byte> &replacement) {
    if (!path || !*path || !backup || !*backup || std::string(path) == backup || replacement.empty() ||
        !io.exists || !io.read || !io.create || !io.write || !io.remove || !io.launch) return Result::invalid;
    const std::string marker = std::string(path) + ".mp_transaction";
    bool present = false, marker_present = false;
    if (!io.exists(io.context, marker.c_str(), &marker_present) || !io.exists(io.context, path, &present)) return Result::inspect;
    if (marker_present) return Result::unresolved;
    std::vector<byte> original;
    if (present) {
        if (!io.read(io.context, path, &original) || original.empty()) return Result::read;
        if (!io.create(io.context, backup, original.data(), original.size())) return Result::backup;
        std::vector<byte> saved;
        if (!io.read(io.context, backup, &saved) || saved != original) return Result::verify_backup;
    }
    // Plain text deliberately names the backup for a person investigating a stopped transaction.
    std::string record = "MIUS_MAP_TRANSACTION_1\noriginal=";
    record += present ? "present\nbackup=" : "absent\nbackup=";
    record += present ? backup : "(none)";
    record += "\n";
    if (!io.create(io.context, marker.c_str(), record.data(), record.size())) return Result::marker;
    if (!io.write(io.context, path, replacement.data(), replacement.size())) {
        return io.remove(io.context, marker.c_str()) ? Result::image : Result::image_cleanup;
    }
    if (!io.launch(io.context)) {
        bool restored = present ? io.write(io.context, path, original.data(), original.size()) : io.remove(io.context, path);
        if (!restored) return Result::launch_rollback_failed;
        return io.remove(io.context, marker.c_str()) ? Result::launch_rolled_back : Result::launch_cleanup;
    }
    return io.remove(io.context, marker.c_str()) ? Result::ok : Result::ok_marker_retained;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/map_file.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/map_file_windows.h (sha256 45f88d27c0232b8bdc4f90738c2a13272090bc9d8575b712e129cb4a55c131d6) ====================
// Windows adapter for map_file's injectable transaction.
#pragma once
#include "map_file.h"
#include "atomic_file.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace map_file_windows {
struct Context { const char *directory; std::string command; DWORD launch_error = 0; };
inline bool exists(void *, const char *name, bool *out) {
    DWORD attr = GetFileAttributesA(name);
    if (attr != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) { *out = true; return !(attr & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY); }
    DWORD e = GetLastError();
    if (e == ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND || e == ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND) { *out = false; return true; }
    return false;
}
inline bool read(void *, const char *name, std::vector<unsigned char> *out) {
    HANDLE h = CreateFileA(name, GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;
    LARGE_INTEGER size = {};
    bool ok = GetFileSizeEx(h, &size) && size.QuadPart > 0 && size.QuadPart <= (64ll << 20);
    std::vector<unsigned char> data;
    if (ok) {
        data.resize((size_t)size.QuadPart);
        size_t at = 0;
        while (at < data.size()) {
            DWORD n = 0, wanted = (DWORD)(data.size() - at);
            if (!ReadFile(h, data.data() + at, wanted, &n, nullptr) || !n || n > wanted) { ok = false; break; }
            at += n;
        }
    }
    if (!CloseHandle(h)) ok = false;
    if (ok) out->swap(data);
    return ok;
}
inline bool create(void *, const char *name, const void *data, size_t size) { return atomic_file::create_new(name, data, size); }
inline bool write(void *, const char *name, const void *data, size_t size) { return atomic_file::replace(name, data, size); }
inline bool remove(void *, const char *name) { return DeleteFileA(name) != 0; }
inline bool launch(void *p) {
    Context &ctx = *(Context *)p;
    std::vector<char> cmd(ctx.command.begin(), ctx.command.end()); cmd.push_back(0);
    STARTUPINFOA si = { sizeof si }; PROCESS_INFORMATION pi = {};
    if (!CreateProcessA(nullptr, cmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, 0, nullptr, ctx.directory, &si, &pi)) {
        ctx.launch_error = GetLastError(); return false;
    }
    CloseHandle(pi.hProcess); CloseHandle(pi.hThread); return true;
}
inline std::string backup_name(const char *path) {
    static volatile LONG serial = 0;
    return std::string(path) + ".before_mp_" + std::to_string((unsigned long)GetCurrentProcessId()) +
           "_" + std::to_string((unsigned long)InterlockedIncrement(&serial));
}
inline map_file::Result replace_and_launch(const char *directory, const std::string &command, const char *path,
                                           const std::vector<unsigned char> &data, std::string *backup = nullptr,
                                           DWORD *launch_error = nullptr) {
    Context ctx = { directory, command, 0 };
    std::string keep = backup_name(path);
    map_file::Io io = { &ctx, exists, read, create, write, remove, launch };
    map_file::Result result = map_file::replace_and_launch(io, path, keep.c_str(), data);
    if (backup) *backup = keep;
    if (launch_error) *launch_error = ctx.launch_error;
    return result;
}

// Temporarily set the parent environment only while CreateProcess takes its copy. Every failed
// setter stops launch, and both successful and failed launches restore the parent's prior values.
// As with Win32 process environment generally, another thread must not change these keys concurrently.
struct Environment {
    struct Entry { std::string name, value; bool present; };
    std::vector<Entry> entries;
    bool set(const char *name, const char *value) {
        if (!name || !*name) { SetLastError(ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER); return false; }
        SetLastError(ERROR_SUCCESS);
        DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableA(name, nullptr, 0), error = GetLastError();
        Entry e; e.name = name; e.present = n != 0 || error == ERROR_SUCCESS;
        if (!n && error != ERROR_SUCCESS && error != ERROR_ENVVAR_NOT_FOUND) return false;
        if (n) {
            std::vector<char> b(n);
            SetLastError(ERROR_SUCCESS);
            DWORD got = GetEnvironmentVariableA(name, b.data(), n);
            if (got >= n || (!got && GetLastError() != ERROR_SUCCESS)) return false; // changed or system failure
            e.value.assign(b.data(), got);
        }
        if (!value && !e.present) return true; // deleting an absent variable is already satisfied
        if (!SetEnvironmentVariableA(name, value)) return false;
        entries.push_back(e);
        return true;
    }
    bool restore() {
        bool ok = true;
        for (auto i = entries.rbegin(); i != entries.rend(); ++i)
            if (!SetEnvironmentVariableA(i->name.c_str(), i->present ? i->value.c_str() : nullptr)) ok = false;
        entries.clear();
        return ok;
    }
    ~Environment() { restore(); }
};
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/map_file_windows.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/mp_join.cpp (sha256 121707d36f39cc7d5ceb1a17223a45bf50656c5c50c3b03c29e6162843efd17d) ====================
// mp_join.exe <host address>[:port]
// Optional standalone launcher; the in-game menu is the primary Steam path. This process initializes
// Steam explicitly because it does not inherit a loaded DLL or Steam interfaces from the game.
#include "joiner.h"

struct StandaloneSteam {
    HMODULE library = nullptr;
    void (*shutdown_api)() = nullptr;
    bool initialised = false;
    bool start(const char *root) {
        char fake[32];
        if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_STEAM_FAKE", fake, sizeof fake)) return true; // isolated CI stand-in
        char path[MAX_PATH];
        if (sprintf_s(path, "%s\\steam_api64.dll", root) > 0)
            library = LoadLibraryExA(path, nullptr, LOAD_WITH_ALTERED_SEARCH_PATH);
        if (!library && sprintf_s(path, "%s\\bin\\rel\\x64\\steam_api64.dll", root) > 0)
            library = LoadLibraryExA(path, nullptr, LOAD_WITH_ALTERED_SEARCH_PATH);
        if (!library) return false;
        typedef bool (*Init)();
        Init init = (Init)GetProcAddress(library, "SteamAPI_Init");
        shutdown_api = (void (*)())GetProcAddress(library, "SteamAPI_Shutdown");
        if (!init || !shutdown_api || !init()) return false;
        initialised = true;
        return true;
    }
    ~StandaloneSteam() {
        steam_shutdown();
        if (initialised && shutdown_api) shutdown_api();
        if (library) FreeLibrary(library);
    }
};

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    if (argc < 2) {
        printf("usage: mp_join <host address>[:port] [germans | soviets]\n");
        return 2;
    }
    char root[MAX_PATH];
    if (!GetModuleFileNameA(0, root, MAX_PATH)) return 1;
    if (char *s = strrchr(root, '\\')) *s = 0;
    if (!SetCurrentDirectoryA(root)) { printf("Cannot open the game folder.\n"); return 1; }
    printf("net_v21 launcher %s\n", k_wire_tag);
    StandaloneSteam steam;
    if (!_strnicmp(argv[1], "steam:", 6) && !steam.start(root)) {
        printf("Steam could not be initialized in the standalone launcher. Start Steam and use the in-game lobby, or use IP.\n");
        return 1;
    }
    Joiner j;
    j.lobby_ready = true;
    if (argc > 2) j.prefer_side = !_stricmp(argv[2], "soviets") ? k_side_soviets : k_side_germans;
    if (!j.start(argv[1])) { printf("%s\n", j.error); return 1; }
    printf("Joining %s...\n", argv[1]);
    Joiner::State shown = Joiner::IDLE;
    unsigned noted = 0;
    for (;;) {
        steam_frame();
        j.poll();
        if (j.state != shown) {
            shown = j.state;
            if (shown == Joiner::WAITING_FOR_BATTLE) printf("Connected. Waiting for the host...\n");
            if (shown == Joiner::RECEIVING) printf("Receiving a snapshot (%zu KB)...\n", j.image.size() / 1024);
        }
        if (j.state == Joiner::CONNECTING && j.waited_seconds() >= noted + 10) {
            noted = j.waited_seconds();
            printf("  no answer yet (%u s) - check the address and transport\n", noted);
        }
        if (j.state == Joiner::FAILED) { printf("%s\n", j.error); return 1; }
        if (j.state == Joiner::DONE) break;
        Sleep(10);
    }
    if (!(j.want_map ? j.launch_map(root) : j.launch(root))) { printf("%s\n", j.error); return 1; }
    printf("Snapshot received. Starting the game...\n");
    return 0;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/mp_join.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/netlog.cpp (sha256 7860b019bc6c7b5c6d4738adc0f3a5a2b56ec9070b822473471875c23ec212b4) ====================
// Implementation of netlog.h.  No dependencies beyond the C runtime and kernel32, so the same file
// can be linked into the module, into mp_join and into the self-test.
#include "netlog.h"

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <share.h>
#include <atomic>
#include <limits.h>

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- state
static FILE *g_file;
static CRITICAL_SECTION g_cs;
static INIT_ONCE g_cs_once = INIT_ONCE_STATIC_INIT;
static char g_path[MAX_PATH], g_part_path[MAX_PATH], g_identity[1024];
static char g_program[32] = "";
static DWORD g_started;
static std::atomic<unsigned long long> g_frames{0};

static unsigned long long g_rx_packets, g_rx_bytes, g_tx_packets, g_tx_bytes;
static unsigned long long g_rx_type[NETLOG_TYPES], g_tx_type[NETLOG_TYPES];
static unsigned long long g_rx_logged[NETLOG_TYPES], g_tx_logged[NETLOG_TYPES];
static DWORD g_last_summary;

static int g_phase = -1;
static unsigned g_sim_ms;
static int g_peers;
static unsigned g_sides;

// Named counters, fixed table: a fixed table cannot allocate, cannot fail and cannot grow without
// bound while a battle is running.
enum { NETLOG_NAMES = 16 };
static struct { char name[20]; long long value; } g_names[NETLOG_NAMES];
static int g_name_count;

// Keep the current and previous 8 MiB segments plus the previous run; never truncate a segment
// unless its rename succeeded. The collector includes both current and .part/.prev files.
static const long k_max_bytes = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
static long g_rotate_at = k_max_bytes;

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- locking
static BOOL CALLBACK init_cs(PINIT_ONCE, PVOID, PVOID *)
{
    InitializeCriticalSection(&g_cs);
    return TRUE;
}

static void lock()
{
    InitOnceExecuteOnce(&g_cs_once, init_cs, nullptr, nullptr);
    EnterCriticalSection(&g_cs);
}

static void unlock() { LeaveCriticalSection(&g_cs); }

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- writing
static void prefix(const char *channel)
{
    SYSTEMTIME t;
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    GetLocalTime(&t);
    fprintf(g_file, "%02u:%02u:%02u.%03u [t%05lu f%06llu +%6.1fs] %-7s ",
            t.wHour, t.wMinute, t.wSecond, t.wMilliseconds, GetCurrentThreadId(),
            g_frames.load(std::memory_order_relaxed), (now - g_started) / 1000.0, channel);
}

// Caller holds g_cs. If rotation fails, append to the original and retry only after another MiB;
// a locked destination must never turn a failed rename into a truncated log.
static void rotate_locked(bool force = false)
{
    if (!g_file) return;
    long at = ftell(g_file);
    if (!force && (at < 0 || at < g_rotate_at)) return;
    fflush(g_file);
    fclose(g_file);
    g_file = nullptr;
    if (!MoveFileExA(g_path, g_part_path, MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)) {
        DWORD error = GetLastError();
        g_file = _fsopen(g_path, "a", _SH_DENYNO);
        g_rotate_at = at >= 0 && at < LONG_MAX - 1024 * 1024 ? at + 1024 * 1024 : LONG_MAX;
        if (g_file) {
            prefix("note");
            fprintf(g_file, "log rotation failed (%lu); continuing the existing file\n", error);
            fflush(g_file);
        }
        return;
    }
    g_file = _fsopen(g_path, "w", _SH_DENYNO);
    g_rotate_at = k_max_bytes;
    if (g_file) {
        prefix("boot");
        fprintf(g_file, "continued after log rotation; previous segment is .part.log\n");
        prefix("boot");
        fprintf(g_file, "%s\n", g_identity[0] ? g_identity : "identity was not yet available");
        fflush(g_file);
    }
}

static void write_va(const char *channel, const char *fmt, va_list args)
{
    lock();
    if (g_file) {
        prefix(channel);
        vfprintf(g_file, fmt, args);
        fputc('\n', g_file);
        fflush(g_file);
        rotate_locked();
    }
    unlock();
}

static void write_line(const char *channel, const char *fmt, ...)
{
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    write_va(channel, fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- identity helpers
static unsigned long long fnv1a(FILE *file, unsigned long long *size)
{
    unsigned long long hash = 14695981039346656037ULL; // standard FNV-1a 64-bit offset basis
    unsigned char buffer[65536];
    size_t got;
    *size = 0;
    while ((got = fread(buffer, 1, sizeof buffer, file)) > 0) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < got; i++) {
            hash ^= buffer[i];
            hash *= 1099511628211ULL;
        }
        *size += got;
    }
    return hash;
}

static unsigned long pe_stamp(HMODULE self)
{
    // TimeDateStamp of our own image: the one number that changes with every rebuild, so a report
    // can be matched to the exact binary that produced it.
    const unsigned char *base = (const unsigned char *)self;
    if (!base)
        return 0;
    unsigned long offset = *(const unsigned long *)(base + 0x3C);
    if (offset < 0x40 || offset > 0x1000)
        return 0;
    return *(const unsigned long *)(base + offset + 8);
}

static const char *env_or(const char *name, const char *fallback)
{
    static char values[8][64];
    static int next;
    char *slot = values[next++ % 8];
    DWORD got = GetEnvironmentVariableA(name, slot, 64);
    if (!got || got >= 64)
        return fallback;
    return slot;
}

static int counter_slot(const char *name)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < g_name_count; i++)
        if (strcmp(g_names[i].name, name) == 0)
            return i;
    if (g_name_count == NETLOG_NAMES)
        return -1;
    strncpy_s(g_names[g_name_count].name, name, _TRUNCATE);
    g_names[g_name_count].value = 0;
    return g_name_count++;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- open / close
static bool open_file(const char *out_dir, const char *program)
{
    char previous[MAX_PATH], previous_part[MAX_PATH];
    sprintf_s(g_path, sizeof g_path, "%s\\net_v21_%s.log", out_dir, program);
    sprintf_s(g_part_path, sizeof g_part_path, "%s\\net_v21_%s.part.log", out_dir, program);
    sprintf_s(previous, sizeof previous, "%s\\net_v21_%s.prev.log", out_dir, program);
    sprintf_s(previous_part, sizeof previous_part, "%s\\net_v21_%s.part.prev.log", out_dir, program);
    g_identity[0] = 0;
    g_rotate_at = k_max_bytes;
    if (GetFileAttributesA(g_path) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) {
        if (!MoveFileExA(g_path, previous, MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)) {
            // A reader may have the old file open. Append rather than erase the only surviving log.
            g_file = _fsopen(g_path, "a", _SH_DENYNO);
            return g_file != nullptr;
        }
        if (GetFileAttributesA(g_part_path) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES)
            MoveFileExA(g_part_path, previous_part, MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING);
    }
    g_file = _fsopen(g_path, "w", _SH_DENYNO);
    return g_file != nullptr;
}

void netlog_open(HMODULE self, const char *program, const char *out_dir)
{
    if (!out_dir || !program) return;
    lock();
    if (g_file) { unlock(); return; }
    strncpy_s(g_program, program, _TRUNCATE);
    if (!open_file(out_dir, program)) { unlock(); return; }

    g_started = GetTickCount();
    g_last_summary = g_started;

    char path[MAX_PATH] = "";
    GetModuleFileNameA(self, path, MAX_PATH);
    FILE *image = nullptr;
    unsigned long long size = 0;
    unsigned long long hash = 0;
    if (fopen_s(&image, path, "rb") == 0 && image) {
        hash = fnv1a(image, &size);
        fclose(image);
    }

    sprintf_s(g_identity, sizeof g_identity, "net_v21 v1.0.7-test, %s %s, program %s; image %s, %llu bytes, fnv1a64 %016llx, pe stamp %08lx",
              __DATE__, __TIME__, program, path, size, hash, pe_stamp(self));
    write_line("boot", "=== net_v21: our own network module, v1.0.7-test ===");
#ifdef _MSC_VER
    write_line("boot", "build: compiled %s %s, MSVC %d", __DATE__, __TIME__, (int)_MSC_VER);
#else
    write_line("boot", "build: compiled %s %s, non-MSVC compiler", __DATE__, __TIME__);
#endif
    write_line("boot", "image: %s", path);
    write_line("boot", "image: %llu bytes, fnv1a64 %016llx, pe stamp %08lx",
               size, hash, pe_stamp(self));
    write_line("boot", "process: %s, pid %lu, program %s",
               env_or("COMPUTERNAME", "?"), GetCurrentProcessId(), program);
    write_line("boot", "command line: %s", GetCommandLineA());
    write_line("boot", "flags: MP_STEAM=%s MP_STEAM_FAKE=%s MP_SYNC_STATE=%s MP_VERSUS=%s MP_ROLE=%s "
                       "MP_SWAP=%s MP_MAP=%s",
               env_or("MP_STEAM", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_STEAM_FAKE", "<unset>"),
               env_or("MP_SYNC_STATE", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_VERSUS", "<unset>"),
               env_or("MP_ROLE", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_SWAP", "<unset>"),
               env_or("MP_MAP", "<unset>"));
    write_line("boot", "battle flags: MP_SYNC_SHOTS=%s MP_LOG_SHOTS=%s MP_SYNC_HITS=%s MP_LOG_HITS=%s MP_SYNC_ANIM=%s",
               env_or("MP_SYNC_SHOTS", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_LOG_SHOTS", "<unset>"),
               env_or("MP_SYNC_HITS", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_LOG_HITS", "<unset>"),
               env_or("MP_SYNC_ANIM", "<unset>"));
    write_line("boot", "vitals flags: MP_VITALS=%s MP_VEHICLES=%s MP_STATS=%s MP_IDLE_MS=%s MP_LOG_VITALS=%s",
               env_or("MP_VITALS", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_VEHICLES", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_STATS", "<unset>"),
               env_or("MP_IDLE_MS", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_LOG_VITALS", "<unset>"));
    write_line("boot", "pacing flags: MP_BG_FPS=%s MP_FG_FPS=%s", env_or("MP_BG_FPS", "<unset>"), env_or("MP_FG_FPS", "<unset>"));
    write_line("boot", "feature status: hit and soldier animation replication are not implemented in this candidate");
    write_line("boot", "log: %s (previous run kept as .prev.log)", g_path);
    unlock();
}

void netlog_shutdown()
{
    lock();
    if (g_file) {
        prefix("bye");
        fprintf(g_file, "module going away after %.1f s, %llu frames\n",
                (GetTickCount() - g_started) / 1000.0, g_frames.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
        fflush(g_file);
        fclose(g_file);
        g_file = nullptr;
    }
    unlock();
}

const char *netlog_path() { return g_path; }

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- lines
void netlog_mirror(const char *text)
{
    write_line("module", "%s", text ? text : "");
}

void netlog_note(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    write_va("note", fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
}

void netlog_mark(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    write_va("battle", fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
}

void netlog_count(const char *name, long long delta)
{
    if (!name) return;
    lock();
    if (g_file) {
        int index = counter_slot(name);
        if (index >= 0) g_names[index].value += delta;
    }
    unlock();
}

void netlog_state(int phase, unsigned sim_ms, int peers, unsigned sides)
{
    lock();
    g_phase = phase;
    g_sim_ms = sim_ms;
    g_peers = peers;
    g_sides = sides;
    unlock();
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- traffic
static const char *type_name(unsigned char type)
{
    switch (type) {
    case 1: return "HELLO";
    case 2: return "WELCOME";
    case 3: return "KEEPALIVE";
    case 4: return "IMAGE_REQ";
    case 5: return "IMAGE_DATA";
    case 6: return "STATE";
    case 7: return "BYE";
    case 8: return "OBJECT";
    case 9: return "OBJECT_ACK";
    case 10: return "SHOT";
    case 11: return "SHOT_ACK";
    case 12: return "END";
    default: return "?";
    }
}

void netlog_packet(const char *dir, const unsigned char *data, int size,
                   unsigned int peer_ip, unsigned short peer_port, unsigned short family)
{
    if (!data || size <= 0) return;
    bool out = dir && dir[0] == 't';
    unsigned char type = size > 5 ? data[4] : 0;   // Head: magic(4), type(1)
    if (type >= NETLOG_TYPES)
        type = 0;

    lock();
    if (!g_file) { unlock(); return; }
    if (out) {
        g_tx_packets++;
        g_tx_bytes += (unsigned)size;
        g_tx_type[type]++;
    } else {
        g_rx_packets++;
        g_rx_bytes += (unsigned)size;
        g_rx_type[type]++;
    }
    // Rate limit: the first 20 of every type are written in full, after that every 100th.  The
    // five-second summary carries the true totals, so nothing is lost that matters.
    unsigned long long *seen = out ? &g_tx_logged[type] : &g_rx_logged[type];
    bool write = (*seen)++ < 20 || (*seen % 100) == 0;
    if (write) {
        char peer[64];
        if (family == 0x5354)
            sprintf_s(peer, "steam");
        else if (family == 2 /* AF_INET */)
            sprintf_s(peer, "%u.%u.%u.%u:%u", (peer_ip >> 24) & 255, (peer_ip >> 16) & 255,
                      (peer_ip >> 8) & 255, peer_ip & 255, peer_port);
        else
            sprintf_s(peer, "-");
        prefix(out ? "tx" : "rx");
        fprintf(g_file, "%s %-10s %5d B  peer %s  #%llu\n", out ? "->" : "<-", type_name(type),
                size, peer, *seen);
        fflush(g_file);
        rotate_locked();
    }
    unlock();
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- summary
static void summary_locked();

void netlog_test_summary()
{
    lock();
    if (g_file) summary_locked();
    unlock();
}

void netlog_test_rotate()
{
    lock();
    rotate_locked(true);
    unlock();
}

// Called with the lock held: the caller decides whether the five seconds are up.
static void summary_locked()
{
    g_last_summary = GetTickCount();
    prefix("summary");
    fprintf(g_file, "%llu frames, rx %llu/%llu B, tx %llu/%llu B",
            g_frames.load(std::memory_order_relaxed), g_rx_packets, g_rx_bytes, g_tx_packets, g_tx_bytes);
    if (g_phase >= 0)
        fprintf(g_file, ", phase %d, sim %.1f s, %d peer(s), sides 0x%x",
                g_phase, g_sim_ms / 1000.0, g_peers, g_sides);
    fputc('\n', g_file);
    // Per-type split only for the types that actually moved: a line per type per five seconds is
    // noise, one line with the nonzero entries is a picture of the battle.
    prefix("summary");
    fprintf(g_file, "types:");
    for (int i = 0; i < NETLOG_TYPES; i++)
        if (g_rx_type[i] || g_tx_type[i])
            fprintf(g_file, " %s rx%llu/tx%llu", type_name((unsigned char)i), g_rx_type[i],
                    g_tx_type[i]);
    fputc('\n', g_file);
    if (g_name_count) {
        prefix("summary");
        fprintf(g_file, "battle:");
        for (int i = 0; i < g_name_count; i++)
            fprintf(g_file, " %s=%lld", g_names[i].name, g_names[i].value);
        fputc('\n', g_file);
    }
    fflush(g_file);
    rotate_locked();
}

void netlog_frame()
{
    g_frames.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    lock();
    if (g_file && GetTickCount() - g_last_summary >= 5000) summary_locked();
    unlock();
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/netlog.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/netlog.h (sha256 d4a3b6f320a65b67fed72a9d6efedda9d354cf4095f9a830a1f51514f10addc2) ====================
// Detailed, automatic logging for our own network module.
//
// The published module logs into out\basic_network_<program>_<pid>.log, one line per event, and it
// is written by whoever remembers to call mp_log().  That is fine while a feature is being built
// and useless when a player reports "the battle went out of sync at minute ten": by then the file
// has rolled over with the process, the pid changes every run, and nothing in it says which build
// produced it.
//
// This layer fixes the three things that make a log useless for a bug report:
//
//   1. identity - the first lines say which build this is, the file's size and hash, the flags the
//      process was started with, the command line and the OS.  A log without that cannot be
//      matched to a binary, and a report about "the new dll" is worthless if nobody can tell which
//      new dll it was;
//   2. a stable name - out\net_v21_<program>.log, truncated per run, the previous run kept as
//      .prev.  The player is told "send out\net_v21_opgame.log", not "find the newest file whose
//      name has a pid in it";
//   3. traffic and battle accounting - every datagram is counted by type and direction, a line is
//      written every five seconds with the totals, and the battle's own milestones (first
//      snapshot, hits, craters, ceasefire, end) go in with the same timestamp as everything else.
//
// Everything here is best-effort: if the log cannot be opened the module keeps running, because a
// mod that refuses to play when it cannot write a file is worse than a mod with no log.
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>

// How many packet types the counters keep.  The protocol has 11 today; the table is deliberately
// larger so a new type does not silently fall off the end.
enum { NETLOG_TYPES = 24 };

// Opens the log for this process.  `out_dir` is the module's out\ folder (the caller already knows
// how to derive it), `program` is opgame/tacgame.  Safe to call twice; the second call is a no-op.
void netlog_open(HMODULE self, const char *program, const char *out_dir);

// Flushes and closes.  Called from DllMain detach; also safe to call when nothing was opened.
void netlog_shutdown();

// One line from the module's own log, mirrored here so a single file has everything in order.
void netlog_mirror(const char *text);

// Free-form line that is always written (no rate limiting).  Used for identity and settings.
void netlog_note(const char *fmt, ...);

// A datagram went out or came in.  `family` is the socket address family of the peer: AF_INET for
// UDP, 0x5354 for a Steam peer, 0 when unknown.  Rate limited per type, so a battle at 60 packets a
// second cannot fill the disk - the five-second summary carries the true totals.
void netlog_packet(const char *dir, const unsigned char *data, int size,
                   unsigned int peer_ip, unsigned short peer_port, unsigned short family);

// Called once per frame from session_frame(): advances the frame counter and, every five seconds,
// writes the summary line.
void netlog_frame();

// A named counter (shots, hits, craters, resyncs, ...).  Its total appears in every summary.
void netlog_count(const char *name, long long delta = 1);

// A battle milestone.  Written as "battle: ..." with the usual prefix; never rate limited.
void netlog_mark(const char *fmt, ...);

// The battle's current state, written into the summary: phase, sim time, players, sides.
void netlog_state(int phase, unsigned sim_ms, int peers, unsigned sides);

const char *netlog_path();

// Test hooks: force the summary or a rotation instead of waiting for time/size limits.
void netlog_test_summary();
void netlog_test_rotate();
==================== END development/net-v21/src/netlog.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/objects.inc (sha256 5873d1f76ea2db32a7d852bafe5455ed9ef4e12af353b0775f664a1777249e17) ====================
// generated from root\programs.flatdata (obj_list, pipelines, config sections)
{ "i_window", "window" },
