// Protocol 15 spectator mode uses validation/identity helpers only; ordered guest-turn
// routines below are retained for historical tests and are NOT connected to the session.
// Ordered M5 operation-map ownership. The host owns input first; after a checked handoff, the
// client owns it; the host may resolve only after collecting the client's matching snapshot.
// This is transport-independent and deliberately fails closed on stale/invalid identities.
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>

namespace operation_turn {
enum class Phase : uint8_t { host = 0, handoff = 1, client = 2, collect = 3, resolve = 4 };
enum class Observation { invalid, stale, current, snapshot_needed };
static const uint32_t epoch_mask = 0x00ffffffu;
static const unsigned phase_shift = 2;
static const uint32_t phase_mask = 7u << phase_shift;

inline bool valid_epoch(uint32_t epoch) { return epoch && !(epoch & ~epoch_mask); }
inline bool valid_phase(Phase phase) { return (unsigned)phase <= (unsigned)Phase::resolve; }
// A saved phase 2 denotes battles pending; phase 0 must have advanced the frames table.
// Unknown intermediate phases cannot prove that the engine finished the joint calculation.
inline bool resolution_saved(int phase, uint32_t frames, uint32_t before) {
    return phase == 2 || (phase == 0 && frames > before);
}
inline uint32_t next_revision(uint32_t revision) { ++revision; return revision ? revision : 1; }
inline bool newer_revision(uint32_t value, uint32_t previous) {
    uint32_t delta = value - previous;
    return value && delta && delta < 0x80000000u;
}
inline bool newer_epoch(uint32_t value, uint32_t previous) {
    uint32_t delta = (value - previous) & epoch_mask;
    return valid_epoch(value) && valid_epoch(previous) && delta && delta < 0x00800000u;
}
inline uint32_t flags(uint32_t epoch, Phase phase, bool collecting, bool host_done) {
    return ((epoch & epoch_mask) << 8) | ((uint32_t)phase << phase_shift) |
           (collecting ? 1u : 0u) | (host_done ? 2u : 0u);
}
inline bool decode(uint32_t packed, uint32_t *epoch, Phase *phase) {
    uint32_t e = packed >> 8;
    Phase p = (Phase)((packed & phase_mask) >> phase_shift);
    if (!valid_epoch(e) || !valid_phase(p) || (packed & 0xe0u)) return false;
    if (epoch) *epoch = e;
    if (phase) *phase = p;
    return true;
}

struct Host {
    uint32_t epoch = 0, revision = 0;
    Phase phase = Phase::host;
    bool shared = false, published = false;

    bool begin(uint32_t new_epoch, uint32_t initial_revision) {
        if (!valid_epoch(new_epoch) || !initial_revision) return false;
        epoch = new_epoch; revision = initial_revision; phase = Phase::host;
        shared = true; published = false;
        return true;
    }
    bool finish_host() {
        if (!shared || phase != Phase::host) return false;
        phase = Phase::handoff; published = false;
        return true;
    }
    bool publish(uint32_t new_revision) {
        if (!shared || phase != Phase::handoff || published || !newer_revision(new_revision, revision)) return false;
        revision = new_revision; published = true;
        return true;
    }
    bool peer_loaded(uint32_t peer_epoch, uint32_t peer_revision) {
        if (!shared || phase != Phase::handoff || !published || peer_epoch != epoch || peer_revision != revision) return false;
        phase = Phase::client;
        return true;
    }
    bool peer_finished(uint32_t peer_epoch, uint32_t peer_revision) {
        if (!shared || phase != Phase::client || peer_epoch != epoch || peer_revision != revision) return false;
        phase = Phase::collect;
        return true;
    }
    bool committed(uint32_t peer_epoch, uint32_t peer_revision) {
        if (!shared || phase != Phase::collect || peer_epoch != epoch || peer_revision != revision) return false;
        phase = Phase::resolve;
        return true;
    }
    bool host_input() const { return shared && phase == Phase::host; }
    bool may_resolve() const { return shared && phase == Phase::resolve; }
    void clear() { epoch = revision = 0; phase = Phase::host; shared = published = false; }
};

struct Client {
    uint32_t epoch = 0, revision = 0, pending_epoch = 0, pending_revision = 0;
    Phase phase = Phase::host;
    bool done = false;

    bool begin(uint32_t loaded_epoch, uint32_t loaded_revision) {
        if (!valid_epoch(loaded_epoch) || !loaded_revision) return false;
        epoch = loaded_epoch; revision = loaded_revision; pending_epoch = pending_revision = 0;
        phase = Phase::host; done = false;
        return true;
    }
    Observation observe(uint32_t offered_epoch, uint32_t offered_revision, Phase offered_phase) {
        if (!valid_epoch(epoch) || !revision || !valid_epoch(offered_epoch) || !offered_revision || !valid_phase(offered_phase))
            return Observation::invalid;
        if (offered_epoch != epoch && !newer_epoch(offered_epoch, epoch)) return Observation::stale;
        if (offered_epoch == epoch && offered_revision != revision && !newer_revision(offered_revision, revision))
            return Observation::stale;
        if (offered_epoch != epoch || offered_revision != revision) {
            // A later offer may replace an earlier incomplete one, never the reverse.
            if (pending_epoch) {
                if (offered_epoch != pending_epoch && !newer_epoch(offered_epoch, pending_epoch)) return Observation::stale;
                if (offered_epoch == pending_epoch && offered_revision != pending_revision &&
                    !newer_revision(offered_revision, pending_revision)) return Observation::stale;
            }
            pending_epoch = offered_epoch; pending_revision = offered_revision;
            return Observation::snapshot_needed;
        }
        if (pending_epoch) return Observation::stale; // never unlock an old snapshot while a new one is pending
        if ((unsigned)offered_phase < (unsigned)phase) return Observation::stale;
        phase = offered_phase;
        return Observation::current;
    }
    bool loaded(uint32_t loaded_epoch, uint32_t loaded_revision) {
        if (!pending_epoch || pending_epoch != loaded_epoch || pending_revision != loaded_revision) return false;
        epoch = loaded_epoch; revision = loaded_revision; pending_epoch = pending_revision = 0;
        phase = Phase::host; done = false; // wait for a matching host phase announcement
        return true;
    }
    bool input() const { return epoch && revision && !pending_epoch && phase == Phase::client && !done; }
    bool finish() {
        if (!input()) return false;
        done = true;
        return true;
    }
};
} // namespace operation_turn
==================== END development/net-v21/src/operation_turn.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/opsave.h (sha256 0683eac493c1226ee4e04851f885a7df59f42b19833c9f46baf77279413e1abd) ====================
// Operation saves (<campaign>_<operation>.opsave in the profile folder): the same binary config format as
// the battle description (see image.h). What matters for multiplayer:
//
//   header name (offset 0x20)   the operation's name, e.g. "07_02_odintsov_rus"
//   frames                      one row per turn of the operation
//   p_<formation>               one struct per formation with `side` ('GER', 'USSR') and `ai`:
//                               'USER' for the formations the player commands, a computer group code
//                               ('EN1', 'U286', ...) for the others, 0 for formations nobody moves
//   <battle group>              one struct per battle group, tagged the same way
//
// The tags decide whose moves the operational map accepts. A save with the tags turned round opens the
// same operation from the other side: that is how the map is handed to the other player and back.
#pragma once
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace opsave {

typedef unsigned char byte;
static const unsigned k_user = 0x52455355;      // 'USER'

inline unsigned u32_at(const std::vector<byte> &b, size_t o) { unsigned v; memcpy(&v, b.data() + o, 4); return v; }

// calls f(node index, cell index within the node, offset of the cell) for every cell of the file
template <class F> inline void each_cell(const std::vector<byte> &b, F f)
{
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table = u32_at(b, 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        unsigned type = u32_at(b, o + 0x20), cols = u32_at(b, o + 0x24), rows = u32_at(b, o + 0x28), data = u32_at(b, o + 0x30);
        size_t stride = type == 1 ? 0x40 : 0x20, n = type == 1 ? rows : type == 2 ? (size_t)rows * cols : 0;
        if (!n || data + n * stride > b.size()) continue;
        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k++) f(i, k, data + k * stride);
    }
}

// One formation or battle group: where its tag and its other fields sit in the file.
struct Tagged {
    std::string name;
    size_t ai = 0, vis = 0, px = 0, pz = 0, side = 0;     // offsets of the cells (0: the struct has no such field)
    bool pool = false;
};

// every struct that has an `ai` field
inline std::vector<Tagged> tagged(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::vector<Tagged> out;
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return out;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table = u32_at(b, 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        if (u32_at(b, o + 0x20) != 1) continue;
        unsigned rows = u32_at(b, o + 0x28), data = u32_at(b, o + 0x30);
        if (data + (size_t)rows * 0x40 > b.size()) continue;
        Tagged t;
        for (unsigned r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
            size_t c = data + (size_t)r * 0x40;
            const char *field = (const char *)b.data() + c + 0x20;
            if (u32_at(b, c + 16) != 4) continue;
            if (!strncmp(field, "ai", 0x20)) t.ai = c;
            else if (!strncmp(field, "vis_mode", 0x20)) t.vis = c;
            else if (!strncmp(field, "px", 0x20)) t.px = c;
            else if (!strncmp(field, "pz", 0x20)) t.pz = c;
            else if (!strncmp(field, "side", 0x20)) t.side = c;
        }
        if (!t.ai) continue;
        char name[0x21] = { 0 };
        memcpy(name, b.data() + o, 0x20);
        t.name = name;
        t.pool = !strncmp(name, "p_", 2);
        out.push_back(t);
    }
    return out;
}

// What mirror() changed that unmirror() cannot work out by itself, by the struct's name.
struct Sidecar {
    unsigned code = 0;                                  // the computer code the player's own structs were given
    std::map<std::string, unsigned> ai, vis;            // the original tag / visibility of each struct
};

// The side ('GER', 'USSR') of the structs the player leads: his own side. 0 when it cannot be told.
inline unsigned user_side(const std::vector<byte> &b, const std::vector<Tagged> &all)
{
    std::map<unsigned, int> votes;
    for (const Tagged &t : all) if (t.side && u32_at(b, t.ai) == k_user) votes[u32_at(b, t.side)]++;
    unsigned side = 0;
    int best = 0;
    for (auto &kv : votes) if (kv.second > best) { best = kv.second; side = kv.first; }
    return side;
}

// Is this struct on the host player's side? His own formations are, and so are computer-led allies of his
// (some operations have them: 'AL1' next to 'USER'); they are not the other player's to command.
inline bool on_side(const std::vector<byte> &b, const Tagged &t, unsigned side)
{
    if (t.side && side) return u32_at(b, t.side) == side;
    return u32_at(b, t.ai) == k_user;
}

// For the other player: every struct of the opposing side becomes 'USER' and fully visible, the structs
// of this player's side (his own and his computer-led allies) get a computer code. Visibility
// (`vis_mode`: 3 own, 1 seen but not identified, 0 unseen) is kept by the game for the host only. The other
// player's view is made from it by taking contact to be mutual: he is shown a battle group of the host's
// side - as an unidentified contact - where it stands next to one of his own groups that the host can
// see. Where the host sees nothing of him, he sees nothing of the host.
inline bool mirror(std::vector<byte> &b, Sidecar *side, unsigned *changed)
{
    std::vector<Tagged> all = tagged(b);
    unsigned host = user_side(b, all);
    std::map<unsigned, int> pool_codes, any_codes;
    bool user = false;
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        unsigned v = u32_at(b, t.ai);
        if (v == k_user) user = true;
        else if (v && !on_side(b, t, host)) { any_codes[v]++; if (t.pool) pool_codes[v]++; }
    }
    if (!user || any_codes.empty()) return false;
    unsigned main = 0;
    int best = 0;
    for (auto &kv : (pool_codes.empty() ? any_codes : pool_codes)) if (kv.second > best) { best = kv.second; main = kv.first; }
    side->code = main;
    side->ai.clear();
    side->vis.clear();
    std::vector<std::pair<int, int>> theirs;          // squares of the battle groups that change to the other player
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        unsigned v = u32_at(b, t.ai);
        if (v && !on_side(b, t, host) && t.px && t.pz && t.vis && u32_at(b, t.vis) >= 1)
            theirs.push_back({ (int)u32_at(b, t.px), (int)u32_at(b, t.pz) });
    }
    unsigned n = 0;
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        unsigned v = u32_at(b, t.ai), to, vis;
        if (!v) continue;
        side->ai[t.name] = v;
        if (t.vis) side->vis[t.name] = u32_at(b, t.vis);
        if (on_side(b, t, host)) {
            to = main;
            vis = 0;
            if (t.px && t.pz)
                for (auto &sq : theirs)
                    if (llabs((int64_t)sq.first - (int32_t)u32_at(b, t.px)) <= 1 &&
                        llabs((int64_t)sq.second - (int32_t)u32_at(b, t.pz)) <= 1) { vis = 1; break; }
        } else {
            to = k_user;
            vis = 3;
        }
        memcpy(b.data() + t.ai, &to, 4);
        if (t.vis) memcpy(b.data() + t.vis, &vis, 4);
        n++;
    }
    if (changed) *changed = n;
    return n > 0;
}

// Back again, after the other player has made his moves in the mirrored save.
inline bool unmirror(std::vector<byte> &b, const Sidecar &side, unsigned *changed)
{
    if (!side.code) return false;
    unsigned n = 0;
    for (const Tagged &t : tagged(b)) {
        unsigned v = u32_at(b, t.ai);
        if (!v) continue;
        auto was = side.ai.find(t.name);
        unsigned to = was != side.ai.end() ? was->second : v == k_user ? side.code : k_user;
        memcpy(b.data() + t.ai, &to, 4);
        auto vis = side.vis.find(t.name);
        if (t.vis && vis != side.vis.end()) memcpy(b.data() + t.vis, &vis->second, 4);
        n++;
    }
    if (changed) *changed = n;
    return n > 0;
}

inline std::string operation_name(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return "";
    char name[0x21] = { 0 };
    memcpy(name, b.data() + 0x20, 0x20);
    return name;
}

inline void set_operation_name(std::vector<byte> &b, const std::string &name)
{
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || name.size() >= 0x20) return;
    memset(b.data() + 0x20, 0, 0x20);
    memcpy(b.data() + 0x20, name.data(), name.size());
}

// "07_02_odintsov_rus" <-> "07_02_odintsov_ger": the same operation played from the other side, when the
// game has one. Started on the other player's state it gives him his own side's view: his territory, fog
// of war over the opponent.
inline std::string counterpart(const std::string &operation)
{
    size_t n = operation.size();
    if (n > 4 && !operation.compare(n - 4, 4, "_rus")) return operation.substr(0, n - 4) + "_ger";
    if (n > 4 && !operation.compare(n - 4, 4, "_ger")) return operation.substr(0, n - 4) + "_rus";
    return "";
}

// One struct of a save: where its fields are.
struct Fields { size_t data = 0; unsigned rows = 0; };
inline std::map<std::string, Fields> structs(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::map<std::string, Fields> out;
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return out;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table = u32_at(b, 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        if (u32_at(b, o + 0x20) != 1) continue;
        Fields f;
        f.rows = u32_at(b, o + 0x28);
        f.data = u32_at(b, o + 0x30);
        if (f.data + (size_t)f.rows * 0x40 > b.size()) continue;
        char name[0x21] = { 0 };
        memcpy(name, b.data() + o, 0x20);
        out[name] = f;
    }
    return out;
}

// Every node of a save, in file order.
struct NodeRef { std::string name; unsigned type = 0, cols = 0, rows = 0; size_t data = 0, header = 0; };
inline std::vector<NodeRef> nodes(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::vector<NodeRef> out;
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return out;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table = u32_at(b, 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        NodeRef n;
        char name[0x21] = { 0 };
        memcpy(name, b.data() + o, 0x20);
        n.name = name;
        n.header = o;
        n.type = u32_at(b, o + 0x20);
        n.cols = u32_at(b, o + 0x24);
        n.rows = u32_at(b, o + 0x28);
        n.data = u32_at(b, o + 0x30);
        size_t cells = n.type == 1 ? (size_t)n.rows * 2 : n.type == 2 ? (size_t)n.rows * n.cols : 0;
        if (n.data + cells * 0x20 > b.size()) n.rows = 0;
        out.push_back(n);
    }
    return out;
}

// While a turn is processed, both players' sides are player-led: the game then carries out the orders both
// players gave and its computer commander plans nothing for the other player's groups (it would otherwise
// move every group of his that he left where it was). Computer-led allies of the host stay the computer's.
// Returns how many structs were changed.
inline unsigned all_player_led(std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::vector<Tagged> all = tagged(b);
    unsigned host = user_side(b, all), n = 0;
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        unsigned v = u32_at(b, t.ai);
        if (!v || v == k_user || on_side(b, t, host)) continue;
        memcpy(b.data() + t.ai, &k_user, 4);
        n++;
    }
    return n;
}

// After the turn: who commands what goes back to what the sidecar recorded. The host sees the other
// player's battle groups again only where they stand next to one of his own.
inline unsigned restore_tags(std::vector<byte> &b, const Sidecar &side)
{
    std::vector<Tagged> all = tagged(b);
    std::vector<std::pair<int, int>> mine;
    // the host's side: the side of the structs he led before the turn
    std::map<unsigned, int> votes;
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        auto was = side.ai.find(t.name);
        if (was != side.ai.end() && was->second == k_user && t.side) votes[u32_at(b, t.side)]++;
    }
    unsigned host = 0;
    int best = 0;
    for (auto &kv : votes) if (kv.second > best) { best = kv.second; host = kv.first; }
    auto own = [&](const Tagged &t, unsigned tag) { return t.side && host ? u32_at(b, t.side) == host : tag == k_user; };
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        auto was = side.ai.find(t.name);
        if (was != side.ai.end() && own(t, was->second) && t.px && t.pz) mine.push_back({ (int)u32_at(b, t.px), (int)u32_at(b, t.pz) });
    }
    unsigned n = 0;
    for (const Tagged &t : all) {
        auto was = side.ai.find(t.name);
        if (was == side.ai.end()) continue;
        unsigned vis = 3;
        if (!own(t, was->second)) {
            vis = 0;
            if (t.px && t.pz)
                for (auto &sq : mine)
                    if (llabs((int64_t)sq.first - (int32_t)u32_at(b, t.px)) <= 1 &&
                        llabs((int64_t)sq.second - (int32_t)u32_at(b, t.pz)) <= 1) { vis = 1; break; }
        }
        if (u32_at(b, t.ai) != was->second) n++;
        memcpy(b.data() + t.ai, &was->second, 4);
        if (t.vis) memcpy(b.data() + t.vis, &vis, 4);
    }
    return n;
}

// The sidecar as a small text file, so it survives the restarts of the host's operation program.
inline bool write_sidecar(const char *path, const Sidecar &side)
{
    FILE *f = 0;
    if (fopen_s(&f, path, "w") || !f) return false;
    fprintf(f, "%u\n", side.code);
    for (auto &kv : side.ai) fprintf(f, "%s %u\n", kv.first.c_str(), kv.second);
    fclose(f);
    return true;
}

inline bool read_sidecar(const char *path, Sidecar *side)
{
    FILE *f = 0;
    if (fopen_s(&f, path, "r") || !f) return false;
    char name[64];
    unsigned v = 0;
    side->ai.clear();
    side->vis.clear();
    bool ok = fscanf_s(f, "%u", &side->code) == 1;
    while (ok && fscanf_s(f, "%63s %u", name, (unsigned)sizeof name, &v) == 2) side->ai[name] = v;
    fclose(f);
    return ok && !side->ai.empty();
}

inline unsigned rows_of(const std::vector<byte> &b, const char *table)
{
    for (const NodeRef &n : nodes(b)) if (n.name == table && n.type == 2) return n.rows;
    return 0;
}

inline bool has_node(const std::vector<byte> &b, const char *name)
{
    for (const NodeRef &n : nodes(b)) if (n.name == name) return true;
    return false;
}

// Both players have moved, each in his own copy of the operation. This puts the other player's moves into
// the host's save as a three-way merge: `base` is the copy the other player was given, `theirs` what he
// sent back, `mine` the host's own save.
//   - every cell he changed (numbers; never text, and never who commands a group or how well it is seen)
//     is copied over the host's;
//   - the rows he added to `user_orders` - one per battle group he gave an order to - are appended to the
//     host's.
// An order on the operational map is a row in user_orders plus cells in the node and marker tables, which
// is why whole tables are compared and not just the battle groups.
inline unsigned merge_moves(std::vector<byte> &mine, const std::vector<byte> &base, const std::vector<byte> &theirs, unsigned *orders)
{
    std::vector<NodeRef> A = nodes(mine), B = nodes(base), T = nodes(theirs);
    // nodes are matched by name and by which of the same-named nodes they are ("pool" occurs once per formation)
    auto index = [](const std::vector<NodeRef> &v) {
        std::map<std::pair<std::string, int>, size_t> m;
        std::map<std::string, int> seen;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); i++) m[{ v[i].name, seen[v[i].name]++ }] = i;
        return m;
    };
    std::map<std::pair<std::string, int>, size_t> ia = index(A), ib = index(B);
    std::map<std::string, int> seen;
    unsigned cells = 0, added = 0;
    std::vector<byte> new_orders;
    size_t orders_node = (size_t)-1;
    // The orders table only exists once somebody has given an order. His new rows are the ones after those
    // of the copy he was given (all of them when that copy had no table yet).
    {
        auto ft = std::find_if(T.begin(), T.end(), [](const NodeRef &n) { return n.name == "user_orders" && n.type == 2; });
        auto fa = ia.find({ "user_orders", 0 }), fb = ib.find({ "user_orders", 0 });
        if (ft != T.end() && fa != ia.end() && A[fa->second].type == 2 && A[fa->second].cols == ft->cols) {
            unsigned from = fb != ib.end() && B[fb->second].cols == ft->cols && B[fb->second].rows <= ft->rows ? B[fb->second].rows : 0;
            if (ft->rows > from) {
                size_t row = (size_t)ft->cols * 0x20;
                new_orders.assign(theirs.begin() + ft->data + (size_t)from * row, theirs.begin() + ft->data + (size_t)ft->rows * row);
                orders_node = fa->second;
                added = ft->rows - from;
            }
        }
    }
    for (const NodeRef &t : T) {
        std::pair<std::string, int> key = { t.name, seen[t.name]++ };
        auto fa = ia.find(key), fb = ib.find(key);
        if (fa == ia.end() || fb == ib.end()) continue;
        const NodeRef &a = A[fa->second], &b = B[fb->second];
        if (a.type != t.type || b.type != t.type) continue;
        if (t.type == 1) {
            unsigned rows = t.rows < b.rows ? t.rows : b.rows;
            if (a.rows < rows) rows = a.rows;
            for (unsigned r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
                size_t ct = t.data + (size_t)r * 0x40, cb = b.data + (size_t)r * 0x40, ca = a.data + (size_t)r * 0x40;
                const char *field = (const char *)theirs.data() + ct + 0x20;
                if (strncmp(field, (const char *)base.data() + cb + 0x20, 0x20) || strncmp(field, (const char *)mine.data() + ca + 0x20, 0x20)) continue;
                if (!strncmp(field, "ai", 0x20) || !strncmp(field, "vis_mode", 0x20)) continue;
                unsigned kind = u32_at(theirs, ct + 16);
                if (kind == 2 || u32_at(base, cb + 16) != kind || u32_at(mine, ca + 16) != kind) continue;
                if (!memcmp(theirs.data() + ct, base.data() + cb, 16)) continue;
                memcpy(mine.data() + ca, theirs.data() + ct, 16);
                cells++;
            }
        } else if (t.type == 2 && t.cols == b.cols && t.cols == a.cols) {
            if (t.name == "user_orders" || t.rows != b.rows || t.rows != a.rows) continue;
            size_t n = (size_t)t.rows * t.cols;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k++) {
                size_t ct = t.data + k * 0x20, cb = b.data + k * 0x20, ca = a.data + k * 0x20;
                unsigned kind = u32_at(theirs, ct + 16);
                if (kind == 2 || u32_at(base, cb + 16) != kind || u32_at(mine, ca + 16) != kind) continue;
                if (!memcmp(theirs.data() + ct, base.data() + cb, 16)) continue;
                memcpy(mine.data() + ca, theirs.data() + ct, 16);
                cells++;
            }
        }
    }
    if (orders_node != (size_t)-1 && !new_orders.empty()) {
        // the table grows: it moves to the end of the file (offsets in this format are absolute)
        const NodeRef a = A[orders_node];
        size_t old = (size_t)a.rows * a.cols * 0x20, at = mine.size();
        std::vector<byte> block(mine.begin() + a.data, mine.begin() + a.data + old);
        block.insert(block.end(), new_orders.begin(), new_orders.end());
        mine.insert(mine.end(), block.begin(), block.end());
        unsigned rows = a.rows + added, data = (unsigned)at;
        memcpy(mine.data() + a.header + 0x28, &rows, 4);
        memcpy(mine.data() + a.header + 0x30, &data, 4);
    }
    if (orders) *orders = added;
    return cells;
}

// op_stream.turn_phase: 0 while orders are given, 2 with battles pending; -1 when it cannot be read
inline int turn_phase(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    std::map<std::string, Fields> all = structs(b);
    auto it = all.find("op_stream");
    if (it == all.end()) return -1;
    for (unsigned r = 0; r < it->second.rows; r++) {
        size_t c = it->second.data + (size_t)r * 0x40;
        if (!strncmp((const char *)b.data() + c + 0x20, "turn_phase", 0x20))
            return u32_at(b, c + 16) == 4 ? (int)u32_at(b, c) : -1;
    }
    return -1;
}

// rows of the `frames` table: the number of turns
inline unsigned turns(const std::vector<byte> &b)
{
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4)) return 0;
    unsigned count = u32_at(b, 4), table = u32_at(b, 8);
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        if (o + 0x80 > b.size()) break;
        if (!strncmp((const char *)b.data() + o, "frames", 0x20)) return u32_at(b, o + 0x28);
    }
    return 0;
}

// "<campaign>_<operation>.opsave" + the operation's name -> "<campaign>:<operation>", the id the game's
// programs are started with. Empty when the file name does not fit.
inline std::string operation_id(const std::string &file, const std::string &operation)
{
    std::string tail = "_" + operation + ".opsave";
    if (operation.empty() || file.size() <= tail.size() || file.compare(file.size() - tail.size(), tail.size(), tail)) return "";
    return file.substr(0, file.size() - tail.size()) + ":" + operation;
}

// a bare file name of an operation save, nothing that could leave the profile folder
inline bool safe_file_name(const char *name)
{
    size_t n = strlen(name);
    if (n < 8 || n > 90 || strcmp(name + n - 7, ".opsave")) return false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        char c = name[i];
        bool ok = (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '_' || c == '-' || (c == '.' && i == n - 7);
        if (!ok) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

} // namespace opsave
==================== END development/net-v21/src/opsave.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/opsave_checked.h (sha256 87abd8e289ec20925e2ea88762b7acebbd5187136d9039841c3c0f12a6255596) ====================
// Protocol 15 spectator mode uses validation/identity helpers only; ordered guest-turn
// routines below are retained for historical tests and are NOT connected to the session.
// Conservative checks for the ordered operation handoff. The guest receives the host's *updated*
// save, so a checked guest save can be kept whole: no foreign absolute pointers are copied into a
// different file. Unknown structures are rejected rather than silently losing either player's moves.
#pragma once
#include "opsave.h"
#include <stdint.h>
#include <limits>

namespace opsave_checked {
using opsave::byte;
using opsave::NodeRef;
using opsave::Sidecar;

inline bool reject(std::string *why, const std::string &text) { if (why) *why = text; return false; }
inline bool span(size_t size, size_t at, size_t count, size_t width) {
    return at <= size && width && count <= (size - at) / width;
}
inline bool safe_profile_id(const char *name) {
    if (!name || !*name || strlen(name) > 63) return false;
    for (const char *p = name; *p; ++p) {
        unsigned char c = (unsigned char)*p;
        if (!((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ||
              (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '_' || c == '-')) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Validate every node/cell before calling the older convenience readers, which assume bounded offsets.
// Type 0 is the empty root seen in the game's progpack files; 1 is a struct, 2 a table. A numeric
// list's meaning is not understood here, so it is not followed as a pointer or relocated.
inline bool validate(const std::vector<byte> &b, std::string *why = nullptr) {
    if (b.size() < 0x40 || b.size() > (64u << 20) || memcmp(b.data(), "AZCF", 4))
        return reject(why, "not a bounded AZCF file");
    const uint32_t count = opsave::u32_at(b, 4), table = opsave::u32_at(b, 8);
    if (!count || !span(b.size(), table, count, 0x80)) return reject(why, "node table is out of bounds");
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        size_t o = table + (size_t)i * 0x80;
        uint32_t type = opsave::u32_at(b, o + 0x20), cols = opsave::u32_at(b, o + 0x24);
        uint32_t rows = opsave::u32_at(b, o + 0x28), data = opsave::u32_at(b, o + 0x30);
        if (type > 2) return reject(why, "unknown node type");
        if (type == 0) {
            if (cols || rows || data > b.size()) return reject(why, "invalid root node");
            continue;
        }
        if (type == 2 && rows && !cols) return reject(why, "table has rows but no columns");
        size_t cells = rows;
        if (type == 2) {
            if (cols && cells > (std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max)() / cols) return reject(why, "table cell count overflow");
            cells *= cols;
        }
        size_t width = type == 1 ? 0x40 : 0x20;
        if (!span(b.size(), data, cells, width)) return reject(why, "node data is out of bounds");
        for (size_t j = 0; j < cells; ++j) {
            size_t cell = data + j * width;
            uint32_t kind = opsave::u32_at(b, cell + 16);
            if (kind > 6) return reject(why, "unknown cell kind");
            if (kind == 2) {
                uint32_t len = opsave::u32_at(b, cell), off = opsave::u32_at(b, cell + 4);
                if (off >= b.size() || len >= b.size() - off || b[(size_t)off + len] != 0)
                    return reject(why, "invalid text reference");
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline bool same_cell(const std::vector<byte> &a, size_t ca, const std::vector<byte> &b, size_t cb) {
    uint32_t kind = opsave::u32_at(a, ca + 16);
    if (kind != opsave::u32_at(b, cb + 16) || memcmp(a.data() + ca + 16, b.data() + cb + 16, 16)) return false;
    if (kind != 2) return memcmp(a.data() + ca, b.data() + cb, 16) == 0;
    uint32_t an = opsave::u32_at(a, ca), bn = opsave::u32_at(b, cb);
    uint32_t ao = opsave::u32_at(a, ca + 4), bo = opsave::u32_at(b, cb + 4);
    return an == bn && memcmp(a.data() + ao, b.data() + bo, an) == 0 &&
           memcmp(a.data() + ca + 8, b.data() + cb + 8, 8) == 0;
}

inline bool mirror(std::vector<byte> &save, Sidecar *side, unsigned *changed, std::string *why = nullptr) {
    if (!side || !validate(save, why)) return false;
    std::vector<opsave::Tagged> tags = opsave::tagged(save);
    std::set<std::string> names;
    unsigned host_side = 0, enemy_side = 0;
    for (const auto &t : tags) {
        if (t.name.empty() || !names.insert(t.name).second) return reject(why, "duplicate or empty tagged group name");
        unsigned ai = opsave::u32_at(save, t.ai);
        if (!ai) continue;
        if (!t.side || !opsave::u32_at(save, t.side)) return reject(why, "tagged group has no side");
        unsigned s = opsave::u32_at(save, t.side);
        if (ai == opsave::k_user) {
            if (host_side && host_side != s) return reject(why, "host controls multiple sides");
            host_side = s;
        }
    }
    if (!host_side) return reject(why, "host side is unknown");
    for (const auto &t : tags) {
        if (!opsave::u32_at(save, t.ai)) continue;
        unsigned s = opsave::u32_at(save, t.side);
        if (s != host_side) {
            if (enemy_side && enemy_side != s) return reject(why, "more than two sides in save");
            enemy_side = s;
        }
    }
    if (!enemy_side) return reject(why, "opposing side is unknown");
    Sidecar prepared;
    if (!opsave::mirror(save, &prepared, changed)) return reject(why, "save could not be mirrored");
    *side = prepared;
    return true;
}

// Bounded binary sidecar preserves BOTH ai and visibility. The caller persists these bytes with the
// same-directory atomic-file helper; decode rejects truncation, duplicates, extra bytes and unknown flags.
inline void put32(std::vector<byte> &b, uint32_t n) {
    b.push_back((byte)n); b.push_back((byte)(n >> 8)); b.push_back((byte)(n >> 16)); b.push_back((byte)(n >> 24));
}
inline uint32_t get32(const std::vector<byte> &b, size_t at) {
    return (uint32_t)b[at] | (uint32_t)b[at + 1] << 8 | (uint32_t)b[at + 2] << 16 | (uint32_t)b[at + 3] << 24;
}
inline bool encode_sidecar(const Sidecar &side, std::vector<byte> *out) {
    if (!out || !side.code || side.ai.empty() || side.ai.size() > 100000) return false;
    std::vector<byte> result;
    const byte magic[8] = {'M','V','S','I','D','E','2',0};
    result.insert(result.end(), magic, magic + sizeof magic);
    put32(result, side.code); put32(result, (uint32_t)side.ai.size());
    for (const auto &it : side.ai) {
        if (it.first.empty() || it.first.size() > 32 || it.first.find('\0') != std::string::npos || !it.second) return false;
        auto v = side.vis.find(it.first);
        put32(result, (uint32_t)it.first.size()); put32(result, it.second);
        put32(result, v == side.vis.end() ? 0 : 1); put32(result, v == side.vis.end() ? 0 : v->second);
        result.insert(result.end(), it.first.begin(), it.first.end());
    }
    for (const auto &it : side.vis) if (!side.ai.count(it.first)) return false;
    out->swap(result);
    return true;
}
inline bool decode_sidecar(const std::vector<byte> &data, Sidecar *side) {
    const byte magic[8] = {'M','V','S','I','D','E','2',0};
    if (!side || data.size() < 16 || data.size() > (8u << 20) || memcmp(data.data(), magic, sizeof magic)) return false;
    Sidecar result; result.code = get32(data, 8);
    uint32_t count = get32(data, 12);
    if (!result.code || !count || count > 100000) return false;
    size_t at = 16;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        if (!span(data.size(), at, 1, 16)) return false;
        uint32_t length = get32(data, at), ai = get32(data, at + 4), flags = get32(data, at + 8), vis = get32(data, at + 12);
        at += 16;
        if (!length || length > 32 || !ai || flags > 1 || (!flags && vis) || !span(data.size(), at, length, 1)) return false;
        std::string name((const char *)data.data() + at, length); at += length;
        if (name.find('\0') != std::string::npos || !result.ai.emplace(name, ai).second) return false;
        if (flags) result.vis[name] = vis;
    }
    if (at != data.size()) return false;
    *side = result;
    return true;
}

using Key = std::pair<std::string, unsigned>;
inline std::map<Key, NodeRef> index_nodes(const std::vector<NodeRef> &nodes) {
    std::map<Key, NodeRef> result;
    std::map<std::string, unsigned> seen;
    for (const auto &n : nodes) result[{n.name, seen[n.name]++}] = n;
    return result;
}

// Compare re-serialized snapshots by value. Used to detect host-side changes after handoff;
// a pointer-like numeric cell that moved is conservatively treated as a change, never guessed at.
inline bool equivalent(const std::vector<byte> &a, const std::vector<byte> &b, std::string *why = nullptr) {
    if (!validate(a, why) || !validate(b, why) || opsave::operation_name(a) != opsave::operation_name(b))
        return reject(why, "host operation changed");
    auto left = index_nodes(opsave::nodes(a)), right = index_nodes(opsave::nodes(b));
    if (left.size() != right.size()) return reject(why, "host node count changed");
    for (const auto &it : left) {
        auto found = right.find(it.first);
        if (found == right.end()) return reject(why, "host node changed: " + it.first.first);
        const NodeRef &x = it.second, &y = found->second;
        if (x.type != y.type || x.cols != y.cols || x.rows != y.rows) return reject(why, "host structure changed: " + x.name);
        if (!x.type) continue;
        size_t cells = x.type == 1 ? x.rows : (size_t)x.rows * x.cols;
        size_t stride = x.type == 1 ? 0x40 : 0x20;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cells; ++i) {
            size_t cx = x.data + i * stride, cy = y.data + i * stride;
            if ((x.type == 1 && memcmp(a.data() + cx + 0x20, b.data() + cy + 0x20, 0x20)) || !same_cell(a, cx, b, cy))
                return reject(why, "host value changed after handoff: " + x.name);
        }
    }
    return true;
}

// `base` is EXACTLY the mirrored handoff after the host finished; `guest` is the returned save.
// Existing orders must survive byte-for-value, including text. We never transplant cells from
// guest to base, so any absolute offsets in newly added rows keep pointing into their own file.
inline bool accept(const std::vector<byte> &base, const std::vector<byte> &guest, const Sidecar &side,
                   std::vector<byte> *out, unsigned *added_orders, std::string *why = nullptr) {
    if (!out || !validate(base, why) || !validate(guest, why)) return false;
    std::vector<byte> encoded;
    if (!encode_sidecar(side, &encoded)) return reject(why, "sidecar is missing or invalid");
    std::string name = opsave::operation_name(base), their_name = opsave::operation_name(guest);
    if (name.empty() || (their_name != name && their_name != opsave::counterpart(name)))
        return reject(why, "returned save belongs to another operation");
    auto base_nodes = index_nodes(opsave::nodes(base)), guest_nodes = index_nodes(opsave::nodes(guest));
    unsigned base_orders = 0, guest_orders = 0;
    auto ord = Key("user_orders", 0);
    if (base_nodes.count(Key("user_orders", 1)) || guest_nodes.count(Key("user_orders", 1)))
        return reject(why, "ambiguous user_orders tables");
    if (base_nodes.count(ord)) base_orders = base_nodes[ord].rows;
    if (guest_nodes.count(ord)) guest_orders = guest_nodes[ord].rows;
    if (guest_orders < base_orders || (base_nodes.count(ord) && !guest_nodes.count(ord)))
        return reject(why, "host orders were removed");

    std::map<std::string, opsave::Tagged> tags;
    unsigned host_side = 0;
    for (const auto &t : opsave::tagged(base)) {
        if (t.name.empty() || !tags.emplace(t.name, t).second) return reject(why, "ambiguous tagged group");
        auto it = side.ai.find(t.name);
        if (it == side.ai.end()) {
            if (opsave::u32_at(base, t.ai)) return reject(why, "sidecar misses a tagged group");
            continue;
        }
        if (!t.side || !opsave::u32_at(base, t.side)) return reject(why, "tagged group lacks a side");
        if (it->second == opsave::k_user) {
            unsigned s = opsave::u32_at(base, t.side);
            if (host_side && host_side != s) return reject(why, "ambiguous host side");
            host_side = s;
        }
        if ((t.vis != 0) != (side.vis.count(t.name) != 0)) return reject(why, "sidecar visibility is incomplete");
    }
    if (!host_side) return reject(why, "host side missing from sidecar");
    for (const auto &it : side.ai) if (!tags.count(it.first)) return reject(why, "sidecar has an unknown group");

    for (const auto &item : base_nodes) {
        auto found = guest_nodes.find(item.first);
        if (found == guest_nodes.end()) return reject(why, "a node was removed: " + item.first.first);
        const NodeRef &a = item.second, &b = found->second;
        if (a.type != b.type || a.cols != b.cols) return reject(why, "node type or width changed: " + a.name);
        bool orders = item.first == ord;
        if (orders) {
            if (a.type != 2 || b.rows < a.rows) return reject(why, "invalid orders table");
        } else if (a.rows != b.rows) return reject(why, "unsupported structural change: " + a.name);
        if (a.type == 0) continue;
        auto ti = tags.find(a.name);
        bool host_group = a.type == 1 && ti != tags.end() && ti->second.side &&
                          opsave::u32_at(base, ti->second.side) == host_side;
        bool guest_group = a.type == 1 && ti != tags.end() && ti->second.side &&
                           opsave::u32_at(base, ti->second.side) != host_side;
        size_t cells = a.type == 1 ? a.rows : (size_t)a.rows * a.cols;
        size_t stride = a.type == 1 ? 0x40 : 0x20;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cells; ++i) {
            size_t ca = a.data + i * stride, cb = b.data + i * stride;
            if (opsave::u32_at(base, ca + 16) != opsave::u32_at(guest, cb + 16))
                return reject(why, "cell type changed: " + a.name);
            // Unknown global/table values cannot safely be attributed to the guest. Only guest
            // group fields and host visibility (which is restored from the sidecar) may differ.
            bool preserve = !guest_group;
            if (a.type == 1) {
                const char *fa = (const char *)base.data() + ca + 0x20;
                const char *fb = (const char *)guest.data() + cb + 0x20;
                if (memcmp(fa, fb, 0x20)) return reject(why, "struct fields changed: " + a.name);
                if (host_group && !strncmp(fa, "vis_mode", 0x20)) preserve = false;
                if (ti != tags.end() && (!strncmp(fa, "ai", 0x20) || !strncmp(fa, "side", 0x20))) preserve = true;
            }
            if (preserve && !same_cell(base, ca, guest, cb))
                return reject(why, orders ? "host order was changed" : "protected host/global value was changed: " + a.name);
        }
    }
    for (const auto &item : guest_nodes)
        if (!base_nodes.count(item.first) && !(item.first == ord && item.second.type == 2))
            return reject(why, "unsupported new node: " + item.first.first);

    std::vector<byte> result = guest;
    unsigned restored = 0;
    if (!opsave::unmirror(result, side, &restored) || !restored) return reject(why, "cannot restore host ownership");
    opsave::set_operation_name(result, name);
    if (!validate(result, why)) return false;
    if (added_orders) *added_orders = guest_orders - base_orders;
    out->swap(result);
    return true;
}
// After the engine has processed the joint turn, put the recorded ownership and the host's
// pre-handoff visibility back. Refuse unknown/ambiguous groups instead of inventing a tag.
// This conservative visibility is NOT proof of the game's fog-of-war semantics.
inline bool restore_after_turn(std::vector<byte> &save, const Sidecar &side, unsigned *changed, std::string *why = nullptr) {
    if (!validate(save, why)) return false;
    std::vector<byte> encoded;
    if (!encode_sidecar(side, &encoded)) return reject(why, "invalid sidecar after turn");
    std::set<std::string> seen;
    std::vector<byte> restored = save;
    unsigned n = 0;
    for (const auto &t : opsave::tagged(restored)) {
        if (t.name.empty() || !seen.insert(t.name).second) return reject(why, "ambiguous group after turn");
        auto it = side.ai.find(t.name);
        unsigned current = opsave::u32_at(restored, t.ai);
        if (it == side.ai.end()) {
            if (current) return reject(why, "unknown group after turn: " + t.name);
            continue;
        }
        if (!current || (t.vis != 0) != (side.vis.count(t.name) != 0))
            return reject(why, "changed group layout after turn: " + t.name);
        if (current != it->second) ++n;
        memcpy(restored.data() + t.ai, &it->second, 4);
        if (t.vis) { unsigned vis = side.vis.at(t.name); memcpy(restored.data() + t.vis, &vis, 4); }
    }
    for (const auto &it : side.ai) if (!seen.count(it.first)) return reject(why, "missing group after turn: " + it.first);
    if (changed) *changed = n;
    save.swap(restored);
    return true;
}
} // namespace opsave_checked
==================== END development/net-v21/src/opsave_checked.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/peer_guard.h (sha256 bdf5576e026317b64ba0c77c584d54f5c45f86109ea1fdf9fc9c455f0b0dec76) ====================
// Tactical host: don't let an unrelated HELLO reset a recently active opponent's battle.
// Keep the historic three-second reconnect window; operational-map behaviour is unchanged.
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
namespace peer_guard {
inline bool reject_hello(bool tactical, bool connected, bool from_peer, uint32_t now, uint32_t last_rx) {
    return tactical && connected && !from_peer && (uint32_t)(now - last_rx) < 3000u;
}
}

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/protocol.h (sha256 b07f632c28651cf0cd6c5ebe6f56e54f39217c52c6c227f09b1ff2ebf34dddd5) ====================
// Wire format shared by the game module (session.cpp, statesync.cpp, ui.cpp) and the join launcher
// (mp_join.cpp). UDP, little endian.
#pragma once
#include <stddef.h>
#include <string.h>

enum { PKT_HELLO = 1, PKT_WELCOME, PKT_KEEPALIVE, PKT_IMAGE_REQUEST, PKT_IMAGE_DATA, PKT_STATE, PKT_BYE, PKT_OBJECT, PKT_OBJECT_ACK, PKT_SHOT, PKT_SHOT_ACK, PKT_END, PKT_VITALS, PKT_VITALS_ACK };
enum { HOST_MODE_SKIRMISH = 1, HOST_MODE_OPERATION = 2, HOST_MODE_MISSION = 3 };       // mission: made in the battle editor
// Hello/Welcome Head.flags: a tactical peer advertises only hooks actually installed and ready.
// Absence means do not suppress the local simulation for that feature.
enum { PEER_HIT_SYNC = 1, PEER_ANIM_SYNC = 2, PEER_FEATURES = PEER_HIT_SYNC | PEER_ANIM_SYNC };
static const unsigned k_magic = 0x504d5447;          // "GTMP"
static const unsigned k_version = 17;      // 17: ordered events bind both tactical process epochs
static const char k_wire_tag[] = "NETV21_WIRE_PROTOCOL_17"; // embedded in both binaries and checked by the packager
static const char k_protocol[12] = "GTMF_TA_A1";     // the engine's own protocol id for this game
static const unsigned k_chunk = 1000;
static const unsigned short k_default_port = 1943;   // the port the engine's own network settings name
static const char k_image_path[] = "data\\common\\shared\\config\\tacgame_image.cfgpack";
static const unsigned k_side_germans = 0x00524547, k_side_soviets = 0x52535355;      // 'GER', 'USSR'

#pragma pack(push, 1)
struct Head { unsigned magic; unsigned char type, flags; unsigned short count; };
// prefer_side: the side code the joining player wants to command (0: whichever the host does not play)
// ready: the battle phase this player has asked to move on to (see StateHead); phase: where its game is
// lobby_ready: the joining player pressed READY in the lobby; swap_req: counts that player's SWAP SIDES presses
// name: the player's profile name
// phase: 0xff in the menus, 0xfe on the operational map with the map in hand, otherwise the battle phase.
// map_size/map_hash/turn_done are reserved and ignored: a spectator never uploads orders.
// map_epoch/map_revision identify the host snapshot loaded by the spectator.
// force_size / force_hash: this player's own force is offered - his battle description, from which the
// other game takes his squads and units (force.h). 0: nothing to offer.
struct Hello {
    Head h; char proto[12]; unsigned version, prefer_side; unsigned char ready, phase, lobby_ready, swap_req; char name[24];
    unsigned map_size, map_hash, turn_done, map_epoch;
    unsigned force_size, force_hash;
    unsigned map_revision; // exact snapshot loaded by the map process, including same-turn refreshes
    unsigned vitals_epoch; // tactical process identity; 0 outside a battle
};
// battle description = the file the operational phase hands to the tactical phase (units, map, sides, seed).
// in_battle 0: the host is still in its menus or on the operational map; the joining game waits.
// versus 1: the joining player commands the host's opponent; 0: both look at the battle from the host's side.
struct Welcome {
    Head h; char proto[12]; unsigned version, image_size, image_hash, user_side, enemy_side;
    unsigned char in_battle, host_mode, versus, swap;      // swap: the players have exchanged forces (skirmish)
    char name[24];                                          // the host's profile name
    // Operation: the unmodified checked host snapshot goes host -> spectator only.
    unsigned map_size, map_hash, map_turns;
    char map_file[96];
    // bits 2..4 host snapshot phase, bits 8..31 epoch; other bits reserved.
    unsigned map_flags;
    unsigned map_revision; // changes on each snapshot, even if its bytes have not changed
    unsigned map_ack_epoch, map_ack_revision; // reserved; always zero for spectator mode
    unsigned vitals_epoch; // tactical process identity; 0 outside a battle
};
// Head.flags: 0 = battle description, 1 = operation map, 2 = tactical force. Map fragments
// carry the host epoch/revision; other image transfers set both fields to zero.
struct ImageRequest { Head h; unsigned index, map_epoch, map_revision; };
struct ImageData { Head h; unsigned index, size, map_epoch, map_revision; unsigned char data[k_chunk]; };
// Unit transforms from the game that owns those units, plus (host only) the battle clock.
// speed: the rate the host's battle time actually advances at (its speed setting, less whatever the engine's
// automatic slow-down takes off); sim_ms: battle time so far.
// phase: 0 deployment, 1 initial orders, 2 battle, 3 battle over (the value of the engine's "gamemode" broadcast)
// ready: deployment and initial orders end only when both players have pressed the game's Start button;
// 1 = this player has finished deployment, 2 = has finished the initial orders as well.
struct StateHead { Head h; unsigned snapshot, sim_ms; float speed, cur_speed; unsigned char paused, phase, ready, pad; };
struct UnitRec { unsigned id; unsigned char kind, pad[3]; float pos[3], rot[9], lin[3], ang[3]; };
// One datagram of an object's saved state: a run-length coded XOR against the version the receiver holds.
struct ObjectFrag { Head h; unsigned long long key; unsigned version, base_version, raw_size, delta_size; unsigned short frag, nfrags; };
struct ObjectAck { Head h; unsigned long long key; unsigned version; };
// Shell launches by units the sending game owns (shots.cpp). They are events, not state: each one has to
// arrive, once, and in the order it happened. A PKT_SHOT datagram is a ShotHead and h.count ShotRecs with
// consecutive sequence numbers; the receiver answers every datagram with the last contiguous record handed
// to its feature. A shot is acknowledged only after replay or an explicit validation refusal; a control
// event is acknowledged after it is handed to its handler. The sender repeats what is not acknowledged.
// epoch changes when the sender starts counting again (a new battle, a new connection).
// owner/target are the process epochs negotiated in Hello/Welcome. They prevent an old event
// or ACK addressed to a different tactical process from entering the current ordered stream.
struct ShotHead { Head h; unsigned epoch, owner, target; };
struct ShotAck { Head h; unsigned epoch, seq, owner, target; };
// kind 0: one launch; kind 2: one squad digging in (trench.cpp; its values are in `launch`); kind 3, 4: a
// piece of the sender's table of description / object numbers (xlate.cpp). launch: the engine's launch record with its pointers blanked - they travel as ammo / weapon /
// desc3 (the engine's savegame ids of the descriptions, 0xffffffff: none) and shooter / obj2 / obj5 (unit
// numbers, 0: none). ammo_check / ammo_code: what the receiver compares its own description against.
struct ShotRec {
    unsigned seq;
    unsigned char kind, flags, has_pos, pad;
    unsigned ammo, weapon, desc3, arg6, ammo_code;
    float ammo_check[2];
    unsigned long long shooter, obj2, obj5;
    float pos[4];
    unsigned char launch[0x90];
};
// M3: proposal, actual end, host restart, and (optionally) 16-bit packet ordering in Head.count.
// Older protocol-13 senders leave bit 3 clear and count unused. The result is the sender's outcome
// when END_OVER is set; periodic state is repeated, but an extended partition can still delay it.
enum { END_PROPOSE = 1, END_OVER = 2, END_RESTART = 4, END_SEQUENCED = 8 };
struct EndState { Head h; unsigned sim_ms, result; };
#pragma pack(pop)

// A joiner must not accept a host's battle or events merely because its packet has our magic.
// The tactical process can join directly, bypassing the menu's Joiner, so both paths use this.
inline bool valid_welcome(const void *data, size_t size)
{
    if (!data || size < sizeof(Welcome)) return false;
    Welcome w;
    memcpy(&w, data, sizeof w);
    return w.h.magic == k_magic && w.h.type == PKT_WELCOME && w.version == k_version &&
           memcmp(w.proto, k_protocol, sizeof k_protocol) == 0;
}

inline unsigned fnv(const unsigned char *p, size_t n)
{
    unsigned h = 2166136261u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) h = (h ^ p[i]) * 16777619u;
    return h;
}

inline Head make_head(int type, int count = 0)
{
    Head h = { k_magic, (unsigned char)type, 0, (unsigned short)count };
    return h;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/protocol.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/reliable_inbox.h (sha256 7b45720afbc427c3dedfc3c0db737ed852b117772e9b4768c020d643e03fb98b) ====================
// Ordered, deduplicated receive queue for a datagram event stream.
//
// Receipt and completion are deliberately separate: the acknowledgement is the highest contiguous
// sequence actually handed to its feature, not the highest datagram merely copied into memory.
// The owner calls this on its session/simulation thread; it is not a multi-producer queue.
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <deque>

namespace reliable {
template <class Record, size_t Capacity = 1024>
class Inbox {
public:
    struct Item { Record record; bool complete; };
    enum Result { accepted, duplicate, gap, full, invalid };

    Inbox() : epoch_(0), received_(0), committed_(0), retired_count_(0) {}

    // A fresh epoch must start at sequence 1. Previously retired epochs cannot roll a stream back
    // when an old datagram arrives late. Call reset() when the enclosing connection is replaced.
    bool epoch(uint32_t value, uint32_t first_sequence) {
        if (!value) return false;
        if (value == epoch_) return true;
        if (first_sequence != 1) return false;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < retired_count_; ++i) if (retired_[i] == value) return false;
        if (epoch_) {
            if (retired_count_ < k_retired) retired_[retired_count_++] = epoch_;
            else {
                for (size_t i = 1; i < k_retired; ++i) retired_[i - 1] = retired_[i];
                retired_[k_retired - 1] = epoch_;
            }
        }
        items_.clear();
        epoch_ = value;
        received_ = committed_ = 0;
        return true;
    }

    Result receive(const Record &record) {
        if (!epoch_ || !record.seq) return invalid;
        if (record.seq <= received_) return duplicate;
        // The sender retransmits gaps; never ACK an event we did not receive.
        if (received_ == UINT32_MAX || record.seq != received_ + 1) return gap;
        if (items_.size() >= Capacity) return full;
        items_.push_back({record, false});
        received_ = record.seq;
        return accepted;
    }

    // Consumers can finish independent control records ahead of a temporarily blocked event; the
    // cumulative ACK still cannot move past that event until it too is complete.
    std::deque<Item> &items() { return items_; }
    uint32_t commit() {
        while (!items_.empty() && items_.front().complete) {
            committed_ = items_.front().record.seq;
            items_.pop_front();
        }
        return committed_;
    }
    void reset() {
        items_.clear();
        epoch_ = received_ = committed_ = 0;
        retired_count_ = 0;
    }
    uint32_t current_epoch() const { return epoch_; }
    uint32_t received() const { return received_; }
    uint32_t committed() const { return committed_; }
    size_t pending() const { return items_.size(); }
    bool empty() const { return items_.empty(); }

private:
    static const size_t k_retired = 8;
    std::deque<Item> items_;
    uint32_t epoch_, received_, committed_, retired_[k_retired];
    size_t retired_count_;
};
} // namespace reliable
==================== END development/net-v21/src/reliable_inbox.h ====================
