                    mp_log("ready: the other player has finished %s", g_peer_ready >= 2 ? "the initial orders" : g_peer_ready ? "deployment" : "nothing yet");
                }
                g_have_host_clock = true;
            } else {
                g_peer_sim_ms = sh->sim_ms;
                g_peer_phase = sh->phase;
                g_peer_clock_at = now ? now : 1;
            }
            const UnitRec *recs = (const UnitRec *)(sh + 1);
            queue_transforms_for_mode(recs, sh->h.count);
            g_stat_packets++;
        }
        break;
    case PKT_OBJECT:
    case PKT_OBJECT_ACK:
        state_packet_for_mode(data, size, send_peer, g_versus);
        break;
    case PKT_SHOT:
    case PKT_SHOT_ACK:
        shot_packet_for_mode(data, size, send_peer);
        break;
    case PKT_VITALS:
    case PKT_VITALS_ACK:
        if (g_tactical && g_connected) vitals_on_packet(data, size, send_peer);
        break;
    case PKT_END:
        end_on_packet(data, size, now);
        break;
    case PKT_BYE:
        mp_log("net: the other player's game closed");
        g_connected = false;
        if (g_tactical) vitals_pause();
        if (g_role == ROLE_JOIN && g_tactical && !g_host_lost) g_host_lost = now ? now : 1;
        if (g_role == ROLE_HOST) { g_have_peer = false; g_peer_on_map = false; }
        break;
    }
}

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
// The no-game fixture substitutes save/load only; packet/transport and identity checks are real.
bool session_test_map_seed(const std::vector<unsigned char> &source, bool refresh)
{
    if (g_role != ROLE_HOST || !spectator_snapshot::publishable(source, refresh, g_map_turns, g_map_out)) return false;
    std::vector<unsigned char> save = source;
    return adopt_host_snapshot(save, "camp_test_rus.opsave", refresh);
}
static unsigned g_test_turn_calls;
static void test_stock_next(void *, u64, const char *) { ++g_test_turn_calls; }
bool session_test_map_control() {
    NextTurn old = g_next_turn.load(); bool block = g_block_guest_turn.load();
    alignas(4) char event[16] = {}; *(float *)(event + 8) = -1.0f;
    g_next_turn = test_stock_next; g_test_turn_calls = 0;
    g_block_guest_turn.store(true); next_turn_hook(nullptr, 0, event);
    bool spectator_blocked = g_test_turn_calls == 0 && !session_map_blocking();
    g_block_guest_turn.store(false); next_turn_hook(nullptr, 0, event);
    bool host_passed = g_test_turn_calls == 1 && !session_map_blocking();
    g_next_turn.store(old); g_block_guest_turn.store(block);
    return spectator_blocked && host_passed;
}
u32 session_test_map_out_hash() { return g_map_out_hash; }
bool session_test_map_no_guest_upload() { return g_map_in.empty(); }
bool session_test_map_peer_loaded() { return g_peer_on_map; }
bool session_test_map_input() { return !session_map_blocking(); }
bool session_test_map_download_ready() { return g_map_reload_at != 0 && !g_map_in.empty() && g_map_got == g_map_have.size(); }
bool session_test_map_adopt_download(std::vector<unsigned char> *out) {
    if (!out || !session_test_map_download_ready() || !opsave_checked::validate(g_map_in) ||
        !g_map_client.loaded(g_map_in_epoch, g_map_in_revision)) return false;
    *out = g_map_in;
    g_map_base_epoch = g_map_in_epoch; g_map_base_revision = g_map_in_revision;
    g_map_in.clear(); g_map_reload_at = 0; g_map_note[0] = 0; g_last_hello = 0;
    return true;
}
void session_test_map_disconnect() {
    g_connected = false;
    if (g_role == ROLE_HOST) g_have_peer = g_peer_on_map = false;
}
void session_test_map_forbidden_packets() {
    StateHead state = {}; state.h = make_head(PKT_STATE);
    on_packet(g_peer, (const unsigned char *)&state, sizeof state, GetTickCount());
    Head obj = make_head(PKT_OBJECT), shot = make_head(PKT_SHOT);
    on_packet(g_peer, (const unsigned char *)&obj, sizeof obj, GetTickCount());
    on_packet(g_peer, (const unsigned char *)&shot, sizeof shot, GetTickCount());
}
void session_test_map_old_hello() {
    if (g_role != ROLE_HOST || !g_have_peer || g_map_revision < 2) return;
    Hello old = { make_head(PKT_HELLO) };
    memcpy(old.proto, k_protocol, sizeof k_protocol); old.version = k_version;
    old.phase = 0xfe; old.lobby_ready = 1; old.turn_done = 1;
    old.map_size = 1000; old.map_hash = 0x1234;
    old.map_epoch = g_map_epoch; old.map_revision = g_map_revision - 1;
    on_packet(g_peer, (const unsigned char *)&old, sizeof old, GetTickCount());
}
#endif

static void spectator_screen_lifecycle(ObjMan *om, DWORD now)
{
    if (g_map_mode) {
        bool on_map = map_ready_for_hello(om);
        if (on_map) { g_guest_seen_map = true; g_guest_left_since = 0; }
        else if (g_guest_seen_map && !g_map_battle_at && !g_map_reload_at) {
            if (!g_guest_left_since) g_guest_left_since = now ? now : 1;
            else if (now - g_guest_left_since >= 2000) {
                mp_log("map: spectator left the operation; stopping updates and returning control to the game");
                session_single_player();
                g_map_mode = false; g_map_in.clear(); g_map_have.clear();
                SetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_MAP", nullptr); SetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_AUTOJOIN", nullptr);
                SetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_MAP_EPOCH", nullptr); SetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_MAP_REV", nullptr);
            }
        }
    }
}

void session_frame(ObjMan *om)
{
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    g_prof_frames++;
    // Publish battle state at most once a second rather than taking another log lock every frame.
    static DWORD last_log_state;
    if (g_tactical && (!last_log_state || now - last_log_state >= 1000)) {
        last_log_state = now ? now : 1;
        netlog_state((int)g_phase, (unsigned)g_sim_ms, g_connected ? 1 : 0, g_user_side);
    }
    netlog_frame();
    steam_tick();
    // Retry a failed advertisement and recover if the game's other process briefly cleared the
    // shared Steam presence during a menu/battle transition. Do not stop the IP listener.
    static DWORD last_presence;
    if (g_role == ROLE_HOST && steam_ready() && (!last_presence || now - last_presence >= 5000)) {
        last_presence = now ? now : 1;
        steam_broadcast(true, 0);
    }
    update_ai_commander();
    if (g_tactical && g_role != ROLE_NONE) { render_guard_install(); render_guard_report(now); }
    if (g_tactical && (g_role != ROLE_NONE || GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_LOG_SHOTS", 0, 0))) shots_install(om);
    if (g_tactical && g_role != ROLE_NONE) { trench_install(); hits_install(om); anim_install(om); }
    shorten_battle_for_tests(om);
    if (g_role == ROLE_HOST && g_bind_pending && now - g_last_bind_try >= 500) host_listen();
    // Map input safety and leaving its screen must not depend on UDP or Steam being up.
    install_map_detours();
    spectator_screen_lifecycle(om, now);
    // The menu Joiner and this session share one Steam P2P queue. While the Joiner has reserved
    // it, only the Joiner may read its WELCOME/IMAGE_DATA; otherwise this earlier frame would eat
    // and discard those packets before ui_frame calls Joiner::poll(). UDP keeps its own socket.
    if (steam_ready() && !steam_lobby_receiving()) {
        uint64_t from_id = 0;
        for (int guard = 0; guard < 64; guard++) {
            unsigned char steam_buf[2048];
            int n = steam_recv(&from_id, steam_buf, sizeof steam_buf);
            if (n <= 0) break;
            g_stat_rx_bytes += n;
            sockaddr_in from = {};
            steam_peer_make(from_id, &from);
            on_packet(from, steam_buf, n, now);
        }
    }
    if (!transport_policy::drive_session(g_tactical, g_sock != INVALID_SOCKET, steam_ready())) return;

    if (g_sock != INVALID_SOCKET) {
        unsigned char buf[2048];
        for (int i = 0; i < 2048; i++) {
            sockaddr_in from;
            int flen = sizeof from;
            int n = recvfrom(g_sock, (char *)buf, sizeof buf, 0, (sockaddr *)&from, &flen);
            if (n <= 0) break;
            g_stat_rx_bytes += n;
            on_packet(from, buf, n, now);
            if (g_sock == INVALID_SOCKET) return;
        }
    }

    install_detours();
    end_keys();
    end_frame(om, now);
    force_frame(om, now);
    resign_if_asked(om);
    host_map_frame(om, now);
    if (g_map_mode) request_map_pieces(now);
    if (g_map_mode && g_test_save_asked && operation_on_screen(om)) {      // tests: how many orders has this player given?
        g_test_save_asked = false;
        std::vector<unsigned char> save;
        char file[96] = "";
        bool ok = save_operation(om, save, file, sizeof file);
        mp_log("test: operation %s (%s, %zu bytes, turn phase %d, %u orders)", ok ? "saved" : "not saved", file, save.size(),
               ok ? opsave::turn_phase(save) : -1, ok ? opsave::rows_of(save, "user_orders") : 0);
    }
    if (g_map_mode && g_end_turn_asked) {
        g_end_turn_asked = false;
        mp_log("test: spectator cannot advance the host's turn");
    }
    if (g_map_mode && g_map_battle_at && now >= g_map_battle_at) {
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
        if (g_test_map_no_game && !g_test_spawn_battle_lobby) { g_test_battle_notice = true; g_map_battle_at = 0; }
        else
#endif
        joiner_into_battle();
    }
    if (g_map_mode && g_map_reload_at && now >= g_map_reload_at) {
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
        if (!g_test_map_no_game)
#endif
        joiner_reload_map();
    }
    replace_timer_setters(om);
    if (g_connected && g_tactical) advance_phase(om, now);
    if (g_role == ROLE_JOIN) {
        if (now - g_last_hello > (g_connected ? 1000u : 500u)) {
            g_last_hello = now;
            // the hello doubles as keep-alive and carries the side wish, so a restarted host learns it again
            Hello hello = { make_head(PKT_HELLO) };
            if (g_tactical && hits_available()) hello.h.flags |= PEER_HIT_SYNC;
            if (g_tactical && anim_available()) hello.h.flags |= PEER_ANIM_SYNC;
            memcpy(hello.proto, k_protocol, sizeof k_protocol);
            hello.version = k_version;
            hello.prefer_side = g_prefer_side;
            hello.ready = (unsigned char)g_my_ready;
            hello.phase = g_map_mode ? (map_ready_for_hello(om) ? 0xfe : 0xff) : (unsigned char)g_phase;
            hello.lobby_ready = 1;
            strcpy_s(hello.name, g_my_name);
            hello.map_epoch = g_map_base_epoch;
            hello.map_revision = g_map_base_revision;
            hello.vitals_epoch = g_tactical ? vitals_epoch() : 0;
            force_offer_to_hello(&hello);
            send_to(g_peer, &hello, sizeof hello);
        }
    }
    if (g_connected && g_tactical) {
        if (now - g_last_snapshot >= 66) {
            g_last_snapshot = now;
            send_transforms(om, now);
        }
        double t0 = seconds_now();
        if (sync_state_enabled()) statesync_owner_frame(om, now, owns_object, send_peer);
        g_prof_save += seconds_now() - t0;
        xlate_frame(om);
        anim_frame(om, now);
        shots_frame(send_peer);
        vitals_frame(om, now, send_peer);
        refresh_event_epochs(); // vitals_frame may discover a new local object manager
    }

    // The other game is quiet while it loads a battle (its menu closes and the tactical game takes over),
    // and eight seconds of that is not a lost connection.  Before the battle the wait is 20 s, in the
    // battle itself 8 s.
    DWORD patience = (g_tactical && g_phase >= 2) ? 8000u : 20000u;
    if (g_connected && now - g_last_rx > patience) {
        mp_log("net: connection lost (nothing received for %u s)", (unsigned)(patience / 1000));
        g_connected = false;
        if (g_tactical) { vitals_pause(); hits_reset_peer(); anim_reset_peer(); }
        g_peer_ready = 0;
        if (g_role == ROLE_JOIN && g_tactical && !g_host_lost) g_host_lost = now ? now : 1;
        if (g_role == ROLE_HOST) { g_have_peer = false; g_peer_on_map = false; }
    }
    if (now - g_last_report >= 5000) {
        if (g_connected && g_tactical && (g_stat_sent || g_stat_applied || g_stat_zero_skipped || g_stat_cache_pruned)) {
            mp_log("net: 5 s: sent %u and applied %u unit transforms (%u coalesced, %u identical writes elided, %u invalid/full), mean correction %.2f m, largest %.2f m, %u jumps, rx %.1f / tx %.1f KB/s; zero-ms %u (%u idle skipped), cache pruned %u",
                   g_stat_sent, g_stat_applied, g_stat_coalesced, g_stat_elided, g_stat_bad_transforms,
                   g_stat_applied ? g_stat_err_sum / g_stat_applied : 0.0, g_stat_err_max, g_stat_snaps,
                   g_stat_rx_bytes / 5120.0, g_stat_tx_bytes / 5120.0, g_stat_zero_steps, g_stat_zero_skipped, g_stat_cache_pruned);
            statesync_report();
            shots_report();
            hits_report();
            anim_report();
            trench_report();
            if (g_role == ROLE_JOIN && g_have_host_clock)
                mp_log("clock: battle time here %.1f s, on the host %.1f s (time scaled by %.2f to stay on it)",
                       g_sim_ms / 1000.0, g_host_sim_ms / 1000.0, g_clock_factor);
            else if (g_role == ROLE_HOST)
                mp_log("clock: battle time %.1f s, in the other game %.1f s (time scaled by %.2f to wait for it)",
                       g_sim_ms / 1000.0, g_peer_sim_ms / 1000.0, g_clock_factor);
        }
        if (g_tactical && g_role != ROLE_NONE)
            mp_log("load: 5 s: %u network ticks (not FPS), %u simulation steps covering %u ms, %.0f ms saving state, %.0f ms loading state, peer %s",
                   g_prof_frames, g_prof_steps, g_prof_step_ms, g_prof_save * 1000, g_prof_load * 1000,
                   g_connected ? "connected" : "absent");
        g_prof_frames = g_prof_steps = g_prof_step_ms = 0;
        g_prof_save = g_prof_load = 0;
        g_last_report = now;
        g_stat_sent = g_stat_applied = g_stat_elided = g_stat_coalesced = g_stat_bad_transforms = 0;
        g_stat_zero_steps = g_stat_zero_skipped = g_stat_cache_pruned = 0;
        g_stat_packets = g_stat_rx_bytes = g_stat_tx_bytes = g_stat_snaps = 0;
        g_stat_err_sum = 0;
        g_stat_err_max = 0;
    }
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/session.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/shots.cpp (sha256 7345597d535340f3e27664749dcc659a0376ddc043d8738a424364f1c4b18f08) ====================
// Shots: every shell, bullet, grenade and flare in a battle is created by one engine function, the
// "launch" of the shell manager (i_shellphys, virtual slot 3, basic_physic+0x18430). It makes the
// projectile, its trail and tracer, the muzzle flash, the sound of the shot and the report the computer
// opponents hear. A unit's own update calls it when the unit fires.
//
// Each game simulates every unit, including the ones the other player commands, so left alone each game
// fires those units' weapons by itself: at slightly different moments, in slightly different directions,
// and - when a newly loaded state says the weapon has already fired - not at all. Those were the shots
// that looked different on the two PCs or were missing on one of them.
//
// With another player connected the launch is therefore the owner's alone:
//   - a launch by a unit this game owns happens as usual and is sent to the other game;
//   - a launch this game's own simulation makes for a unit the other game owns is dropped;
//   - a launch received from the other game is carried out here with exactly the owner's values.
// The launch record holds pointers to the ammunition and weapon descriptions and to the firing unit. They
// travel as the ids the engine's own savegame code uses for them (object manager, virtual slots 56/57) and
// as unit numbers, and are checked against the local data before use.
//
// MP_SYNC_SHOTS=0 switches all of this off; MP_LOG_SHOTS=1 writes every launch to out\mp_shots_<pid>.txt.
#include "common.h"
#include "env_value.h"
#include "netlog.h"
#include "protocol.h"
#include "vitals.h"
#include "reliable_inbox.h"
#include "hooks.h"
#include "detour.h"
#include <intrin.h>
#include <share.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <deque>
#include <vector>

typedef void (*Launch)(char *sp, char *rec, u64 flags, float *pos, void *obj5, u32 arg6);
typedef void (*Explode)(char *sp, char *rec);
static Launch g_launch;
static Explode g_explode;
static ObjMan *g_om;
static char *g_shellphys;
static DWORD g_main_thread;
static bool g_injecting, g_install_done, g_enabled = true;
static FILE *g_file;
static u32 g_seq, g_epoch, g_dumped;
static u64 g_stat_own, g_stat_dropped, g_stat_injected, g_stat_refused, g_stat_free, g_stat_sent_bytes, g_stat_repeats;
struct Pending { ShotRec rec; DWORD sent; unsigned char tries; };
static std::deque<Pending> g_out;               // launched here, not yet acknowledged by the other game
static reliable::Inbox<ShotRec> g_in;           // receipt is separate from completion / acknowledgement
// Non-tactical processes never run shots_sim_step, but preserve their old receive/ACK semantics.
static std::vector<ShotRec> g_in_legacy;
static u32 g_epoch_in_legacy, g_last_seq_in_legacy;
static void (*g_ack_send)(const void *, int);

// launch record: pointers at +0x38 (ammunition), +0x40 (weapon), +0x48 (firing object), +0x50 (object),
// +0x60 (description)
enum { L_AMMO = 0x38, L_WEAPON = 0x40, L_SHOOTER = 0x48, L_OBJ2 = 0x50, L_DESC3 = 0x60, L_SIZE = 0x90 };

static u32 desc_id(void *p) { return p ? ((u32 (*)(ObjMan *, void *))g_om->vtbl[56])(g_om, p) : 0xffffffffu; }
static void *desc_ptr(u32 id) { return id == 0xffffffffu ? 0 : ((void *(*)(ObjMan *, u32))g_om->vtbl[57])(g_om, id); }

static bool is_object(void *p)
{
    if (!readable(p, sizeof(Object))) return false;
    Object *o = (Object *)p;
    return o->objman == g_om && readable(o->iface, 4);
}

static u64 object_key(void *p)
{
    u64 key = 0;
    if (p && is_object(p)) session_unit_key((Object *)p, &key);
    return key;
}

// what identifies an ammunition description besides its id: its first two numbers and its type code
static void ammo_check(void *ammo, float *two, u32 *code)
{
    two[0] = two[1] = 0;
    *code = 0;
    if (!readable(ammo, 0xc8)) return;
    memcpy(two, ammo, 8);
    memcpy(code, (char *)ammo + 0xc4, 4);
}

static const char *name_of(void *p)
{
    return !p ? "-" : is_object(p) ? ((Object *)p)->full : "?";
}

static void log_launch(const char *who, char kind, const char *rec, u64 flags, void *obj5, void *caller)
{
    if (!g_file) return;
    const float *f = (const float *)rec;
    void *ammo = *(void **)(rec + L_AMMO), *weapon = *(void **)(rec + L_WEAPON), *shooter = *(void **)(rec + L_SHOOTER);
    HMODULE m = 0;
    char mod[MAX_PATH] = "?";
    if (GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT, (LPCSTR)caller, &m) && m)
        GetModuleFileNameA(m, mod, MAX_PATH);
    const char *base = strrchr(mod, '\\');
    u32 aflags = readable(ammo, 0xc8) ? *(u32 *)((char *)ammo + 0xc0) : 0, acode = readable(ammo, 0xc8) ? *(u32 *)((char *)ammo + 0xc4) : 0;
    fprintf(g_file, "%c %llu %s %s ammo=%u weapon=%u aflags=%08x acode=%08x flags=%llx pos=%.2f,%.2f,%.2f dir=%.4f,%.4f,%.4f speed=%.1f n=%u obj2=%s obj5=%s from=%s+0x%llx\n",
            kind, session_battle_ms(), who, name_of(shooter), desc_id(ammo), desc_id(weapon), aflags, acode, flags,
            f[0], f[1], f[2], f[4], f[5], f[6], kind == 'L' ? f[0xc] : 0.0f, kind == 'L' ? *(u32 *)(rec + 0x70) : 0,
            kind == 'L' ? name_of(*(void **)(rec + L_OBJ2)) : "-", name_of(obj5), base ? base + 1 : mod, m ? (u64)((char *)caller - (char *)m) : 0);
    fflush(g_file);
    if (g_dumped < 6 && kind == 'L') {
        g_dumped++;
        char line[3 * L_SIZE + 8];
        size_t w = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < L_SIZE; i++) w += sprintf_s(line + w, sizeof line - w, "%02x%s", (unsigned char)rec[i], (i & 7) == 7 ? " " : "");
        mp_log("shots: launch record of %s: %s", name_of(shooter), line);
    }
}

static bool replicating(void)
{
    return g_enabled && session_connected() && session_role() != ROLE_NONE;
}

// Whose launch is it? A unit's launch belongs to the game that owns the unit; anything else (a launch with
// no firing unit behind it) to the host.
static bool mine(void *shooter)
{
    if (shooter && is_object(shooter)) return session_owns((Object *)shooter);
    return session_role() == ROLE_HOST;
}

static void queue_shot(char kind, const char *rec, u64 flags, const float *pos, void *obj5, u32 arg6)
{
    ShotRec s = {};
    if (!g_epoch) g_epoch = (GetTickCount() ^ (GetCurrentProcessId() << 16)) | 1;
    s.seq = ++g_seq;
    s.kind = kind == 'L' ? 0 : 1;
    s.flags = (unsigned char)flags;
    s.has_pos = pos != 0;
    void *ammo = *(void **)(rec + L_AMMO);
    s.ammo = desc_id(ammo);
    s.weapon = desc_id(*(void **)(rec + L_WEAPON));
    ammo_check(ammo, s.ammo_check, &s.ammo_code);
    if (kind == 'L') {
        s.desc3 = desc_id(*(void **)(rec + L_DESC3));
        s.shooter = object_key(*(void **)(rec + L_SHOOTER));
        s.obj2 = object_key(*(void **)(rec + L_OBJ2));
        s.obj5 = object_key(obj5);
        s.arg6 = arg6;
        if (pos) memcpy(s.pos, pos, 16);
        memcpy(s.launch, rec, L_SIZE);
        memset(s.launch + L_AMMO, 0, 0x20);         // the four pointers
        memset(s.launch + L_DESC3, 0, 8);
    } else {
        s.desc3 = 0xffffffffu;
        memcpy(s.launch, rec, 0x40);                // an explosion is a place and the two descriptions
    }
    g_out.push_back({ s, 0, 0 });
}

// Another kind of record for the same channel (trench.cpp).
void shots_queue(ShotRec &s)
{
    if (!g_epoch) g_epoch = (GetTickCount() ^ (GetCurrentProcessId() << 16)) | 1;
    s.seq = ++g_seq;
    g_out.push_back({ s, 0, 0 });
}

// Do not let a new producer add unbounded events to the legacy queue. A refused admission
// must not consume a sequence number or create a gap in the other producer's stream.
bool shots_try_queue(ShotRec &s, size_t limit)
{
    if (!limit || g_out.size() >= limit || g_seq == 0xffffffffu) return false;
    shots_queue(s);
    return true;
}

bool shots_queue_latest(ShotRec &s, size_t limit)
{
    if (s.kind != 8 || !s.shooter || !limit) return false;
    // Never mutate a record already sent, and never move a post-hit snapshot across a hit or a
    // launch. That preserves causal order while coalescing an unsent suffix of snapshot records.
    for (auto it = g_out.rbegin(); it != g_out.rend(); ++it) {
        if (it->sent || it->rec.kind != s.kind) break;
        if (it->rec.shooter == s.shooter) {
            s.seq = it->rec.seq;
            it->rec = s;
            return true;
        }
    }
    return shots_try_queue(s, limit);
}

static void launch_hook(char *sp, char *rec, u64 flags, float *pos, void *obj5, u32 arg6)
{
    // bit 1: asked for from another thread; the shell manager queues it and launches it later through
    // this same function, on the simulation's thread
    if ((flags & 2) || g_injecting || GetCurrentThreadId() != g_main_thread || !readable(rec, L_SIZE)) {
        g_launch(sp, rec, flags, pos, obj5, arg6);
        return;
    }
    g_shellphys = sp;
    void *shooter = *(void **)(rec + L_SHOOTER);
    bool repl = replicating(), own = !repl || mine(shooter);
    log_launch(!repl ? "free" : own ? "own" : "dropped", 'L', rec, flags, obj5, _ReturnAddress());
    if (!repl) { g_stat_free++; g_launch(sp, rec, flags, pos, obj5, arg6); return; }
    if (!own) { g_stat_dropped++; return; }
    g_stat_own++;
    g_launch(sp, rec, flags, pos, obj5, arg6);
    if (*(void **)(rec + L_AMMO) && *(void **)(rec + L_WEAPON)) queue_shot('L', rec, flags, pos, obj5, arg6);
}

// Virtual slot 4 (basic_physic+0x19260): an ammunition going off at a place, without a flight - only
// watched for now (MP_LOG_SHOTS), to learn what uses it.
static void explode_hook(char *sp, char *rec)
{
    if (g_file && GetCurrentThreadId() == g_main_thread && readable(rec, 0x50)) {
        char fake[L_SIZE] = {};
        memcpy(fake, rec, 0x30);
        memcpy(fake + L_AMMO, rec + 0x48, 8);
        memcpy(fake + L_WEAPON, rec + 0x40, 8);
        log_launch("explosion", 'X', fake, 0, 0, _ReturnAddress());
    }
    g_explode(sp, rec);
}

void shots_install(ObjMan *om)
{
    static const unsigned char launch_sig[] = { 0x48, 0x89, 0x54, 0x24, 0x10, 0x55, 0x53, 0x56, 0x57, 0x41, 0x54, 0x41, 0x56,
                                                0x48, 0x8d, 0xac, 0x24, 0xe8, 0xfd, 0xff, 0xff };
    static const unsigned char explode_sig[] = { 0x48, 0x89, 0x5c, 0x24, 0x08, 0x57, 0x48, 0x81, 0xec, 0x70, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x48, 0x8b, 0xf9 };
    if (g_install_done) return;
    HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleA("basic_physic.codelib");
    if (!m) return;
    g_install_done = true;
    g_om = om;
    g_main_thread = GetCurrentThreadId();
    int value;
    if (env_integer("MP_SYNC_SHOTS", &value)) g_enabled = value != 0;
    if (env_integer("MP_LOG_SHOTS", &value) && value) {
        char path[MAX_PATH];
        sprintf_s(path, "%s\\mp_shots_%lu.txt", g_out_dir, GetCurrentProcessId());
        g_file = _fsopen(path, "w", _SH_DENYNO);
    }
    if (!g_enabled && !g_file) { mp_log("shots: each game fires for itself (MP_SYNC_SHOTS=0)"); return; }
    const HookSig launch_where[] = { { 0x18430, launch_sig, sizeof launch_sig } };
    const HookSig explode_where[] = { { 0x19260, explode_sig, sizeof explode_sig } };
    char *launch = hook_resolve_checked("basic_physic.codelib", launch_where, 1, "shell launch");
    if (!launch) { mp_log("shots: launch hook refused, each game fires for itself"); g_enabled = false; return; }
    g_launch = (Launch)install_detour(launch, sizeof launch_sig, launch_hook);
    if (g_file) {
        char *explode = hook_resolve_checked("basic_physic.codelib", explode_where, 1, "shell explosion log");
        if (explode) g_explode = (Explode)install_detour(explode, sizeof explode_sig, explode_hook);
    }
    mp_log("shots: %s%s", !g_launch ? "could not be hooked" : g_enabled ? "a unit's shots are its owner's: sent to the other game, not made up locally" : "watched only",
           g_file ? ", every launch is written to mp_shots" : "");
    if (!g_launch) g_enabled = false;
}

// Every frame: what the other game has not acknowledged goes out - new launches at once, older ones again
// after a pause. Nothing is given up: a launch stays in the queue until the other game has it.
void shots_frame(void (*send)(const void *, int))
{
    if (g_out.empty()) return;
    if (!(session_connected() && session_role() != ROLE_NONE)) { g_out.clear(); return; }
    const u32 owner = vitals_epoch(), target = vitals_peer_epoch();
    if (!owner || !target) return; // wait for both tactical process identities
    const size_t per = 5, window = 60;
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    size_t n = g_out.size() < window ? g_out.size() : window, at = 0;
    while (at < n) {
        // a datagram starts at the first launch that is due and carries the ones after it; a launch the
        // other game keeps missing is repeated with a growing pause instead of six times a second
        if (g_out[at].sent) {
            const Pending &p = g_out[at];
            DWORD wait = 150u << (p.tries < 4 ? p.tries : 4);        // 150, 300, 600, 1200, 1200 ms
            if (now - p.sent < wait) { at++; continue; }
        }
        unsigned char packet[sizeof(ShotHead) + per * sizeof(ShotRec)];
        size_t k = n - at < per ? n - at : per;
        ShotHead h = { make_head(PKT_SHOT, (int)k), g_epoch, owner, target };
        memcpy(packet, &h, sizeof h);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < k; i++) {
            memcpy(packet + sizeof h + i * sizeof(ShotRec), &g_out[at + i].rec, sizeof(ShotRec));
            if (g_out[at + i].sent) g_stat_repeats++;
            if (g_out[at + i].tries < 4) g_out[at + i].tries++;
            g_out[at + i].sent = now ? now : 1;
        }
        send(packet, (int)(sizeof h + k * sizeof(ShotRec)));
        g_stat_sent_bytes += sizeof h + k * sizeof(ShotRec);
        netlog_count("shots_sent", (long long)k);
        at += k;
    }
}

static void shots_ack(void (*send)(const void *, int))
{
    if (!send || !g_in.current_epoch() || !vitals_epoch() || !vitals_peer_epoch()) return;
    ShotAck ack = { make_head(PKT_SHOT_ACK), g_in.current_epoch(), g_in.committed(), vitals_peer_epoch(), vitals_epoch() };
    send(&ack, sizeof ack);
}

void shots_on_packet(const unsigned char *data, int size, void (*send)(const void *, int))
{
    if (!data || size < (int)sizeof(Head)) return;
    const Head *head = (const Head *)data;
    if (head->type == PKT_SHOT_ACK) {
        if (size != (int)sizeof(ShotAck) || head->magic != k_magic || head->flags || head->count) return;
        const ShotAck *a = (const ShotAck *)data;
        if (!vitals_epoch() || !vitals_peer_epoch() || a->owner != vitals_epoch() || a->target != vitals_peer_epoch() ||
            a->epoch != g_epoch || a->seq > g_seq) return;
        // An event that has never been sent cannot have been acknowledged by the peer.
        while (!g_out.empty() && g_out.front().sent && g_out.front().rec.seq <= a->seq) g_out.pop_front();
        return;
    }
    if (head->type != PKT_SHOT || head->magic != k_magic || head->flags || !head->count || head->count > 5 ||
        size < (int)sizeof(ShotHead) || size != (int)(sizeof(ShotHead) + head->count * sizeof(ShotRec))) return;
    const ShotHead *h = (const ShotHead *)data;
    if (!vitals_epoch() || !vitals_peer_epoch() || h->owner != vitals_peer_epoch() || h->target != vitals_epoch()) return;
    const ShotRec *recs = (const ShotRec *)(h + 1);
    u32 old_epoch = g_in.current_epoch();
    size_t old_pending = g_in.pending();
    if (!g_in.epoch(h->epoch, recs[0].seq)) return;
    if (old_epoch && old_epoch != h->epoch && old_pending)
        mp_log("shots: peer restarted its event stream; %zu unfinished events from the old epoch were discarded", old_pending);
    g_ack_send = send;
    unsigned long long fresh = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < head->count; i++)
        if (g_in.receive(recs[i]) == reliable::Inbox<ShotRec>::accepted) fresh++;
    netlog_count("shots_in", (long long)fresh);
    // ACK only what the simulation has completed, not the records just copied into the queue.
    shots_ack(send);
}

// Receive and acknowledge exactly as before the tactical reliability changes when in a non-tactical
// process. That mode does not run the shot producer or consumer; keep its historical packet behavior.
void shots_on_packet_legacy(const unsigned char *data, int size, void (*send)(const void *, int))
{
    const Head *head = (const Head *)data;
    if (head->type == PKT_SHOT_ACK) {
        if (size < (int)sizeof(ShotAck)) return;
        const ShotAck *a = (const ShotAck *)data;
        if (a->epoch != g_epoch) return;
        while (!g_out.empty() && g_out.front().rec.seq <= a->seq) g_out.pop_front();
        return;
    }
    if (size < (int)sizeof(ShotHead) || size < (int)(sizeof(ShotHead) + head->count * sizeof(ShotRec))) return;
    const ShotHead *h = (const ShotHead *)data;
    const ShotRec *recs = (const ShotRec *)(h + 1);
    if (h->epoch != g_epoch_in_legacy) { g_epoch_in_legacy = h->epoch; g_last_seq_in_legacy = 0; }
    unsigned long long fresh = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < head->count; i++) {
        if (recs[i].seq != g_last_seq_in_legacy + 1) continue;
        g_last_seq_in_legacy = recs[i].seq;
        g_in_legacy.push_back(recs[i]);
        fresh++;
    }
    netlog_count("shots_in", (long long)fresh);
    ShotAck ack = { make_head(PKT_SHOT_ACK), g_epoch_in_legacy, g_last_seq_in_legacy };
    send(&ack, sizeof ack);
}

// A new connection: both directions start again.
void shots_reset(void)
{
    g_in.reset();
    g_in_legacy.clear();
    g_ack_send = 0;
    g_out.clear();
    g_seq = 0;
    g_epoch = (GetTickCount() ^ (GetCurrentProcessId() << 16) ^ (g_epoch * 2654435761u)) | 1;
    hits_reset_peer();
    anim_reset_peer();
    trench_resend();
    xlate_resend();
}

// Before a simulation step: the other game's launches are carried out here.
void shots_sim_step(ObjMan *om)
{
    if (g_in.empty()) return;
    // Tables and trenches do not depend on the shell manager. Handle them once even if an earlier
    // shot must wait; the cumulative ACK still cannot move past that earlier shot.
    for (auto &item : g_in.items()) {
        if (item.complete) continue;
        const ShotRec &s = item.record;
        if (s.kind == 2) { trench_received(s); item.complete = true; }
        else if (s.kind == 3 || s.kind == 4) { xlate_received(s, om); item.complete = true; }
        else if (s.kind != 0 && s.kind != 7 && s.kind != 8) {
            // kind 1 was never emitted by this module; reject unknown records explicitly.
            g_stat_refused++;
            item.complete = true;
        }
    }
    if (g_launch && g_enabled && !g_shellphys)
        g_shellphys = (char *)engine_find(om, "i_shellphys", "shellphys");
    g_om = om;
    for (auto &item : g_in.items()) {
        if (item.complete) continue;
        const ShotRec &s = item.record;
        if (s.kind == 7 || s.kind == 8) {
            bool done = s.kind == 7 ? hits_received(s, om) : anim_received(s, om);
            if (!done) break; // do not replay a later shot ahead of a waiting hit/snapshot
            item.complete = true;
            continue;
        }
        if (s.kind != 0) continue;
        if (!g_install_done || (g_launch && g_enabled && !g_shellphys)) {
            // The engine may appear on a later simulation step. Do not clear or acknowledge it.
            static DWORD last_wait;
            DWORD now = GetTickCount();
            if (!last_wait || now - last_wait >= 5000) {
                last_wait = now ? now : 1;
                mp_log("shots: %zu incoming events are waiting for the shell manager", g_in.pending());
            }
            break;
        }
        if (!g_launch || !g_enabled) {
            // This build explicitly refused/disabled its hook; do not pretend the shot was played.
            g_stat_refused++;
            item.complete = true;
            continue;
        }
        void *ammo = desc_ptr(xlate_desc(s.ammo)), *weapon = desc_ptr(xlate_desc(s.weapon));
        float two[2];
        u32 code;
        ammo_check(ammo, two, &code);
        Object *shooter = s.shooter ? session_unit(s.shooter) : 0;
        // not the same ammunition here, or a unit this game does not have: no shot rather than a wrong one
        if (!ammo || !weapon || memcmp(two, s.ammo_check, 8) || code != s.ammo_code || (s.shooter && !shooter)) {
            g_stat_refused++;
            item.complete = true;
            continue;
        }
        char rec[L_SIZE];
        memcpy(rec, s.launch, L_SIZE);
        *(void **)(rec + L_AMMO) = ammo;
        *(void **)(rec + L_WEAPON) = weapon;
        *(void **)(rec + L_SHOOTER) = shooter;
        *(void **)(rec + L_OBJ2) = s.obj2 ? session_unit(s.obj2) : 0;
        *(void **)(rec + L_DESC3) = desc_ptr(xlate_desc(s.desc3));
        float pos[4];
        memcpy(pos, s.pos, 16);
        log_launch("received", 'L', rec, s.flags, 0, 0);
        g_injecting = true;
        g_launch(g_shellphys, rec, s.flags & ~2u, s.has_pos ? pos : 0, s.obj5 ? session_unit(s.obj5) : 0, s.arg6);
        g_injecting = false;
        g_stat_injected++;
        item.complete = true;
    }
    u32 before = g_in.committed();
    if (g_in.commit() != before) shots_ack(g_ack_send);
}

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
// The Windows no-game integration test supplies a fake shell manager; none of these seams is
// compiled into the game module.
void shots_test_engine(ObjMan *om, char *shell, Launch launch)
{
    g_om = om;
    g_shellphys = shell;
    g_launch = launch;
    g_enabled = true;
    g_install_done = true;
}
size_t shots_test_outstanding() { return g_out.size(); }
size_t shots_test_legacy_pending() { return g_in_legacy.size(); }
u32 shots_test_epoch() { return g_epoch; }
#endif

void shots_report(void)
{
    if (!g_stat_own && !g_stat_dropped && !g_stat_injected && !g_stat_refused) return;
    mp_log("shots: 5 s: %llu fired by this game's units and sent (%.1f KB/s, %llu repeats, %zu waiting for an answer), %llu received and fired here, %llu local ones for the other player's units dropped, %llu refused (ammunition or unit not matched)",
           g_stat_own, g_stat_sent_bytes / 5120.0, g_stat_repeats, g_out.size(), g_stat_injected, g_stat_dropped, g_stat_refused);
    g_stat_own = g_stat_dropped = g_stat_injected = g_stat_refused = g_stat_sent_bytes = g_stat_repeats = 0;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/shots.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/spectator_policy.h (sha256 ebefe961f35bfd5ae33b0ca22d4cda4978752813eddde5693c61ddd6ba775024) ====================
// Separate operation observation from tactical traffic. Only locally inherited spectator
// processes are covered; real engine process/environment behaviour still needs game testing.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
namespace spectator_policy {
inline bool local_battle_offline(bool tactical, bool joiner, bool spectator_marker) {
    return tactical && joiner && spectator_marker;
}
inline bool incoming(bool operation_host, bool spectator, unsigned type, unsigned flags) {
    if (!operation_host && !spectator) return true; // leave tactical and other modes unchanged
    if (type == PKT_BYE || type == PKT_KEEPALIVE) return true;
    if (operation_host && type == PKT_IMAGE_REQUEST && flags == 1) return true;
    if (spectator && type == PKT_WELCOME) return true;
    if (spectator && type == PKT_IMAGE_DATA && flags == 1) return true;
    return false;
}
inline bool accept_hello(bool operation_host, unsigned phase) {
    // A tactical process on the guest's local copy is not an authoritative map peer.
    return !operation_host || phase >= 0xfe;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/spectator_policy.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/spectator_snapshot.h (sha256 08ba2372ff5cad2d658bc599ed210e242047098268f6d9ad5b5ba4740c252b85) ====================
// Host-authoritative spectator snapshot eligibility. These checks validate the serialized
// fixture format and turn markers, not the engine's real save/completion lifecycle.
#pragma once
#include "operation_turn.h"
#include "opsave_checked.h"
#include <vector>

namespace spectator_snapshot {
inline bool publishable(const std::vector<unsigned char> &candidate, bool after_worker,
                        unsigned previous_frames, const std::vector<unsigned char> &previous) {
    if (!opsave_checked::validate(candidate)) return false;
    int phase = opsave::turn_phase(candidate);
    if (phase != 0 && phase != 2) return false;
    if (after_worker && !previous.empty()) {
        if (!operation_turn::resolution_saved(phase, opsave::turns(candidate), previous_frames)) return false;
        if (candidate == previous) return false;
    }
    return true;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/spectator_snapshot.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/state.cpp (sha256 4d9395f3392812e95998133c483178e6349410a5a215c2b7806272aaa77aaa88) ====================
// Full object state through the engine's own savegame code.
//
// Every simulation object answers the commands "save" and "load" with a message whose +0x10 field points
// at a byte stream (the same code the game uses to save and restore a battle). Units write their plain
// state fields, their dynamic arrays, and references to other objects as stable object ids, so a blob
// saved in one game can be loaded into the same unit of another game running the same battle.
//
//   stream: +0x00 begin, +0x08 end, +0x10 capacity end, +0x18 read position, +0x1c underrun count
#include "common.h"
#include "hooks.h"
#include "detour.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <map>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

struct Stream { unsigned char *begin, *end, *cap; u32 pos, underrun; };
struct StateMsg { void **vtbl; const char *name; Stream *stream; u64 spare[8]; };

static void *msg_dtor(void *self, u64) { return self; }
static void *g_msg_vtbl[4] = { (void *)msg_dtor, (void *)msg_dtor, (void *)msg_dtor, (void *)msg_dtor };

// The engine grows the stream itself when it runs out of room; a buffer this large is never outgrown,
// so the memory stays ours.
static const size_t k_scratch = 8u << 20;
static unsigned char *g_scratch;

bool state_save(Object *o, std::vector<unsigned char> &out)
{
    Handler save = engine_lookup(o, "save");
    if (!save) return false;
    if (!g_scratch) g_scratch = (unsigned char *)malloc(k_scratch);
    Stream s = { g_scratch, g_scratch, g_scratch + k_scratch, 0, 0 };
    StateMsg m = { g_msg_vtbl, "save", &s };
    save(o, &m);
    if (s.begin != g_scratch) {          // outgrown after all: take the data, keep the engine's buffer alive
        mp_log("state: %s wrote more than %zu bytes", o->full, k_scratch);
        g_scratch = s.begin;
    }
    out.assign(s.begin, s.end);
    return true;
}

// Returns the number of bytes the object asked for beyond the end of the blob (0 = clean load), or -1.
int state_load(Object *o, const unsigned char *data, size_t size)
{
    Handler load = engine_lookup(o, "load");
    if (!load) return -1;
    Stream s = { (unsigned char *)data, (unsigned char *)data + size, (unsigned char *)data + size, 0, 0 };
    StateMsg m = { g_msg_vtbl, "load", &s };
    load(o, &m);
    return (int)s.underrun;
}

// "save" means "write your state to the byte stream" only for simulation objects. Rendering and resource
// objects use the same command name for something else, so anything outside these modules is left alone.
bool state_is_simulation(Object *o)
{
    static HMODULE mods[3];
    static bool init;
    if (!init) {
        init = true;
        mods[0] = GetModuleHandleA("adv_physic.codelib");
        mods[1] = GetModuleHandleA("adv_game.codelib");
        mods[2] = GetModuleHandleA("basic_physic.codelib");
    }
    Handler save = engine_lookup(o, "save");
    HMODULE m = 0;
    if (!save || !GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT, (LPCSTR)save, &m)) return false;
    return m && (m == mods[0] || m == mods[1] || m == mods[2]);
}

bool state_is_unit(Object *o)
{
    return !strcmp(o->iface, "i_techn") || !strcmp(o->iface, "i_human");
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- render handles stay local
// Every physical object - a unit, and the objects nested inside it - is saved through the engine's base
// routine (adv_physic+0x81c90) and loaded through its counterpart (adv_physic+0x82090). Part of that data
// is the list of attached model parts (array at +0x850, count at +0x85c, 0x60 bytes each), and each part
// is saved together with the renderer's handles for it (scene node at +0x48, model instance at +0x4c).
// Those handles only mean something in the
// process that saved them; drawn through another game's handles the unit crashes the renderer.
// While a state from the other game is being loaded, the base load is therefore wrapped: the object's own
// handles are noted before and put back after. Loading a saved game the normal way is not touched.
typedef void (*BaseLoad)(char *, void *);
static BaseLoad g_base_load;
static int g_remote_load;

static void base_load_hook(char *o, void *msg)
{
    if (!g_remote_load) { g_base_load(o, msg); return; }
    std::vector<u32> handles;
    {
        u32 n = *(u32 *)(o + 0x85c);
        const char *parts = *(const char **)(o + 0x850);
        if (n && n < 4096 && readable(parts, (size_t)n * 0x60))
            for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                handles.push_back(*(const u32 *)(parts + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x48));
                handles.push_back(*(const u32 *)(parts + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x4c));
            }
    }
    g_base_load(o, msg);
    // The parts this game already had keep their handles. Parts the other game has in addition are dropped
    // from the list: there is no local handle to draw them with.
    u32 n = *(u32 *)(o + 0x85c), had = (u32)handles.size() / 2;
    char *parts = *(char **)(o + 0x850);
    if (n > had) *(u32 *)(o + 0x85c) = n = had;
    if (n && n < 4096 && readable(parts, (size_t)n * 0x60))
        for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            *(u32 *)(parts + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x48) = handles[i * 2];
            *(u32 *)(parts + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x4c) = handles[i * 2 + 1];
        }
}

// Last line of defence. A handle is a place in the renderer's node tables (group << 10 | node). The
// renderer's node look-up (basic_render+0x13ab0) follows whatever it is given; with a handle from the
// other game that names no live node here it walks into unmapped memory and the game is gone - seen from
// several places in the unit update, always at this function. With another player's states being loaded
// the look-up is therefore wrapped: an access violation inside it ends the look-up (the caller gets the
// first transform slot) instead of ending the game.
typedef u32 (*NodeLookup)(char *, u64, int);
static NodeLookup g_node_lookup;
static u64 g_bad_lookups;

static u32 node_lookup_hook(char *render, u64 group, int node)
{
    __try {
        return g_node_lookup(render, group, node);
    } __except (GetExceptionCode() == EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION ? EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER : EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH) {
        g_bad_lookups++;
        return 0;
    }
}

u64 state_bad_lookups(void) { u64 n = g_bad_lookups; g_bad_lookups = 0; return n; }
bool state_remote_loading(void) { return g_remote_load > 0; }

static void install_part_guard(void)
{
    static const unsigned char sig[] = { 0x48, 0x89, 0x5c, 0x24, 0x10, 0x48, 0x89, 0x6c, 0x24, 0x18, 0x56, 0x57, 0x41, 0x54, 0x41, 0x56 };
    // The older 22-byte prefix also occurs at +0x12fd0 in the supplied build. Include the next
    // instructions so a moved node-lookup can be found uniquely instead of detouring its neighbour.
    static const unsigned char lookup_sig[] = { 0x48, 0x89, 0x6c, 0x24, 0x10, 0x48, 0x89, 0x74, 0x24, 0x18, 0x57, 0x48, 0x83, 0xec, 0x20,
                                                0x4c, 0x8b, 0x89, 0x40, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x43, 0x8d, 0x34, 0x80, 0xc1, 0xe6, 0x06, 0x48, 0x8b, 0xe9 };
    static bool tried_load, tried_render;
    // These DLLs may appear at different times. Do not permanently mark a missing module as tried.
    if (!tried_load && GetModuleHandleA("adv_physic.codelib")) {
        tried_load = true;
        const HookSig where[] = { { 0x82020, sig, sizeof sig }, { 0x82090, sig, sizeof sig } };
        char *load = hook_resolve_checked("adv_physic.codelib", where, 2, "state part-handle guard");
        if (load) g_base_load = (BaseLoad)install_detour(load, 14, base_load_hook);
        mp_log("state: render handles %s", g_base_load ? "stay local when states are loaded" : "could not be guarded");
    }
    if (!tried_render && GetModuleHandleA("basic_render.codelib")) {
        tried_render = true;
        const HookSig where[] = { { 0x13ab0, lookup_sig, sizeof lookup_sig } };
        char *lookup = hook_resolve_checked("basic_render.codelib", where, 1, "render node-lookup guard");
        if (lookup) g_node_lookup = (NodeLookup)install_detour(lookup, 15, node_lookup_hook);
        mp_log("state: node look-ups %s", g_node_lookup ? "are guarded" : "could not be guarded");
    }
}

// Is a state saved by the other game safe to load into this unit? The saved state carries render handles
// in more places than the part list guarded above, and a unit drawn through another process's handles
// crashes the renderer. Two games that built their scene in the same order hand out the same handles; once
// they differ for a unit, its state is not loaded (the unit is then followed by position only).
// The test compares the part list inside the blob with the unit's own. Layout of the base save
// (adv_physic+0x81c90): 0x296 fixed bytes, then counted lists - {u32 capacity, u32 count, records}:
// 17-byte records, 8-byte records, two object ids, u32 records, 0x40-byte records, and the parts
// (0x40 matrix, node handle, instance handle, flags).
bool state_handles_match(Object *o, const unsigned char *data, size_t size)
{
    size_t pos = 0x296;
    auto list = [&](size_t record) -> long long {
        if (pos + 8 > size) return -1;
        u32 n;
        memcpy(&n, data + pos + 4, 4);
        pos += 8;
        if (n > 0x10000 || pos + (size_t)n * record > size) return -1;
        size_t at = pos;
        pos += (size_t)n * record;
        return (long long)at | ((long long)n << 40);
    };
    if (list(17) < 0 || list(8) < 0) return false;
    pos += 8;
    if (list(4) < 0 || list(0x40) < 0) return false;
    long long parts = list(0x4c);
    if (parts < 0) return false;
    u32 n = (u32)(parts >> 40);
    const unsigned char *rec = data + (size_t)(parts & 0xffffffffffll);
    const char *mine = *(const char **)((char *)o + 0x850);
    if (n != *(u32 *)((char *)o + 0x85c)) return false;
    if (n && !readable(mine, (size_t)n * 0x60)) return false;
    for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++)
        if (memcmp(rec + (size_t)i * 0x4c + 0x40, mine + (size_t)i * 0x60 + 0x48, 8)) return false;
    return true;
}

// Loads a blob into a unit but keeps the unit's current place and motion. Position is handled by the
// smooth transform stream; a state blob is always a little older and would pull the unit back.
//
// keep_view: also keep the fields that describe the unit from this game's player's point of view. The
// engine saves them with the unit (base unit save, adv_physic+0x81c90, field tables at +0xd7e90/+0xd7fc0):
//   +0x6b0, +0x6b4, +0x6e8, +0x6ec, +0x6f0, +0x714  its place in the player's own force (-1 / 0 for the
//           opponent's units)
//   +0x72c  the "seen" countdown, in seconds: the unit is drawn while it is above zero. The game's
//           visibility manager (i_visman) sets it to 10 for the player's own units every 7.5 seconds and
//           for an opponent's unit when one of the player's units has spotted it; the unit counts it down.
//   +0x745, +0x749  flags that go with it (set on hidden units of the opponent)
//   soldier +0x228c, vehicle +0x1e66  "is drawn", worked out from the countdown by the unit's own update -
//           not on every step, so an owner's "drawn" loaded into a hidden unit showed it for a moment
//           after each load: units in the fog of war flickered
// In a versus game the two players see these the other way round. A unit loaded with its owner's values
// is one its owner always sees: here it would be shown for another ten seconds after every load - that is,
// always, spotted or not.
int state_load_keep_motion(Object *o, const unsigned char *data, size_t size, bool keep_view)
{
    static const unsigned short view[] = { 0x6b0, 0x6b4, 0x6e8, 0x6ec, 0x6f0, 0x714, 0x72c };
    static const unsigned short view_bytes[] = { 0x745, 0x749 };
    unsigned char body[UNIT_MOTION_SIZE], kept_bytes[_countof(view_bytes)], kept_drawn;
    const unsigned short drawn = !strcmp(o->iface, "i_human") ? 0x228c : 0x1e66;
    u32 kept[_countof(view)];
    memcpy(body, (char *)o + UNIT_ROT, sizeof body);          // +0xf0 .. +0x2c0: the whole rigid body
    for (int i = 0; i < _countof(view); i++) memcpy(&kept[i], (char *)o + view[i], 4);
    for (int i = 0; i < _countof(view_bytes); i++) kept_bytes[i] = ((unsigned char *)o)[view_bytes[i]];
    kept_drawn = ((unsigned char *)o)[drawn];

    // a soldier's animation instance (+0x1d60) and its second render handle (+0x1d64) are process-local too
    bool human = !strcmp(o->iface, "i_human");
    u32 anim[2];
    memcpy(anim, (char *)o + 0x1d60, 8);

    // A vehicle keeps a list of points fixed to nodes of its model (+0x1298: 0x20-byte records, the node's
    // render handle at +0x10; count at +0x12a4, room at +0x12a8). It is made when the model is, and saved
    // raw with the vehicle - handles and all. This game's handles are the only ones that mean anything here.
    bool techn = !strcmp(o->iface, "i_techn");
    std::vector<unsigned char> points;
    if (techn) {
        u32 n = *(u32 *)((char *)o + 0x12a4);
        const unsigned char *p = *(const unsigned char **)((char *)o + 0x1298);
        if (n && n < 4096 && readable(p, (size_t)n * 0x20)) points.assign(p, p + (size_t)n * 0x20);
    }
    // Two more lists of a vehicle start every record with a pointer to a description (+0x15e8: records of
    // 0x120 bytes, count at +0x15f4; +0x1600: records of 0x1d0 bytes, count at +0x160c). They are written
    // as description numbers, and a number of the other game does not always name the same description
    // here; the vehicle update then reads a "name" out of something that is not one (the crash at
    // adv_game+0xd9917 that kept state replication out of operation battles). What a vehicle is fitted
    // with does not change during a battle, so this game's own pointers are kept.
    static const struct { unsigned short list, count, size; } k_fitted[] = { { 0x15e8, 0x15f4, 0x120 }, { 0x1600, 0x160c, 0x1d0 } };
    std::vector<unsigned char> fitted[2];
    if (techn)
        for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
            u32 n = *(u32 *)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].count);
            const unsigned char *p = *(const unsigned char **)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].list);
            if (n && n < 1024 && readable(p, (size_t)n * k_fitted[k].size)) fitted[k].assign(p, p + (size_t)n * k_fitted[k].size);
        }

    install_part_guard();
    g_remote_load++;
    int r = state_load(o, data, size);
    g_remote_load--;
    if (human) memcpy((char *)o + 0x1d60, anim, 8);
    if (techn)
        for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
            u32 n = *(u32 *)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].count), room = *(u32 *)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].count + 4);
            u32 had = (u32)(fitted[k].size() / k_fitted[k].size);
            unsigned char *p = *(unsigned char **)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].list);
            if (!had) {
                if (n) *(u32 *)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].count) = 0;      // this game's vehicle has none: nor has it now
            } else if (n == had && readable(p, fitted[k].size())) {
                // same fittings: their state is the owner's, the descriptions they point at are this game's
                for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++) memcpy(p + (size_t)i * k_fitted[k].size, fitted[k].data() + (size_t)i * k_fitted[k].size, 8);
            } else if (room >= had && readable(p, fitted[k].size())) {
                memcpy(p, fitted[k].data(), fitted[k].size());           // another set over there: this game's stays whole
                *(u32 *)((char *)o + k_fitted[k].count) = had;
            }
        }
    if (techn && !points.empty()) {
        u32 n = *(u32 *)((char *)o + 0x12a4), room = *(u32 *)((char *)o + 0x12a8), had = (u32)(points.size() / 0x20);
        unsigned char *p = *(unsigned char **)((char *)o + 0x1298);
        if (n == had && readable(p, points.size())) {
            for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++) memcpy(p + (size_t)i * 0x20 + 0x10, points.data() + (size_t)i * 0x20 + 0x10, 4);
        } else if (room >= had && readable(p, points.size())) {
            // the other game has another model for this vehicle by now (a wreck, say): this game's list stays
            memcpy(p, points.data(), points.size());
            *(u32 *)((char *)o + 0x12a4) = had;
        }
    }

    memcpy((char *)o + UNIT_ROT, body, sizeof body);
    if (keep_view) {
        for (int i = 0; i < _countof(view); i++) memcpy((char *)o + view[i], &kept[i], 4);
        for (int i = 0; i < _countof(view_bytes); i++) ((unsigned char *)o)[view_bytes[i]] = kept_bytes[i];
        ((unsigned char *)o)[drawn] = kept_drawn;
    }
    return r;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- debug: survey and self round trip
// MP_STATE=survey      once, 20 simulated seconds in: size of every object's saved state, by class
// MP_STATE=roundtrip   twice a second: save every unit and load it straight back (tests loading in place)
void state_debug_step(ObjMan *om, u64 sim_ms)
{
    static int mode = -1;
    static u64 last_ms;
    static bool surveyed;
    if (mode < 0) {
        char v[32] = "";
        GetEnvironmentVariableA("MP_STATE", v, sizeof v);
        mode = !strcmp(v, "survey") ? 1 : !strcmp(v, "roundtrip") ? 2 : !strcmp(v, "dump") ? 3 : 0;
    }
    if (!mode) return;
    u32 count;
    Object **all = all_objects(om, &count);
    std::vector<unsigned char> blob;
    // dump: every unit's saved state at two moments, as files, to compare between two games
    static int dumps;
    if (mode == 3 && dumps < 2 && sim_ms >= (dumps ? 30000u : 6000u)) {
        char dir[MAX_PATH], path[MAX_PATH], name[80];
        sprintf_s(dir, "%s/mp_blobs_%lu_%d", g_out_dir, GetCurrentProcessId(), dumps);
        CreateDirectoryA(dir, 0);
        int n = 0;
        for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            if (!state_is_unit(all[i]) || !state_save(all[i], blob)) continue;
            strcpy_s(name, all[i]->full);
            for (char *c = name; *c; c++) if (*c == ':') *c = '-';
            sprintf_s(path, "%s/%s.bin", dir, name);
            FILE *f = 0;
            if (!fopen_s(&f, path, "wb") && f) { fwrite(blob.data(), 1, blob.size(), f); fclose(f); n++; }
        }
        mp_log("state dump %d at %llu ms: %d units in %s", dumps, sim_ms, n, dir);
        dumps++;
    }
    if (!surveyed && sim_ms >= 20000) {
        surveyed = true;
        std::map<std::string, std::pair<int, size_t>> by_class;
        size_t total = 0;
        for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            if (!state_is_simulation(all[i])) continue;
            try {
                if (!state_save(all[i], blob)) continue;
            } catch (...) {
                mp_log("state survey: %s failed while saving", all[i]->full);
                continue;
            }
            auto &c = by_class[all[i]->iface];
            c.first++;
            c.second += blob.size();
            total += blob.size();
        }
        mp_log("state survey at %llu ms: %zu bytes in total", sim_ms, total);
        for (auto &kv : by_class)
            mp_log("  %-18s x%-4d %9zu bytes  (%zu each)", kv.first.c_str(), kv.second.first, kv.second.second, kv.second.second / kv.second.first);
    }
    if (mode == 2 && sim_ms - last_ms >= 500) {
        static u64 rounds, bytes, underruns;
        last_ms = sim_ms;
        std::vector<Object *> units;
        for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) if (state_is_unit(all[i])) units.push_back(all[i]);
        for (Object *u : units) {
            if (!state_save(u, blob)) continue;
            int r = state_load(u, blob.data(), blob.size());
            bytes += blob.size();
            if (r > 0) underruns += r;
        }
        if (++rounds % 20 == 0)
            mp_log("state roundtrip: %llu rounds, %zu units, %llu KB moved, %llu underruns", rounds, units.size(), bytes / 1024, underruns);
    }
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/state.cpp ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/state_fragments.h (sha256 2b11edccb8ffd18d4edbdddfc3c21b47aed25c216c899fda3ba049fbf6d3c8aa) ====================
// Shape validation for the existing full-state wire protocol, before allocating or copying.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

namespace state_fragments {
static const uint32_t fragment_bytes = 1100;
static const uint32_t maximum_bytes = 16u << 20;
// Nonzero versions advance in a wrapping 32-bit serial number space.
inline bool newer(uint32_t value, uint32_t previous) {
    uint32_t delta = value - previous;
    return delta != 0 && delta < 0x80000000u;
}
inline bool valid(const ObjectFrag &f, size_t packet_bytes)
{
    uint32_t len = f.h.count;
    if (f.h.type != PKT_OBJECT || !f.version || f.raw_size > maximum_bytes || f.delta_size > maximum_bytes ||
        !f.nfrags || f.frag >= f.nfrags || len > fragment_bytes ||
        packet_bytes < sizeof(ObjectFrag) || len > packet_bytes - sizeof(ObjectFrag)) return false;
    uint32_t count = f.delta_size ? (f.delta_size + fragment_bytes - 1) / fragment_bytes : 1;
    if (f.nfrags != count) return false;
    uint32_t offset = (uint32_t)f.frag * fragment_bytes;
    uint32_t remaining = f.delta_size - offset;
    uint32_t expected = remaining < fragment_bytes ? remaining : fragment_bytes;
    return len == expected;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/state_fragments.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/statesync.cpp (sha256 5ece1933674a7d65e57add746c30f5b65dbb02b74e65c912085b65252f7925d4) ====================
// Replication of full object state (see state.cpp) from the game that owns an object to the other game.
//
// The owner saves each object a few times a second. When the bytes differ from what the other side is
// known to have, it sends the difference: XOR against that known version, run-length encoded, cut into
// datagrams. The receiver rebuilds the blob, acknowledges the version, and loads it into its own copy of
// the object before the next simulation step. Versions only advance on acknowledgement, so a lost
// datagram costs one retry and never corrupts state.
#include "common.h"
#include "env_value.h"
#include "netlog.h"
#include "protocol.h"
#include "state_fragments.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

typedef std::vector<unsigned char> Blob;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- delta coding
static void put_varint(Blob &out, u32 v)
{
    while (v >= 0x80) { out.push_back((unsigned char)(v | 0x80)); v >>= 7; }
    out.push_back((unsigned char)v);
}

static bool get_varint(const unsigned char *&p, const unsigned char *end, u32 &v)
{
    v = 0;
    for (int shift = 0; p < end && shift < 35; shift += 7) {
        unsigned char b = *p++;
        if (shift == 28 && (b & 0xf0)) return false; // a u32 varint has only four bits left
        v |= (u32)(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// (unchanged run, changed run, changed bytes)* ; bytes are cur XOR base, base padded with zeros
static void encode_delta(const Blob &base, const Blob &cur, Blob &out)
{
    out.clear();
    size_t n = cur.size(), i = 0;
    auto same = [&](size_t k) { return cur[k] == (k < base.size() ? base[k] : 0); };
    while (i < n) {
        size_t z = i;
        while (z < n && same(z)) z++;
        size_t l = z;
        // a changed run ends at four or more unchanged bytes (shorter gaps are cheaper to send as data)
        for (size_t gap = 0; l < n; l++) {
            if (same(l)) { if (++gap >= 4) { l -= gap - 1; break; } }
            else gap = 0;
        }
        if (l > n) l = n;
        while (l > z && same(l - 1)) l--;
        put_varint(out, (u32)(z - i));
        put_varint(out, (u32)(l - z));
        for (size_t k = z; k < l; k++) out.push_back(cur[k] ^ (k < base.size() ? base[k] : 0));
        i = l;
    }
}

static bool decode_delta(const Blob &base, const unsigned char *p, size_t len, u32 raw_size, Blob &out)
{
    if (raw_size > (16u << 20)) return false;
    out.assign(raw_size, 0);
    size_t initial = min(base.size(), (size_t)raw_size);
    if (initial) memcpy(out.data(), base.data(), initial);
    if (!len) return true;
    if (!p) return false;
    const unsigned char *end = p + len;
    size_t i = 0;
    while (p < end) {
        u32 z, l;
        if (!get_varint(p, end, z) || !get_varint(p, end, l)) return false;
        i += z;
        if (i > raw_size || l > raw_size - i || l > (size_t)(end - p)) return false;
        for (u32 k = 0; k < l; k++) {
            // XOR against the padded base: bytes beyond the old size were zero
            out[i + k] = (unsigned char)((i + k < base.size() ? base[i + k] : 0) ^ p[k]);
        }
        p += l;
        i += l;
    }
    return true;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- shared
static const u32 k_frag = state_fragments::fragment_bytes;
static void (*g_send)(const void *, int);
static u64 g_stat_bytes, g_stat_objects, g_stat_raw, g_stat_loads, g_stat_bad, g_stat_received, g_stat_unsafe;
static bool g_translate;      // versus: the two games see every unit from opposite sides

u64 statesync_key(Object *o)
{
    u64 kind = !strcmp(o->iface, "i_techn") ? 1 : !strcmp(o->iface, "i_human") ? 2 : 3;
    if (kind == 3) return ((u64)3 << 32) | fnv((const unsigned char *)o->full, strlen(o->full));
    const char *us = strrchr(o->full, '_');
    return (kind << 32) | (us ? strtoul(us + 1, 0, 16) : 0);
}

// Singleton simulation objects that are replicated besides the units. Each was checked to load cleanly
// into a running battle; the others either hold only local presentation state or are too large to matter.
static bool replicated_singleton(Object *o)
{
    static const char *names[] = { "i_divisions", "i_statistic", "i_trigman", "i_formation", "i_fireman", "i_shellphys" };
    static int enabled = -1;
    if (enabled < 0) {
        enabled = 0;
        env_integer("MP_SYNC_WORLD", &enabled);
    }
    if (!enabled) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < enabled && i < 6; i++)
        if (!strcmp(o->iface, names[i])) return true;
    return false;
}

static bool replicated(Object *o) { return state_is_unit(o) || replicated_singleton(o); }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- owner side
struct Sent { u32 ver; Blob blob; };
struct Track {
    Blob base;                     // the newest version the other side has confirmed
    u32 base_ver = 0, last_ver = 0;
    std::vector<Sent> sent_versions;   // sent since then, newest last; any of them may be confirmed next
    DWORD checked = 0, sent = 0;
};
static std::unordered_map<u64, Track> g_tracks;
static u32 g_cursor;
static double g_budget;
static DWORD g_last_frame;

static void send_object(u64 key, Track &t, DWORD now)
{
    static Blob delta;
    const Blob &cur = t.sent_versions.back().blob;
    encode_delta(t.base, cur, delta);
    if (cur.size() > state_fragments::maximum_bytes || delta.size() > state_fragments::maximum_bytes) {
        static DWORD last_oversize;
        if (!last_oversize || now - last_oversize >= 5000) {
            last_oversize = now ? now : 1;
            mp_log("state: object state exceeds the 16 MiB wire limit; not sent (%zu/%zu bytes)", cur.size(), delta.size());
        }
        ++g_stat_bad;
        return;
    }
    u32 nfrags = (u32)((delta.size() + k_frag - 1) / k_frag);
    if (!nfrags) nfrags = 1;
    for (u32 f = 0; f < nfrags; f++) {
        unsigned char packet[sizeof(ObjectFrag) + k_frag];
        ObjectFrag *h = (ObjectFrag *)packet;
        u32 off = f * k_frag, len = (u32)min((size_t)k_frag, delta.size() - off);
        h->h = make_head(PKT_OBJECT, (int)len);
        h->key = key;
        h->version = t.sent_versions.back().ver;
        h->base_version = t.base_ver;
        h->raw_size = (u32)cur.size();
        h->delta_size = (u32)delta.size();
        h->frag = (unsigned short)f;
        h->nfrags = (unsigned short)nfrags;
        if (len) memcpy(h + 1, delta.data() + off, len);
        g_send(packet, (int)(sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len));
        g_budget -= sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len + 28;
        g_stat_bytes += sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len;
    }
    t.sent = now;
    g_stat_objects++;
    g_stat_raw += cur.size();
}

// Called every frame by the side that owns objects. `owns` decides per object.
void statesync_owner_frame(ObjMan *om, DWORD now, bool (*owns)(Object *), void (*send)(const void *, int))
{
    static int kbps = -1;
    static Blob blob;
    if (kbps < 0) {
        kbps = 600;
        int requested;
        if (env_integer("MP_STATE_KBPS", &requested) && requested >= 0) kbps = requested;
    }
    g_send = send;
    double dt = g_last_frame ? (now - g_last_frame) / 1000.0 : 0;
    g_last_frame = now;
    // Convert before multiplication: a valid but very large MP_STATE_KBPS must not overflow int.
    const double bytes_per_second = (double)kbps * 1024.0;
    g_budget += dt * bytes_per_second;
    if (g_budget > bytes_per_second * 0.25) g_budget = bytes_per_second * 0.25; // at most a quarter second saved up
    u32 count;
    Object **all = all_objects(om, &count);
    for (u32 n = 0; n < count && g_budget > 0; n++) {
        Object *o = all[g_cursor++ % count];
        if (!replicated(o) || !owns(o) || !session_state_wanted(o)) continue;
        Track &t = g_tracks[statesync_key(o)];
        bool unit = state_is_unit(o);
        if (now - t.checked < (!strcmp(o->iface, "i_techn") ? 700u : 400u)) continue;
        t.checked = now;
        if (!state_save(o, blob)) continue;
        if (unit && blob.size() >= UNIT_MOTION_SIZE) memset(blob.data(), 0, UNIT_MOTION_SIZE);
        const Blob &newest = t.sent_versions.empty() ? t.base : t.sent_versions.back().blob;
        if (blob.size() == newest.size() && (blob.empty() || !memcmp(blob.data(), newest.data(), blob.size()))) {
            if (!t.sent_versions.empty() && now - t.sent > 400) send_object(statesync_key(o), t, now);    // not confirmed: again
            continue;
        }
        if (t.sent_versions.size() >= 6) t.sent_versions.erase(t.sent_versions.begin());
        if (++t.last_ver == 0) ++t.last_ver; // zero denotes "no base" on the wire
        t.sent_versions.push_back({ t.last_ver, blob });
        send_object(statesync_key(o), t, now);
    }
}

void statesync_reset(void)
{
    g_tracks.clear();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- receiving side
struct Incoming {
    Blob base;                     // last complete state received
    u32 base_ver = 0;
    Blob partial;                  // delta being reassembled
    u32 part_ver = 0, part_have = 0, part_frags = 0;
    u32 part_base = 0, part_raw = 0, part_size = 0;
    std::vector<unsigned char> got;
    bool fresh = false;            // base not yet loaded into the object
};
static std::unordered_map<u64, Incoming> g_incoming;
static std::unordered_map<u64, Object *> g_state_index;
static u32 g_state_seen_count = 0xffffffffu;

// Ownership and version numbers are scoped to a peer. Keeping a previous peer's base can make a
// new peer's version 1 look like an already acknowledged duplicate or load the old player's state.
void statesync_forget_peer(void)
{
    g_incoming.clear();
    g_state_index.clear();
    g_state_seen_count = 0xffffffffu;
    g_translate = false;
}

void statesync_on_packet(const unsigned char *data, int size, void (*send)(const void *, int), bool versus)
{
    g_translate = versus;
    const Head *head = (const Head *)data;
    if (head->type == PKT_OBJECT_ACK && size >= (int)sizeof(ObjectAck)) {
        const ObjectAck *a = (const ObjectAck *)data;
        // The other side reports the version it now holds. Later sends are differences against that one.
        auto it = g_tracks.find(a->key);
        if (it == g_tracks.end() || a->version == it->second.base_ver) return;
        Track &t = it->second;
        if (t.base_ver && a->version && !state_fragments::newer(a->version, t.base_ver)) return;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < t.sent_versions.size(); i++)
            if (t.sent_versions[i].ver == a->version) {
                t.base.swap(t.sent_versions[i].blob);
                t.base_ver = a->version;
                t.sent_versions.erase(t.sent_versions.begin(), t.sent_versions.begin() + i + 1);
                return;
            }
        // it holds something we no longer keep: start over from nothing
        t.base.clear();
        t.base_ver = 0;
        return;
    }
    if (head->type != PKT_OBJECT || size < (int)sizeof(ObjectFrag)) return;
    const ObjectFrag *f = (const ObjectFrag *)data;
    u32 len = head->count;
    if (!state_fragments::valid(*f, (size_t)size)) { ++g_stat_bad; return; }
    Incoming &in = g_incoming[f->key];
    ObjectAck ack = { make_head(PKT_OBJECT_ACK), f->key, f->version };
    if (f->version == in.base_ver && in.base_ver) { send(&ack, sizeof ack); return; }      // already have it: the ack was lost
    if (in.base_ver && !state_fragments::newer(f->version, in.base_ver)) {
        // A delayed old baseline must not roll a newer object state backwards.
        if (f->frag == 0) { ack.version = in.base_ver; send(&ack, sizeof ack); }
        return;
    }
    if (f->base_version != in.base_ver && f->base_version != 0) {
        // built on a version we do not hold: say which one we do hold, so the owner can rebase
        if (f->frag == 0) { ack.version = in.base_ver; send(&ack, sizeof ack); }
        return;
    }
    if (in.part_ver && f->version != in.part_ver && !state_fragments::newer(f->version, in.part_ver))
        return; // a delayed older version must not displace a newer partial assembly
    if (in.part_ver != f->version || in.part_frags != f->nfrags || in.part_base != f->base_version ||
        in.part_raw != f->raw_size || in.part_size != f->delta_size) {
        in.part_ver = f->version;
        in.part_frags = f->nfrags;
        in.part_base = f->base_version;
        in.part_raw = f->raw_size;
        in.part_size = f->delta_size;
        in.part_have = 0;
        in.got.assign(f->nfrags, 0);
        in.partial.assign(f->delta_size, 0);
    }
    if ((size_t)f->frag * k_frag + len > in.partial.size() || in.got[f->frag]) return;
    if (len) memcpy(in.partial.data() + (size_t)f->frag * k_frag, f + 1, len);
    in.got[f->frag] = 1;
    if (++in.part_have < in.part_frags) return;
    Blob next;
    static const Blob nothing;
    if (!decode_delta(f->base_version ? in.base : nothing, in.partial.data(), in.partial.size(), f->raw_size, next)) {
        ++g_stat_bad;
        in.part_ver = in.part_have = in.part_frags = 0;
        Blob().swap(in.partial);
        std::vector<unsigned char>().swap(in.got);
        return; // the sender receives no ACK and can retry a complete copy
    }
    in.base.swap(next);
    in.base_ver = f->version;
    in.part_ver = 0;
    in.fresh = true;
    g_stat_received += in.partial.size();
    Blob().swap(in.partial);                       // completed assembly need not retain a second copy
    std::vector<unsigned char>().swap(in.got);
    send(&ack, sizeof ack);
}

// Before a simulation step: load every newly received state into its object.
void statesync_apply(ObjMan *om)
{
    bool any = false;
    for (auto &kv : g_incoming) if (kv.second.fresh) { any = true; break; }
    if (!any) return;
    u32 count;
    Object **all = all_objects(om, &count);
    if (count != g_state_seen_count) {
        g_state_index.clear();
        for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) if (replicated(all[i])) g_state_index[statesync_key(all[i])] = all[i];
        g_state_seen_count = count;
    }
    for (auto &kv : g_incoming) {
        Incoming &in = kv.second;
        if (!in.fresh) continue;
        auto it = g_state_index.find(kv.first);
        if (it == g_state_index.end()) continue; // a late-created object may be found on a later step
        in.fresh = false;
        Object *o = it->second;
        // debugging aids: MP_LOG_LOADS=1 names every state before it is loaded, MP_LOAD_ONLY=techn|human
        // restricts loading to one class
        static int trace = -1, only;
        if (trace < 0) {
            char v[16] = "";
            trace = env_text("MP_LOG_LOADS", v, sizeof v);
            if (env_text("MP_LOAD_ONLY", v, sizeof v)) only = v[0] == 't' ? 1 : 2;
        }
        if (only && strcmp(o->iface, only == 1 ? "i_techn" : "i_human")) continue;
        if (!session_state_wanted(o)) continue;
        if (state_is_unit(o) && !state_handles_match(o, in.base.data(), in.base.size())) {
            g_stat_unsafe++;        // see state_handles_match: followed by position only
            continue;
        }
        if (trace) mp_log("load %s (%zu bytes)", o->full, in.base.size());
        // versus: the two games look at every unit from opposite sides (see state_load_keep_motion)
        int r = state_is_unit(o) ? state_load_keep_motion(o, in.base.data(), in.base.size(), g_translate)
                                 : state_load(o, in.base.data(), in.base.size());
        g_stat_loads++;
        netlog_count("state_loaded");
        if (r) {
            g_stat_bad++;
            netlog_count("state_rejected");
        }
    }
}

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
size_t statesync_test_incoming_count() { return g_incoming.size(); }
bool statesync_test_fresh(u64 key) {
    auto it = g_incoming.find(key);
    return it != g_incoming.end() && it->second.fresh;
}
double statesync_test_budget() { return g_budget; }
#endif

void statesync_report(void)
{
    if (!g_stat_objects && !g_stat_loads && !g_stat_unsafe) return;
    mp_log("state: 5 s: sent %llu object states (%.1f KB/s on the wire for %.1f KB/s of state), loaded %llu (%.1f KB/s received, %llu rejected), %llu not loaded (render handles differ)",
           g_stat_objects, g_stat_bytes / 5120.0, g_stat_raw / 5120.0, g_stat_loads, g_stat_received / 5120.0, g_stat_bad, g_stat_unsafe);
    g_stat_bytes = g_stat_objects = g_stat_raw = g_stat_loads = g_stat_bad = g_stat_received = g_stat_unsafe = 0;
    if (u64 bad = state_bad_lookups()) mp_log("state: 5 s: %llu model node look-ups ended early (a handle from the other game named no node here)", bad);
}

// Frozen non-tactical state handler. M5 is out of scope, so keep the exact implementation
// from 30405d1 separately from the hardened tactical implementation in statesync.cpp.
// Load headers outside the namespace; the included copy's own includes are then guarded.
// The namespace isolates not only functions but also its different Track/Incoming layouts
// and their template instantiations from the newer tactical code (avoiding ODR corruption).
#include "common.h"
#include "env_value.h"
#include "netlog.h"
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

namespace frozen_non_tactical {
#include "statesync_legacy_30405d1.inc"
}

void statesync_legacy_on_packet(const unsigned char *data, int size, void (*send)(const void *, int), bool versus)
{
    frozen_non_tactical::statesync_on_packet(data, size, send, versus);
}
void statesync_legacy_reset(void)
{
    frozen_non_tactical::statesync_reset();
}
