==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/statesync_legacy_30405d1.inc (sha256 d932d7f46423621f1628c480fe01f874a0c2703d1c088236a444b6cf8c348671) ====================
// Replication of full object state (see state.cpp) from the game that owns an object to the other game.
//
// The owner saves each object a few times a second. When the bytes differ from what the other side is
// known to have, it sends the difference: XOR against that known version, run-length encoded, cut into
// datagrams. The receiver rebuilds the blob, acknowledges the version, and loads it into its own copy of
// the object before the next simulation step. Versions only advance on acknowledgement, so a lost
// datagram costs one retry and never corrupts state.
#include "common.h"
#include "env_value.h"
#include "netlog.h"
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

typedef std::vector<unsigned char> Blob;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- delta coding
static void put_varint(Blob &out, u32 v)
{
    while (v >= 0x80) { out.push_back((unsigned char)(v | 0x80)); v >>= 7; }
    out.push_back((unsigned char)v);
}

static bool get_varint(const unsigned char *&p, const unsigned char *end, u32 &v)
{
    v = 0;
    for (int shift = 0; p < end && shift < 35; shift += 7) {
        unsigned char b = *p++;
        v |= (u32)(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// (unchanged run, changed run, changed bytes)* ; bytes are cur XOR base, base padded with zeros
static void encode_delta(const Blob &base, const Blob &cur, Blob &out)
{
    out.clear();
    size_t n = cur.size(), i = 0;
    auto same = [&](size_t k) { return cur[k] == (k < base.size() ? base[k] : 0); };
    while (i < n) {
        size_t z = i;
        while (z < n && same(z)) z++;
        size_t l = z;
        // a changed run ends at four or more unchanged bytes (shorter gaps are cheaper to send as data)
        for (size_t gap = 0; l < n; l++) {
            if (same(l)) { if (++gap >= 4) { l -= gap - 1; break; } }
            else gap = 0;
        }
        if (l > n) l = n;
        while (l > z && same(l - 1)) l--;
        put_varint(out, (u32)(z - i));
        put_varint(out, (u32)(l - z));
        for (size_t k = z; k < l; k++) out.push_back(cur[k] ^ (k < base.size() ? base[k] : 0));
        i = l;
    }
}

static bool decode_delta(const Blob &base, const unsigned char *p, size_t len, u32 raw_size, Blob &out)
{
    if (raw_size > (16u << 20)) return false;
    out.assign(raw_size, 0);
    memcpy(out.data(), base.data(), min(base.size(), (size_t)raw_size));
    const unsigned char *end = p + len;
    size_t i = 0;
    while (p < end) {
        u32 z, l;
        if (!get_varint(p, end, z) || !get_varint(p, end, l)) return false;
        i += z;
        if (i + l > raw_size || p + l > end) return false;
        for (u32 k = 0; k < l; k++) {
            // XOR against the padded base: bytes beyond the old size were zero
            out[i + k] = (unsigned char)((i + k < base.size() ? base[i + k] : 0) ^ p[k]);
        }
        p += l;
        i += l;
    }
    return true;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- shared
static const u32 k_frag = 1100;
static void (*g_send)(const void *, int);
static u64 g_stat_bytes, g_stat_objects, g_stat_raw, g_stat_loads, g_stat_bad, g_stat_received, g_stat_unsafe;
static bool g_translate;      // versus: the two games see every unit from opposite sides

u64 statesync_key(Object *o)
{
    u64 kind = !strcmp(o->iface, "i_techn") ? 1 : !strcmp(o->iface, "i_human") ? 2 : 3;
    if (kind == 3) return ((u64)3 << 32) | fnv((const unsigned char *)o->full, strlen(o->full));
    const char *us = strrchr(o->full, '_');
    return (kind << 32) | (us ? strtoul(us + 1, 0, 16) : 0);
}

// Singleton simulation objects that are replicated besides the units. Each was checked to load cleanly
// into a running battle; the others either hold only local presentation state or are too large to matter.
static bool replicated_singleton(Object *o)
{
    static const char *names[] = { "i_divisions", "i_statistic", "i_trigman", "i_formation", "i_fireman", "i_shellphys" };
    static int enabled = -1;
    if (enabled < 0) {
        enabled = 0;
        env_integer("MP_SYNC_WORLD", &enabled);
    }
    if (!enabled) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < enabled && i < 6; i++)
        if (!strcmp(o->iface, names[i])) return true;
    return false;
}

static bool replicated(Object *o) { return state_is_unit(o) || replicated_singleton(o); }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- owner side
struct Sent { u32 ver; Blob blob; };
struct Track {
    Blob base;                     // the newest version the other side has confirmed
    u32 base_ver = 0, last_ver = 0;
    std::vector<Sent> sent_versions;   // sent since then, newest last; any of them may be confirmed next
    DWORD checked = 0, sent = 0;
};
static std::unordered_map<u64, Track> g_tracks;
static u32 g_cursor;
static double g_budget;
static DWORD g_last_frame;

static void send_object(u64 key, Track &t, DWORD now)
{
    static Blob delta;
    const Blob &cur = t.sent_versions.back().blob;
    encode_delta(t.base, cur, delta);
    u32 nfrags = (u32)((delta.size() + k_frag - 1) / k_frag);
    if (!nfrags) nfrags = 1;
    for (u32 f = 0; f < nfrags; f++) {
        unsigned char packet[sizeof(ObjectFrag) + k_frag];
        ObjectFrag *h = (ObjectFrag *)packet;
        u32 off = f * k_frag, len = (u32)min((size_t)k_frag, delta.size() - off);
        h->h = make_head(PKT_OBJECT, (int)len);
        h->key = key;
        h->version = t.sent_versions.back().ver;
        h->base_version = t.base_ver;
        h->raw_size = (u32)cur.size();
        h->delta_size = (u32)delta.size();
        h->frag = (unsigned short)f;
        h->nfrags = (unsigned short)nfrags;
        memcpy(h + 1, delta.data() + off, len);
        g_send(packet, (int)(sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len));
        g_budget -= sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len + 28;
        g_stat_bytes += sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len;
    }
    t.sent = now;
    g_stat_objects++;
    g_stat_raw += cur.size();
}

// Called every frame by the side that owns objects. `owns` decides per object.
void statesync_owner_frame(ObjMan *om, DWORD now, bool (*owns)(Object *), void (*send)(const void *, int))
{
    static int kbps = -1;
    static Blob blob;
    if (kbps < 0) {
        kbps = 600;
        int requested;
        if (env_integer("MP_STATE_KBPS", &requested) && requested >= 0) kbps = requested;
    }
    g_send = send;
    double dt = g_last_frame ? (now - g_last_frame) / 1000.0 : 0;
    g_last_frame = now;
    g_budget += dt * kbps * 1024;
    if (g_budget > kbps * 1024 * 0.25) g_budget = kbps * 1024 * 0.25;       // at most a quarter second saved up
    u32 count;
    Object **all = all_objects(om, &count);
    for (u32 n = 0; n < count && g_budget > 0; n++) {
        Object *o = all[g_cursor++ % count];
        if (!replicated(o) || !owns(o) || !session_state_wanted(o)) continue;
        Track &t = g_tracks[statesync_key(o)];
        bool unit = state_is_unit(o);
        if (now - t.checked < (!strcmp(o->iface, "i_techn") ? 700u : 400u)) continue;
        t.checked = now;
        if (!state_save(o, blob)) continue;
        if (unit && blob.size() >= UNIT_MOTION_SIZE) memset(blob.data(), 0, UNIT_MOTION_SIZE);
        const Blob &newest = t.sent_versions.empty() ? t.base : t.sent_versions.back().blob;
        if (blob.size() == newest.size() && !memcmp(blob.data(), newest.data(), blob.size())) {
            if (!t.sent_versions.empty() && now - t.sent > 400) send_object(statesync_key(o), t, now);    // not confirmed: again
            continue;
        }
        if (t.sent_versions.size() >= 6) t.sent_versions.erase(t.sent_versions.begin());
        t.sent_versions.push_back({ ++t.last_ver, blob });
        send_object(statesync_key(o), t, now);
    }
}

void statesync_reset(void)
{
    g_tracks.clear();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- receiving side
struct Incoming {
    Blob base;                     // last complete state received
    u32 base_ver = 0;
    Blob partial;                  // delta being reassembled
    u32 part_ver = 0, part_have = 0, part_frags = 0;
    std::vector<unsigned char> got;
    bool fresh = false;            // base not yet loaded into the object
};
static std::unordered_map<u64, Incoming> g_incoming;

void statesync_on_packet(const unsigned char *data, int size, void (*send)(const void *, int), bool versus)
{
    g_translate = versus;
    const Head *head = (const Head *)data;
    if (head->type == PKT_OBJECT_ACK && size >= (int)sizeof(ObjectAck)) {
        const ObjectAck *a = (const ObjectAck *)data;
        // The other side reports the version it now holds. Later sends are differences against that one.
        auto it = g_tracks.find(a->key);
        if (it == g_tracks.end() || a->version == it->second.base_ver) return;
        Track &t = it->second;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < t.sent_versions.size(); i++)
            if (t.sent_versions[i].ver == a->version) {
                t.base.swap(t.sent_versions[i].blob);
                t.base_ver = a->version;
                t.sent_versions.erase(t.sent_versions.begin(), t.sent_versions.begin() + i + 1);
                return;
            }
        // it holds something we no longer keep: start over from nothing
        t.base.clear();
        t.base_ver = 0;
        return;
    }
    if (head->type != PKT_OBJECT || size < (int)sizeof(ObjectFrag)) return;
    const ObjectFrag *f = (const ObjectFrag *)data;
    u32 len = head->count;
    if (size < (int)(sizeof(ObjectFrag) + len) || f->frag >= f->nfrags || f->delta_size > (16u << 20)) return;
    Incoming &in = g_incoming[f->key];
    ObjectAck ack = { make_head(PKT_OBJECT_ACK), f->key, f->version };
    if (f->version == in.base_ver && in.base_ver) { send(&ack, sizeof ack); return; }      // already have it: the ack was lost
    if (f->base_version != in.base_ver && f->base_version != 0) {
        // built on a version we do not hold: say which one we do hold, so the owner can rebase
        if (f->frag == 0) { ack.version = in.base_ver; send(&ack, sizeof ack); }
        return;
    }
    if (in.part_ver != f->version || in.part_frags != f->nfrags) {
        in.part_ver = f->version;
        in.part_frags = f->nfrags;
        in.part_have = 0;
        in.got.assign(f->nfrags, 0);
        in.partial.assign(f->delta_size, 0);
    }
    if ((size_t)f->frag * k_frag + len > in.partial.size() || in.got[f->frag]) return;
    memcpy(in.partial.data() + (size_t)f->frag * k_frag, f + 1, len);
    in.got[f->frag] = 1;
    if (++in.part_have < in.part_frags) return;
    Blob next;
    static const Blob nothing;
    if (!decode_delta(f->base_version ? in.base : nothing, in.partial.data(), in.partial.size(), f->raw_size, next)) { g_stat_bad++; return; }
    in.base.swap(next);
    in.base_ver = f->version;
    in.part_ver = 0;
    in.fresh = true;
    g_stat_received += in.partial.size();
    send(&ack, sizeof ack);
}

// Before a simulation step: load every newly received state into its object.
void statesync_apply(ObjMan *om)
{
    static std::unordered_map<u64, Object *> index;
    static u32 seen_count;
    bool any = false;
    for (auto &kv : g_incoming) if (kv.second.fresh) { any = true; break; }
    if (!any) return;
    u32 count;
    Object **all = all_objects(om, &count);
    if (count != seen_count) {
        index.clear();
        for (u32 i = 0; i < count; i++) if (replicated(all[i])) index[statesync_key(all[i])] = all[i];
        seen_count = count;
    }
    for (auto &kv : g_incoming) {
        Incoming &in = kv.second;
        if (!in.fresh) continue;
        in.fresh = false;
        auto it = index.find(kv.first);
        if (it == index.end()) continue;
        Object *o = it->second;
        // debugging aids: MP_LOG_LOADS=1 names every state before it is loaded, MP_LOAD_ONLY=techn|human
        // restricts loading to one class
        static int trace = -1, only;
        if (trace < 0) {
            char v[16] = "";
            trace = env_text("MP_LOG_LOADS", v, sizeof v);
            if (env_text("MP_LOAD_ONLY", v, sizeof v)) only = v[0] == 't' ? 1 : 2;
        }
        if (only && strcmp(o->iface, only == 1 ? "i_techn" : "i_human")) continue;
        if (!session_state_wanted(o)) continue;
        if (state_is_unit(o) && !state_handles_match(o, in.base.data(), in.base.size())) {
            g_stat_unsafe++;        // see state_handles_match: followed by position only
            continue;
        }
        if (trace) mp_log("load %s (%zu bytes)", o->full, in.base.size());
        // versus: the two games look at every unit from opposite sides (see state_load_keep_motion)
        int r = state_is_unit(o) ? state_load_keep_motion(o, in.base.data(), in.base.size(), g_translate)
                                 : state_load(o, in.base.data(), in.base.size());
        g_stat_loads++;
        netlog_count("state_loaded");
        if (r) {
            g_stat_bad++;
            netlog_count("state_rejected");
        }
    }
}

void statesync_report(void)
{
    if (!g_stat_objects && !g_stat_loads && !g_stat_unsafe) return;
    mp_log("state: 5 s: sent %llu object states (%.1f KB/s on the wire for %.1f KB/s of state), loaded %llu (%.1f KB/s received, %llu rejected), %llu not loaded (render handles differ)",
           g_stat_objects, g_stat_bytes / 5120.0, g_stat_raw / 5120.0, g_stat_loads, g_stat_received / 5120.0, g_stat_bad, g_stat_unsafe);
    g_stat_bytes = g_stat_objects = g_stat_raw = g_stat_loads = g_stat_bad = g_stat_received = g_stat_unsafe = 0;
    if (u64 bad = state_bad_lookups()) mp_log("state: 5 s: %llu model node look-ups ended early (a handle from the other game named no node here)", bad);
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/statesync_legacy_30405d1.inc ====================

// Implementation of the Steam layer described in docs/V20_STEAM_LAYER.md.
//
// Everything here was reconstructed from the v20 module's machine code, so the names of the
// environment variables, the log lines, the callback ids and the 21 resolved entry points are not
// invented - they are what the released module does.  What is new is the arrangement: this is a
// plain C++ file with no SDK and no link-time dependency on Steam, so it can be built, reviewed and
// tested like any other file in the tree.
#include "steam.h"

#include <atomic>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- Steam flat API
// Only the pieces this file touches.  A CSteamID is a uint64 by value; every function returns bool
// unless noted, which is how the flat wrappers in steam_api64.dll behave.
typedef uint64_t csteamid;

typedef void *(*fn_steamfriends_v017)();
typedef void *(*fn_steamuser_v021)();
typedef void *(*fn_steamnetworking_v006)();
typedef void *(*fn_steamutils_v010)();

typedef uint64_t (*fn_user_get_id)(void *user);
typedef bool (*fn_user_logged_on)(void *user);
typedef uint32_t (*fn_utils_app_id)(void *utils);
typedef const char *(*fn_friends_name)(void *friends);
typedef bool (*fn_friends_set_presence)(void *friends, const char *key, const char *value);
typedef const char *(*fn_friends_get_presence)(void *friends, csteamid friend_id, const char *key);
typedef void (*fn_friends_request_presence)(void *friends, csteamid friend_id);
typedef int (*fn_friends_count)(void *friends, int flags);
typedef csteamid (*fn_friends_by_index)(void *friends, int index, int flags);
typedef const char *(*fn_friends_persona_name)(void *friends, csteamid friend_id);
// FriendGameInfo_t from Steamworks is 24 bytes with the lobby ID at +16 on Windows x64.
struct FriendGameInfo {
    uint64_t game_id;
    uint32_t ip;
    uint16_t game_port, query_port;
    uint64_t lobby_id;
};
static_assert(sizeof(FriendGameInfo) == 24 && offsetof(FriendGameInfo, lobby_id) == 16, "Steam FriendGameInfo ABI");
typedef bool (*fn_friends_game_played)(void *friends, csteamid friend_id, FriendGameInfo *info);

typedef bool (*fn_net_send)(void *net, csteamid peer, const void *data, uint32_t len,
                            int send_type, int channel);
typedef bool (*fn_net_available)(void *net, uint32_t *size, int channel);
typedef bool (*fn_net_read)(void *net, void *dest, uint32_t capacity, uint32_t *size,
                            csteamid *peer, int channel);
typedef bool (*fn_net_accept)(void *net, csteamid peer);
typedef bool (*fn_net_relay)(void *net, bool allow);
typedef void (*fn_register_callback)(void *callback, int id);

struct steam_api {
    // interfaces
    fn_steamfriends_v017 friends_iface;
    fn_steamuser_v021 user_iface;
    fn_steamnetworking_v006 net_iface;
    fn_steamutils_v010 utils_iface;
    // functions
    fn_user_get_id user_get_id;
    fn_user_logged_on user_logged_on;
    fn_utils_app_id utils_app_id;
    fn_friends_name friends_name;
    fn_friends_set_presence set_presence;
    fn_friends_get_presence get_presence;
    fn_friends_request_presence request_presence;
    fn_friends_count friends_count;
    fn_friends_by_index friend_by_index;
    fn_friends_persona_name friend_persona;
    fn_friends_game_played friend_game_played;
    fn_net_send net_send;
    fn_net_available net_available;
    fn_net_read net_read;
    fn_net_accept net_accept;
    fn_net_relay net_relay;
    fn_register_callback register_callback;
};

// The 21 names, in the order the v20 module resolves them.
static const char *const kEntryPoints[] = {
    "SteamAPI_SteamFriends_v017", "SteamAPI_SteamUser_v021",
    "SteamAPI_SteamNetworking_v006", "SteamAPI_SteamUtils_v010",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamUser_GetSteamID", "SteamAPI_ISteamUser_BLoggedOn",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamUtils_GetAppID", "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_GetPersonaName",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_SetRichPresence", "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_GetFriendRichPresence",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_RequestFriendRichPresence", "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_GetFriendCount",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_GetFriendByIndex", "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_GetFriendPersonaName",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamFriends_GetFriendGamePlayed", "SteamAPI_ISteamNetworking_SendP2PPacket",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamNetworking_IsP2PPacketAvailable", "SteamAPI_ISteamNetworking_ReadP2PPacket",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamNetworking_AcceptP2PSessionWithUser",
    "SteamAPI_ISteamNetworking_AllowP2PPacketRelay", "SteamAPI_RegisterCallback",
};

// Steamworks callback bases are 1200 (networking) and 300 (friends): presence update is
// +36, while Join Game is +37. Treating 336 as the 264-byte join payload reads past a
// 16-byte presence update and never handles the real join event (337).
enum { CALLBACK_SESSION_REQUEST = 1202, CALLBACK_SESSION_FAIL = 1203,
       CALLBACK_PRESENCE_UPDATED = 336, CALLBACK_JOIN_REQUESTED = 337 };

// EP2PSend.  The game's protocol is written for UDP (it does its own pacing), so unreliable is the
// faithful default; k_EP2PSendReliable is one constant away if a test wants ordering instead.
enum { SEND_UNRELIABLE = 0, SEND_RELIABLE = 2 };
static const int kSendType = SEND_UNRELIABLE;

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- callback plumbing
// Layout required by the flat API: vtable, one byte of flags, the callback id, then the payload.
// This is the SDK's CCallbackBase, restated so the module does not need the SDK headers.
class callback_base {
public:
    callback_base() : flags(0), id(0) {}
    // Steam's CCallbackBase ABI explicitly has NO virtual destructor. Adding one moves every
    // vtable slot, so Steam would call the wrong function for Run/GetCallbackSizeBytes.
    virtual void run(void *param) = 0;
    virtual void run(void *param, bool io_failure, uint64_t call) = 0;
    virtual int size_of() = 0;

    unsigned char flags;
    int id;
};

struct session_request_cb : callback_base {
    uint64_t remote;
    void run(void *param) override;
    void run(void *param, bool, uint64_t) override { run(param); }
    int size_of() override { return (int)sizeof(uint64_t); }
};

struct session_fail_cb : callback_base {
    uint64_t remote;
    unsigned char error;
    void run(void *param) override;
    void run(void *param, bool, uint64_t) override { run(param); }
    int size_of() override { return 16; }
};

struct join_requested_cb : callback_base {
    uint64_t friend_id;
    char connect[256];
    void run(void *param) override;
    void run(void *param, bool, uint64_t) override { run(param); }
    int size_of() override { return (int)(sizeof(uint64_t) + 256); }
};

struct presence_updated_cb : callback_base {
    void run(void *param) override;
    void run(void *param, bool, uint64_t) override { run(param); }
    int size_of() override { return 16; }     // FriendRichPresenceUpdate_t, Windows x64 packing
};

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- state
static bool g_initialised;      // steam_init() has run to completion at least once
static bool g_ready;            // a transport is usable
static bool g_fake;             // MP_STEAM_FAKE stand-in instead of real Steam
static bool g_fake_requested;   // do not silently fall back to real Steam after a stand-in error
static bool g_off;              // MP_STEAM=0: explicitly no Steam
static std::atomic<bool> g_lobby_inbox(false); // one owner for the menu's Steam receive queue
static DWORD g_retry_at;         // the game may load/initialise Steam after the network module
static steam_api g_api;
static void *g_friends;
static void *g_user;
static void *g_net;
static void *g_utils;
static uint64_t g_user_id;
static uint32_t g_app_id;
static int g_friend_count;
static char g_persona[STEAM_NAME_MAX] = {0};
static char g_presence[128] = {0};
static char g_connect[256] = {0};
static bool g_broadcasting;      // last "can be joined" state we told Steam about
static bool g_broadcast_valid;   // both Steam writes actually succeeded
static unsigned g_operation;     // and the operation it was for

static SOCKET g_fake_socket = INVALID_SOCKET;
static uint16_t g_fake_port;
static char g_fake_file[MAX_PATH] = {0};
static char g_fake_persona[STEAM_NAME_MAX] = {0};
static unsigned long long g_sent_packets, g_received_packets;   // since the last five-second line
static unsigned long long g_sent_total, g_received_total;
static DWORD g_stats_at;

static session_request_cb g_cb_request;
static session_fail_cb g_cb_fail;
static join_requested_cb g_cb_join;
static presence_updated_cb g_cb_presence;

static uint64_t g_join_id;
static bool g_join_pending;
static const char *g_join_source = "";
static char g_command_line[2048] = {0};

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- small helpers
static bool env_number(const char *name, uint64_t *out)
{
    char text[64] = {0};
    DWORD got = GetEnvironmentVariableA(name, text, (DWORD)sizeof(text));
    if (!got || got >= sizeof(text))
        return false;
    return sscanf_s(text, "%llu", out) == 1;
}

static void sanitise(char *text, size_t size)
{
    // Persona names come from outside; everything but letters, digits, '_' and '-' becomes '_',
    // so a name can never inject a path or a command (v20 does the same).
    for (size_t i = 0; i < size && text[i]; ++i) {
        unsigned char c = (unsigned char)text[i];
        bool ok = (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9')
                  || c == '_' || c == '-';
        if (!ok)
            text[i] = '_';
    }
}

static void copy_text(char *dest, size_t size, const char *source)
{
    if (!source)
        source = "";
    size_t length = strlen(source);
    if (length >= size)
        length = size - 1;
    memcpy(dest, source, length);
    dest[length] = 0;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- stand-in mode
static bool fake_open(uint16_t port)
{
    WSADATA wsa;
    if (WSAStartup(MAKEWORD(2, 2), &wsa) != 0)
        return false;
    g_fake_socket = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, IPPROTO_UDP);
    if (g_fake_socket == INVALID_SOCKET) {
        WSACleanup();
        return false;
    }
    sockaddr_in address = {};
    address.sin_family = AF_INET;
    address.sin_port = htons(port);
    address.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_LOOPBACK);
    if (bind(g_fake_socket, (sockaddr *)&address, sizeof(address)) != 0) {
        closesocket(g_fake_socket);
        g_fake_socket = INVALID_SOCKET;
        WSACleanup();
        return false;
    }
    u_long nonblocking = 1;
    ioctlsocket(g_fake_socket, FIONBIO, &nonblocking);

    char temp[MAX_PATH] = {0};
    DWORD got = GetTempPathA((DWORD)sizeof(temp), temp);
    if (got && got < sizeof(temp))
        sprintf_s(g_fake_file, sizeof(g_fake_file), "%sgtmf_fake_steam_%u.txt", temp, port);
    return true;
}

static void fake_flush()
{
    // The stand-in keeps its notes in a file the way v20's does: a second process can read where the
    // first one says it is, without any Steam involvement.  Two keys, like real rich presence.
    if (!g_fake_file[0])
        return;
    FILE *file = nullptr;
    if (fopen_s(&file, g_fake_file, "w") == 0 && file) {
        fprintf(file, "persona=%s\ngtmf=%s\nconnect=%s\n", g_fake_persona, g_presence, g_connect);
        fclose(file);
    }
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- callbacks
void session_request_cb::run(void *param)
{
    if (!param)
        return;
    uint64_t from = *(uint64_t *)param;
    if (g_api.net_accept && g_net) {
        g_api.net_accept(g_net, from);
        steam_log("steam: session asked for by %llu - accepted", from);
        return;
    }
    steam_log("steam: session asked for by %llu - dropped (no networking interface)", from);
}

void session_fail_cb::run(void *param)
{
    if (!param)
        return;
    uint64_t from = *(uint64_t *)param;
    unsigned char error = *((unsigned char *)param + 8);
    steam_log("steam: no connection to %llu could be made (error %u)", from, error);
}

void join_requested_cb::run(void *param)
{
    if (!param) return;
    // Steam supplies a fixed 256-byte field, which need not end in NUL. Its contents can be either
    // steam:<id> or the full connect string published by steam_broadcast().
    char connect[257];
    memcpy(connect, (const char *)param + 8, 256);
    connect[256] = 0;
    steam_log("steam: Steam's Join Game asks to join %.256s (a friend's game, while this game runs)", connect);
    if (_strnicmp(connect, "steam:", 6) == 0) {
        g_join_id = _strtoui64(connect + 6, nullptr, 10);
        g_join_pending = g_join_id != 0;
        if (g_join_pending) g_join_source = "a friend's game";
    } else if (const char *marker = strstr(connect, "+gtmf_join=steam:")) {
        uint64_t id = _strtoui64(marker + strlen("+gtmf_join=steam:"), nullptr, 10);
        if (id) {
            g_join_id = id;
            g_join_pending = true;
            g_join_source = "a friend's game";
        }
    }
}

void presence_updated_cb::run(void *param)
{
    (void)param; // presence update 336 is not a Join Game request
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- interface binding
static bool bind_interfaces(HMODULE library)
{
    if (!library) {
        steam_log("steam: the game has not loaded Steam's library - no Steam joining");
        return false;
    }
    void *slots[21] = {0};
    for (int i = 0; i < 21; ++i) {
        slots[i] = (void *)GetProcAddress(library, kEntryPoints[i]);
        if (!slots[i]) {
            // Name the missing entry point: after a game update this is the line that explains it.
            steam_log("steam: steam_api64.dll has no %s - no Steam joining", kEntryPoints[i]);
            return false;
        }
    }
    g_api.friends_iface = (fn_steamfriends_v017)slots[0];
    g_api.user_iface = (fn_steamuser_v021)slots[1];
    g_api.net_iface = (fn_steamnetworking_v006)slots[2];
    g_api.utils_iface = (fn_steamutils_v010)slots[3];
    g_api.user_get_id = (fn_user_get_id)slots[4];
    g_api.user_logged_on = (fn_user_logged_on)slots[5];
    g_api.utils_app_id = (fn_utils_app_id)slots[6];
    g_api.friends_name = (fn_friends_name)slots[7];
    g_api.set_presence = (fn_friends_set_presence)slots[8];
    g_api.get_presence = (fn_friends_get_presence)slots[9];
    g_api.request_presence = (fn_friends_request_presence)slots[10];
    g_api.friends_count = (fn_friends_count)slots[11];
    g_api.friend_by_index = (fn_friends_by_index)slots[12];
    g_api.friend_persona = (fn_friends_persona_name)slots[13];
    g_api.friend_game_played = (fn_friends_game_played)slots[14];
    g_api.net_send = (fn_net_send)slots[15];
    g_api.net_available = (fn_net_available)slots[16];
    g_api.net_read = (fn_net_read)slots[17];
    g_api.net_accept = (fn_net_accept)slots[18];
    g_api.net_relay = (fn_net_relay)slots[19];
    g_api.register_callback = (fn_register_callback)slots[20];
    return true;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle
bool steam_init()
{
    if (g_initialised) {
        if (g_ready || g_off || g_fake_requested) return g_ready;
        // basic_steam.codelib imports steam_api64.dll, but it can be loaded after this module.
        // Never load or initialise a foreign DLL ourselves; retry the game's own library once it
        // exists, at most once per five seconds while its interfaces are still being brought up.
        DWORD now = GetTickCount();
        if (!GetModuleHandleA("steam_api64.dll") || now - g_retry_at < 5000) return false;
    }
    g_initialised = true;
    g_retry_at = GetTickCount();

    uint64_t value = 0;
    if (env_number("MP_STEAM", &value) && value == 0) {
        g_off = true;
        steam_log("steam: switched off (MP_STEAM=0)");
        return false;
    }

    if (env_number("MP_STEAM_FAKE", &value) && value != 0) {
        g_fake_requested = true;
        if (value > 65535) {
            steam_log("steam: test stand-in port %llu is outside 1..65535", value);
            return false;
        }
        g_fake = true;
        g_fake_port = (uint16_t)value;
        if (!fake_open(g_fake_port)) {
            steam_log("steam: test stand-in: UDP port %llu is taken", value);
            g_fake = false;
            return false;
        }
        g_user_id = value;
        g_app_id = 0;
        sprintf_s(g_persona, sizeof(g_persona), "steam:%llu", value);
        g_ready = true;
        copy_text(g_fake_persona, sizeof(g_fake_persona), g_persona);
        steam_log("steam: TEST STAND-IN for Steam: this game is 'steam:%llu',"
                  " its datagrams travel over UDP on this PC", value);
        fake_flush();
        return true;
    }

#if defined(__MINGW32__) && !defined(_MSC_VER)
    // The GNU C++ ABI orders overloaded virtuals differently from the MSVC Steam DLL. This
    // toolchain is used for cross-compilation checks only; never register incompatible callbacks.
    g_off = true;
    steam_log("steam: incompatible GNU callback ABI; build with the Windows MSVC toolchain%s", "");
    return false;
#endif
    HMODULE library = GetModuleHandleA("steam_api64.dll");
    if (!bind_interfaces(library))
        return false;

    g_friends = g_api.friends_iface ? g_api.friends_iface() : nullptr;
    g_user = g_api.user_iface ? g_api.user_iface() : nullptr;
    g_net = g_api.net_iface ? g_api.net_iface() : nullptr;
    g_utils = g_api.utils_iface ? g_api.utils_iface() : nullptr;
    if (!g_friends || !g_user || !g_net || !g_utils) {
        steam_log("steam: Steam is not running for this game - no Steam joining");
        return false;
    }

    g_user_id = g_api.user_get_id(g_user);
    g_app_id = g_api.utils_app_id(g_utils);
    const char *persona = g_api.friends_name(g_friends);
    copy_text(g_persona, sizeof(g_persona), persona ? persona : "");
    sanitise(g_persona, sizeof(g_persona));
    if (!g_user_id) {
        steam_log("steam: no Steam user - no Steam joining");
        return false;
    }

    g_api.net_relay(g_net, true);
    g_cb_request.id = CALLBACK_SESSION_REQUEST;
    g_cb_fail.id = CALLBACK_SESSION_FAIL;
    g_cb_join.id = CALLBACK_JOIN_REQUESTED;
    g_cb_presence.id = CALLBACK_PRESENCE_UPDATED;
    g_api.register_callback(&g_cb_request, CALLBACK_SESSION_REQUEST);
    g_api.register_callback(&g_cb_fail, CALLBACK_SESSION_FAIL);
    g_api.register_callback(&g_cb_presence, CALLBACK_PRESENCE_UPDATED);
    g_api.register_callback(&g_cb_join, CALLBACK_JOIN_REQUESTED);
    g_api.request_presence(g_friends, g_user_id);

    g_friend_count = g_api.friends_count ? g_api.friends_count(g_friends, 4) : 0;
    g_ready = true;
    steam_log("steam: ready - user %llu (%s), application %u, %d friends, logged on: %s",
              g_user_id, g_persona, g_app_id, g_friend_count,
              g_api.user_logged_on(g_user) ? "yes" : "no");
    steam_log("steam: Steam's callbacks arrive in this program");
    return true;
}

bool steam_ready() { return g_ready; }
bool steam_is_fake() { return g_fake; }
uint64_t steam_user_id() { return g_user_id; }
const char *steam_user_name() { return g_ready ? g_persona : ""; }
uint32_t steam_app_id() { return g_app_id; }
int steam_friend_count() { return g_friend_count; }

namespace {
struct SteamFriendsSource {
    int count() { return g_api.friends_count(g_friends, 4); } // k_EFriendFlagImmediate
    uint64_t id(int index) { return g_api.friend_by_index(g_friends, index, 4); }
    bool game(uint64_t id, uint64_t *game_id) {
        FriendGameInfo info = {};
        if (!g_api.friend_game_played(g_friends, id, &info)) return false;
        *game_id = info.game_id;
        return true;
    }
    const char *name(uint64_t id) { return g_api.friend_persona(g_friends, id); }
    const char *note(uint64_t id) { return g_api.get_presence(g_friends, id, "gtmf"); }
    void request(uint64_t id) { g_api.request_presence(g_friends, id); }
};
}
size_t steam_friends_playing(lobby_friends::Friend *out, size_t capacity, bool request_presence)
{
    if (!g_ready || g_fake || !g_friends || !g_api.friends_count || !g_api.friend_by_index ||
        !g_api.friend_game_played || !g_api.friend_persona || !g_api.get_presence || !g_api.request_presence)
        return 0;
    SteamFriendsSource source;
    return lobby_friends::collect(source, g_app_id, g_user_id, out, capacity, request_presence);
}

bool steam_lobby_claim()
{
    bool expected = false;
    return g_lobby_inbox.compare_exchange_strong(expected, true, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
}
void steam_lobby_release() { g_lobby_inbox.store(false, std::memory_order_release); }
bool steam_lobby_receiving() { return g_lobby_inbox.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }

void steam_shutdown()
{
    steam_lobby_release();
    if (g_fake_socket != INVALID_SOCKET) {
        closesocket(g_fake_socket);
        g_fake_socket = INVALID_SOCKET;
        WSACleanup();
    }
    g_ready = false;
}

void steam_traffic_totals(unsigned long long *sent, unsigned long long *received)
{
    if (sent)
        *sent = g_sent_total;
    if (received)
        *received = g_received_total;
}

// v20 counts what actually crosses the transport and says so every five seconds: in a battle that
// is the only line that tells "Steam is being used" from "Steam was initialised and nothing goes
// through it".  The stand-in carries the same counter, so the number is meaningful without Steam.
static void report_traffic()
{
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    if (now - g_stats_at < 5000)
        return;
    g_stats_at = now;
    if (!g_sent_packets && !g_received_packets)
        return;
    steam_log("steam: 5 s: %llu datagrams sent through Steam, %llu received%s",
              g_sent_packets, g_received_packets, g_fake ? " (test stand-in)" : "");
    steam_traffic_note(g_sent_packets, g_received_packets);
    g_sent_packets = g_received_packets = 0;
}

void steam_frame()
{
    if (!g_ready)
        return;
    report_traffic();
    if (g_fake)
        return;
    // RunCallbacks is what makes the registered callbacks fire.  It lives in the game's
    // steam_api64.dll as a flat export; we resolve it lazily rather than add a 22nd slot for it.
    static void (*run_callbacks)() = nullptr;
    if (!run_callbacks) {
        HMODULE library = GetModuleHandleA("steam_api64.dll");
        if (library)
            run_callbacks = (void (*)())GetProcAddress(library, "SteamAPI_RunCallbacks");
    }
    if (run_callbacks)
        run_callbacks();
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- datagrams
bool steam_send(uint64_t peer, const void *data, int len)
{
    if (!g_ready || !data || len <= 0)
        return false;
    if (g_fake) {
        sockaddr_in address = {};
        address.sin_family = AF_INET;
        address.sin_port = htons((uint16_t)peer);
        address.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_LOOPBACK);
        int sent = sendto(g_fake_socket, (const char *)data, len, 0, (sockaddr *)&address,
                          sizeof(address));
        if (sent == len) {
            ++g_sent_packets;
            ++g_sent_total;
        }
        return sent == len;
    }
    bool ok = g_api.net_send(g_net, peer, data, (uint32_t)len, kSendType, STEAM_CHANNEL);
    if (ok) {
        ++g_sent_packets;
        ++g_sent_total;
    }
    return ok;
}

int steam_recv(uint64_t *peer, void *data, int capacity)
{
    if (!g_ready || !data || capacity <= 0)
        return -1;
    if (g_fake) {
        sockaddr_in address = {};
        int size = (int)sizeof(address);
        int got = recvfrom(g_fake_socket, (char *)data, capacity, 0, (sockaddr *)&address, &size);
        if (got <= 0)
            return 0;
        if (peer)
            *peer = ntohs(address.sin_port);
        ++g_received_packets;
        ++g_received_total;
        return got;
    }
    uint32_t size = 0;
    if (g_api.net_available(g_net, &size, STEAM_CHANNEL) && size > 0) {
        uint32_t read = 0;
        csteamid from = 0;
        if (g_api.net_read(g_net, data, (uint32_t)capacity, &read, &from, STEAM_CHANNEL)) {
            if (peer)
                *peer = from;
            ++g_received_packets;
            ++g_received_total;
            return (int)read;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- presence and join
bool steam_presence(const char *value)
{
    if (!g_ready || !value)
        return false;
    copy_text(g_presence, sizeof(g_presence), value);
    if (g_fake) {
        fake_flush();
        return true;
    }
    return g_api.set_presence && g_api.set_presence(g_friends, "gtmf", g_presence);
}

const char *steam_presence_value() { return g_presence; }

const char *steam_connect_text() { return g_connect; }

bool steam_broadcast(bool hosting, unsigned operation)
{
    if (!g_ready)
        return false;

    char note[32] = {0};
    char connect[256] = {0};
    if (hosting) {
        // The same words v20 puts there, character for character: a friend's Steam client shows
        // "Join Game" from the second key, and the first one is what the lobby list is keyed on.
        sprintf_s(note, sizeof(note), "%u game", operation);
        sprintf_s(connect, sizeof(connect), "root\\programs\\opgame.progpack, +gtmf_join=steam:%llu",
                  (unsigned long long)g_user_id);
    }

    if (g_broadcast_valid && g_broadcasting == hosting && g_operation == operation &&
        strcmp(g_presence, note) == 0 && strcmp(g_connect, connect) == 0) {
        if (g_fake) return true;
        // The menu and tactical programs can overlap. One may clear the account's presence after
        // this process advertised it. Verify both keys before treating the cached result as valid.
        const char *held_note = g_api.get_presence ? g_api.get_presence(g_friends, g_user_id, "gtmf") : nullptr;
        bool same_note = strcmp(held_note ? held_note : "", note) == 0;
        const char *held_connect = g_api.get_presence ? g_api.get_presence(g_friends, g_user_id, "connect") : nullptr;
        if (same_note && strcmp(held_connect ? held_connect : "", connect) == 0) return true;
    }

    copy_text(g_presence, sizeof(g_presence), note);
    copy_text(g_connect, sizeof(g_connect), connect);
    g_broadcasting = hosting;
    g_operation = operation;
    g_broadcast_valid = false;

    if (g_fake) {
        fake_flush();
        g_broadcast_valid = true;
        if (hosting)
            steam_log("steam: friends are told that this game can be joined (%s)", g_presence);
        else
            steam_log("steam: no longer shown to friends as a game to join%s", "");
        return true;
    }

    bool noted = g_api.set_presence && g_api.set_presence(g_friends, "gtmf", g_presence);
    const char *held = g_api.get_presence ? g_api.get_presence(g_friends, g_user_id, "gtmf") : nullptr;
    if (!noted || strcmp(held ? held : "", g_presence) != 0)
        steam_log("steam: Steam did not take the note for friends (asked for '%s', it holds '%s')",
                  g_presence, held ? held : "");
    bool connected = g_api.set_presence && g_api.set_presence(g_friends, "connect", g_connect);
    if (!connected) steam_log("steam: Steam did not take the \"connect\" text for its Join Game");
    // A failed attempt must not be cached as a success: a later frame can retry it.
    g_broadcast_valid = noted && connected;
    if (!g_broadcast_valid) return false;
    if (hosting)
        steam_log("steam: friends are told that this game can be joined (%s)", g_presence);
    else
        steam_log("steam: no longer shown to friends as a game to join%s", "");
    return true;
}

bool steam_presence_check(char *out, size_t size)
{
    if (out && size)
        out[0] = 0;
    if (!g_ready || g_fake)
        return false;
    const char *held = g_api.get_presence ? g_api.get_presence(g_friends, g_user_id, "gtmf") : nullptr;
    if (out && size)
        copy_text(out, size, held ? held : "");
    if (!held || strcmp(held, g_presence) != 0) {
        steam_log("steam: Steam did not take the note for friends (asked for '%s', it holds '%s')",
                  g_presence, held ? held : "");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void steam_note_command_line(const char *command_line)
{
    copy_text(g_command_line, sizeof(g_command_line), command_line ? command_line : "");
    const char *marker = strstr(g_command_line, "+gtmf_join=");
    if (!marker)
        return;
    marker += strlen("+gtmf_join=");
    if (_strnicmp(marker, "steam:", 6) != 0) {
        steam_log("steam: a join request that is not a multiplayer game's ('%.80s') - ignored", marker);
        return;
    }
    uint64_t id = _strtoui64(marker + 6, nullptr, 10);
    if (!id)
        return;
    g_join_id = id;
    g_join_pending = true;
    g_join_source = "the command line";
    steam_log("steam: Steam's Join Game asks to join steam:%llu (on the command line)", id);
}

bool steam_join_take(uint64_t *id)
{
    if (!g_join_pending)
        return false;
    if (id)
        *id = g_join_id;
    g_join_pending = false;
    return true;
}

const char *steam_join_source() { return g_join_source; }

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
bool steam_test_wait_packet(unsigned timeout_ms)
{
    if (!g_fake || g_fake_socket == INVALID_SOCKET) return false;
    fd_set readable;
    FD_ZERO(&readable); FD_SET(g_fake_socket, &readable);
    timeval timeout = { (long)(timeout_ms / 1000), (long)((timeout_ms % 1000) * 1000) };
    return select(0, &readable, nullptr, nullptr, &timeout) > 0;
}
#endif

void steam_test_set_command_line(const char *text) { steam_note_command_line(text); }

void steam_test_join_callback(const char *text, size_t size)
{
    unsigned char param[8 + 256] = {};
    if (text) memcpy(param + 8, text, size < 256 ? size : 256);
    g_cb_join.run(param);
}

bool steam_test_callback_abi()
{
    // Invoke the actual compiler-generated slots as SteamAPI does, not a direct C++ call.
    // x64 CCallbackBase is vptr + flag byte + padded callback id. MSVC reverses the two
    // overloaded Run slots; the Itanium ABI used by the cross-compiler keeps declaration order.
    callback_base *callbacks[] = { &g_cb_request, &g_cb_fail, &g_cb_presence, &g_cb_join };
    const int sizes[] = { 8, 16, 16, 264 };
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        callback_base *cb = callbacks[i];
        ptrdiff_t flags_at = (char *)&cb->flags - (char *)cb, id_at = (char *)&cb->id - (char *)cb;
        if (flags_at != 8 || id_at != 12) {
            steam_log("steam ABI selftest: callback %d fields at %td/%td instead of 8/12", i, flags_at, id_at);
            return false;
        }
        void **vt = *(void ***)cb;
        int actual = ((int (*)(callback_base *))vt[2])(cb);
        if (actual != sizes[i]) {
            steam_log("steam ABI selftest: callback %d slot 2 returned %d instead of %d", i, actual, sizes[i]);
            return false;
        }
    }
    if (CALLBACK_PRESENCE_UPDATED != 336 || CALLBACK_JOIN_REQUESTED != 337) return false;
    unsigned char payload[264] = {};
    memcpy(payload + 8, "steam:42", 9);
    void **vt = *(void ***)&g_cb_join;
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    const int single = 1, multi = 0;
#else
    const int single = 0, multi = 1; // e.g. the GNU/Itanium ABI used by the syntax-only build
#endif
    ((void (*)(callback_base *, void *))vt[single])(&g_cb_join, payload);
    if (!g_join_pending || g_join_id != 42) {
        steam_log("steam ABI selftest: single-argument Run dispatch failed%s", "");
        return false;
    }
    g_join_pending = false;
    g_join_id = 0;
    ((void (*)(callback_base *, void *, bool, uint64_t))vt[multi])(&g_cb_join, payload, false, 0);
    return g_join_pending && g_join_id == 42;
}

void steam_test_reset()
{
    steam_lobby_release();
    g_initialised = false;
    g_ready = false;
    g_fake = false;
    g_fake_requested = false;
    g_off = false;
    g_retry_at = 0;
    g_join_pending = false;
    g_join_id = 0;
    g_presence[0] = 0;
    g_connect[0] = 0;
    g_broadcasting = false;
    g_broadcast_valid = false;
    g_operation = 0;
    g_sent_packets = g_received_packets = g_sent_total = g_received_total = 0;
    g_stats_at = 0;
    if (g_fake_socket != INVALID_SOCKET) {
        closesocket(g_fake_socket);
        g_fake_socket = INVALID_SOCKET;
        WSACleanup();
    }
}

// Steam transport for the network module - our own implementation of the layer v20 has and the
// published v12 source does not (docs/V20_STEAM_LAYER.md describes what the v20 binary does).
//
// The point of the design is that nothing above this file knows Steam exists.  A peer is a
// sockaddr_in whose sa_family is STEAM_FAMILY and whose sa_data carries the Steam ID, exactly the
// way the v20 module does it, so the packet code, the HELLO handshake and the lobby keep working
// unchanged whether the bytes go over UDP or through Steam's P2P relay.
//
// Three modes, checked in this order:
//   MP_STEAM=0            off            - plain UDP, no steam_api64.dll needed
//   MP_STEAM_FAKE=<n>     stand-in       - identity "steam:<n>", datagrams over loopback UDP
//   otherwise             real Steam     - steam_api64.dll of the game, flat API through GetProcAddress
//
// No Steamworks SDK is required: the module resolves 21 entry points at run time, so it does not
// link against steam_api64.lib and cannot break when the game updates - a missing entry point is
// reported in the log instead of crashing at load.
#pragma once

// winsock2 must come before windows.h, or the old winsock1 headers win the race
#include <winsock2.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "steam_friends.h"

// A peer that lives inside Steam: sa_family is STEAM_FAMILY, the Steam ID sits at +8, the name
// at +32.  Same encoding as v20, so a packet can be addressed the same way for both transports.
enum { STEAM_FAMILY = 0x5354, STEAM_NAME_MAX = 64 };
enum { STEAM_CHANNEL = 0 };

struct steam_peer {
    unsigned short family;      // STEAM_FAMILY
    unsigned short pad;
    uint64_t id;                // CSteamID / the stand-in's number
    char name[STEAM_NAME_MAX];  // persona name, sanitised
};

// Logging is supplied by whoever links this file: the module forwards to mp_log so Steam lines land
// in the same log as everything else, the self-test prints to stdout.
void steam_log(const char *fmt, ...);

// Where the five-second traffic totals go.  The module feeds them to the run log; a console program
// (self-test, join launcher) links the empty implementation in steam_logger.cpp.  One of the two is
// always linked, so steam.cpp never needs to know which world it is in.
void steam_traffic_note(unsigned long long sent, unsigned long long received);

// Lifecycle.  steam_init() is idempotent and safe to call every frame; it returns true when the
// transport is usable.  It never throws and never aborts the game: every failure is a log line.
bool steam_init();
bool steam_ready();
void steam_shutdown();

// Identity.
bool steam_is_fake();
uint64_t steam_user_id();
const char *steam_user_name();   // "" until ready
uint32_t steam_app_id();
int steam_friend_count();
// Friends playing this application (including those whose hosting presence has not arrived yet).
// Returns the total matching friends; writes at most capacity records. Never connects by itself.
size_t steam_friends_playing(lobby_friends::Friend *out, size_t capacity, bool request_presence);

// The lobby Joiner and the game session share Steam's one P2P receive queue. The Joiner reserves it
// while connecting/downloading, so session_frame cannot consume and discard the lobby's replies.
bool steam_lobby_claim();
void steam_lobby_release();
bool steam_lobby_receiving();

// Pump: delivers callbacks and drains P2P packets.  Call once per frame.
void steam_frame();

// A peer on the Steam transport is an ordinary sockaddr_in whose family is STEAM_FAMILY and whose
// Steam ID sits at +8, where sin_zero starts - the encoding v20 uses, so one address describes a peer
// whichever way its bytes travel.  These two turn it around; they are inline so the session code and
// the joiner can use them without linking anything extra.
inline bool steam_peer_make(uint64_t id, sockaddr_in *out)
{
    if (!out || !id)
        return false;
    *out = sockaddr_in();
    out->sin_family = STEAM_FAMILY;
    memcpy(out->sin_zero, &id, sizeof id);
    return true;
}

// Strict decimal parsing: reject zero, suffixes, signs and uint64 overflow rather than joining a
// different peer than the user selected. The stand-in's small numeric IDs remain valid in tests.
inline bool steam_parse_id(const char *text, uint64_t *id)
{
    if (!text || !*text || !id) return false;
    uint64_t value = 0;
    for (const char *p = text; *p; ++p) {
        if (*p < '0' || *p > '9') return false;
        unsigned digit = (unsigned)(*p - '0');
        if (value > (UINT64_MAX - digit) / 10) return false;
        value = value * 10 + digit;
    }
    if (!value) return false;
    *id = value;
    return true;
}

inline uint64_t steam_peer_id(const sockaddr_in *address)
{
    if (!address || address->sin_family != STEAM_FAMILY)
        return 0;
    uint64_t id = 0;
    memcpy(&id, address->sin_zero, sizeof id);
    return id;
}

// Totals since the layer came up: what the transport has actually moved.  The self-test uses them;
// the five-second line is built from the same counters.
void steam_traffic_totals(unsigned long long *sent, unsigned long long *received);

// Datagrams.  Returns false when the peer is unreachable or the transport is off.
bool steam_send(uint64_t peer, const void *data, int len);
// >0 bytes received, 0 nothing pending, -1 transport off.  Fills *peer when something arrives.
int steam_recv(uint64_t *peer, void *data, int capacity);

// Rich presence: the note friends see, which is how they know what to join.
bool steam_presence(const char *value);
const char *steam_presence_value();          // last value we asked Steam to hold
bool steam_presence_check(char *out, size_t size);  // read it back and compare, like v20 does

// Tell friends that this game can be joined, or that it can no longer be joined.
//
// v20 does this in one place (snapshot_operation_saves) and with two presence keys: "gtmf" gets
// "<operation number> game", and "connect" gets the text Steam puts behind the Join Game entry -
// "root\programs\opgame.progpack, +gtmf_join=steam:<our id>".  Clicking that entry starts the
// game with "+gtmf_join=steam:<our id>" on the command line, which steam_note_command_line()
// picks up.  With hosting false both keys are emptied, which hides the game from friends.
bool steam_broadcast(bool hosting, unsigned operation);
const char *steam_connect_text();            // the last "connect" text, for the log and tests

// Join requests: "+gtmf_join=steam:<id>" on the command line, or a friend pressing Join Game.
void steam_note_command_line(const char *command_line);
bool steam_join_take(uint64_t *id);          // true once per request
const char *steam_join_source();             // "the command line" / "a friend's game"

// Glue supplied by steam_module.cpp: read the command line for a join request and bring the layer
// up; deliver callbacks and P2P packets once per frame.  Both are cheap to call repeatedly.
void steam_install();
void steam_tick();

// Test hooks (self-test only; the module never calls them).
void steam_test_set_command_line(const char *text);
void steam_test_join_callback(const char *text, size_t size);
bool steam_test_callback_abi();
void steam_test_reset();
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
bool steam_test_wait_packet(unsigned timeout_ms); // fake transport only; does not consume the packet
#endif

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/steam_friends.h (sha256 464cf3df92be76485efa793227983022ed51aebf62730d63a2c2c4d72f3d6321) ====================
// Platform-independent selection of friends who are running this game.  Steam's flat API is
// adapted in steam.cpp; the same filtering and bounded name handling are exercised by no-game tests.
#pragma once
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>

namespace lobby_friends {

enum { NAME_BYTES = 64, MAX_SCAN = 8192 };
struct Friend {
    uint64_t id;
    char name[NAME_BYTES];            // UTF-8, suitable for display only, never used as a path
    bool hosting;                     // friend advertised our gtmf "<number> game" note
};

inline bool hosting_note(const char *text)
{
    if (!text || *text < '0' || *text > '9') return false;
    while (*text >= '0' && *text <= '9') ++text;
    return strcmp(text, " game") == 0;
}

template<size_t N> inline void copy_name(char (&dst)[N], const char *src)
{
    if (!src) src = "";
    size_t n = 0;
    while (n + 1 < sizeof dst && src[n]) ++n;
    // Steam names are UTF-8. When truncating, do not leave half of a multibyte character.
    if (src[n]) while (n && ((unsigned char)src[n] & 0xc0) == 0x80) --n;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        unsigned char c = (unsigned char)src[i];
        dst[i] = (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7f) ? ' ' : (char)c;
    }
    dst[n] = 0;
}

// Provider supplies count(), id(index), game(id, &game_id), name(id), note(id), request(id).
// CGameID's low 24 bits are the application ID; a mod/shortcut may use its upper bits.
// Returns the total matching friends, with at most capacity entries written.
template<class Provider>
size_t collect(Provider &source, uint32_t app_id, uint64_t self_id, Friend *out, size_t capacity,
               bool request_presence)
{
    if (!app_id || (!out && capacity)) return 0;
    int count = source.count();
    if (count < 0) return 0;
    if (count > MAX_SCAN) count = MAX_SCAN;
    size_t found = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        uint64_t id = source.id(i), game_id = 0;
        if (!id || id == self_id || !source.game(id, &game_id) || (game_id & 0xffffffu) != app_id)
            continue;
        if (found < capacity) {
            // Only ask Steam about entries the UI can actually show; the total is still counted.
            if (request_presence) source.request(id);
            Friend &entry = out[found];
            entry.id = id;
            entry.hosting = hosting_note(source.note(id));
            copy_name(entry.name, source.name(id));
        }
        ++found;
    }
    return found;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/steam_friends.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/steam_logger.cpp (sha256 b4aedc48baa9af51510cf78fca2b24e8229e5c1144ddfca57c25b25d1ecc8c60) ====================
// steam_log for the two programs that have no mp_log: the self-test and the join launcher.
//
// The module forwards Steam's lines into its own log (steam_module.cpp); a console program has no log
// to forward to, so it prints them.  Keeping this in its own file means steam.cpp never needs to know
// which of the two worlds it is in.

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>

// No run log in a console program: the totals are only interesting when something collects them.
void steam_traffic_note(unsigned long long, unsigned long long) {}

void steam_log(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    vprintf(fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
    printf("\n");
    fflush(stdout);

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/steam_module.cpp (sha256 bb27cc38573cedaaeca1692f83e31467da5377fa74a5c12b85c9a76f39a0f451) ====================
// Glue between the Steam layer and the rest of the module.
//
// steam.cpp deliberately knows nothing about this codebase: it only needs `steam_log`.  This file
// supplies that by forwarding to `mp_log`, so Steam lines land in the same log, and it is the single
// place the module touches to switch the layer on.
#include "steam.h"
#include "common.h"
#include "netlog.h"

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>

void steam_log(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    char text[1024];
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    vsprintf_s(text, sizeof(text), fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
    mp_log("%s", text);
}

// The five-second Steam totals, for the run log's summary line.
void steam_traffic_note(unsigned long long sent, unsigned long long received)
{
    netlog_count("steam_sent", (long long)sent);
    netlog_count("steam_received", (long long)received);
}

// Called from session_start() and ui_frame(): read the join request once, then bring the layer up.
// If the game's Steam DLL is loaded late, steam_init() retries at a bounded rate.
void steam_install()
{
    // ui_frame calls this every frame. Re-reading the command line there re-queues the same
    // Join Game request forever and floods the log. The API may still need a later retry if the
    // game's Steam library has not been loaded/initialised yet, so only the parsing is once-only.
    static bool command_line_noted;
    if (!command_line_noted) {
        command_line_noted = true;
        steam_note_command_line(GetCommandLineA());
    }
    steam_init();
}

// Called once per frame from session_frame(): delivers Steam callbacks and P2P packets.
void steam_tick()
{
    if (!steam_ready() && !steam_init()) return;
    steam_frame();
}

// The session and UI now call this layer. Keep the exported table as an anchor for its public
// entry points as well: /OPT:REF must not silently remove an API that is currently reached only by
// a different launcher or test. It also acts as a manifest in the DLL's exports and link map.
extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) const void *const gtmf_steam_layer[] = {
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_init),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_shutdown),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_ready),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_is_fake),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_user_id),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_user_name),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_app_id),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_friend_count),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_frame),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_send),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_recv),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_presence),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_presence_value),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_presence_check),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_broadcast),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_connect_text),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_note_command_line),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_join_take),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_join_source),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_install),
    reinterpret_cast<const void *>(&steam_tick),
};

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/tactical_mode.h (sha256 7167c78d6f778e0c59957394b2fdc50e4071368d94777bcc9fd5c539890b370b) ====================
// Recognise the tactical operation launched directly by starter_x64 when its parent did not set
// MP_HOST_MODE. Require the tactical progpack and the game's comma-delimited 19YY_MM_campaign:battle
// token; an unrelated custom mission must keep its normal full-state policy.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"

namespace tactical_mode {
inline char lower_ascii(char c) { return c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z' ? (char)(c + ('a' - 'A')) : c; }
inline bool tactical_program(const char *line)
{
    if (!line) return false;
    static const char name[] = "tacgame.progpack";
    for (const char *p = line; *p; ++p) {
        if (p != line && p[-1] != '\\' && p[-1] != '/' && p[-1] != ' ' && p[-1] != '"') continue;
        const char *a = p, *b = name;
        while (*a && *b && lower_ascii(*a) == *b) { ++a; ++b; }
        if (!*b && (!*a || *a == ',' || *a == ' ' || *a == '\t' || *a == '"')) return true;
    }
    return false;
}
inline bool operation_battle(const char *line)
{
    if (!tactical_program(line)) return false;
    for (const char *p = line; *p;) {
        while (*p == ' ' || *p == '\t' || *p == '"') ++p;
        const char *end = p;
        while (*end && *end != ',') ++end;
        // Check the field length before accessing the date prefix; malformed short fields are safe.
        if (end - p >= 10 && p[0] == '1' && p[1] == '9' &&
            p[2] >= '0' && p[2] <= '9' && p[3] >= '0' && p[3] <= '9' && p[4] == '_' &&
            p[5] >= '0' && p[5] <= '9' && p[6] >= '0' && p[6] <= '9' && p[7] == '_') {
            for (const char *q = p + 9; q < end; ++q)
                if (*q == ':' && q + 1 < end && q[1] != '"' && q[1] != ' ') return true;
        }
        p = *end ? end + 1 : end;
    }
    return false;
}
// An explicit MP_SYNC_STATE overrides this default exactly as before.
inline bool default_full_state(int host_mode, bool direct_operation_battle)
{
    return host_mode != HOST_MODE_OPERATION && !direct_operation_battle;
}
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/tactical_mode.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/transform_gc.h (sha256 0a54b697f69cb852f7730a86b21235d4b5049f0e774f68071986ff30dc234be9) ====================
// Discard cached transforms for objects no longer present in the current object manager. The key
// set is rebuilt before calling this. Surviving keys and their cached data are left unchanged.
#pragma once
#include <stddef.h>

template <class Cache, class Index>
size_t prune_absent(Cache &cache, const Index &live)
{
    size_t removed = 0;
    for (auto it = cache.begin(); it != cache.end();) {
        if (live.find(it->first) == live.end()) { it = cache.erase(it); ++removed; }
        else ++it;
    }
    return removed;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/transform_gc.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/transform_inbox.h (sha256 30676cda4b5e5e55bfcb7a63da492ae0cad573434eeafe2ed257ccf3812fc614) ====================
// Between physics steps the engine only keeps the last arrival per unit. Coalesce exactly those
// arrivals at receipt so a paused or slow game cannot accumulate repeated copies of the same unit.
// No cross-step snapshot ordering is assumed: the wire format has no connection epoch.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

namespace transforms {
enum class Put { inserted, replaced, invalid, full };
inline bool valid(const UnitRec &r)
{
    if (r.kind != 1 && r.kind != 2) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        if (!std::isfinite(r.pos[i]) || !std::isfinite(r.lin[i]) || !std::isfinite(r.ang[i])) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) if (!std::isfinite(r.rot[i])) return false;
    return true;
}
class Inbox {
    static const size_t limit = 1000000;  // local object tables are bounded at the same size
    std::vector<UnitRec> records_;
    std::unordered_map<uint64_t, size_t> index_;
public:
    Put put(const UnitRec &r)
    {
        if (!valid(r)) return Put::invalid;
        uint64_t key = (uint64_t(r.kind) << 32) | r.id;
        auto it = index_.find(key);
        if (it != index_.end()) { records_[it->second] = r; return Put::replaced; }
        if (records_.size() >= limit) return Put::full;
        index_.emplace(key, records_.size());
        records_.push_back(r);
        return Put::inserted;
    }
    const std::vector<UnitRec> &records() const { return records_; }
    void clear() { records_.clear(); index_.clear(); }
};
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/transform_inbox.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/transform_send.h (sha256 347acb4f830fafd480adc045662afd05ba370787251a8999e4a9a0e4d7cc22c0) ====================
// Tactical sender: compare all three rows of the 3x4 engine rotation (padding is ignored).
// Preserve the native per-row movement threshold and staggered two-second idle refresh.
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <string.h>

namespace transforms {
struct Sent {
    float pos[3] = {}, rot[9] = {};
    uint32_t when = 0;
    bool valid = false;

    bool due(const float *p, const float *r, uint32_t now, uint32_t id, int idle_ms = -1) const {
        if (!valid) return true;
        if (fabsf(p[0] - pos[0]) + fabsf(p[1] - pos[1]) + fabsf(p[2] - pos[2]) > 0.002f) return true;
        for (int row = 0; row < 3; ++row) {
            const float *cur = r + row * 4, *old = rot + row * 3;
            if (fabsf(cur[0] - old[0]) + fabsf(cur[1] - old[1]) + fabsf(cur[2] - old[2]) > 0.0005f) return true;
        }
        // Legacy MP_IDLE_MS is an explicit diagnostic override, not an in-memory code patch.
        // Exactly 2000 keeps the historical stagger, as did the original patch.
        uint32_t interval = idle_ms >= 0 && idle_ms <= 6000 && idle_ms != 2000
                              ? (uint32_t)idle_ms : 2000u + (id % 16u) * 30u;
        return (uint32_t)(now - when) >= interval;
    }
    void remember(const float *p, const float *r, uint32_t now) {
        memcpy(pos, p, sizeof pos);
        for (int row = 0; row < 3; ++row) memcpy(rot + row * 3, r + row * 4, 3 * sizeof(float));
        when = now;
        valid = true;
    }
};
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/transform_send.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/transform_write.h (sha256 6f9699f038e4b20c3a5707ddadcf6a8387061856a9f7a1904d27d87b98e59ae2) ====================
// Exact idempotent-write elision for a remote unit's already-interpolated motion. No epsilon, no
// physics pause, and no change to the shot/trench pipeline: if any written bit differs, perform the
// original writes, including the inverse rotation. Arrays with a 4-float stride retain their padding.
#pragma once
#include <string.h>

inline bool write_remote_transform(float *pos, float *rot, float *inv, float *lin, float *ang,
                                   const float *p, const float *r, const float *v, const float *w, bool moving)
{
    const float zero[3] = {};
    const float *want_lin = moving ? v : zero, *want_ang = moving ? w : zero;
    bool same = !memcmp(pos, p, 3 * sizeof(float)) &&
                !memcmp(lin, want_lin, 3 * sizeof(float)) && !memcmp(ang, want_ang, 3 * sizeof(float));
    for (int i = 0; i < 3 && same; i++) {
        if (memcmp(rot + i * 4, r + i * 3, 3 * sizeof(float))) same = false;
        for (int j = 0; j < 3 && same; j++)
            if (memcmp(inv + i * 4 + j, r + j * 3 + i, sizeof(float))) same = false;
    }
    if (same) return false;
    memcpy(pos, p, 3 * sizeof(float));
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
        rot[i * 4 + j] = r[i * 3 + j];
        inv[j * 4 + i] = r[i * 3 + j];
    }
    memcpy(lin, want_lin, 3 * sizeof(float));
    memcpy(ang, want_ang, 3 * sizeof(float));
    return true;
}
==================== END development/net-v21/src/transform_write.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/transport_policy.h (sha256 12a621b9ef3eb802e0f61f66680192facf70bd1896141dfe4a71cdde82ecbd57) ====================
// A live Steam P2P path is independent of the IP socket in both tactical and operation sessions.
// An IP peer still requires its own UDP socket; the caller retries Steam initialization separately.
#pragma once
namespace transport_policy {
inline bool drive_session(bool tactical, bool udp_open, bool steam_ready)
{
    (void)tactical;
    return udp_open || steam_ready;
}
inline bool send_peer(bool tactical, bool udp_open, bool have_peer, bool steam_address)
{
    (void)tactical;
    return have_peer && (udp_open || steam_address);
}
}
