static bool span_write(const void *ptr, size_t n) {
    if (!ptr || !n) return false;
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION m = {};
    if (!VirtualQuery(ptr, &m, sizeof m) || m.State != MEM_COMMIT || (m.Protect & PAGE_GUARD)) return false;
    DWORD p = m.Protect & 0xff;
    if (p != PAGE_READWRITE && p != PAGE_WRITECOPY && p != PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE && p != PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY) return false;
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t)ptr, b = (uintptr_t)m.BaseAddress;
    return a >= b && a - b <= m.RegionSize && n <= m.RegionSize - (a - b);
}
static bool image_stamp(HMODULE module, uint32_t stamp, uint32_t size) {
    const unsigned char *p = (const unsigned char *)module;
    if (!readable(p, sizeof(IMAGE_DOS_HEADER))) return false;
    const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *dos = (const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER *)p;
    if (dos->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE || dos->e_lfanew < (long)sizeof(*dos) || dos->e_lfanew > 0x100000) return false;
    const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64 *nt = (const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64 *)(p + dos->e_lfanew);
    return readable(nt, sizeof *nt) && nt->Signature == IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE &&
           nt->OptionalHeader.Magic == IMAGE_NT_OPTIONAL_HDR64_MAGIC && nt->FileHeader.TimeDateStamp == stamp &&
           nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage == size;
}
static bool ensure_layout() {
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
    if (mock_layout) return true;
#endif
    if (layout_state) return layout_state > 0;
    HMODULE phys = GetModuleHandleA("adv_physic.codelib");
    if (!phys) return false;
    layout_state = -1;
    if (!image_stamp(phys, 0x66f2a91e, 0xf2000)) {
        mp_log("vitals: unrecognised adv_physic image; no health or vehicle memory is accessed");
        return false;
    }
    const HookSig member[] = { { 0x9c410, member_sig, sizeof member_sig } };
    const HookSig human[] = { { 0x66c90, human_sig, sizeof human_sig } };
    const HookSig techn[] = { { 0x43230, techn_sig, sizeof techn_sig } };
    member_fn = hook_resolve_checked("adv_physic.codelib", member, 1, "vitals squad member lookup");
    void *human_fn = hook_resolve_checked("adv_physic.codelib", human, 1, "vitals human hit layout");
    void *techn_fn = hook_resolve_checked("adv_physic.codelib", techn, 1, "vitals vehicle hit layout");
    if (!member_fn || !human_fn || !techn_fn) {
        mp_log("vitals: engine signatures do not agree; health and vehicle sync stay off");
        return false;
    }
    layout_state = 1;
    mp_log("vitals: checked 3598/1 physics image and member/hit signatures; runtime behaviour remains unverified");
    return true;
}
static int current_mode() {
    int value;
    if (env_integer("MP_VITALS", &value) && value >= 0) return value > 3 ? 3 : value;
    return session_host_mode() == HOST_MODE_OPERATION ? 3 : 1;
}
static int vehicle_mode(int mode) {
    if (!mode) return 0;
    int value;
    if (env_integer("MP_VEHICLES", &value) && value >= 0) return value > 2 ? 2 : value;
    return mode >= 2 ? 2 : 1;
}
static bool stats_mode() { int v; return !env_integer("MP_STATS", &v) || v != 0; }
static bool event_log() { int v; return env_integer("MP_LOG_VITALS", &v) && v != 0; }
static void clear_local() {
    soldiers.clear(); vehicles.clear(); since = last_scan = last_report = scan_no = sanity_seen = sanity_good = 0;
    baseline_mask = 0; on = waiting = baseline_checked = start_checked = broken = false;
    scan_us_total = scan_us_max = 0; scan_samples = 0;
    stats_enabled.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
}
static bool name_and_kind(Object *o, ObjMan *om, bool *machine, uint32_t *id) {
    if (!readable(o, sizeof *o) || o->objman != om || !memchr(o->full, 0, sizeof o->full)) return false;
    if (!readable(o->iface, 8)) return false;
    if (!memcmp(o->iface, "i_human", 8)) *machine = false;
    else if (!memcmp(o->iface, "i_techn", 8)) *machine = true;
    else return false;
    const char *end = (const char *)memchr(o->full, 0, sizeof o->full);
    if (end == o->full) return false;
    if (*machine) { *id = fnv((const unsigned char *)o->full, (size_t)(end - o->full)); return true; }
    const char *last = nullptr;
    for (const char *p = o->full; p < end; ++p) if (*p == '_') last = p;
    if (!last || last + 1 == end || end - last - 1 > 8) return false;
    uint32_t n = 0;
    for (const char *p = last + 1; p < end; ++p) {
        int d = *p >= '0' && *p <= '9' ? *p - '0' : *p >= 'a' && *p <= 'f' ? *p - 'a' + 10 :
                *p >= 'A' && *p <= 'F' ? *p - 'A' + 10 : -1;
        if (d < 0) return false;
        n = (n << 4) | (unsigned)d;
    }
    *id = n; return true;
}
// Reconstruct the checked get_member's array access without calling into the game. A record must
// belong to this exact soldier; all source pointers/counts and each write are independently checked.
static char *squad_record(char *human) {
    if (!readable(human + H_SQUAD, sizeof(void *)) || !readable(human + H_INDEX, 4)) return nullptr;
    char *squad; int index; memcpy(&squad, human + H_SQUAD, sizeof squad); memcpy(&index, human + H_INDEX, sizeof index);
    if (!squad || index < 0 || !readable(squad, S_COUNT + 4)) return nullptr;
    void **vt; memcpy(&vt, squad, sizeof vt);
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
    if (!mock_layout)
#endif
    if (!readable(vt, 0x140) || vt[0x138 / 8] != member_fn) return nullptr;
    char *rows; uint32_t count; memcpy(&rows, squad + S_MEMBERS, sizeof rows); memcpy(&count, squad + S_COUNT, sizeof count);
    if (!rows || count > 100000 || (uint32_t)index >= count || (size_t)index > (SIZE_MAX / M_SIZE)) return nullptr;
    uintptr_t at = (uintptr_t)rows + (size_t)index * M_SIZE;
    if (at < (uintptr_t)rows || !readable((void *)at, M_SIZE)) return nullptr;
    char *rec = (char *)at; void *owner; memcpy(&owner, rec + M_OWNER, sizeof owner);
    return owner == human ? rec : nullptr;
}
static bool set_health(char *human, float new_health) {
    if (!span_write(human + H_SELF, 4)) return false;
    memcpy(human + H_SELF, &new_health, 4); return true;
}
static bool mark_dead(char *human, float health, float rec_health) {
    if (!(health >= 0.f && health < 0.5f)) return false;
    char *rec = squad_record(human);
    // Do not detach an alive soldier if the squad record cannot be proven and updated as well.
    if (!rec || !span_write(rec + M_STATE, 4) || !span_write(rec + M_HEALTH, 4) ||
        !span_write(human + H_SELF, 4) || !span_write(human + H_SQUAD, 8) || !span_write(human + H_SQUAD_COPY, 8)) return false;
    float current; memcpy(&current, rec + M_HEALTH, 4);
    float target = rec_health >= 0.f && rec_health < 1000000.f ? rec_health : 0.f;
    if (current >= 0.f && current < target) target = current;
    uint32_t dead = STATE_KILLED; void *none = nullptr;
    memcpy(rec + M_STATE, &dead, 4); memcpy(rec + M_HEALTH, &target, 4);
    memcpy(human + H_SELF, &health, 4); memcpy(human + H_SQUAD, &none, 8); memcpy(human + H_SQUAD_COPY, &none, 8);
    return true;
}
static void frame(ObjMan *om, uint32_t now, vitals::Send send, bool force);
static void stat_hook(void *object, long long arg2, char arg3) {
    StatFn original = original_stat.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    if (stats_enabled.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && stats_mode() &&
        (uint32_t)(now - stats_last_active.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) < 2000) {
        if (GetCurrentThreadId() == stats_thread.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && last_om && last_send && !in_frame) {
            frame(last_om, now, last_send, true);
            mp_log("vitals: final local scan before the game's statistics (delivery is not guaranteed before the calculation)");
        } else {
            mp_log("vitals: statistics called on another thread or during a scan; no unsafe final scan");
        }
        if (object && span_write((char *)object + STAT_SEED_OFF, 4)) {
            const uint32_t seed = 0x4d495553;
            memcpy((char *)object + STAT_SEED_OFF, &seed, 4);
            mp_log("vitals: checked statistics RNG seed set; equal final results are not guaranteed");
        }
    }
    if (original) original(object, arg2, arg3);
}
static void try_stat_hook() {
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
    if (mock_layout) return;
#endif
    if (stat_tried) return;
    HMODULE game = GetModuleHandleA("adv_game.codelib");
    if (!game) return;
    stat_tried = true;
    if (!image_stamp(game, 0x66f2d3a9, 0x178000)) {
        mp_log("vitals: unknown statistics image; RNG left unchanged"); return;
    }
    const HookSig where[] = { { 0xf80c0, stat_sig, sizeof stat_sig } };
    char *fn = hook_resolve_checked("adv_game.codelib", where, 1, "vitals statistics");
    if (!fn || !readable(fn, 11000)) { mp_log("vitals: statistics body unreadable; RNG left unchanged"); return; }
    unsigned occurrences = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i + sizeof stat_rng <= 11000; ++i)
        if (!memcmp(fn + i, stat_rng, sizeof stat_rng)) ++occurrences;
    if (occurrences != 3 || !install_published_detour(fn, 15, stat_hook, original_stat)) {
        mp_log("vitals: statistics body/patch refused (%u RNG signatures); RNG left unchanged", occurrences); return;
    }
    mp_log("vitals: checked statistics hook installed; only a connected, validated tactical session can set its RNG");
}

static void frame(ObjMan *om, uint32_t now, vitals::Send send, bool force) {
    if (!om || !send || in_frame) return;
    in_frame = true;
    struct Done { ~Done() { in_frame = false; } } done;
    if (!session_connected() || session_role() == ROLE_NONE || !current_mode() || !channel.peer_epoch()) {
        stats_enabled.store(false, std::memory_order_release); return;
    }
    if (!ensure_layout()) { stats_enabled.store(false, std::memory_order_release); return; }
    if (!xlate_ready()) {
        stats_enabled.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
        if (!waiting) { waiting = true; mp_log("vitals: waiting for the checked object-number exchange"); }
        return;
    }
    if (waiting) { waiting = false; mp_log("vitals: number exchange ready; tactical state channel enabled"); }
    if (!on) {
        on = true; since = now; last_scan = now - 100; last_report = now; frame_thread = GetCurrentThreadId();
        mp_log("vitals: on (soldiers %d, vehicles %d); initial validation runs for 3 seconds", current_mode(), vehicle_mode(current_mode()));
    }
    stats_thread.store(frame_thread, std::memory_order_release);
    stats_last_active.store(now, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    bool stat_ready = !broken && start_checked && current_mode() >= 2 && stats_mode();
    // Avoid patching game code before connection, number exchange and initial validation, or
    // at all when statistics synchronisation is disabled.
    if (stat_ready) try_stat_hook();
    stats_enabled.store(stat_ready, std::memory_order_release);
    // A failed or unfinished layout sanity check must neither emit new state nor replay an
    // already queued state. ACK processing itself remains available in the packet handler.
    if (start_checked && !broken) channel.tick(now, send);
    if (broken || (!force && (uint32_t)(now - last_scan) < 100)) return;
    last_scan = now; ++scan_no;
    LARGE_INTEGER scan_begin = {}, scan_end = {};
    bool timing = QueryPerformanceCounter(&scan_begin) != 0;
    if (!readable(om, 0x100)) return;
    uint32_t count; Object **all;
    memcpy(&count, (char *)om + 0xfc, 4); memcpy(&all, (char *)om + 0xf0, sizeof all);
    if (count > 1000000 || (count && !readable(all, (size_t)count * sizeof(*all)))) {
        mp_log("vitals: invalid object list; scan skipped"); return;
    }
    std::vector<Candidate> candidates;
    std::unordered_map<uint64_t, unsigned> ids;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        Object *obj = all[i]; bool machine; uint32_t id;
        if (!name_and_kind(obj, om, &machine, &id)) continue;
        uint64_t key = ((uint64_t)(machine ? 1 : 2) << 32) | id;
        ++ids[key]; candidates.push_back({ obj, id, machine });
    }
    uint32_t seen = 0, dead = 0, veh_seen = 0, veh_nonzero[vitals::vehicle_bytes] = {}, duplicates = 0, applied = 0, refused = 0;
    std::vector<Entry> pending;
    pending.reserve(candidates.size());
    // Rotate traversal so a persistently busy connection does not always privilege the first units.
    size_t start = candidates.empty() ? 0 : ((size_t)scan_no * 137) % candidates.size();
    for (size_t k = 0; k < candidates.size(); ++k) {
        const Candidate &c = candidates[(start + k) % candidates.size()];
        uint64_t key = ((uint64_t)(c.machine ? 1 : 2) << 32) | c.id;
        if (ids[key] != 1) { ++duplicates; continue; }
        char *p = (char *)c.obj;
        if (c.machine) {
            int vm = vehicle_mode(current_mode());
            if (!vm || !readable(p + 0xd00, 0x20)) continue;
            uint8_t values[vitals::vehicle_bytes];
            for (int b = 0; b < vitals::vehicle_bytes; ++b) { values[b] = (uint8_t)p[vehicle_offsets[b]]; if (values[b]) ++veh_nonzero[b]; }
            ++veh_seen;
            if (!baseline_checked) continue;
            for (int b = 0; b < vitals::vehicle_bytes; ++b) if (baseline_mask & (1u << b)) values[b] = 0;
            vitals::Vehicle *remote = channel.find_vehicle(c.id);
            if (remote && start_checked) for (int b = 0; b < vitals::vehicle_bytes; ++b) {
                uint16_t bit = (uint16_t)(1u << b);
                if (!(remote->pending & bit)) continue;
                if (baseline_mask & bit) { remote->pending &= (uint16_t)~bit; continue; }
                if (values[b]) { remote->pending &= (uint16_t)~bit; continue; }
                if (vm >= 2 && span_write(p + vehicle_offsets[b], 1)) {
                    p[vehicle_offsets[b]] = (char)remote->pending_value[b]; values[b] = remote->pending_value[b];
                    remote->pending &= (uint16_t)~bit; ++applied;
                } else if (vm == 1) { remote->pending &= (uint16_t)~bit; ++refused; }
            }
            if (!vehicles.count(c.id) && vehicles.size() >= vitals::max_vehicles) { ++refused; continue; }
            LocalVehicle &local = vehicles[c.id];
            bool any = false;
            for (int b = 0; b < vitals::vehicle_bytes; ++b) {
                uint16_t bit = (uint16_t)(1u << b);
                if (values[b]) {
                    any = true;
                    if (!local.observed[b]) local.transitions |= bit;
                } else local.transitions &= (uint16_t)~bit;
                local.observed[b] = values[b];
            }
            bool changed = memcmp(local.sent, values, sizeof values) != 0;
            if (changed || local.transitions || (any && (uint32_t)(now - local.last_sent) >= 2000))
                pending.push_back(vitals::machine(c.id, values, local.transitions));
            continue;
        }
        if (!readable(p + H_SELF, 4)) continue;
        float health; memcpy(&health, p + H_SELF, 4); ++seen;
        bool valid = health >= 0.f && health <= 2.f;
        if (sanity_seen < 40) { ++sanity_seen; if (valid) ++sanity_good; }
        if (!valid) { ++refused; continue; }
        if (health < 0.5f) ++dead;
        if (!soldiers.count(c.id) && soldiers.size() >= vitals::max_soldiers) { ++refused; continue; }
        LocalSoldier &local = soldiers[c.id];
        const vitals::Soldier *remote = channel.find_soldier(c.id);
        if (remote && start_checked && current_mode() >= 2 && remote->health < health - 0.005f) {
            if (health >= 0.5f && remote->is_dead) {
                if (mark_dead(p, remote->health, remote->record_health)) { health = remote->health; ++applied; }
                else ++refused;
            } else if (health < 0.5f || remote->health >= 0.5f) {
                if (set_health(p, remote->health)) { health = remote->health; ++applied; }
                else ++refused;
            }
        }
        bool is_dead = health < 0.5f;
        if (health < 0.999f && (health < local.sent - 0.005f || (is_dead && !local.told_dead) ||
                                (uint32_t)(now - local.last_sent) >= 2000))
            pending.push_back(vitals::soldier(c.id, health));
    }
    size_t retained = (!start_checked || broken || pending.empty()) ? 0 : channel.enqueue(pending.data(), pending.size(), now, send);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < retained; ++i) {
        const Entry &e = pending[i];
        if (e.flags & vitals::vehicle) {
            auto it = vehicles.find(e.id); if (it != vehicles.end()) {
                vitals::machine_bytes(e, it->second.sent);
                it->second.transitions &= (uint16_t)~(e.flags >> 16);
                it->second.last_sent = now;
            }
        } else {
            auto it = soldiers.find(e.id); if (it != soldiers.end()) {
                it->second.sent = vitals::value(e.a); it->second.told_dead = (e.flags & vitals::dead) != 0; it->second.last_sent = now;
            }
        }
    }
    if (!start_checked && (uint32_t)(now - since) >= 3000 && sanity_seen >= 40) {
        if (sanity_good * 10 < sanity_seen * 9 || (seen >= 20 && dead * 100 > seen * 30)) {
            broken = true; stats_enabled.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
            mp_log("vitals: unexpected initial health/death distribution (%u/%u sane, %u/%u dead); sync stopped", sanity_good, sanity_seen, dead, seen);
        } else { start_checked = true; mp_log("vitals: initial health validation passed (%u/%u samples)", sanity_good, sanity_seen); }
    }
    if (!baseline_checked && (uint32_t)(now - since) >= 3000) {
        baseline_checked = true;
        if (veh_seen >= 10) for (int b = 0; b < vitals::vehicle_bytes; ++b)
            if (veh_nonzero[b] * 100 > veh_seen * 20) baseline_mask |= (uint16_t)(1u << b);
        mp_log("vitals: %u initial vehicles; baseline status mask %03x is excluded", veh_seen, baseline_mask);
    }
    static LARGE_INTEGER frequency = {};
    if (timing && QueryPerformanceCounter(&scan_end) && scan_end.QuadPart >= scan_begin.QuadPart &&
        (frequency.QuadPart || QueryPerformanceFrequency(&frequency)) && frequency.QuadPart > 0) {
        double us = (double)(scan_end.QuadPart - scan_begin.QuadPart) * 1000000.0 / (double)frequency.QuadPart;
        scan_us_total += us; if (us > scan_us_max) scan_us_max = us; ++scan_samples;
    }
    if ((uint32_t)(now - last_report) >= 5000 || (force && event_log())) {
        const vitals::Counters &n = channel.counters();
        mp_log("vitals: soldiers %u dead %u, vehicles %u, applied %u refused %u ambiguous %u; channel sent %llu acked %llu rx %llu retry %llu rejected %llu pending %zu",
               seen, dead, veh_seen, applied, refused, duplicates, n.sent, n.acked, n.received, n.retries, n.rejected, channel.outstanding());
        if (scan_samples) mp_log("vitals: %u scans, average %.2f us, maximum %.2f us (wall time; not FPS)",
                                 scan_samples, scan_us_total / scan_samples, scan_us_max);
        scan_us_total = scan_us_max = 0; scan_samples = 0; last_report = now;
    }
}
} // namespace

void vitals_start() {
    next_nonce += 0x9e3779b9u;
    uint32_t epoch = GetTickCount() ^ (GetCurrentProcessId() * 2654435761u) ^ next_nonce;
    if (!epoch) epoch = 1;
    channel.start(epoch); clear_local(); last_om = active_om = nullptr; last_send = nullptr;
    last_refused_peer = last_refused_at = 0;
}
void vitals_pause() { stats_enabled.store(false, std::memory_order_release); }
uint32_t vitals_epoch() { return channel.epoch(); }
uint32_t vitals_peer_epoch() { return channel.peer_epoch(); }
void vitals_peer(uint32_t epoch) {
    if (channel.set_peer(epoch)) {
        clear_local(); last_refused_peer = last_refused_at = 0;
        mp_log("vitals: peer process identity changed; local state will be republished");
    } else if (epoch && epoch != channel.peer_epoch()) {
        uint32_t now = GetTickCount();
        if (epoch != last_refused_peer || (uint32_t)(now - last_refused_at) >= 5000) {
            last_refused_peer = epoch; last_refused_at = now;
            mp_log("vitals: refused retired peer identity or too many process changes; restart if the current peer cannot connect");
        }
    }
}
void vitals_on_packet(const unsigned char *data, int size, void (*send)(const void *, int)) {
    if (!data || size < 0) return;
    bool ready = session_connected() && session_role() != ROLE_NONE && current_mode() > 0 &&
                 ensure_layout() && xlate_ready() && start_checked && !broken;
    channel.packet(data, (size_t)size, ready, send);
}
void vitals_frame(ObjMan *om, uint32_t now, void (*send)(const void *, int)) {
    if (active_om && om && active_om != om) {
        mp_log("vitals: object manager changed; a fresh tactical handshake is required");
        vitals_start();
    }
    active_om = om; last_om = om; last_send = send; frame(om, now, send, false);
}
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
void vitals_test_mock_layout(bool enabled) { mock_layout = enabled; }
uint32_t vitals_test_peer_epoch() { return channel.peer_epoch(); }
unsigned vitals_test_outstanding() { return (unsigned)channel.outstanding(); }
uint64_t vitals_test_received() { return channel.counters().received; }
void vitals_test_stat_invoke(void *object, void (*original)(void *, long long, char)) {
    if (!mock_layout || original_stat.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return;
    original_stat.store(original, std::memory_order_release);
    stat_hook(object, 17, 1);
    original_stat.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);
}
#endif

==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/vitals_core.h (sha256 2c503d04ff27d9287e992655c460b3e407d161c3c9147b91754c2bd761c439ee) ====================
// Portable, bounded Vitals state channel. Adapted from the monotonic state exchange in
// development/kit-source/src/mp_vitals.c, not from the old DLL's v20 hooks.
//
// Only the session thread calls this class. The session checks the peer address (not a
// cryptographic identity) and supplies an epoch learned from the versioned Hello/Welcome. Receipt
// and validation precede the ACK; a full table or an unavailable number exchange is never ACKed.
// Packet loss, reordering and duplicates are normal. Soldiers merge by minimum health/death;
// vehicle flags use per-vehicle sequence numbers plus explicit nonzero-transition bits, so a
// later 0->1 transition survives even when the intervening zero packet arrives out of order.
#pragma once
#include "protocol.h"
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <deque>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>

namespace vitals {
enum { dead = 1, hurt = 2, record = 4, vehicle = 8, vehicle_bytes = 9 };
enum { per_packet = 70, max_packets = 32, max_soldiers = 6144, max_vehicles = 3072, max_retired = 64, retry_ms = 400 };

#pragma pack(push, 1)
// Data identifies both process incarnations. ACK echoes both epochs and the exact sequence.
struct PacketHead { Head h; uint32_t epoch, target, seq; };
struct Entry { uint32_t id, flags, a, b; };
#pragma pack(pop)
static_assert(sizeof(PacketHead) == 20 && sizeof(Entry) == 16, "vitals wire layout");
static_assert(sizeof(PacketHead) + per_packet * sizeof(Entry) <= 1200, "vitals datagram MTU");

inline uint32_t bits(float value) { uint32_t b; memcpy(&b, &value, sizeof b); return b; }
inline float value(uint32_t b) { float v; memcpy(&v, &b, sizeof v); return v; }
inline Entry soldier(uint32_t id, float health) {
    Entry e = { id, (uint32_t)(hurt | (health < 0.5f ? dead : 0)), bits(health), 0 };
    return e;
}
inline Entry machine(uint32_t id, const uint8_t b[vehicle_bytes], uint16_t transitions = 0) {
    Entry e = { id, (uint32_t)(vehicle | ((uint32_t)b[8] << 8) | ((uint32_t)transitions << 16)), 0, 0 };
    memcpy(&e.a, b, 4); memcpy(&e.b, b + 4, 4); return e;
}
inline void machine_bytes(const Entry &e, uint8_t b[vehicle_bytes]) {
    memcpy(b, &e.a, 4); memcpy(b + 4, &e.b, 4); b[8] = (uint8_t)(e.flags >> 8);
}
inline bool valid(const Entry &e) {
    if (e.flags & vehicle) {
        if (e.flags & ~(uint32_t)(vehicle | 0xff00u | 0x01ff0000u)) return false;
        uint8_t b[vehicle_bytes]; machine_bytes(e, b);
        uint16_t transitions = (uint16_t)(e.flags >> 16);
        for (int k = 0; k < vehicle_bytes; ++k) if ((transitions & (1u << k)) && !b[k]) return false;
        return true;
    }
    if (e.flags & ~(uint32_t)(dead | hurt | record)) return false;
    if (!(e.flags & hurt)) return false;
    float h = value(e.a);
    if (!(h >= 0.f && h <= 2.f)) return false; // rejects NaN, infinity, negative and implausible values
    if (((e.flags & dead) != 0) != (h < 0.5f)) return false;
    if (e.flags & record) { float r = value(e.b); if (!(r >= 0.f && r < 1000000.f)) return false; }
    return true;
}

struct Soldier {
    float health = 9.f, record_health = 1000000.f;
    bool is_dead = false;
};
struct Vehicle {
    uint32_t last_seq = 0;
    uint16_t peer_nonzero = 0, pending = 0;
    uint8_t pending_value[vehicle_bytes] = {};
};
struct Counters {
    uint64_t sent = 0, received = 0, acked = 0, retries = 0, rejected = 0, full = 0;
};
typedef void (*Send)(const void *, int);

class Channel {
public:
    void start(uint32_t epoch) {
        epoch_ = epoch; peer_ = seq_ = 0; retired_count_ = 0;
        out_.clear(); soldiers_.clear(); vehicles_.clear(); counts_ = Counters{};
    }
    uint32_t epoch() const { return epoch_; }
    uint32_t peer_epoch() const { return peer_; }
    size_t outstanding() const { return out_.size(); }
    const Counters &counters() const { return counts_; }

    // Called only with a nonce from a verified current peer's Hello/Welcome. A peer process change
    // invalidates its pending data and requires the engine adapter to republish local state. Keep
    // our sequence/epoch: the other endpoint may have restarted while we stayed alive.
    bool set_peer(uint32_t epoch) {
        if (!epoch || epoch == peer_) return false;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < retired_count_; ++i) if (retired_[i] == epoch) return false;
        if (peer_) {
            // Never forget an old incarnation and accidentally accept it again. On exhaustion,
            // require a process restart instead of silently evicting a retired epoch.
            if (retired_count_ == max_retired) return false;
            retired_[retired_count_++] = peer_;
        }
        peer_ = epoch; out_.clear(); soldiers_.clear(); vehicles_.clear(); return true;
    }
    const Soldier *find_soldier(uint32_t id) const {
        auto it = soldiers_.find(id); return it == soldiers_.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
    }
    Vehicle *find_vehicle(uint32_t id) {
        auto it = vehicles_.find(id); return it == vehicles_.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
    }

    // On saturation returns the number actually retained. The caller must not mark the rest sent.
    // Retained data never expires or evicts older entries while connected; retries are bounded in
    // memory and the adapter can retry unsent entries on the next scan.
    size_t enqueue(const Entry *items, size_t count, uint32_t now, Send send) {
        if (!epoch_ || !peer_ || !items || !send) return 0;
        size_t at = 0;
        while (at < count && out_.size() < max_packets && seq_ != UINT32_MAX) {
            size_t n = count - at < (size_t)per_packet ? count - at : (size_t)per_packet;
            bool okay = true; for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) if (!valid(items[at + i])) okay = false;
            if (!okay) { ++counts_.rejected; break; }
            PacketHead h = { make_head(PKT_VITALS, (int)n), epoch_, peer_, ++seq_ };
            Tx tx; tx.seq = h.seq; tx.sent_at = now; tx.bytes.resize(sizeof h + n * sizeof(Entry));
            memcpy(tx.bytes.data(), &h, sizeof h);
            memcpy(tx.bytes.data() + sizeof h, items + at, n * sizeof(Entry));
            out_.push_back(tx);
            ++counts_.sent;
            send(out_.back().bytes.data(), (int)out_.back().bytes.size());
            at += n;
        }
        if (at < count) ++counts_.full;
        return at;
    }
    void tick(uint32_t now, Send send) {
        if (!send) return;
        for (Tx &tx : out_) if ((uint32_t)(now - tx.sent_at) >= retry_ms) {
            tx.sent_at = now; ++counts_.retries; send(tx.bytes.data(), (int)tx.bytes.size());
        }
    }

    // A false ready gate leaves data unacknowledged for retry. ACKs are independent of the gate.
    bool packet(const void *data, size_t size, bool ready, Send send) {
        if (!data || size < sizeof(PacketHead)) { ++counts_.rejected; return false; }
        PacketHead h; memcpy(&h, data, sizeof h);
        if (h.h.magic != k_magic || h.h.flags != 0 || !h.epoch || !h.target || !h.seq) { ++counts_.rejected; return false; }
        if (h.h.type == PKT_VITALS_ACK) {
            if (size != sizeof h || h.h.count || h.epoch != epoch_ || h.target != peer_) { ++counts_.rejected; return false; }
            for (auto it = out_.begin(); it != out_.end(); ++it) if (it->seq == h.seq) {
                out_.erase(it); ++counts_.acked; return true;
            }
            return false; // stale/duplicate ACK cannot retire another packet
        }
        if (h.h.type != PKT_VITALS || !ready || !send || h.epoch != peer_ || h.target != epoch_ ||
            !h.h.count || h.h.count > per_packet || size != sizeof h + h.h.count * sizeof(Entry)) {
            ++counts_.rejected; return false;
        }
        Entry entries[per_packet];
        memcpy(entries, (const uint8_t *)data + sizeof h, h.h.count * sizeof(Entry));
        std::unordered_set<uint32_t> new_soldiers, new_vehicles;
        for (unsigned i = 0; i < h.h.count; ++i) {
            const Entry &e = entries[i];
            if (!valid(e)) { ++counts_.rejected; return false; }
            if (e.flags & vehicle) { if (!vehicles_.count(e.id)) new_vehicles.insert(e.id); }
            else if (!soldiers_.count(e.id)) new_soldiers.insert(e.id);
        }
        if (new_soldiers.size() > max_soldiers - soldiers_.size() ||
            new_vehicles.size() > max_vehicles - vehicles_.size()) { ++counts_.full; return false; }
        for (unsigned i = 0; i < h.h.count; ++i) {
            const Entry &e = entries[i];
            if (e.flags & vehicle) {
                Vehicle &v = vehicles_[e.id];
                if (h.seq <= v.last_seq) continue; // ignore an old zero or stale nonzero transition
                v.last_seq = h.seq;
                uint8_t b[vehicle_bytes]; machine_bytes(e, b);
                uint16_t transitions = (uint16_t)(e.flags >> 16);
                for (int k = 0; k < vehicle_bytes; ++k) {
                    uint16_t bit = (uint16_t)(1u << k);
                    if (b[k] && ((transitions & bit) || !(v.peer_nonzero & bit))) {
                        v.pending |= bit; v.pending_value[k] = b[k];
                    }
                    if (b[k]) v.peer_nonzero |= bit; else v.peer_nonzero &= (uint16_t)~bit;
                }
            } else {
                Soldier &s = soldiers_[e.id]; float health = value(e.a);
                if (health < s.health) s.health = health;
                if (e.flags & dead) s.is_dead = true;
                if (e.flags & record) { float r = value(e.b); if (r < s.record_health) s.record_health = r; }
            }
        }
        ++counts_.received;
        PacketHead ack = { make_head(PKT_VITALS_ACK), h.epoch, h.target, h.seq };
        send(&ack, sizeof ack);
        return true;
    }
private:
    struct Tx { uint32_t seq = 0, sent_at = 0; std::vector<uint8_t> bytes; };
    uint32_t epoch_ = 0, peer_ = 0, seq_ = 0, retired_[max_retired] = {};
    size_t retired_count_ = 0;
    std::deque<Tx> out_;
    std::unordered_map<uint32_t, Soldier> soldiers_;
    std::unordered_map<uint32_t, Vehicle> vehicles_;
    Counters counts_;
};
} // namespace vitals
==================== END development/net-v21/src/vitals_core.h ====================


==================== BEGIN development/net-v21/src/xlate.cpp (sha256 a21315034a5fd9773af6757db1c64d2b5abb8ae1668c22b8c3c296c66b758e3e) ====================
// Numbers that mean different things in the two games.
//
// The engine's savegame code never writes a pointer. A pointer to a description (ammunition, weapon,
// equipment ...) is written as the number the object manager gave that description when it was registered,
// and a pointer to another object as that object's place in the object manager's list. In one game, and in
// a saved game loaded later, those numbers are simply right. Between two games running the same battle
// they are only right if both registered and created everything in the same order - true for a skirmish,
// not for an operation battle (seen in Saur-Mogila: 2697 descriptions in both games, 1432 of them under
// different numbers). A unit state from the other game loaded with the wrong numbers points at the wrong
// things: that was the crash of state replication in operation battles (adv_game+0xd9917: a "name" read
// through a description that was not one).
//
// So each game tells the other what its numbers stand for - per description a fingerprint of its contents,
// per object a hash of its name - and while a state of the other game is being loaded, the object manager's
// two look-ups (virtual slots 57: description by number, 54: object by place) are given the local number
// for the thing the other game meant. The tables travel on the shots' reliable channel (ShotRec.kind 3, 4).
#include "common.h"
#include "protocol.h"
#include "hooks.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <map>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

typedef void *(*Lookup)(ObjMan *, u32);
static Lookup g_obj_by_place, g_desc_by_num;
static std::unordered_map<u32, u32> g_desc, g_obj;          // the other game's number -> this game's
static std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> g_their_desc, g_their_obj;      // (number, fingerprint) as received
static std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> g_last_desc, g_last_obj;        // last tables actually queued
static u32 g_expect_desc, g_expect_obj, g_sent_desc, g_sent_obj;
static bool g_ready, g_hooked, g_hook_tried, g_send_due;
static void *g_vtbl_copy[97];

static u32 text_hash(const char *s, size_t max)
{
    u32 h = 2166136261u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < max && s[i]; i++) h = (h ^ (unsigned char)s[i]) * 16777619u;
    return h;
}

// What a description is, whatever its address: its first 0x60 bytes, with every value that is a pointer
// replaced by the text it points at (names are) or left out.
static u32 fingerprint(const void *p)
{
    if (!readable(p, 0x60)) return 0;
    u64 w[12];
    memcpy(w, p, sizeof w);
    u32 h = 2166136261u;
    for (u64 v : w) {
        if (v >= 0x10000 && v < 0x800000000000ull && readable((void *)v, 16)) {
            const unsigned char *s = (const unsigned char *)v;
            int n = 0;
            while (n < 16 && s[n] >= 0x20 && s[n] < 0x7f) n++;
            if (n >= 3 && (n == 16 || !s[n])) {
                // Only the first 16 bytes were checked. A long name can end at a page boundary:
                // hash a fully readable 64-byte span fast, otherwise check every additional byte.
                if (n < 16 || readable(s, 64)) v = text_hash((const char *)s, 64);
                else {
                    u32 hash = text_hash((const char *)s, 16);
                    bool safe = true;
                    for (size_t i = 16; i < 64; i++) {
                        if (!readable(s + i, 1)) { safe = false; break; }
                        if (!s[i]) break;
                        hash = (hash ^ s[i]) * 16777619u;
                    }
                    v = safe ? hash : 0;
                }
            } else v = 0;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) h = (h ^ (unsigned char)(v >> (i * 8))) * 16777619u;
    }
    return h ? h : 1;
}

#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
// Exercise the production fingerprint reader against guarded pages without touching game code.
u32 xlate_test_fingerprint(const void *p) { return fingerprint(p); }
bool xlate_test_tables_complete() { return g_ready; }
#endif

static void local_tables(ObjMan *om, std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> &desc, std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> &obj)
{
    const char *base = (const char *)om;
    u32 n = *(const u32 *)(base + 0x1754);
    const char *rows = *(const char **)(base + 0x1748);
    if (n < 100000 && readable(rows, (size_t)n * 0x10))
        for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++)
            desc.push_back({ *(const u32 *)(rows + (size_t)i * 0x10), fingerprint(*(void **)(rows + (size_t)i * 0x10 + 8)) });
    u32 m = *(const u32 *)(base + 0x15c);
    Object **list = *(Object ***)(base + 0x150);
    if (m < 1000000 && readable(list, (size_t)m * 8))
        for (u32 i = 0; i < m; i++)
            if (readable(list[i], sizeof(Object))) obj.push_back({ i, text_hash(list[i]->full, sizeof list[i]->full) });
}

static void send_table(const std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> &t, unsigned char kind)
{
    const size_t per = (sizeof(((ShotRec *)0)->launch) - 8) / 8;
    for (size_t at = 0; at < t.size() || at == 0; at += per) {
        ShotRec s = {};
        s.kind = kind;
        u32 total = (u32)t.size(), n = (u32)(t.size() - at < per ? t.size() - at : per);
        memcpy(s.launch, &total, 4);
        memcpy(s.launch + 4, &n, 4);
        for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            memcpy(s.launch + 8 + i * 8, &t[at + i].first, 4);
            memcpy(s.launch + 12 + i * 8, &t[at + i].second, 4);
        }
        shots_queue(s);
        if (t.empty()) break;
    }
}

// The other game's numbers matched to this game's. Both games hold the same things, registered in runs
// (everything one kind of unit needs, then the next kind's ...) - the runs come in another order, inside a
// run the order is the same. So: what has a fingerprint found exactly once in each game is matched by it
// (an anchor); everything else is matched by its distance from the nearest anchor before it, or failing
// that the nearest one after it. (A fingerprint alone is not enough for the rest: about half the
// descriptions hold values in their first bytes that differ from game to game.)
static void match(const std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> &theirs, const std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> &mine,
                  std::unordered_map<u32, u32> &out, const char *what)
{
    std::map<u32, int> count_t, count_m;
    std::map<u32, size_t> where_m;
    for (auto &t : theirs) count_t[t.second]++;
    for (size_t j = 0; j < mine.size(); j++) { count_m[mine[j].second]++; where_m[mine[j].second] = j; }
    const size_t none = (size_t)-1;
    std::vector<size_t> to(theirs.size(), none);            // place in `mine` for each of `theirs`
    std::vector<char> taken(mine.size(), 0);
    u32 anchors = 0, by_place = 0, by_place_same = 0, lost = 0, same = 0, moved = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < theirs.size(); i++) {
        u32 fp = theirs[i].second;
        if (fp && count_t[fp] == 1 && count_m.count(fp) && count_m[fp] == 1) { to[i] = where_m[fp]; taken[to[i]] = 1; anchors++; }
    }
    for (int pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {
        // pass 0: from the anchor before; pass 1: from the anchor after
        size_t ai = none;
        for (size_t n = 0; n < theirs.size(); n++) {
            size_t i = pass ? theirs.size() - 1 - n : n;
            if (to[i] != none && (pass || true)) {
                // only true anchors and earlier results carry on a run
                ai = i;
                continue;
            }
            if (ai == none) continue;
            long long j = (long long)to[ai] + ((long long)i - (long long)ai);
            if (j < 0 || j >= (long long)mine.size() || taken[(size_t)j]) { continue; }
            to[i] = (size_t)j;
            taken[(size_t)j] = 1;
            by_place++;
            if (mine[(size_t)j].second == theirs[i].second) by_place_same++;
            ai = i;
        }
    }
    out.clear();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < theirs.size(); i++) {
        if (to[i] == none) { lost++; continue; }
        out[theirs[i].first] = mine[to[i]].first;
        if (mine[to[i]].first == theirs[i].first) same++; else moved++;
    }
    mp_log("numbers: %zu %s of the other game: %u have the same number here, %u another one, %u could not be placed (%u matched by what they are, %u by their place in a run - %u of those look alike too)",
           theirs.size(), what, same, moved, lost, anchors, by_place, by_place_same);
}

void xlate_received(const ShotRec &s, ObjMan *om)
{
    if (s.kind != 3 && s.kind != 4) return;
    u32 total, n;
    memcpy(&total, s.launch, 4);
    memcpy(&n, s.launch + 4, 4);
    std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> &t = s.kind == 3 ? g_their_desc : g_their_obj;
    u32 &expect = s.kind == 3 ? g_expect_desc : g_expect_obj;
    const u32 limit = s.kind == 3 ? 100000u : 1000000u;  // same bounds as local_tables
    const bool starting = t.size() >= expect;
    if (total > limit || n > 17 || n > total || (total && !n) ||
        (!starting && total != expect) || (!starting && n > expect - t.size())) {
        g_ready = false;
        g_their_desc.clear(); g_their_obj.clear();
        g_expect_desc = g_expect_obj = 0;
        mp_log("numbers: invalid or inconsistent remote table; full-state loading remains off");
        return;
    }
    if (starting) { t.clear(); g_ready = false; expect = total; }  // a new table begins
    for (u32 i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        u32 id, hash;
        memcpy(&id, s.launch + 8 + i * 8, 4);
        memcpy(&hash, s.launch + 12 + i * 8, 4);
        t.push_back({ id, hash });
    }
    if (g_their_desc.size() >= g_expect_desc && g_their_obj.size() >= g_expect_obj && g_expect_obj) {
        std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> desc, obj;
        local_tables(om, desc, obj);
        match(g_their_desc, desc, g_desc, "descriptions");
        match(g_their_obj, obj, g_obj, "objects");
        g_ready = true;
    }
}

// Tables alone cannot make remote object-state loads safe: the checked vtable hooks must also
// be installed. Shots may still use the tables separately via xlate_desc().
bool xlate_ready(void) { return g_ready && g_hooked; }
u32 xlate_desc(u32 theirs) { auto it = g_desc.find(theirs); return it == g_desc.end() ? theirs : it->second; }
bool xlate_desc_checked(u32 theirs, u32 *local)
{
    if (!local) return false;
    if (theirs == 0xffffffffu) { *local = theirs; return true; }
    if (!g_ready) return false;
    auto it = g_desc.find(theirs);
    if (it == g_desc.end()) return false;
    *local = it->second;
    return true;
}

static void *obj_by_place_hook(ObjMan *om, u32 place)
{
    if (g_ready && state_remote_loading()) { auto it = g_obj.find(place); if (it != g_obj.end()) place = it->second; }
    return g_obj_by_place(om, place);
}

static void *desc_by_num_hook(ObjMan *om, u32 num)
{
    if (g_ready && state_remote_loading()) num = xlate_desc(num);
    return g_desc_by_num(om, num);
}

void xlate_resend(void) { g_send_due = true; g_ready = false; g_their_desc.clear(); g_their_obj.clear(); g_expect_desc = g_expect_obj = 0; }

static void send_changed_tables(ObjMan *om)
{
    u32 nd = *(u32 *)((char *)om + 0x1754), no = *(u32 *)((char *)om + 0x15c);
    if (!g_send_due && nd == g_sent_desc && no == g_sent_obj) return;
    std::vector<std::pair<u32, u32>> desc, obj;
    local_tables(om, desc, obj);
    // Counts can change as temporary objects come and go even when the transferable table did
    // not. Queue only tables whose actual contents changed, but always send both after a reset.
    if (g_send_due || desc != g_last_desc) send_table(desc, 3);
    if (g_send_due || obj != g_last_obj) send_table(obj, 4);
    g_last_desc.swap(desc);
    g_last_obj.swap(obj);
    g_sent_desc = nd;
    g_sent_obj = no;
    g_send_due = false;
}
#ifdef NETV21_TEST_HOOKS
void xlate_test_send_changed(ObjMan *om) { send_changed_tables(om); }
#endif

// Every frame of a connected battle: this game's tables go out once the battle stands, and again when
// something new has been registered.
void xlate_frame(ObjMan *om)
{
    if (!session_connected() || session_role() == ROLE_NONE) return;
    if (!g_hook_tried) {
        HMODULE shell = GetModuleHandleA("base_shell.codelib");
        if (!shell) return;                    // the game can load the shell after the first frame
        g_hook_tried = true;
        static const unsigned char obj_lookup_sig[] = {
            0x3b,0x91,0x5c,0x01,0x00,0x00,0x73,0x0e,0x48,0x8b,0x81,0x50,0x01,
            0x00,0x00,0x8b,0xd2,0x48,0x8b,0x04,0xd0,0xc3,0x33,0xc0,0xc3
        };
        static const unsigned char desc_lookup_sig[] = {
            0x40,0x53,0x44,0x8b,0xd2,0x48,0x8b,0xd9,0x83,0xfa,0xff,0x75,
            0x04,0x33,0xc0,0x5b,0xc3,0x45,0x33,0xdb,0x48,0x89,0x74,0x24
        };
        const HookSig obj_where[] = { { 0x98e0, obj_lookup_sig, sizeof obj_lookup_sig } };
        const HookSig desc_where[] = { { 0x121d0, desc_lookup_sig, sizeof desc_lookup_sig } };
        char *obj_fn = hook_resolve_checked("base_shell.codelib", obj_where, 1, "object number lookup");
        char *desc_fn = hook_resolve_checked("base_shell.codelib", desc_where, 1, "description number lookup");
        void **vt = readable(om, sizeof *om) ? om->vtbl : nullptr;
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi = {};
        bool table_ok = vt && (uintptr_t)vt >= sizeof(void *) && readable(vt - 1, sizeof g_vtbl_copy) &&
                        VirtualQuery(vt - 1, &mbi, sizeof mbi) && mbi.AllocationBase == shell;
        if (obj_fn && desc_fn && table_ok && vt[54] == obj_fn && vt[57] == desc_fn && om->vtbl == vt) {
            memcpy(g_vtbl_copy, vt - 1, sizeof g_vtbl_copy);
            g_obj_by_place = (Lookup)vt[54];
            g_desc_by_num = (Lookup)vt[57];
            g_vtbl_copy[1 + 54] = (void *)obj_by_place_hook;
            g_vtbl_copy[1 + 57] = (void *)desc_by_num_hook;
            om->vtbl = g_vtbl_copy + 1;
            g_hooked = true;
            mp_log("numbers: verified object/description lookup hooks installed (vtable +0x%lx)", hook_offset("base_shell.codelib", vt));
        } else {
            mp_log("numbers: lookup bytes or vtable did not match; remote full-state loading stays off");
        }
        g_send_due = true;
    }
    static DWORD last;
    DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    if (now - last < 3000) return;
    last = now;
    send_changed_tables(om);
}
